Through a frequency-swapping analysis, we further reveal that the discriminative cues exploited by the detectors are mainly localized in the low-to-mid frequency range, rather than only in the high-frequency range, as is the case for artifacts commonly associated with generative models. Finally, a latent-space analysis shows that regenerated images exhibit reduced variance and effective dimensionality, indicating that diffusion models do not fully reproduce the variability of real data. Overall, our results suggest that foundation-model-based detectors succeed by capturing non-semantic low-to-mid frequency distributional discrepancies between real and diffusion-generated images. These findings provide new insight into the robustness and generalization of such detectors and suggest directions for more interpretable forensic methods.

</details>


### [80] [Context Blindness in DPO: Mitigating Object Hallucination in MLLMs via Context-Calibrated Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12158)
*Byungoh Ko,Jinyoung Park,Jongha Kim,Jeehye Na,Jaewon Cho,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Contextual Preference Gain (CPG)指标用于量化多模态大语言模型(MLLM)在偏好优化中利用上下文相关信息的程度，发现高CPG与低幻觉率相关，但标准DPO及其变体CPG有限。为此提出Context-Calibrated DPO (C2-DPO)，直接最大化CPG同时保持原始偏好顺序，在多个基准上显著降低物体幻觉率（如Qwen2-VL-Instruct-2B降低36%）且不损失通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型存在物体幻觉问题，生成与视觉输入不一致的虚假描述。DPO通过偏好优化缓解幻觉，近期工作进一步丰富偏好数据中的相关上下文，但不清楚DPO是否真正利用了这些上下文信息，缺乏衡量该利用程度的机制。

Method: 1) 提出Contextual Preference Gain (CPG)简单指标，度量提供相关上下文后模型偏好增强的程度。2) 通过实验发现标准DPO及其变体CPG较低，表明上下文信息未被充分利用。3) 提出Context-Calibrated DPO (C2-DPO)方法，在保持原始偏好排序的同时直接最大化CPG，引导模型更充分地从上下文中提取消幻觉信号。

Result: 在多个基准上，C2-DPO显著降低物体幻觉率且不损害通用推理能力，其中在Object HalBench上将Qwen2-VL-Instruct-2B的幻觉率相对降低36%。同时验证了CPG指标与幻觉率之间的负相关性。

Conclusion: 上下文信息在DPO中的利用程度与幻觉缓解效果密切相关，通过显式优化CPG可以更有效地利用上下文来抑制物体幻觉，为MLLM幻觉缓解提供了新思路和可复现的工具。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made rapid progress, yet they still exhibit object hallucination, generating plausible but incorrect descriptions that are inconsistent with the visual input. Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) mitigates this by training models to prefer non-hallucinated responses over hallucinated ones, and recent efforts further enrich the preference data with relevant context. However, it remains unclear whether DPO actually leverages such context. To investigate this, we propose Contextual Preference Gain (CPG), a simple metric that measures how much a model's preference strengthens when relevant context is provided. We find that higher CPG consistently corresponds to lower hallucination, yet standard DPO and its variants exhibit only limited CPG, indicating that they underutilize contextual information and thus remain prone to hallucination. To address this, we propose Context-Calibrated DPO (C$^2$-DPO), which directly maximizes CPG while preserving the original preference ordering. Across multiple benchmarks, C$^2$-DPO substantially reduces hallucination without compromising general reasoning, relatively reducing the Object HalBench hallucination rate of Qwen2-VL-Instruct-2B by 36%. Code is available at https://github.com/mlvlab/C2-DPO

</details>


### [81] [TGRHuman: Text-Guided Realistic 3D Human Generation via Diffusion Renderer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12175)
*Muxin Zhang,Chaohui Yu,Yuanwang Yang,Min Wei,Zhuo Su,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TGRHuman是一种从文本生成真实3D人体的新方法，通过解耦几何与纹理生成，利用显式多视图观测生成与优化替代隐式分数蒸馏优化，实现高质量且一致的3D人体几何与纹理的高效生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本生成3D人体方法在生成高质量几何与纹理时，仍面临3D一致性差和推理效率低的问题；基于NeRF的隐式分数蒸馏优化方法速度慢且效果受限。

Method: 该方法将几何与纹理生成解耦。几何方面：提出高分辨率生成模块生成多视图法线图，配合几何雕刻策略保持视图一致性并支持宽松衣物。纹理方面：通过密集环绕视图采样获得空间一致的RGB观测，结合精心设计的纹理先验获取策略和扩散渲染器，实现精细的人体纹理合成。

Result: 实验表明TGRHuman在两个关键维度上均优于现有方法：几何质量（更精确的3D人体形状）与纹理质量（更细致的表面纹理），同时保持3D一致性和高效推理。

Conclusion: 显式多视图观测生成与优化策略是提升文本驱动3D人体生成质量与效率的有效途径，解耦几何与纹理设计可有效规避NeRF类方法固有的问题。

Abstract: Realistic 3D human generation plays a crucial role in many graphics applications. However, current methods still struggle to generate high-quality human geometry and texture while maintaining 3D consistency and inference efficiency. In this work, we address these limitations by introducing TGRHuman, a novel approach for generating realistic 3D humans from text. Our method decouples geometry and texture generation to alleviate the issues commonly encountered in NeRF-based methods. Instead of relying on slow, implicit score-distillation-based optimization, we directly use explicit multi-view observation generation and optimization for efficient 3D synthesis. For geometry generation, we propose a high-resolution generative module for multi-view normals together with a geometry-carving strategy that preserves view consistency and supports loose clothing. For texture generation, we produce spatially consistent RGB observations from densely sampled surrounding views using a carefully designed texture-prior acquisition strategy and a diffusion renderer, enabling detailed human texture synthesis. Experiments show that our method can generate high-quality and consistent 3D human geometry and texture efficiently. TGRHuman outperforms existing text-to-3D human methods in both geometry and texture quality.

</details>


### [82] [Map-Det3D: Metric Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction Prior for Multi-view 3D Object Detection from Streaming Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12179)
*Yung-Hsu Yang,Luigi Piccinelli,Samuel Rota Bulò,Sunghwan Hong,Denis Rozumny,Johannes Schönberger,Zuria Bauer,Hermann Blum,Peter Kontschieder,Marc Pollefeys*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Map-Det3D，一种在线多视角3D目标检测模型，通过将短时间窗口映射为多视角并利用前馈度量3D重建模型作为几何骨干，直接在度量3D空间中预测目标框，避免传统的2D到3D提升方式，实现了稳定的单目3D检测和跨域鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D目标检测中，深度特别是绝对尺度在单张图像中欠约束，传统的2D检测后预测3D属性的方法脆弱，距离误差会主导3D定位，且尺度先验在相机、运动或环境发生域偏移时容易失效。

Method: 提出Map-Det3D在线多视角3D目标检测模型：将短时间窗映射到多个视角，复用前馈度量3D重建模型作为几何骨干并调整其目标感知能力，直接在重建的度量3D空间中预测3D框，无需2D-to-3D提升。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，Map-Det3D支持强在线性能和无需适应的鲁棒跨域迁移。

Conclusion: 训练重建先验用于检测是获得稳定度量3D单目检测的实用路线，为单目视频中的3D检测提供了新方向。

Abstract: Metric 3D object detection is a core capability for embodied agents, yet most reliable systems lean on depth sensors, trading away cost, power, and integration simplicity. This motivates monocular 3D detection, which avoids additional constraints, yet it faces a major obstacle: from a single image, depth, and especially absolute scale, are underconstrained. As a result, the prevailing pattern of detecting in 2D and then predicting 3D attributes is often brittle, since modest range errors can dominate 3D localization, and the learned scale prior can fail when cameras, motion, or environments undergo domain shifts. To address this, we propose Map-Det3D, an online multi-view 3D object detection model that brings detection directly into a 3D space reconstructed from RGB. We map a short temporal window into multiple views and repurpose a feed-forward metric 3D reconstruction model as our geometric backbone while tuning its object-aware capabilities. Building on this representation, Map-Det3D directly predicts boxes in metric 3D space, without the widely used 2D-to-3D lifting. Experiments across different benchmarks show that this design supports strong online performance and robust transfer without adaptation, suggesting that training reconstruction priors for detection is a practical route to stable metric 3D detection from monocular video. Code and models are available at https://royyang0714.github.io/Map-Det3D.

</details>


### [83] [GenFAR: A generalized representation of brain structure, derived from 49,246 multi-cohort MRIs via deep learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12185)
*Vishnu M. Bashyam,Guray Erus,Junhao Wen,Pratik Chaudhari,Randa Melhem,Sindhuja Govindarajan Tirumalai,Gareth Harman,Yong Fan,Colin L. Masters,Paul Maruff,Sterling C. Johnson,Jurgen Fripp,Duygu Tosun,John C. Morris,Daniel S. Marcus,Pamela LaMontagne,Tammie Benzinger,Susan R. Heckbert,Mark Espeland,Marilyn S. Albert,Andrew J. Saykin,Paul M. Thompson,Timothy J. Hohman,Susan M. Resnick,R. Nick Bryan,Murat Bilgel,Yang An,David A. Wolk,Li Shen,Haochang Shou,Ilya M. Nasrallah,Christos Davatzikos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GenFAR是一个模块化深度学习框架，通过学习脑MRI的通用临床特征实现跨任务知识迁移。该框架在49,246名个体、11个队列、17个分类/回归任务上训练，识别出最佳任务序列长度（6个任务）和五个强供体任务（年龄、AD/MCI、MMSE、高血压、高脂血症），并通过Donor Score量化各任务对下游性能的贡献。学习到的表征可提升下游任务的样本效率和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经影像深度学习模型通常针对单一任务开发，限制了跨应用的知识迁移能力。需要一种能够学习通用、临床信息丰富的大脑表征的框架，以便在不同任务之间共享和复用知识。

Method: 提出GenFAR模块化深度学习框架，在49,246名个体和11个队列上通过17项任务（涵盖认知、临床、诊断、人口统计学和生物标志物）进行训练。采用顺序学习方法，使后续任务逐步建立在先前学习的表征之上。通过对5,000条任务序列的分析，提出Donor Score指标以量化每个任务对下游性能的贡献，并确定最佳序列长度为6个任务。

Result: 识别出五个稳定的强供体任务（年龄、AD/MCI、MMSE、高血压、高脂血症）作为顺序模型的基础。学习到的特征表征在训练集之外的各种下游任务中表现良好，能显著提高次级深度学习任务的样本效率和准确性。

Conclusion: GenFAR证明了一种模块化、任务顺序学习的方式可以学习到通用且临床相关的脑MRI表征，可作为专门化预测模型的基础，提高样本效率和下游任务性能，为神经影像领域的知识迁移提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deep learning models for neuroimaging have largely been developed for individual tasks, limiting knowledge transfer across applications. Here we introduce GenFAR, a modular deep learning framework that learns general, clinically informed features from brain MRIs. We trained this modular architecture on 49,246 individuals across 11 cohorts, using 17 diverse classification and regression tasks spanning cognition, clinical, diagnosis, demographics, and biomarkers. This yields aggregated, focused feature sets that capture rich, clinically- and biologically-relevant brain representations. We developed a sequential learning approach where tasks progressively build on previously learned representations. Through an analysis of 5,000 task sequences, we identified an optimal sequence length of six tasks and introduced a Donor Score metric to quantify each task's contribution to downstream performance. This analysis revealed five consistently strong donor tasks (Age, AD/MCI, MMSE, Hypertension, Hyperlipidemia) that formed the base of our sequential model. We demonstrated the utility of our learned representation, in various tasks beyond those included in the training set, to serve as the foundation for specialized secondary predictors. We further showed that using the learned feature representation can substantially increase the sample efficiency of secondary deep learning training tasks and models, as well as improve their accuracy.

</details>


### [84] [AVA-Encoder: Towards Agent-Native Video Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12313)
*Chuyue Li,Jinpeng Yu,Haozhe Wang,Tian Xueyun,Zhijing Zhang,Bingnan Li,Shuqi Gu,Kan Ren,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AVA-Encoder（Agentic Video Auto-Encoder），一种通过代理自动编码学习代理原生视频表示（agent-native video representations）的框架。该框架将视频转化为知识图谱（KG）表示，再重建回视频。通过优化循环中的"文本梯度"（textual-gradient），框架训练了数据独立的编码策略，能够将高质量电影转化为结构化、可供智能体理解、查询和编辑的表示形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创造性智能体仍然缺乏从高质量人类电影中学习的有效方式，限制了其生成电影级视频的能力。核心挑战在于缺少一种既忠实于电影内容、又能直接用于智能体推理和操作的结构化视频表示。

Method: AVA-Encoder采用自动编码框架：视频→知识图谱（KG）→视频。KG包含层级结构节点与状态节点（存结构化文本）以及链接资产层（存生成的图像、音频、视频）。类型化边保留文本描述与资产间的关系，便于智能体理解、查询和编辑。视频重建差异驱动文本梯度优化框架，将评估反馈表达为自然语言更新方向：外层循环用于数据无关编码策略的伪训练（Data-Independent Encoding Policy Pseudo-Training），测试时内层循环可选地进行数据相关的KG表示精炼（Data-Dependent KG Representation Refinement）。

Result: 广泛实验表明，AVA-Encoder相比最强外部基线提升了20.7个百分点。在仅策略（policy-only）的受控设置下，伪训练的镜头级Agentic Video Encoder策略优于人工精心调优的策略，同时系统提示词token减少74.3%。

Conclusion: 作者发布了完整的AVA-Encoder框架、可靠的代理视频重建基准，以及首个高质量电影KG表示数据集，为该领域提供了可复现的基础设施。

Abstract: Creative agents still lack an effective way to learn from high-quality human films, limiting their ability to produce cinematic-grade videos. A key challenge is the absence of a structured video representation that is both faithful to film content and directly usable for agentic reasoning and manipulation. To address the challenge, we propose the Agentic Video Auto-Encoder (AVA-Encoder), a framework for learning agent-native video representations via agentic auto-encoding.
  AVA-Encoder transforms a video into a knowledge graph (KG) representation and then reconstructs it back into video. Its hierarchy and state nodes store structured text, while a linked asset layer holds generated images, audio, and video. Typed edges preserve the relations between these text descriptions and assets in a form that agents can easily understand, query, and edit. The video reconstruction differences drive a textual-gradient optimization framework, which expresses evaluation feedback as natural-language update directions for Data-Independent Encoding Policy Pseudo-Training in the outer loop and optional Data-Dependent KG Representation Refinement in the test-time inner loop.
  Extensive experiments show that AVA-Encoder improves by 20.7 percentage points over the strongest external baseline. In the controlled policy-only setting, its pseudo-trained shot-level Agentic Video Encoder policy also outperforms a carefully human-tuned policy while using 74.3% fewer system-prompt tokens. We release the complete AVA-Encoder framework, a reliable agentic video reconstruction benchmark, and the first dataset of high-quality film KG representations.

</details>


### [85] [HSTGFormer: Hyper Spatial-Temporal Graph Transformer for 3D Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12187)
*Ruochen Li,Shuang Chen,Wenke E,Farshad Arvin,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HSTGFormer，一种图增强Transformer框架，用于单目3D人体姿态估计。该方法将空间-时间推理重构为关节-时间节点上的局部耦合图聚合，通过超空间-时间图（HSTG）进行结构感知的耦合推理，并引入自适应双尺度时间图（ADSTG）捕捉关节特定的长短窗口时间依赖，在Human3.6M和MPI-INF-3DHP上取得高精度和高计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的单目3D人体姿态估计方法大多将空间推理和时间推理分为两个独立阶段，这削弱了人体运动固有的统一空间-时间相互依赖性，并在时间建模之前压缩了帧级结构信息。

Method: HSTGFormer包含三个核心组件：(1) 超空间-时间图（HSTG），通过将每帧骨架图扩展到时间邻域，将全局空间-时间推理分解为围绕单个关节-时间节点的局部空间-时间感受野，实现结构感知的耦合推理；(2) 自适应双尺度时间图（ADSTG），在互补的短窗口和长窗口上捕捉关节特定的时间依赖；(3) 轻量级节点级融合模块，自适应地整合两种图表示用于每个关节-时间节点。

Result: 在Human3.6M和MPI-INF-3DHP两个基准数据集上的实验表明，HSTGFormer在取得高精度的同时保持了高计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过将空间-时间推理建模为局部耦合图聚合，HSTGFormer有效解决了现有方法中空间与时间分离建模导致的信息损失问题，在精度和效率之间取得了良好平衡，验证了耦合空间-时间推理的优越性。

Abstract: Transformer-based methods have achieved strong performance in monocular 3D human pose estimation, but most existing approaches organise spatial and temporal reasoning as separate stages, which may weaken unified spatial-temporal interdependencies inherent in human motion and compress frame-level structural information before temporal modelling. In this paper, we propose HSTGFormer, a graph-enhanced Transformer framework that reformulates spatial-temporal reasoning as localised coupled graph aggregation over joint-time nodes. Specifically, HSTGFormer introduces a Hyper Spatial-Temporal Graph (HSTG), which decomposes global spatial-temporal reasoning into local spatial-temporal receptive fields around individual joint-time nodes by extending per-frame skeleton graphs into temporal neighbourhoods, thereby enabling structure-aware coupled reasoning while preserving local structural motion information. It further incorporates an Adaptive Dual-Scale Temporal Graph (ADSTG) to capture joint-specific temporal dependencies over complementary short- and long-range windows. A lightweight node-wise fusion module further adaptively integrates the two graph representations for each joint-time node. Experiments on Human3.6M and MPI-INF-3DHP show that HSTGFormer achieves strong accuracy with high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [86] [M-Net: Integrating Spectral Features and Physical Field Operators into Deep Learning for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12196)
*Jing Zhu,Ye Wang,Fumin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出M-Net（数学增强网络），通过将矩阵谱分析和向量微积分算子等显式数学先验融入U-Net，在三个医学图像分割基准（LiTS、KiTS、BraTS）上显著超越基线U-Net，证明数学归纳偏置能够为数据驱动的深度学习提供有效补充信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的深度学习在医学图像分割中虽表现优异，但往往忽略了医学图像中固有的丰富数学结构。作者希望探究显式引入数学归纳偏置（矩阵谱分析与向量微积分算子）能否超越纯数据驱动学习的性能上限。

Method: 提出M-Net，包含三个互补的数学先验模块：(1)基于中心化局部像素矩阵条件数的连续谱特征，作为纹理病态性的可微度量；(2)基于图像梯度场的物理场算子（散度及离散旋度类边界不规则算子），捕捉焦强度极值与边缘非光滑性；(3)Math-Attention Gate（MAG），在跳跃连接处自适应融合数学特征与CNN提取的深度特征。

Result: 在LiTS、KiTS和BraTS三个基准上，M-Net分别取得78.42%、76.15%和83.67%的Dice分数，相比基线U-Net在肝脏、肾脏和脑肿瘤分割上分别提升12.37%、3.52%和5.55%。消融实验显示，条件数特征相比二元可逆性特征贡献2.14%的提升，MAG相比简单的特征拼接额外增加1.45%的性能。

Conclusion: M-Net证实了数学归纳偏置能够为医学图像分割提供有效的补充信息。连续条件数特征相比离散替代方案提供更优的梯度信息，MAG机制能在整个网络中保留这些数学先验。该工作为将线性代数和向量微积分融入医学影像深度架构开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Purpose: Deep learning-based medical image segmentation has achieved remarkable success, yet purely data-driven approaches often fail to exploit the rich mathematical structure inherent in medical images. We investigate whether explicit mathematical inductive biases, specifically matrix spectral analysis and vector calculus operators, can enhance segmentation beyond data-driven learning alone. Methods: We propose M-Net (Math-Augmented Network), which integrates three complementary mathematical priors into U-Net: (1) continuous spectral features derived from the condition number of centered local pixel matrices, providing a differentiable measure of texture ill-conditioning; (2) physical field operators (divergence and a discrete curl-like boundary irregularity operator) computed from image gradient fields, capturing focal intensity extrema and edge non-smoothness; and (3) a Math-Attention Gate (MAG) that adaptively fuses mathematical features with CNN-extracted deep features at skip connections. Results: Experiments on three benchmarks (LiTS, KiTS, and BraTS) show that M-Net achieves Dice scores of 78.42%, 76.15%, and 83.67%, outperforming baseline U-Net by 12.37%, 3.52%, and 5.55% on liver, kidney, and brain tumor segmentation, respectively. Ablations reveal that the condition-number feature contributes a 2.14% gain over binary invertibility features, while MAG adds 1.45% over simple concatenation. Conclusion: M-Net establishes that mathematical inductive biases provide effective complementary information for medical image segmentation. The continuous condition-number feature offers superior gradient information over discrete alternatives, and MAG preserves these priors throughout the network. This work opens avenues for integrating linear algebra and vector calculus into deep architectures for medical imaging.

</details>


### [87] [GeoFlow: Efficient Driving Video Generation via Geometry-Aligned Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12203)
*Jiazheng Liu,Hang Li,Jiawei Zhang,Jiahe Li,Xiaohan Yu,Shengyin Fan,Jin Zheng,Xiao Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoFlow是一个利用显式几何先验构建Geometry-Aligned Prior (GAP)分布来替代标准高斯噪声作为扩散模型/流匹配模型初始化的框架，通过缩短采样轨迹实现高效驾驶视频生成，仅需数小时微调或全量训练即可大幅减少推理步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型和流匹配模型在生成驾驶视频时推理延迟高，根源在于使用标准高斯源分布，导致逐帧独立高斯噪声初始化忽略视频中丰富的时空相关性，迫使模型从头噪声中重建前一帧已有的确定性场景结构，既计算冗余又易产生几何不一致。

Method: GeoFlow利用多视图几何和空间自适应噪声注入构建Geometry-Aligned Prior (GAP)分布作为采样起点，替代标准高斯噪声。该分布通过桥接源分布与数据分布之间的差距，使采样轨迹显著更直、更短。

Result: 实验表明GeoFlow在训练和推理效率上均表现卓越：在基线模型上仅需数小时微调即可显著提升少步生成质量；完全收敛训练后，生成SOTA驾驶视频所需的推理步数大幅减少。

Conclusion: 通过显式注入几何先验来构造更接近数据分布的初始分布，能够从根本上解决生成模型因高斯噪声初始化带来的高推理延迟问题，为高效驾驶视频生成开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Generative models like Diffusion Models and Flow Matching have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in synthesizing high-fidelity driving videos, but are severely constrained by high inference latency due to the requirement of extensive sampling steps. We argue that this inefficiency stems from the prevailing reliance on a standard Gaussian source distribution, where consecutive frames are initialized as independent Gaussian noise. This paradigm disregards the rich spatiotemporal correlations inherent in driving videos, compelling the model to regenerate deterministic scene structures existing in previous frames from noise, which is both computationally redundant and prone to geometric inconsistency. To address this problem, we propose GeoFlow, a novel framework designed to achieve efficient driving video generation by harnessing explicit geometric priors. Instead of sampling from standard Gaussian noise, we leverage multi-view geometry and spatially-adaptive noise injection to construct a Geometry-Aligned Prior (GAP) distribution as starting point. This initialization bridges the gap between source distribution and data distribution, yielding a significantly straighter and shorter sampling trajectory. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GeoFlow can achieve remarkable efficiency of both training and inference: merely several hours of fine-tuning on baseline models can significantly boost few-step generation quality, while fully converged training drastically reduces number of inference steps required for state-of-the-art video generation.

</details>


### [88] [Generation as Auxiliary Supervision: Enhancing Visual Understanding at Zero Inference Overhead via Decoupled Embedding Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12209)
*Zhongbin Guo,Jiahao Xie,Dongling Xiao,Qianle Wang,Ruiqi Lu,Xiaomin He,Wanxuan Sun,Cheng Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GAS是一个生成引导训练框架，通过将视觉生成重新解读为表示学习的辅助监督信号，在解耦的Mixture-of-Transformers架构中采用Next Embedding Prediction作为跨模态生成范式，在不增加任何推理开销的情况下提升多模态大语言模型的感知与空间理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM中视觉理解与生成通常被视为相互分歧的目标，统一框架依赖的离散视觉tokenization或扩散目标与理解模型使用的连续表示不一致，导致难以直接迁移到已有预训练MLLM上增强其理解能力。

Method: 提出GAS框架，核心为：(1) 将Next Embedding Prediction (NEP)作为跨模态生成范式；(2) 采用解耦的Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT)架构，共享下层主干+并行上层分支，使生成损失丰富共享视觉通路而不干扰上层理解层；(3) 构建需要深层认知基础的强相关性生成任务以最大化生成与理解的协同效应；(4) 训练后丢弃辅助生成分支，实现零推理开销。

Result: 跨模型规模和训练阶段，GAS均提升了多模态理解的整体性能，最稳定的提升体现在感知与空间理解任务上；通过大规模受控对比实验和表示层面分析，阐明了生成引导训练何时及为何有利于理解能力。

Conclusion: GAS证明了生成引导训练可以作为提升多模态理解的一种实用路线，由于推理时丢弃生成分支，所有增益均在零额外推理开销下获得，为统一视觉理解与生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, visual understanding and generation are typically treated as divergent objectives. Existing unified frameworks often rely on discrete visual tokenization or diffusion objectives whose generative targets differ from the continuous representations consumed by visual understanding models, making direct transfer to enhance existing pretrained MLLMs non-trivial. In this work, we present GAS, a generation-guided training framework that reinterprets visual generation as auxiliary supervision for representation learning. Concretely, GAS adapts Next Embedding Prediction (NEP) as a cross-modal generation paradigm within a decoupled Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture. By maintaining a shared lower trunk and parallel upper layers, GAS lets generation losses enrich the shared visual pathway with finer spatial precision and stronger visual retention while shielding the upper understanding layers from direct generation gradients. To maximize this synergy, we further construct highly correlated generation tasks that demand deep cognitive grounding rather than generic synthesis alone. Across model scales and training stages, GAS improves aggregate multimodal understanding, with its most reliable gains on perception and spatial comprehension. Crucially, because the auxiliary generation branch is discarded after training, these gains incur zero inference overhead. Extensive controlled comparisons and representation-level analyses further clarify when and why generation-guided training benefits understanding, and demonstrate the feasibility of generation-guided training as a practical route to stronger multimodal understanding.

</details>


### [89] [SCOUT: Unlocking Enhanced Spatial Reasoning via Structured Chain-of-Thought and Multi-Objective Process Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12220)
*Zile Zhou,Huining Yuan,Weichen Zhang,Xinlei Chen,Xiao-ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCOUT提出了一种结合结构化思维链与过程监督强化学习的框架，用于增强视觉语言模型的空间推理能力。通过显式建模3D环境感知、引入多目标过程奖励和定制优势估计方法，SCOUT在多个空间基准上取得了显著提升，并展现出跨模态（多图像和视频）的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）在空间推理方面存在关键瓶颈。基于结果的强化学习方法虽然利用可验证结果进行优化，但中间推理步骤的信用分配较差；结构化推理方法则忽略了3D理解所需的深度感知。这两种方法的不足使得VLM难以进行鲁棒的空间理解与推理。

Method: 提出SCOUT框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 结构化思维链（CoT）框架，显式建模3D环境感知，确保鲁棒的空间理解与推理；(2) 新型强化学习算法，采用多目标过程奖励和定制优势估计方法，在推理轨迹的不同分段上实现细粒度信用分配；(3) SCOUT-24k数据集，通过定制化流水线合成结构化空间推理CoT数据。

Result: SCOUT-3B在通用空间基准和复杂空间推理任务上分别比基线模型提升16.85%和6.3%；SCOUT-7B超越GPT-4o达4.28%。尽管仅在单图像上训练，SCOUT-7B对多图像和视频场景展现出强大的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: SCOUT通过将结构化推理与过程监督强化学习相结合，有效解决了VLM空间推理中的信用分配和深度感知缺失问题，是迈向下一代具备空间感知能力的视觉语言模型的关键一步。

Abstract: Existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibits a critical bottleneck in robust spatial reasoning. Recent reinforcement learning (RL) methods aim to close this gap with verifiable outcomes, yet they suffer from poor credit assignment across intermediate reasoning steps. Concurrently, structured reasoning approaches overlook the critical depth perception necessary for comprehensive 3D understanding. To address these challenges, we propose SCOUT (Structured Chain-Of-Thought Utilizing Process-Supervised RL Training). Specifically, we design a structured Chain-of-Thought (CoT) framework that explicitly models 3D environmental perception to ensure robust spatial understanding and reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a novel RL algorithm featuring multi-objective process rewards and a tailored advantage estimation method, facilitating fine-grained credit assignment across distinct segments of the reasoning trajectory. To support our framework, we develop SCOUT-24k, a structured spatial reasoning CoT dataset synthesized through a customized pipeline. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that SCOUT-3B improves upon baseline models by 16.85% and 6.3% on general spatial benchmarks and complex spatial reasoning tasks respectively. Notably, our larger SCOUT-7B even outperforms GPT-4o by a margin of 4.28%. Moreover, despite being trained exclusively on single image, SCOUT-7B exhibits robust out-of-domain generalization to multi-image and video scenarios. These empirical results render SCOUT as a critical step towards next generation of spatially-aware VLMs.

</details>


### [90] [Few-Shot Ordinal Learning for Day-Wise Freshness Estimation with Hyperspectral Fish Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12230)
*Kazi Nabiul Alam,Pooneh Bagheri Zadeh,Akbar Sheikh-Akbari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于少样本学习的高光谱成像（HSI）食品新鲜度预测框架。每个鱼片定义为一个独立的episodic任务，采用CORAL风格序数预测头建模新鲜度的排序性质，并结合生物启发的单调性和嵌入平滑性约束。在16天三文鱼HSI数据集上，仅用每个鱼片3个标注日即可实现1.58天的平均绝对误差和72.3%的2天准确率，显著优于全监督标量回归和标签分布基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HSI新鲜度预测的深度学习方法全部依赖全监督，需要大量逐产品级别的密集标注数据，成本高昂且难以获取。同时，不同鱼片之间存在强烈的个体差异，且每个产品的标注样本稀缺，导致传统的监督学习方法难以有效泛化。

Method: 提出一个少样本学习框架：1) 将每个鱼片定义为一个独立的episodic task来实现类级别的个性化建模；2) 使用CORAL风格的序数预测头，通过累积阈值建模捕获新鲜度进展的排序性质；3) 引入基于生物学先验的单调性约束和嵌入平滑性约束，引导预测朝向合理的轨迹演化。

Result: 在16天三文鱼HSI数据集、严格不可见鱼片协议下，所提方法在每鱼片仅3个标注日的条件下达到1.58天的平均绝对误差和72.3%的2天准确率，大幅超越在相同协议下的标量回归和标签分布基线方法。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了少样本学习范式能够有效应对HSI食品新鲜度评估中标签稀缺和个体差异大的挑战，为低成本、非破坏性的逐产品新鲜度监测提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Non-destructive food quality assessment has increasingly benefited from hyperspectral imaging (HSI), which captures spectral signatures linked to biochemical changes during storage. Estimating day-wise freshness, however, remains challenging owing to strong inter-fillet variability and scarce labelled data per product. All existing deep learning approaches for HSI-based freshness prediction operate under full supervision, requiring densely annotated training sets that are costly to obtain at the individual-product level. We introduce, to the best of our knowledge, the first few-shot learning framework for HSI-based food quality estimation. Each fillet defines a distinct episodic task, and a CORAL-style ordinal prediction head captures the ranked nature of freshness progression through cumulative threshold modelling. Biologically grounded monotonicity and embedding smoothness constraints further guide predictions toward plausible trajectories. On a 16-day salmon HSI dataset under a strict unseen-fillet protocol, our method achieves a mean absolute error of 1.58 days and 2-day accuracy of 72.3% with only three labelled days per fillet, substantially outperforming scalar regression and label-distribution baselines under an identical unseen-fillet protocol.

</details>


### [91] [ScaleVid: Geometry-Aware Video Object Scaling with Mesh-Free Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12232)
*Youze Huang,Penghui Ruan,Bojia Zi,Xianbiao Qi,Shihao Zhao,Rong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种渐进式两阶段训练框架，用于几何感知的视频物体缩放任务。该框架将前景几何变换与背景保留及视频合成解耦，无需网格-像素对齐和显式3D重建。第一阶段学习平面变换下的前景-背景合成，第二阶段引入物体中心3D变形引导实现几何感知缩放。通过从真实视频构造几何扰动的伪源，以原始完整视频作为重建目标，实现了无需配对真实缩放目标的真实视频合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本引导方法主要在2D图像平面操作，深度引导方法控制粗糙，基于网格的方法需要昂贵的3D重建。这些方法无法同时满足几何合理性、时间一致性和背景一致性的要求，且推理成本高。因此需要一种无需显式3D重建且能实现高质量几何感知缩放的方法。

Method: 提出渐进式两阶段训练框架：1) 从真实视频构造几何扰动的伪源样本，以原始完整视频作为重建目标；2) 第一阶段使用平面变换学习鲁棒的前景-背景合成能力；3) 第二阶段引入物体中心3D变形指导实现几何感知缩放；4) 构建互补的配对几何基准和真实背景基准用于评估。

Result: 在配对几何基准、真实背景基准以及野外视频上进行了广泛实验。结果表明该方法在几何一致性、前景保真度和背景保留方面均优于现有方法，且推理速度比需要显式3D重建的方法更快、更实用。

Conclusion: 通过解耦前景变换与背景合成、利用伪源重建的监督策略，该方法在没有配对真实缩放数据的情况下实现了高质量、高效的几何感知视频物体缩放，为视频编辑任务提供了一种更实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Geometry-aware video object scaling aims to anisotropically resize the object along object-centric axes while preserving geometric plausibility, temporal coherence, and background consistency. Existing text-guided methods mainly operate in the 2D image plane, while depth-guided approaches provide coarse control and mesh-based methods require costly 3D reconstruction. We present a progressive two-stage training framework that decouples geometry-aware foreground transformation from background preservation and realistic video composition, without mesh-pixel alignment and explicit 3D reconstruction at inference. In both stages, geometrically perturbed pseudo-sources are constructed from real videos, while the original complete videos are retained as reconstruction targets. The first stage uses planar transformations to learn robust foreground-background composition, whereas the second introduces object-centric 3D deformation guidance for geometry-aware scaling. This pseudo-source reconstruction formulation enables real-video synthesis without paired real-world scaling targets. We construct complementary paired-geometry and real-background benchmarks and further evaluate on in-the-wild videos. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior geometric consistency, foreground fidelity, and background preservation, together with faster and more practical inference than methods requiring explicit 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [92] [HAMP-LIC: Hessian-Aware Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12239)
*Yuefeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HAMP-LIC，一个面向预训练学习图像压缩（LIC）模型的Hessian感知混合精度后训练量化（PTQ）框架，通过四阶段优化策略实现高效低比特部署，在Minnen2018和Cheng2020等模型上达到最高4.85倍模型压缩率，仅损失0.59%的BD-rate，并完全消除跨平台的编码-解码不匹配误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LIC模型虽然率失真性能优异，但计算复杂度高且在不同硬件平台间存在编码-解码不匹配问题。均匀固定精度量化虽然简单有效，但在低比特宽度下由于忽略了各层对量化敏感性的差异而遭受严重的质量退化，因此需要一种能感知层间差异的低比特部署方案。

Method: 提出HAMP-LIC框架，采用四阶段优化策略：(1) 基于Hessian迹估计块级敏感性，捕捉二阶重要性；(2) 通过任务感知细化模块，联合考虑量化失真和率失真性能来调整敏感性；(3) 依据细化的敏感性分布，在全局模型大小约束下分配比特宽度，平衡效率与重建质量；(4) 使用小型校准集进行块级重建以进一步抑制量化误差。

Result: 在Minnen2018和Cheng2020等代表性LIC模型上进行实验，HAMP-LIC实现最高4.85倍模型压缩，仅损失0.59%的BD-rate；在多个数据集上持续优于现有的固定精度和混合精度PTQ方法，并完全消除跨平台编码-解码误差。

Conclusion: HAMP-LIC通过Hessian感知的混合精度PTQ方法，在保证高压缩率的同时显著降低性能损失，为LIC模型的低比特高效部署提供了一种有效的解决方案，并彻底解决了异构硬件平台间的编码-解码不匹配问题。

Abstract: Use this plain-text version for the arXiv abstract field: Learned image compression (LIC) models achieve strong rate-distortion performance but are hindered by high computational complexity and encoding-decoding mismatches across heterogeneous hardware platforms. Uniform fixed-precision quantization alleviates these issues but suffers severe quality degradation at low bit widths because it ignores differences in the quantization sensitivities of individual layers. To enable efficient and accurate low-bit deployment of pretrained LIC models, we propose HAMP-LIC, a Hessian-aware mixed-precision post-training quantization (PTQ) framework with a four-stage optimization strategy. First, block-wise sensitivity is estimated from the Hessian trace to capture second-order importance. Second, a task-aware refinement module adjusts these sensitivities by jointly considering quantization distortion and rate-distortion performance. Third, guided by the refined sensitivity profile, bit widths are allocated under a global model-size constraint to balance efficiency and reconstruction quality. Finally, block-wise reconstruction using a small calibration set further suppresses quantization error. Experiments on representative LIC models, including Minnen2018 and Cheng2020, demonstrate that HAMP-LIC achieves up to 4.85x model compression with as little as 0.59% BD-rate loss. It consistently outperforms existing fixed- and mixed-precision PTQ methods across multiple datasets while completely eliminating cross-platform encoding-decoding errors.

</details>


### [93] [Automated Borehole Core Analysis with Report-Derived Weak Labels and Supervised Crack Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12252)
*Usama Imdad,Ali Khan,Luke Lu,Zubair Khalid,Arif Mahmood*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个从钻孔岩心档案（含岩心托盘照片和数字日志报告）中提取裂纹间距信息的双通道框架：一是从报告文本中提取结构化间距类别作为弱区间级标签，配合DINO编码器进行分类；二是手动标注5,087张岩心行图像并训练门控U-Net进行裂纹分割（F1=0.860，裂纹IoU=0.754），后续通过确定性后处理将预测裂纹位置转换为间距类别。此外还设计了独立的基于规则的支路估计岩心相对层理角和岩性颜色描述符，与日志报告参考值分别有75.4%和84.7%的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钻孔岩心档案中通常包含岩心托盘照片和对应的数字日志报告，但缺乏原生的像素级裂纹标注。现有方法难以从这类非结构化档案中系统提取缺陷间距信息，需要探索同时利用文本弱标签和图像监督信号的互补方案来克服标注缺失的瓶颈。

Method: 采用两条互补路径：(1) 从报告文本层恢复结构化间距类别作为弱区间级标签，用DINO编码器在未标注岩心裁剪图上预训练获得领域特定表示，并用人工验证子集识别标签不一致性；(2) 手动标注5,087张岩心行图像，训练全监督裂纹分割模型——门控U-Net通过学习的空间门控机制融合PiDiNet边缘图和Mask R-CNN掩码。确定性后处理将分割得到的裂纹位置转换为间距类别。另有独立的基于规则分支分别估计岩心相对层理角度和岩性颜色描述符。

Result: 门控U-Net在所有评估的分割配置中表现最佳，F1分数为0.860，裂纹类IoU为0.754。规则分支对层理角和岩性颜色描述符的预测分别与日志参考值在75.4%和84.7%的1,200张评估图像上一致。值得注意的是，这些参考值本身来自现有报告，衡量的是与记录地质观测的一致性而非独立物理精度。

Conclusion: 该框架有效结合了报告派生的弱监督（用于间距分类）与全监督分割（用于基于图像的裂纹定位），形成了一套无需原生像素级标注即可从既有钻孔档案中系统性提取裂纹间距信息的完整方案，验证了数据驱动与规则驱动方法互补的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Borehole archives commonly contain core tray photographs and corresponding digital log reports, but no native pixel-level crack annotations. We investigate two complementary approaches for extracting defect-spacing information from these archives. First, structured spacing categories recovered from the report text layer provide weak interval-level labels for classification. A DINO encoder trained on unlabeled core crops supplies domain-specific representations, and a manually verified subset is used to identify label inconsistencies. Second, we manually annotate 5,087 extracted core-row images and evaluate fully supervised crack-segmentation models. Our gated U-Net combines PiDiNet edge maps with Mask R-CNN masks through a learned spatial gating mechanism. This configuration achieves an F1 score of 0.860 and a crack-class IoU of 0.754, the highest result among the evaluated segmentation configurations. Deterministic post-processing converts predicted crack locations into defect-spacing categories. Separate rule-based branches estimate core-relative bedding angles and lithological color descriptors; their predictions agree with log-report references on 75.4% and 84.7% of 1,200 evaluated images, respectively. Because these references are extracted from existing reports, the reported values measure agreement with recorded geological observations rather than independent physical accuracy. The resulting framework combines report-derived weak supervision for spacing classification with fully supervised segmentation for image-based crack localization.

</details>


### [94] [Diagram-MMU: A Multi-Modal Benchmark for Scientific Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12262)
*Weihao Bo,Shan Zhang,Yanpeng Sun,Jie Liu,Yongke Yao,Jinhao Du,Wei He,Kai Zou,Zechao Li,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Diagram-MMU，一个用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）科学图表解析与理解能力的多模态基准。该基准包含3.7k个精选图表和18.3k条人工验证的问题，覆盖六个领域，评估三类任务：图表到代码解析、图表到代码编辑和图表问答。对12个MLLMs的评估表明，图表到代码任务比问答更具挑战性，且在代理（agentic）设置下，大多数模型在解析和编辑任务上有所提升，但在问答上性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大语言模型在科学写作与协作中的能力不断增强（如OpenAI Prism可将科学图表直接转换为LaTeX TikZ代码），但目前缺乏一个系统性的基准来评估MLLMs在这一关键任务上的图表解析与理解能力，因此需要一个全面的评测基准。

Method: 构建Diagram-MMU基准，包含3.7k个精选图表和18.3k条人工验证的问题，覆盖六个科学领域。设计了三类任务（图表到代码解析、图表到代码编辑、图表问答），并为每项任务配备了代理（agentic）设置，然后对12个MLLMs进行系统评估。

Result: 评估12个MLLMs发现：图表到代码任务比图表问答更具挑战性——模型能够很好地对图表进行推理，但在解析和编辑图表方面存在困难，凸显了提升MLLMs图表到代码生成能力的必要性。在代理设置下，大多数模型在解析和编辑性能上有所提升，但在问答任务上性能下降，而Claude-4.6 Opus在三个任务上均持续提升。

Conclusion: Diagram-MMU揭示了当前MLLMs在科学图表处理上的能力差异，特别是图表到代码生成仍是瓶颈领域。研究结果强调了开发增强MLLMs图表到代码生成方法的迫切需求，并验证了代理设置在部分任务上的有效性，为未来多模态科学写作工具的发展提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been growing the capability for scientific writing and collaboration. For example, OpenAI Prism is a free workspace for scientific writing and collaboration. One important feature in Prism is turning scientific diagrams directly into LaTeX TikZ code. In this paper, we build a benchmark, Diagram-MMU, a multi-modal benchmark designed to assess MLLMs' ability for scientific diagram parsing and understanding. Diagram-MMU features 3.7k curated diagrams and 18.3k human-validated questions across six domains. It evaluates MLLMs on three tasks common in vibe writing workspaces: diagram-to-code parsing, diagram-to-code editing, and diagram question answering, alongside agentic settings per task. The evaluation of 12 MLLMs reveals that diagram-to-code tasks are more challenging than diagram question answering: models can reason well over diagrams but struggle to parse and edit them, underscoring the need for methods to enhance MLLMs' capability in diagram-to-code generation. Under agentic settings, most models improve parsing and editing performance but degrade on question answering, while Claude-4.6 Opus consistently improves across all three tasks. Project Page: https://vi-ocean.github.io/projects/diagram-mmu.

</details>


### [95] [A Neighborhood Attention Transformer Network for Enhanced 3D Segmentation of the Left Anterior Descending Artery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12274)
*Rafi Ibn Sultan,Chengyin Li,Yiannos Demetriou,Ahmed I. Ghanem,Joshua P. Kim,Justine Cunningham,Hassan Bagher-Ebadian,Dongxiao Zhu,Kundan S. Thind*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NA-UNETR，一种基于Transformer的三维LAD动脉分割框架，通过邻域注意力(DiNA)模块联合建模局部细节与全局上下文，结合LoRA参数高效微调和同方差不确定性加权的复合损失函数，在非增强自由呼吸CT上实现了优于nnU-Net和Swin UNETR的分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LAD动脉在三维自由呼吸非增强CT中尺寸极小、软组织对比度差且患者间变异大，即使人工勾画也因血管边界模糊而具有有限的观察者间一致性。精确的LAD分割对胸部放疗中的心脏剂量保护至关重要，但现有方法难以在低对比度和类别不平衡条件下实现准确轮廓勾画。

Method: 提出NA-UNETR，一种三维Transformer分割模型，包含邻域注意力(NA)和膨胀邻域注意力(DiNA)模块以联合捕获精细结构细节和长程上下文。在1,000个CTA体数据上预训练，通过LoRA参数高效微调策略在20例自由呼吸非增强CT上进行适配。损失函数采用Dice-Focal和Hausdorff损失的复合形式，通过同方差不确定性动态平衡以同时优化重叠度和边界精度。

Result: NA-UNETR在内部数据集上达到45.64% Dice、38.16mm HD95和10.01mm ASD，Dice较nnU-Net提升3.10个百分点，HD95较Swin UNETR降低2.96mm，在所有模型中具有最强边界精度和更优的中心线稳定性。在ImageCAS公共数据集上获得79.49% Dice、8.89mm HD95和1.02mm ASD。消融实验证实残差块、可变卷积核和不确定性加权损失均有效。

Conclusion: NA-UNETR在细长、低对比度的LAD结构上实现了局部精度与全局上下文的平衡，为放疗计划中的亚结构级心脏分割提供了计算高效的框架，验证了参数高效迁移学习与不确定性引导优化在稀缺标注医学影像任务中的价值。

Abstract: Background: Accurate segmentation of the Left Anterior Descending (LAD) artery in 3D free-breathing, non-contrast CT is critical for cardiac dose sparing in thoracic radiotherapy. The LAD is extremely small, has poor soft-tissue contrast, and varies substantially across patients; even manual contours show limited inter-observer agreement, underscoring the ambiguity of the vessel boundaries. Purpose: To develop a transformer-based framework that improves LAD delineation in low-contrast, imbalanced CT through local-global context modeling and uncertainty-guided optimization. Methods: We propose NA-UNETR, a 3D transformer-based segmentation model whose Neighborhood Attention (NA) and Dilated NA (DiNA) blocks jointly capture fine structural detail and long-range context. Given the scarcity of annotated LAD data, the model is pretrained on 1,000 CTA volumes of general coronary anatomy and fine-tuned with LoRA-based parameter-efficient adaptation on 20 free-breathing institutional CT scans. A composite Dice-Focal and Hausdorff loss, dynamically balanced via homoscedastic uncertainty, improves overlap and boundary accuracy. Results: NA-UNETR reached 45.64% Dice, 38.16 mm HD95, and 10.01 mm ASD, improving Dice by 3.10 percentage points over nnU-Net and reducing HD95 by 2.96 mm relative to Swin UNETR, with the strongest boundary accuracy among all models and improved centerline stability. On ImageCAS it achieved 79.49% Dice, 8.89 mm HD95, and 1.02 mm ASD. Ablations confirmed that residual blocks, variable kernels, and uncertainty-weighted loss each contributed. Conclusions: NA-UNETR balances local precision and global context for thin, low-contrast LAD structures, offering a computationally efficient framework for substructure-level cardiac segmentation in radiotherapy planning.

</details>


### [96] [XYZFlow:Scaling Multi dimensional Shortcut Flows for Efficient Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12276)
*Jinxiu Liu,Xuanming Liu,Kangfu Mei,Yandong Wen,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: XYZFlow提出了一种通过多维缩放流匹配来重新思考高效图像生成的新框架，通过时间维度上的非马尔可夫去噪历史条件化（temporal scaling）和空间维度上的Next Shortcut Prediction（基于前序patch去噪轨迹作为先验的序列化生成）两条正交维度，实现了7.2-8.5倍教师模型加速的同时保持有竞争力的FID分数，达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真图像生成面临速度与质量之间的权衡困境：扩散模型视觉效果优异但需要昂贵的迭代采样成本；而现有高效方法主要依赖将预训练模型蒸馏为少步采样器，这一过程极具挑战性且严重依赖教师模型的质量。作者希望摆脱对蒸馏的依赖，重新设计一种从根本上提升概率路径可辨识性和可学习性的高效生成框架。

Method: XYZFlow将自回归建模视为隐式流直线化（implicit flow straightening），通过两个正交维度增强模型表达能力：1) 时间缩放（temporal scaling）——采用非马尔可夫条件化，利用完整的去噪历史作为上下文；2) 空间缩放（spatial scaling）——通过Next Shortcut Prediction，用前序patch的去噪轨迹作为先验，顺序生成后续patch。与传统单步映射不同，这种结构化多维条件化使概率路径更可辨识、更可学习。

Result: 实验表明XYZFlow达到了最先进的性能，实现了7.2-8.5倍的教师模型加速，同时保持有竞争力的FID分数。此外，Next Shortcut Prediction在质量-延迟权衡上优于模型规模缩放或逐步数缩减的方案。

Conclusion: XYZFlow证明了通过多维流量匹配缩放而非简单蒸馏，可以有效解决图像生成的速度-质量权衡问题，为高效生成模型提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: High-fidelity image generation faces a trade-off between speed and quality. Diffusion models produce strong visuals but require costly iterative sampling. Existing efficient methods mainly distill pretrained models into few-step samplers, a challenging process that depends heavily on teacher-model quality. In this paper, we introduce XYZFlow, a framework that rethinks efficient generation through multidimensional scaling of flow matching. Unlike single-step mappings, XYZFlow enhances expressivity by making probability paths more identifiable and learnable through structured multidimensional conditioning. We view autoregressive modeling as implicit flow straightening, where richer context reduces trajectory ambiguity. XYZFlow realizes this idea through two orthogonal dimensions: temporal scaling, which uses non-Markovian conditioning on the full denoising history; and spatial scaling, enabled by Next Shortcut Prediction, which sequentially generates patches using preceding patches' denoising trajectories as priors. Experiments show that XYZFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance, with 7.2-8.5X teacher speedups and competitive FID, while Next Shortcut Prediction delivers superior quality-latency trade-offs over model scaling or step reduction.

</details>


### [97] [Curvature-Aware Zeroth-Order Optimization for Memory-Efficient Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12279)
*Junming Zhang,Shuyu Yin,Peilin Liu,Rendong Ying,Fei Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CAZO，一种基于损失曲面曲率感知的零阶优化方法，用于测试时自适应（TTA）。CAZO通过滑动平均估计对角Hessian构造协方差矩阵，实现各向异性扰动采样，仅需前向传播即可估计梯度，冻结预训练权重只优化少量适配器参数，大幅提升ZO-TTA性能并降低内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TTA方法大多依赖反向传播（BP）进行微调，但在实际设备端部署场景中，BP-free方法（如零阶ZO方法）因仅依赖前向计算而更为理想，可大幅降低复杂度和内存开销。然而ZO方法估计梯度的方差远高于一阶方法，导致性能受限，因此需要改进ZO方法以增强其基于TTA的性能。

Method: CAZO方法包含两大核心：(1) 揭示自适应过程中损失函数具有持久低秩Hessian结构；(2) 基于此洞察，利用滑动平均估计对角Hessian构造协方差矩阵，用于各向异性扰动采样，实现损失曲面曲率感知的零阶梯度估计。CAZO冻结预训练权重，仅通过前向传播优化少量适配器参数。

Result: 大量实验表明CAZO显著超越现有TTA方法，达到最先进（SOTA）性能，同时在准确率和内存效率之间保持了优秀的平衡。与BP方法相比，CAZO大幅降低了内存开销。

Conclusion: CAZO证明利用损失曲率信息可以显著提升零阶优化在TTA中的效果，为设备端部署场景下的高效TTA提供了一种兼顾性能与资源效率的新方案。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) aims to enhance the cross-domain performance of pre-trained models by adapting to unlabeled test data. While most existing TTA methods rely on backpropagation (BP) for finetuning, BP-free methods such as zeroth-order (ZO) methods are more desired in practical on-device scenarios. ZO methods rely only on forward computation, which can largely reduce the complexity and memory overhead of on-device deployment. However, ZO methods suffer from much higher variance compared with first-order methods in estimating the gradient. To address this, we propose an improved ZO method to substantially boost the performance of ZO optimization based TTA. First, we provide an observation to reveal the persistent low-rank Hessian structure of the loss during the adaptation process. Based on this insight, we then propose a loss-landscape curvature-aware zeroth-order (CAZO) method, which leverages a sliding-average estimation of the diagonal Hessian to construct a covariance matrix for anisotropic perturbation sampling. CAZO operates by freezing pretrained weights and optimizing minimal adapter parameters via forward-only passes based gradient estimation, which can substantially reduce the memory overhead compared to BP-based methods. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAZO significantly outperforms existing TTA methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance while maintaining an excellent balance between accuracy and memory efficiency. Code is available at https://github.com/Hollyming/CAZO.

</details>


### [98] [Beyond Trial-and-Error: Agentic Optimization for Image-to-Video Adherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12290)
*Aman Tyagi,Hemanth Boinpally,Jonathan Chen,Douglas Gebert,Steven Hickson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"Agentic Self-Improvement"框架，将黑箱式图像到视频（I2V）生成建模为闭环、目标导向的优化问题。通过两阶段方法——第一阶段利用多模态大语言模型（mLLM）进行迭代式提示词优化（包含DSG语义一致性检查和CMQ伪影检测），第二阶段使用贝叶斯优化联合优化随机种子和CFG尺度——显著提升视频生成的可靠性和可控性，在人类偏好研究中达到最高69%的胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代黑箱式I2V模型虽然具有强大的自动内容生成能力，但缺乏细粒度的控制和可靠性，其内在随机性导致文本提示或超参数的微小变化产生截然不同的输出，迫使专业人员依赖低效的暴力试错过程，亟需一种系统化的优化方法。

Method: 提出两阶段"Agentic Self-Improvement"框架：第一阶段为迭代提示词优化循环，利用多模态大语言模型（mLLM）细化输入提示词，并通过Davidsonian场景图（DSG）查询确保语义一致性、常见错误问题（CMQ）检测伪影；第二阶段使用贝叶斯优化高效联合优化随机种子和CFG尺度，由包括新颖的Video-Text Adherence（VTA）分数在内的质量指标套件引导搜索。

Result: 该框架显著优于无引导的搜索方法：在人类偏好研究中，通过智能体方法生成的视频相较于基线输出的胜率最高达69%。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法论，用于增强最先进视频生成模型的可预测性和可控性，推动该领域从推测性探索走向可靠的生产级工具。

Abstract: Modern black-box Image-to-Video (I2V) models offer powerful capabilities in automated content creation, yet their lack of fine-grained control and reliability presents significant challenges in professional workflows. Their inherent stochasticity causes minor variations in textual prompts or hyperparameters to yield drastically different outputs often necessitating inefficient, brute-force trial-and-error processes. To address these limitations, we introduce the ``Agentic Self-Improvement" framework, which reframes video synthesis into a closed-loop, goal-directed optimization. Our framework systematically navigates the generation parameter space using a novel two-stage approach. In the first stage, an iterative prompt optimization loop uses a multimodal Large Language Model (mLLM) to refine the input prompt. This refinement implements two automated evaluations: Davidsonian Scene Graph (DSG) queries ensure semantic adherence, and Common Mistake Questions (CMQ) for artifact detection. At the second stage, we use Bayesian optimization to efficiently co-optimize stochastic seeds and CFG scales. This search is guided by a suite of quality metrics, including the novel Video-Text Adherence (VTA) score derived from the DSG and CMQ evaluations. Our framework significantly outperforms unguided search methods: in human preference studies, videos generated via our agentic approach were strongly preferred over baseline outputs, achieving win rates up to 69\%. This work provides a practical and extensible methodology for enhancing the predictability and control of state-of-the-art video generation models, moving the field beyond speculative curiosities toward reliable, production-ready tools.

</details>


### [99] [Class Activation Mapping in Explainable Computer Vision: A Method-Centered Review of CNN, Transformer, and Foundation-Model-Era Visual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12299)
*AmirHossein Eshghi,Hamid Saadatfar,Seyyed Ali Hoseini,AmirMohsen Eshghi,Siavash Arjomand Bigdel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对2016年以来57篇CAM（类激活映射）方法论文进行了系统性综述，提出一个按归因机制、架构依赖性和评估目标分类的框架，并总结了领域从单层CNN解释向多层、概率化、token感知和基础模型感知解释演进的趋势，同时指出评估协议碎片化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CAM是XAI中最广泛使用的视觉解释家族之一，但自2016年首次提出以来，该领域已扩展至梯度后验方法、无梯度评分方法、高分辨率上采样、弱监督定位、Transformer注意力等众多方向，缺乏一个统一的综述框架来梳理这些多样化方法的相互关系和演进脉络。

Method: 严格筛选了2016年以来57篇以方法为中心的论文，构建了一个分类体系，按归因机制、架构依赖性和评估目标三个维度对方法进行分类；分别评述了基于梯度的CAM、混合CAM风格方法以及基于模型或架构感知的方法，并分析每类方法留下的研究空白及后续方法的填补方式。

Result: 主要趋势是从解释单个类别在单一低分辨率CNN层中的得分，转向比较性、多层、概率化、token感知以及基础模型感知的解释。评估仍碎片化：忠实度、定位能力、鲁棒性、计算成本和人类信任等往往使用不同协议衡量。

Conclusion: 该综述不仅总结了每类方法的贡献，还强调了每类方法之间的差距和后续方法的演进路线，为理解CAM领域的全貌和未来研究方向提供了系统化视角。

Abstract: Class activation mapping (CAM) is one of the most widely used visual explanation families in explainable artificial intelligence. Its purpose is intuitive: it converts internal model evidence into a heatmap that highlights the image regions, convolutional channels, tokens, or patches that support a target class or concept. Since the first CAM formulation in 2016, the field has moved far beyond global-average-pooled CNN classifiers. CAM-style methods now include gradient-based post-hoc explanations, gradient-free score and ablation methods, high-resolution upscaling, weakly supervised localization and segmentation, transformer token attribution, causal and debiasing methods, and foundation-model-era approaches that use CLIP, DINO, SAM, or feature-distribution comparisons. This review synthesizes a strict corpus of 57 method-centered papers published from 2016 onward. The paper develops a taxonomy that separates methods by attribution mechanism, architectural dependence, and evaluation objective. It then reviews gradient-based CAMs, recent and hybrid CAM-style methods, and model-based or architecture-aware methods. Across the corpus, the main trend is clear: the field is shifting from explaining one class score in one low-resolution CNN layer toward comparative, multi-layer, probabilistic, token-aware, and foundation-model-aware explanations. At the same time, evaluation remains fragmented. Faithfulness, localization, robustness, computational cost, and human trust are often measured with different protocols. The review therefore emphasizes not only what each method contributes, but also which gap it leaves open and which later methods attempt to close that gap.

</details>


### [100] [DreamFly: Causal Memory and Receding-Horizon Diffusion Planning for Aerial Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12308)
*Yan Deng,Fei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamFly是一个基于扩散模型的空中视觉语言导航（VLN）框架，通过因果对齐的历史记忆增强视觉表征、采用receding-horizon扩散规划（plan-K execute-one策略）进行动作预测，并利用LiteStop从动作logits直接估计停止概率，在OpenFly基准上取得了最优的SR和SPL指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA（Vision-Language-Action）模型在适应空中导航时面临三大挑战：历史上下文有限导致时序推理能力不足、规划视野短导致前瞻性差、隐式终止判断不可靠。这些问题阻碍了VLA模型在部分可观测的空中VLN任务中的有效应用。

Method: DreamFly包含三个核心组件：(1) 因果对齐的历史记忆模块，仅使用当前决策步骤之前的观测来增强视觉表征，避免未来信息泄漏；(2) receding-horizon扩散规划策略，预测K步动作块但仅执行第一步即重新规划，利用未来动作作为辅助规划目标同时保持闭环视觉反馈；(3) LiteStop模块，在初始全掩码状态下直接从动作logits估计停止概率，将显式终止判断与动作生成解耦。

Result: 在OpenFly基准上，DreamFly在test-seen/test-unseen分集上分别达到32.04%/29.46%的SR（成功率）和28.22%/23.54%的SPL（路径长度加权成功率），在两项指标上均优于所有对比方法，并取得了最低的导航误差。

Conclusion: 联合建模历史上下文、未来动作结构和显式终止判断对于空中VLN任务至关重要。DreamFly验证了扩散模型结合因果记忆和分块规划的范式能够有效解决空中导航中的时间推理和终止决策问题。

Abstract: Aerial vision-language navigation (VLN) requires an embodied agent to integrate visual evidence over time, plan future actions, and determine when it has reached a navigation goal under partial observability. Although recent VLA models offer a promising perception-to-action paradigm, adapting them to aerial navigation remains challenging due to limited historical context, short planning horizons, and unreliable implicit termination. To address these challenges, we propose DreamFly, a diffusion-based aerial VLN framework built on Dream-VLA. DreamFly introduces a causally aligned historical memory that augments the current visual representation using only observations preceding the current decision step, enabling temporal reasoning without future information leakage. We further formulate navigation as receding-horizon diffusion planning, where the policy predicts a $K$-step action chunk but executes only the first action before replanning. This plan-$K$, execute-one strategy uses future actions as auxiliary planning targets while preserving closed-loop visual feedback. Finally, LiteStop estimates the stop probability directly from action logits at the initial all-mask state, decoupling explicit termination from action generation. Experiments on the OpenFly benchmark demonstrate consistent improvements in seen and unseen environments. DreamFly achieves 32.04%/29.46% SR and 28.22%/23.54% SPL on the test-seen/test-unseen splits, respectively, outperforming all compared methods on both metrics while attaining the lowest navigation error. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of jointly modeling historical context, future action structure, and explicit termination for aerial VLN.

</details>


### [101] [StateFlow: Building, Evolving, and Accessing 3D World States for Previsualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12314)
*Yuyang Yin,Zixiang Li,Longxuan Deng,Hongkai Li,Shifang Zhao,Junnan Liu,Weirong Huang,Mengyu Wang,Tianxiao Fu,Yikai Wang,Peng-Shuai Wang,Xiaojie Jin,Yao Zhao,Yunchao Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StateFlow是一个以状态为中心的生成式预可视化框架，通过维护一个显式持久的可编辑3D世界状态（包括场景元素和相机配置），将生成式视频从单次生成转变为可迭代编辑的工作流，支持场景构建、状态演化和相机规划三个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式预可视化方法依赖简单提示词通过一次性图像或视频合成来联合控制场景、动作、相机和时空动态，缺乏强可控性且不支持迭代编辑。论文认为核心缺失在于缺少一个显式且持久的工作状态（working state），因为世界的本质是由多个具有几何、外观等属性的元素加上相机组成，不同帧只是对这一共享状态进行局部修改或重组。

Method: StateFlow提出一个以3D世界为核心的状态中心框架，包含三个阶段：(1) State construction（状态构建）：通过先验引导、冲突感知的双视图初始化将生成的2D内容提升为连贯的3D世界；(2) State evolution（状态演化）：将用户意图转化为结构化状态转换，同时保留世界记忆，避免每次编辑都进行全场景重新生成；(3) State access（状态访问）：利用渲染反馈反射（render-feedback reflection）将相机规划细化为视觉上可行的轨迹，而非仅依赖VLM语义。框架以现成的视频模型作为质量增强模块，在需要更高保真度时提升视觉质量。

Result: 实验表明StateFlow能够为视频创作和游戏式原型设计生成高质量的3D世界，验证了状态中心范式在生成式预可视化任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式持久的状态表示是生成式预可视化中实现可控性和迭代编辑的关键。StateFlow通过将工作流从一次性生成转向可编辑3D世界状态的组织与演化，为电影、游戏、建筑和城市设计等领域的预可视化提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Previsualization is an intermediate layer between ideas and production in film, games, architecture, and urban design. It lets creators iteratively refine scenes, actions, cameras, and spatial-temporal dynamics. Yet existing generative methods rely on simple prompts to jointly control all of these factors through one-shot image or video synthesis, offering weak controllability and limited support for iterative editing. Fundamentally, a world comprises multiple elements with geometry, appearance, and other attributes, together with cameras. Different frames are produced through local modifications or recombinations of this shared state, which is otherwise largely reused. Therefore, we argue that the missing component is an explicit and persistent working state. To address this, we present StateFlow, a state-centric framework for generative previsualization. Rather than generating videos in one shot, StateFlow uses an editable 3D world to organize scene structure, evolution, and cameras, while off-the-shelf video models enhance visual quality when higher fidelity is desired. This world is maintained as a persistent structured 3D state of scene elements and camera configurations, serving as the core working representation for previsualization. Built on this insight, StateFlow has three stages to construct, evolve, and access the world state. State construction lifts generated 2D content into a coherent 3D world through prior-guided, conflict-aware dual-view initialization, while State evolution translates user intent into structured state transitions while preserving world memory, avoiding full-scene regeneration for each edit. State access uses render-feedback reflection to refine camera plans into visually feasible trajectories, avoiding reliance on VLM semantics alone. Experiments show that StateFlow produces high-quality 3D worlds for video creation and game-like prototyping.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Backtrader-Bench: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Algorithmic Trading with Self-Generated MCQs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11232)
*Ruoxi Zhao,Maziar Raissi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Backtrader-Bench框架，用于评估LLM编码智能体在算法交易中的表现，包含确定性多选问题和生成器-求解器过滤两条流水线，实验表明工具增强的智能体（GPT-5.5和Opus 4.7）准确率达到90%，远超无工具基线（73%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM编码智能体在算法交易中的应用面临两大挑战：静态基准存在数据污染风险，且数值回测输出需要实际代码执行才能获得真实标签。需要一个无污染、可执行验证的评估框架。

Method: 提出Backtrader-Bench双流水线框架：(1)确定性多选问题流水线，基于五种交易策略、33个模板和三个难度等级生成题目，并由独立检查器重新推导每个答案；(2)生成器-求解器过滤流水线，由生成器编写经可执行代码验证的问题并转换为多选题，丢弃无工具求解器无需执行代码即可回答的问题。

Result: 在30道精选题目上评估11个无工具模型（各10次运行）和4个工具配置。工具增强智能体单次通过准确率达90.0%（GPT-5.5和Opus 4.7），比最佳无工具基线（10次运行平均73.0%）高出17个百分点。在38道单独挖掘的更难题目上，无工具准确率进一步下降，半数模型跌至接近随机水平（25%）。

Conclusion: Backtrader-Bench不仅能评估LLM在算法交易编码任务中的能力，其可扩展的多选题基础设施还可用于生成强化学习训练语料库，最终目标是为量化交易工作流构建专用智能体。

Abstract: Evaluating LLM coding agents in algorithmic trading is difficult because static benchmarks risk data contamination and numerical backtest outputs require ground truth from actual code execution. We present Backtrader-Bench, a framework with two complementary pipelines. A deterministic multiple-choice question (MCQ) pipeline generates questions from backtest configurations across five trading strategies, 33 templates, and three difficulty tiers, with an independent checker that re-derives every answer. A generator-solver filtering pipeline autonomously mines harder questions: a generator writes questions verified by executable code, converts them to MCQs, and discards any that a no-tool solver can answer without code execution. We evaluate 11 models without tools (10 runs each) and four with-tools configurations on a 30-question curated set. Tool-augmented agents reach 90.0% accuracy in a single pass (GPT-5.5 and Opus 4.7), outperforming the best no-tools baselines (73.0%, averaged over 10 runs) by 17 percentage points. On 38 separately mined questions, no-tools accuracy drops further, with half the models falling to roughly random-chance level (25%). Beyond evaluation, the scalable MCQ infrastructure is designed to produce a training corpus for reinforcement learning, with the ultimate goal of building a specialized agent for quantitative trading workflows.

</details>


### [103] [Retrofitting Recurrent Depth into a Pretrained Language Model: Installation, Extrapolation, Transfer, and Retention at Two Parameter Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11233)
*Mark Shapiro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将预训练语言模型重构为"前奏-权重共享循环块-尾声"的结构，赋予其循环深度以进行迭代式潜在推理。在保持与非循环基线相当性能的前提下，模型可通过低成本适配器（6M参数）或全块训练（180M参数）安装该机制，具备在监督深度外的泛化能力（约1.5倍），并显著优于同等规模的草稿本训练模型（84%对72%，超过深度10后的保持率53%对2.5%，且推理速度快7.6倍）。但反向任务存在灾难性干扰边界，且循环深度选择仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练语言模型通常具有固定前馈深度，缺乏在显式推理时进行迭代思考的机制。传统做法（如思维链）在潜在空间之外显式生成中间步骤，代价是时间和计算开销。作者希望探索是否可以在不牺牲基线性能的前提下，将固定深度的预训练模型改造成具有循环深度的迭代推理架构，从而在潜在空间内实现更深的推理。

Method: 将Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct拆分为Prelude、权重共享的Recurrent Block和Coda三部分，添加保恒等的一循环路径和后续循环的再入桥接。设置了两种预算：（1）6M训练参数+冻结基座权重（适配器方案）；（2）180M完整块训练。训练中使用中间步骤监督，使模型每循环计算一个任务步骤，并测试仅以最终答案评分的持久性。

Result: （1）循环重构后性能不低于基线（预注册ARC测试组）；（2）适配器方案（83.8%）与全块训练（84.0%）整体相当，适配器在深度11前领先但之后略逊；（3）机制是可复用的迭代程序而非终端答案查找；（4）无中间步骤监督时仍能保持（仅最终答案评分亦有效）；（5）外推至约1.5倍监督深度（深度18保持70%准确率）；（6）循环模型显著优于同规模草稿本模型（84%对72%），超深度10后保持53%对2.5%，速度快7.6倍；（7）反向任务学习时出现灾难性干扰，无法在保留既有机制的同时习得新任务。

Conclusion: 迭代Transformer可在潜在空间中执行比同等或更大规模微调模型更深的推理，且速度更快，这是系统层面的优势。但循环深度的上限受限于学习到的迭代程序的性质，且持续学习存在灾难性干扰边界。学习的深度选择机制仍待解决。

Abstract: A dense, pretrained language model can be retrofitted with recurrent depth and learn an iterative latent transition that persists after outcome-only annealing. Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct is split into a Prelude, a weight-tied Recurrent Block, and a Coda, with an identity-preserving one-loop path and a re-entry bridge on later loops. At loop 1 the retrofit remains non-inferior to its base on a preregistered ARC battery. Three findings. First, the mechanism is a reusable procedure rather than terminal-answer lookup, and installs at two budgets: 6M trained parameters over frozen base weights and 180M full-block. With intermediate-step supervision, the model computes one task step per loop and persists when only final answers are graded. The adapter matched the full block overall (83.8% versus 84.0%), led through depth 11, and trailed beyond. Verbal fine-tuning reached 79-86% on controlled verbal renderings (zero-shot transfer was minimal), and adapter verbal training begun from the installed mechanism outpaced matched fresh training by 18.6 points, including on a held-out test set. Second, the operation extrapolates to roughly 1.5 times its supervised depth, holding 70% accuracy through depth 18. Third, a same-size scratchpad-trained model matched the recurrent model within its learned horizon but collapsed beyond it. The recurrent model won overall, 84% versus 72%, retained 53% versus 2.5% beyond depth 10, and answered 7.6 times faster. An iterative transformer can therefore perform deeper reasoning in latent space faster than comparable or larger models fine-tuned on the same task, in a system-level comparison. A second task, running the rule in reverse, exposed the limits: the inverse was learnable in isolation, but no continuation acquired it while preserving the installed mechanism and general capability, a catastrophic-interference boundary. Learned depth selection remains open.

</details>


### [104] [TRACE Bench: Task-driven Roleplay Agentic Checklist Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11236)
*Jiahui Zhang,Ziwei Zhang,Yipeng Wang,Yibo Liu,Haozhou Pang,Yikai Hu,Hongyan Ren,Lan Zhou,Qi Gan,Kai Sheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TRACE Bench是一个任务驱动的智能体清单式角色扮演评估框架，将角色档案离线分解为固定检查清单，并通过用户智能体与目标模型自然对话、私有更新清单状态，使评分可追溯至清单项和对话证据，而非黑盒整体印象。相比现有基准（如MiniMax Role-play Benchmark），其覆盖率达99.91%（远超现有73.74%），且支持能力分解、稳健性验证及闭环基准演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统角色扮演评估仅给出单一分数，无法揭示哪些角色要求被测试、哪些失败以及哪些对话证据支撑判断，缺乏可解释性和诊断性。

Method: 1) 将每个角色档案离线分解为固定检查清单；2) 使用用户智能体（User Agent）与目标角色扮演模型自然对话，同时从模型响应中私有更新清单状态；3) 评分可追溯到清单项和支撑对话轮次；4) 支持覆盖交叉验证（审计现有基准转录本）和闭环基准演化（蒸馏有效的验证方法到后续评测）。

Result: 公开的M2自由对话转录本仅覆盖关键角色档案点的73.74%，而TRACE Bench以更少轮次达到99.91%覆盖；稳健性实验显示在重复运行和用户智能体替换下排名稳定；在26个模型上报告整体排名、能力分解和清单痕迹。

Conclusion: TRACE Bench提供了一种可解释、可追溯、覆盖度高且稳健的角色扮演评估范式，并支持通过闭环演化持续提升评测能力，弥补了现有评估方法缺乏细粒度诊断的不足。

Abstract: Roleplay evaluation should do more than assign a single score: it should reveal which role requirements were tested, which failed, and which dialogue evidence supports the judgment. We propose TRACE Bench, a task-driven agentic checklist evaluation framework. It decomposes each role profile offline into a fixed checklist, then uses a User Agent to converse naturally with the target roleplay model while privately updating checklist states from model responses. Scores therefore trace back to checklist items and supporting dialogue turns rather than a black-box holistic impression. For coverage cross-validation, we audit released M2 free-dialogue transcripts from the MiniMax Role-play Benchmark against the same role-derived checklist. The released free-chat transcripts cover only 73.74% of key role-profile points, whereas TRACE Bench reaches 99.91% coverage in fewer turns. Robustness experiments show stable rankings under repeated runs and User Agent replacement. Across 26 models, TRACE Bench reports overall rankings together with capability breakdowns and checklist traces. It also supports Closed-Loop Benchmark Evolution, distilling verification methods proven effective in failed traces so later evaluations can more reliably elicit and examine observed failure modes.

</details>


### [105] [Lost in Compaction: Evaluating Side-Constraint Loss under Context Compaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11242)
*Zhiqi Wang,Yichi Zhang,Dongwon Lee,Yuchen Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文识别了大语言模型系统在上下文压缩过程中会静默丢弃用户会话约束（Session Constraints, SCs）的问题，并提出了COMPINT评估套件来量化该损失。实验表明现有压缩器平均仅保留17%的SCs，且多数情况下压缩后性能反而不如不压缩。作者提出了一种SC感知提取器作为即插即用模块，在三个长上下文场景中达到90%以上的保留率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当上下文窗口面临压力时，LLM系统会压缩先前的上下文以继续任务，但用户发出的会话约束（如"在我确认之前不要删除任何邮件"）在压缩过程中会被静默丢弃，导致LLM行为偏离用户意图。作者希望量化这一系统性损失并探索解决方案。

Method: 作者引入COMPINT评估套件，覆盖多轮对话、代理轨迹（agentic trajectory）和长时程研究三个长上下文场景。在此基础上，提出一个SC感知提取器，作为与压缩器并行的即插即用模块，独立识别并保留会话约束，无需修改压缩器或LLM本身。

Result: 现有压缩器平均仅保留17%的注入SCs；保留率因压缩器、提示词、上下文长度、SC措辞和注入位置的不同而显著变化，表明该损失具有系统性。提出的SC感知提取器在三个场景中均达到超90%的保留率。

Conclusion: 会话约束在上下文压缩中的丢失是一个系统性而非个别配置导致的问题，通过独立于压缩器的SC感知提取模块可以高效地兜底这一能力缺口，为长上下文LLM系统的可靠性提供了实用改进方案。

Abstract: When the context window is under pressure, LLM systems compact prior context to continue ongoing tasks. We identify a class of user-issued instructions, Session Constraints (SCs), such as "do not delete any emails until I confirm," that are meant to constrain LLM's behavior for the remainder of a session but are silently dropped during compaction. To quantify this loss, we introduce COMPINT, an evaluation suite that evaluates compactors across three long-context scenarios: multi-turn chat, agentic trajectory, and long-horizon research.
  Current compactors retain only 17% of injected SCs on average, and most perform worse than running the same task without compaction. Retention varies sharply with compactor, prompt, context length, SC phrasing, and injection location, showing that the loss is systematic rather than tied to any single setting. We propose an SC-aware extractor that runs alongside the compactor as a plug-and-play module, achieving over 90% retention across all three scenarios without modifying the compactor or LLM. The COMPINT evaluation suite and accompanying implementation are available at https://github.com/ZhiqiEliWang/compaction-integrity.

</details>


### [106] [Diffuse to Compress: Leveraging Diffusion LMs for Lossless Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11249)
*Angelo Nardone,Paolo Ferragina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将扩散语言模型（DLM）引入无损文本压缩领域，作为自回归LLM压缩方案的替代推理范式，旨在解决LLM压缩方法吞吐量低的问题。作者设计了针对DLM架构特性的编码策略（每次前向传播可独立决定编码符号的数量和位置），在enwik8基准上实现了超越现有方法的压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字文本数据（纯文本、源代码、XML等）快速增长，且基于LLM的压缩方法虽在压缩率上显著优于zstd、gzip等通用压缩器，但吞吐量极低，尚无法实际应用。作者提出利用扩散语言模型替代自回归LLM来克服逐符号生成的吞吐瓶颈。

Method: 在无损神经文本压缩框架中引入扩散语言模型（DLM）作为推理范式替代自回归LLM。DLM允许在前向传播时独立决定编码符号的数量和位置，作者针对这一特性设计了高效的编码策略，解决了将DLM应用于无损压缩时面临的算法挑战，并在enwik8基准上与LLM压缩器和通用压缩器进行对比实验。

Result: 新提出的基于DLM的压缩框架在无损文本压缩方面推进了现有技术水平，在enwik8上取得了优于LLM压缩器和通用压缩器的结果。

Conclusion: DLM作为无损文本压缩的推理范式具有显著潜力，能够克服自回归LLM的吞吐限制并提升压缩性能。由于DLM仍处于快速发展阶段，更多高效模型的出现预示着该方向仍有较大的提升空间。

Abstract: We study the problem of lossless text compression, motivated by the rapid growth in the collection and storage of digital textual data - including plain text, source code, and structured formats such as XML - and by recent advances in neural language model-based compression. In particular, recent LLM-based approaches, whether built on symbol-ranking pipelines or paired with a statistical compressor, have demonstrated compression ratios significantly superior to general-purpose compressors such as zstd, gzip, or bzip on text and code. However, these neural approaches suffer from severe throughput limitations, making them not yet practically usable.
  For the first time in the context of lossless neural text compression, we introduce Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) as an alternative inference paradigm to autoregressive LLM-based approaches. We argue that replacing autoregressive LLMs with DLMs within the same compression framework could overcome the throughput bottleneck caused by their one-symbol-per-step limitation. However, achieving these improvements requires addressing algorithmic challenges introduced by applying DLMs to lossless compression, where the architecture allows the number and positions of symbols encoded at each forward pass to be decided independently. We design efficient and effective strategies to solve these challenges and evaluate them experimentally against LLM-based and general-purpose compressors on enwik8, a well-established textual benchmark.
  Our results show that the newly proposed DLM-based framework advances the state of the art in lossless text compression. Moreover, as DLMs are still a relatively young paradigm, recent advances toward increasingly capable and efficient models suggest substantial room for further improvements.

</details>


### [107] [Gloss-Free Representation Learning for Cross-Dataset Sign Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11332)
*Oğuz Akif Tüfekcioğlu,Ezgi Ekin,Mustafa Kaan Çevik,Hacer Yalim Keles*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源受限语言（土耳其语）中，利用广播新闻中弱对齐的语音转录（而非稠密人工标注）来预训练可复用的手语编码器。通过对比规则形态词形还原与LLM辅助的受约束归一化方法，在TSL-News新语料上预训练，并在跨数据集手语检测基准上验证其可迁移性。LLM辅助方法将top-5时间定位IoU从0.235提升至0.465，BLEU-4从9.60提升至11.04，表明弱转录监督可有效学习包含词汇内容和时间结构的手语表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限语言的手语研究常因稠密语言标注（如注释、时间边界、手语顺序）成本高昂而受限。广播新闻提供了连续手语与口语转录的配对，但这种监督是弱对齐的。土耳其语作为形态丰富语言，同一词汇在不同屈折形式间变化，且词形还原标准化不足会碎片化伪注释目标。作者希望探索弱转录监督能否预训练可复用的手语编码器。

Method: 构建TSL-News（土耳其语手语广播语料库），从转录中生成伪注释标签，而非人工标注。对比两种文本归一化策略：规则形态学词形还原与基于LLM的受约束词汇表辅助归一化。预训练编码器后，通过跨数据集手语检测（TSL Spotting Benchmark，基于TSL Dictionary语料库）评估表示的可迁移性。

Result: LLM辅助编码器将top-5时间定位IoU从0.235提升至0.465，56.2%示例达到IoU≥0.50；频率分析表明增益并非主要来自记忆频繁伪注释。下游翻译实验中，相同预训练将BLEU-4从9.60提升至11.04，ROUGE从23.48提升至27.43。

Conclusion: 松散对齐的广播数据可以为学习手语表示提供有效的弱监督，捕获词汇内容和时间结构。这为资源受限语言的手语表示学习提供了一种低成本的替代路径，避免了昂贵的人工稠密标注。

Abstract: Sign-language research for resource-constrained languages is often limited by the cost of dense linguistic labels such as glosses, temporal boundaries, and sign order. Broadcast news offers a practical alternative by pairing continuous signing with spoken-language transcripts, but this supervision is weak since text and signing are loosely aligned. Morphologically rich languages such as Turkish add further difficulty, as the same lexical meaning can appear in many inflected forms while some derived forms should remain distinct. We study whether weak transcript-based supervision can pretrain a reusable sign encoder in this setting, where poor text normalization can fragment pseudo-gloss targets and weaken representation learning. Unlike prior pseudo-gloss pipelines designed mainly to improve translation, we test whether the pretrained encoder transfers as a reusable representation for cross-dataset sign spotting. We pretrain on TSL-News, a new Turkish broadcast corpus, using pseudo-gloss labels derived from transcripts rather than manual annotation, comparing rule-based morphological lemmatization with constrained LLM-assisted normalization over a fixed vocabulary. We evaluate the learned representations via cross-dataset sign spotting on a new TSL Spotting Benchmark built from the TSL Dictionary corpus. The LLM-assisted encoder raises top-5 temporal localization mean IoU from 0.235 to 0.465, with 56.2% of examples reaching an IoU of at least 0.50; a frequency analysis suggests this gain is not mainly driven by memorizing frequent pseudo-gloss labels. In a downstream translation check, the same pretraining improves BLEU-4 from 9.60 to 11.04 and ROUGE from 23.48 to 27.43. These results show that loosely aligned broadcast data can provide effective weak supervision for learning sign representations that capture both lexical content and temporal structure.

</details>


### [108] [Better, Faster, Stronger: Programmatic Skill Learning Best Reduces Agent Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11338)
*Zixi Huang,Xiheng Wang,Andrew Wang,William Jurayj,Bernal Jiménez Gutiérrez,Daniel Khashabi,Nicholas Andrews*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SpeedRunner，一个通过将技能视为程序、在推理时增量学习并重构技能来降低LLM agent适应新领域成本的编码智能体。实验表明SpeedRunner在三个具身环境中实现了学习效果与成本削减的最佳平衡，同时对分布偏移和环境随机性保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作侧重于通过技能学习提升性能而非降低成本，因此对何种技能学习策略能节省成本知之甚少。作者主张将技能视为程序的方法能实现最佳成本削减，因为程序化的确定性执行可以避免试错和长时序中的退化行为。

Method: 提出SpeedRunner，一种编码智能体，它在推理时分析历史轨迹并增量发现、重构程序化技能以供未来任务使用。该方法基于一个假设：过去轨迹中包含足够的信号来指导技能学习，即使没有回放或验证，只要agent能够学会分析这些轨迹。

Result: 在三个不同的具身环境中，SpeedRunner一致地达到了学习效果与成本削减的前沿，同时对分布偏移和环境随机性保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将技能视为程序并基于轨迹分析进行增量学习，是一种在成本效益上具有显著优势的agent适应新领域策略，能够在降低成本的同时保持良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recently, the practice of augmenting LLM agent capability with skills has gained prevalence. We explore the cost effective adaptation of agents to novel domains by means of learning skills. Existing works focus on performance gain over cost effectiveness. As a result, little is known about what skill learning strategies save cost. We argue that among all the different skill learning methods, those that view skills as programs can achieve the best cost reduction. By executing sequences of actions deterministically, a program-augmented agent can reliably and cheaply achieve goals that would otherwise require trial and error and risk degenerate behavior over long horizons. An agent can learn at inference time by incrementally discovering these programs and equipping them for future tasks. We hypothesize that past trajectories contain enough signal to guide skill learning, even without replay or validation, provided the agent can learn to analyze them. To test our claims, we propose SpeedRunner, a coding agent that analyzes trajectories and refactors skills for better performance on future tasks. Across three different embodied environments, we show that SpeedRunner consistently achieves the frontier in learning and cost reduction while remaining robust against distribution shifts and environmental randomness.

</details>


### [109] [Self-Evolving Embodied Agents via Skill-Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11350)
*Peidong Wang,Zhiming Ma,Ying Chang,Xufang Luo,Xiaocui Yang,Shi Feng,Yuqing Yang,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SHAPER 是一个无需训练的自我进化框架，通过保持模型参数冻结、仅演进可复用技能和上下文代码装备（harness）来使具身智能体适应新环境，在 VLABench 和 ESI-Bench 上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体依赖基础模型构建，其性能不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于技能、上下文、动作接口和执行装备。监督微调和强化学习虽然能适应新环境，但需要额外数据、奖励和训练开销；而现有的免训练代码中心方法依赖可编程机器人API，在固定接口场景中不可用。因此需要一种无需训练就能让智能体适应新环境的方法。

Method: SHAPER 提出自我进化框架，保持模型参数冻结，通过目标环境中的 rollout 不断演进外部可复用技能库和上下文代码装备（context-code harness）。同一冻结模型同时充当规划器和优化器，在不更新参数的情况下对外部技能和装备进行精炼。这种非参数化系统改进是核心机制。

Result: 在 VLABench 和 ESI-Bench 两个基准上评估了不同底层动作接口的具身智能体，并与纯执行、监督微调以及免验证选择、投票等测试时扩展基线进行比较，结果表明技能与装备优化是实现自我进化具身智能体的实用路径。

Conclusion: 当模型训练成本高昂、不可用或不合需要时，通过演进技能和装备（而非模型参数）实现自我进化是一个可行且实用的路线，为具身智能体的适应能力提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Embodied agents are increasingly built as systems around foundation models, where performance depends not only on model weights but also on the skills, context, action interfaces, and execution harness surrounding the model. While supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning can adapt agents to new environments, they require additional data, rewards, and training runs; meanwhile, many train-free code-centric approaches rely on programmable robot APIs that may be unavailable in fixed-interface settings. We propose SHAPER, a self-evolving framework for train-free embodied adaptation that keeps model parameters frozen and improves the non-parametric agent system by evolving reusable skills and a context-code harness through target-environment rollouts. In SHAPER, the same frozen model can serve as both planner and optimizer, refining its external skills and context-code harness without parameter updates. We evaluate SHAPER on VLABench and ESI-Bench, covering embodied agents with different low-level action interfaces, and compare against pure execution, supervised fine-tuning, and test-time-scaling baselines such as verifier-free selection and voting. Our results suggest that skill-and-harness optimization is a practical route to self-evolving embodied agents when model training is expensive, unavailable, or undesirable.

</details>


### [110] [ODE-Based Transformer Decoders for Iterative Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11352)
*Tuğçe Kızıltepe,Hacer Yalim Keles*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数高效的手语翻译方法，从常微分方程（ODE）视角重新审视迭代精炼解码器的残差更新，将其替换为高阶数值积分方案（Runge-Kutta RK-2和RK-4），在不增加解码器参数的情况下提升表示更新的准确性和稳定性，在PHOENIX-2014-T和CSL-Daily基准上超越IPSLT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手语翻译的Transformer架构虽然取得了强劲结果，但近期改进主要依赖扩大模型容量，计算成本高昂。作者希望找到一种不增加模型规模、却能提升表达能力的参数高效替代方案。

Method: 从ODE视角重新解释迭代精炼解码器中的残差精炼更新，将其替换为高阶数值积分方案：Runge-Kutta方法（RK-2和RK-4）。这些方法在每个精炼步骤内进行多次函数评估，生成更准确和稳定的表示更新，且不增加解码器参数。每个精炼步骤对应一个内部解码器迭代，逐步改善翻译生成前的潜在表示。

Result: RK-2在PHOENIX-2014-T测试集上达到22.96 BLEU-4，在CSL-Daily测试集上达到19.34 BLEU-4，在两项基准上均超越IPSLT基线，且在CSL-Daily上使用了更少的解码器层和精炼迭代次数。

Conclusion: 更强的精炼动力学可以在参数高效的解码器设计下提升翻译性能，为传统模型扩展提供了一种互补的替代方案。这是ODE启发的更新动力学首次应用于手语翻译。

Abstract: Sign language translation has achieved strong results with Transformer architectures, yet recent improvements largely rely on scaling model capacity at the cost of increased computation. We propose a parameter-efficient alternative that improves expressiveness without increasing model size. Rather than scaling capacity, we focus on enhancing the update dynamics of iterative refinement decoders, where each refinement step corresponds to one internal decoder iteration that progressively improves the latent representation before translation generation. We reinterpret residual refinement updates from an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) perspective and replace them with higher-order numerical integration schemes, namely Runge--Kutta methods (RK-2 and RK-4). These methods perform multiple function evaluations within each refinement step to produce more accurate and stable representation updates without adding decoder parameters. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first application of ODE-inspired update dynamics to sign language translation. RK-2 achieves 22.96 BLEU-4 on the PHOENIX-2014-T test set and 19.34 BLEU-4 on the CSL-Daily test set, outperforming the IPSLT baseline on both benchmarks, with fewer decoder layers and refinement iterations on CSL-Daily. These results suggest that stronger refinement dynamics can improve translation performance under parameter-efficient decoder designs, providing a complementary alternative to conventional model scaling.

</details>


### [111] [Measure, Don't Optimize: Forecasting Recovery in LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11408)
*Zirui Song,Huaxing Liu,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Xinye Li,Lang Gao,Jinghui Zhang,Zheng Lu,Fengxian Ji,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出J-Access，一种基于雅可比矩阵的推理时审计方法，用于检测大语言模型在"遗忘"后是否仍保留目标知识的残余可访问性。通过对398个公开的遗忘模型进行评估，发现大多数模型仍保留高于基线水平的残余知识访问，且该指标可预测模型层面的恢复速度，但无法定位具体事实，直接将其作为优化目标反而会被模型"欺骗"而产生虚假的安全感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有白盒审计仅提供一次性诊断，无法回答两个关键问题：(1)残余信号能否预测模型在继续训练下的知识恢复？(2)残余信号能否作为可靠的优化目标？解决这些问题对于判断内部审计能否从事后评估走向主动风险监控至关重要。

Method: J-Access使用雅可比（Jacobian）透镜，将模型的中间表示映射到词汇空间，测量目标概念沿着模型输出通路保持可访问的频率。研究覆盖398个公开模型、8种遗忘方法，并通过"攻击"（继续微调）验证预攻击可访问性与恢复速度/程度的关系，同时检验将J-Access最小化作为优化目标的效果。

Result: (1) 大多数遗忘模型保留的访问水平高于仅保留层的黄金基线；(2) 预攻击可访问性在模型层面可预测恢复速度与程度，但无法预测哪些具体事实会被恢复；(3) 直接最小化J-Access不会促进真正的删除，模型学会隐藏知识以逃避审计，导致审计分数降低但攻击后恢复反而更严重。

Conclusion: J-Access可作为模型层面的残余易感性诊断工具，但内部审计应作为遗忘评估的独立诊断维度，不应未经验证就转化为优化目标。
