Abstract: Prior white-box studies show that large language models can retain latent traces of target knowledge after unlearning, even when the knowledge is no longer expressed in their outputs. However, existing audits remain limited to one-off diagnostics: it is unclear whether these residual signals can predict future recovery under continued training or serve as reliable optimization targets. Resolving this gap is essential to determine whether internal auditing can move beyond post-hoc evaluation toward proactive risk monitoring and safer unlearning. We propose J-Access, an inference-time audit that uses the Jacobian lens to map intermediate representations into vocabulary space and measures how often target concepts remain accessible along the model's output pathway. We hypothesize that residual accessibility reflects recovery susceptibility: knowledge that remains closer to the output pathway requires less fine-tuning to restore, leading to faster recovery. We audit 398 public unlearned models spanning eight unlearning methods. We find that: (1) most unlearned models retain access above the retain-only gold level; (2) pre-attack accessibility predicts recovery speed and extent at the model level, but cannot identify which specific facts will be recovered; and (3) directly minimizing J-Access does not promote genuine deletion. Instead, the model learns to hide knowledge from the audit, producing lower audit scores but greater post-attack recovery. These findings position J-Access as a model-level diagnostic for assessing residual susceptibility in unlearned models. We argue internal audits should serve as an independent diagnostic dimension in unlearning evaluation, and should not be converted into optimization targets without validation.

</details>


### [112] [Is Convergence Inevitable? Tracing Output Homogeneity Back to Base Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11426)
*Alexandrine Fortier,Hazel Chen,Peter West*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型输出同质化（语义收敛）并非由对齐过程引入，而是在预训练阶段就已经习得，对齐（如SFT）只是揭示或放大这一现象。通过受控SFT实验和基座模型提示实验，作者证明收敛可由SFT数据揭示和放大但不会被引入，且仅通过提示即可在未对齐模型上诱发类似的坍缩，表明该现象源于LM训练目标的固有特性，难以通过后对齐干预解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究普遍将LM输出的多样性缺失归因于对齐过程，但缺乏对"坍缩从何时、在哪个阶段开始"的精确认识。作者希望厘清语义收敛的起源，判断其究竟是SFT/RLHF引入的，还是在预训练阶段就已存在，从而为缓解该问题提供正确方向。

Method: 1) 在多阶段对齐流程中追踪语义收敛的起始点（发现从SFT阶段即出现）；2) 设计受控SFT实验，系统改变训练数据的输入/输出对，观察收敛是否被引入、揭示或放大；3) 直接测量基座模型（base model）的收敛程度，并通过纯提示（prompting）方式在未对齐模型上诱发类似坍缩。

Result: 语义收敛在SFT阶段（对齐的第一阶段）即可观察到；受控实验表明SFT数据只能揭示和放大收敛，不能引入收敛；基座模型仅通过提示就能产生与对齐模型类似的坍缩现象。综合表明同质化源于预训练阶段的LM训练目标本身。

Conclusion: 输出同质化是LM训练过程的内在产物，而非对齐后引入的伪影。对齐只起到催化剂作用，因此仅靠后对齐阶段（如SFT/RLHF）的干预难以根治该问题，需要重新审视预训练目标或从更早期阶段入手加以缓解。

Abstract: The lack of diversity in LM content is widely attributed to the alignment process, but how and where exactly in the pipeline this collapse begins is unknown. We argue that output homogeneity is likely learned during the pretraining phase, and only \emph{revealed} or magnified during the alignment process. Specifically, we find that semantic convergence is observed from the first alignment stage--the instruction-tuning phase (SFT)--suggesting that homogeneity might already exist in the pre-alignment model. To investigate this, we conduct controlled SFT experiments examining how training data influences output convergence on specific input/output pairs. We find that convergence can be revealed and amplified, but not introduced by the SFT data, supporting its role as a catalyst rather than a cause. To further test whether homogeneity originates before alignment, we measure convergence in base models. We find that instruct-like collapse can be induced through prompting alone, even without alignment. Taken together, our results suggest that semantic convergence may arise naturally from the objectives underlying LM training, making it difficult to mitigate through post-alignment interventions alone.

</details>


### [113] [Stigma and Support in Online Sexual Violence Narratives on Reddit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11433)
*Shirlene Rose Bandela,Karan Bindal,Vaibhav Garg,Rezvaneh Rezapour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SCOPE数据集，将在线性暴力幸存者叙事中的污名信号与评论中的支持类型相关联，通过多维污名分类和支持分类进行标注，发现污名叙事更强调内在痛苦，而社区回应在不同污名类型间保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究分别考察了污名和社会支持，但较少探讨幸存者叙事中表达的污名如何与回应的支持类型相关联，缺乏将两者联系起来的数据集和分析。

Method: 构建SCOPE数据集，对帖子使用包含经历性污名、内化污名、预期污名和结构性污名的多维污名分类注标，对评论使用包含信息支持、情感支持、自尊支持、实际帮助和群体互动的支持分类注标，并采用语境、语言和情感分析进行对比。

Result: 污名叙事更强调内化痛苦，非污名叙事更侧重于解读情境和经历；内化污名是最普遍的类别；社区回应在不同污名类型间保持广泛稳定，其中信息支持和自尊支持出现频率最高。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了污名如何塑造幸存者叙事和同伴回应，为计算建模、内容审核和更安全的在线系统设计提供了启示。

Abstract: Online communities increasingly provide spaces where survivors of sexual violence can share their experiences and seek support. Although prior research has examined stigma and social support separately, less is known about how stigma expressed in survivor narratives relates to the support offered in response. We introduce the SCOPE dataset, linking stigma signals in online survivor narratives to support types in corresponding comment threads. We annotate posts using a multi-dimensional stigma taxonomy, including Experienced, Internalized, Anticipated, and Structural Stigma, and comments using a support taxonomy encompassing Information Support, Emotional Support, Esteem Support, Tangible Assistance, and Group Interaction. Using contextual, linguistic, and emotion analyses, we compare Stigma and No Stigma content and find that Stigma narratives place greater emphasis on internalized distress, whereas No Stigma narratives focus more on interpreting situations and experiences. Internalized Stigma is the most prevalent category, and community responses remain broadly stable across stigma types, with Information and Esteem Support appearing most often. These findings show how stigma shapes survivor narratives and peer responses and have implications for computational modeling, content moderation, and safer online systems.

</details>


### [114] [DonorRank: Donor Language Selection for Low-Resource Cross-Lingual Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11441)
*Akriti Dhasmana,Aarohi Srivastava,David Chiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DonorRank，一个基于学习排序（learning-to-rank）的框架，用于预测零样本低资源自动语音识别（ASR）中的有效"捐赠"语言（donor languages），并在印度语系和非洲语系的多语言语音语料库上验证了其优于基于遗传相似性或高资源语言的常见启发式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语音识别通常依赖从高资源语言进行跨语言迁移，但为自发语音（尤其来自低资源语言社区）选择有效的捐赠语言面临挑战，原因包括语言变异、拼写规范不断演变以及资源分布不均。现有方法如基于遗传相似性或选择高资源语言作为启发式策略往往不够可靠。

Method: DonorRank采用学习到排序（learning-to-rank）框架来预测捐赠语言的有效性排序。在印度语系和非洲语系两个多语言语音语料库上进行评估，同时利用该框架分析捐赠语言选择本身，包括捐赠集构成如何影响有用的语言学线索。

Result: DonorRank能够准确预测捐赠语言排名，并且在捐赠语言选择上优于基于遗传相似性或高资源语言的常见启发式方法。此外，分析表明捐赠集的组成决定了哪些语言学线索对预测成功的迁移是有用的，并识别出对低资源多语言ASR有实际指导意义的迁移模式。

Conclusion: DonorRank不仅改善了跨语言迁移中的捐赠语言选择，还提供了一个通用分析框架，用于深入理解捐赠语言选择的机制及其语言学影响因素，为低资源场景下的多语言ASR提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Low-resource automatic speech recognition (ASR) commonly relies on cross-lingual transfer, where models are adapted from higher-resource donor languages. However, selecting donors remains challenging for spontaneous speech from under-resourced language communities, due to linguistic variation, evolving orthographic conventions, and uneven resource availability. We present DonorRank, a learning-to-rank framework for predicting effective donor languages for zero-shot ASR. We evaluate DonorRank on two multilingual speech corpora of Indic and African language families. It accurately predicts donor language rankings and improves donor selection over common heuristics based on genetic similarity or high-resource languages. Beyond improving transfer, we show how DonorRank is a general framework for analyzing donor language selection itself. Our analyses show that the composition of the donor set determines which linguistic cues are useful in predicting successful transfer. We also identify transfer patterns that provide practical guidance for multilingual ASR in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [115] [Principal Trait Analysis: Towards Deriving "Skills" in Human-AI Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11460)
*Hunter McNichols,Kai Du,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"主特征分析"(Principal Trait Analysis, PTA)的数据驱动方法，用于自动发现大规模语言模型(LLM)驱动的智能体与人类协作过程中有效的交互特征（traits）。PTA基于主成分分析的思想，通过LLM处理流水线从人-AI协作对话语料中提取共同特征，并在教育（AI辅导）和专业（AI编程代理）两个场景上进行评估，结果发现PTA提取的特征能显著解释协作者行为并预测任务成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM驱动的智能体越来越多地进入职场人机协作环境，理解哪些提示特征(prompting traits)有助于任务成功变得至关重要。然而，现有的"人-AI协作"指导准则要么基于自上而下的理论构建，要么基于特定情境的经验观察；由于LLM能力快速迭代，理论可能无法解释新兴交互模式，而经验准则也可能很快过时。因此需要一种自动化、数据驱动的方法来发现与任务结果对齐的有效交互模式。

Method: 提出Principal Trait Analysis (PTA)算法：一种受主成分分析(PCA)启发的算法，包含多个LLM-based处理阶段，用于分析人-AI协作会话轨迹语料库。算法从数据集中推导出共同特征，并为每位人类协作者按各特征打分其使用风格。PTA允许在特征发现过程中注入领域专业知识，并选择在协作者之间方差最高的特征作为最具区分度的特征。

Result: 在两个人-AI协作编程数据集上（教育场景的学生-AI导师、专业场景的开发者-AI编码代理）进行评估，发现PTA推导出的特征在两个场景中均能显著解释协作者行为，并能帮助预测任务成果。然而，关于特征是否能定义为技能(skills)尚不确定，因为泛化性和用户特征随时间变化的实验结果不具决定性。

Conclusion: PTA作为一种数据驱动的方法，能够从人-AI交互数据中发现与任务结果对齐的交互特征，为理解人机协作中的关键技能提供了新视角，也为教育者培养学生相关能力提供了参考。虽然方法在解释力和预测力上表现良好，但特征是否能上升为"通用技能"仍需进一步验证，特别是在泛化性和时间稳定性方面。

Abstract: Large Language Model-powered agents are increasingly used in the workplace via human-artificial intelligence (AI) collaboration. In this new era of work, it is important to understand the kinds of prompting traits that contribute to task success. Moreover, we need to uncover key skills required for modern professionals and inform educators on how to foster these skills among students. Existing guidelines for human-AI collaboration are built from either top-down theory or context-specific observations of human-AI interactions. However, since LLM capabilities are rapidly improving, theory may not be able to explain emerging interaction patterns, and empirical guidelines may become obsolete quickly. In this work, we explore an automated, data-driven approach to uncover patterns, which we term traits, of effective human-AI interaction that are aligned with task outcomes. We propose Principal Trait Analysis, a Principal Component Analysis-inspired algorithm for deriving common traits from patterns in LLM conversations. Our algorithm uses LLM-based processing stages to analyze corpora of human-AI collaborative session traces, deriving common traits across the dataset and scoring each human collaborator's usage style by each trait. The approach also allows domain expertise to be injected during trait discovery and selects the most distinguishing traits to be those that exhibit the highest variance across collaborators. We evaluate PTA on two human-AI collaborative coding datasets, an educational setting (students working with an AI tutor) and a professional setting (developers working with an AI coding agent). We find that PTA-derived traits are significant in explaining collaborator behavior across both settings and can help predict task outcomes. However, whether traits qualify as skills remains to be seen, due to inconclusive results on generalizability and how user traits change over time.

</details>


### [116] [Group Alignment-Induced Sycophancy: A Two-Sided Evaluation of Steerable Pluralistic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11528)
*Haokai Zhao,Yunze Xiao,Weihao Xuan,Flora Salim,Benjamin Tag,Aditya Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"群体对齐诱发的谄媚行为"（GAS）概念，系统评估了3种对齐方法、4个模型、13个人口统计群体在意见对齐增益与谄媚偏移两个维度的表现，发现增益与偏移在群体间非均匀分布，并主张群体对齐应报告为双向多维画像而非单一拟合分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体对齐方法和评估仅关注模型与群体意见的匹配程度，忽略了对齐过程中诱发的谄媚行为（sycophancy）变化。谄媚是对齐的已知副产品，会使模型无论事实如何都过度迎合用户，但对齐评估框架尚未系统衡量这一副作用。

Method: 提出Group Alignment-induced Sycophancy (GAS)概念，在3种对齐方法、4个模型、13个人口统计群体上，分别从"意见对齐的理想增益"和"谄媚的意外偏移"两个维度进行系统评估，分析增益与偏移在不同群体间的分布特征。

Result: 发现意见对齐增益与谄媚偏移在群体间非均匀分布：在相同预算下，部分群体获得的对齐增益显著大于其他群体；谄媚偏移呈现群体特异的画像特征，而非单一维度的统一变化。

Conclusion: 群体对齐评估应从单一拟合分数转向双向、多维画像报告，以反映每类对齐方式在对齐增益与谄媚副作用之间的权衡，并在将LLM适配至多元人群时纳入群体间差异的考量。

Abstract: Group alignment adapts a language model to a demographic group to produce responses that reflect the group's opinions, values, and preferences. Sycophancy, a well-documented by-product of alignment, causes the model to over-agree with the user regardless of factual and objective information. However, existing group alignment methods and evaluations focus only on how closely the model matches the group's opinions, overlooking the induced change in sycophantic behaviour. To bridge this gap, we introduce \textbf{G}roup \textbf{A}lignment-induced \textbf{S}ycophancy (GAS) and systematically evaluate alignment across 3 methods, 4 models and 13 demographic groups, on both the intended gain in opinion alignment and the unintended shift in sycophancy. We find that gain and shift are non-uniform across groups: under an identical budget, some groups receive larger gains in opinion alignment than others, and the induced sycophancy shift forms a group-specific profile rather than a single-dimensional change. These results suggest that group alignment should be reported as a two-sided, multi-dimensional profile rather than a single fit score that accounts for per-group differences when adapting LLMs to diverse populations.

</details>


### [117] [On Weak Bisimilarities in CCSK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11531)
*Baptiste Vallée,Ivan Lanese*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在可逆进程演算CCSK（CCS的可逆扩展）背景下，系统研究了强/弱、仅向前/可逆等不同双模拟关系，并提出了两种新的弱可逆双模拟变体（方向性双模拟和混合双模拟），证明了混合双模拟是合关系且能完全抽象掉τ动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可逆计算是并发计算的重要研究方向，而CCSK作为CCS的可逆扩展，其行为等价理论尚不完善。特别是弱可逆情况下的双模拟关系此前未在文献中被研究过，需要系统地厘清不同双模拟概念之间的区别与联系。

Method: 在CCSK框架下，系统地比较了多种双模拟概念（强/弱、仅向前/可逆），并针对弱可逆情形提出了两种新变体：方向性双模拟（要求τ动作与所匹配动作同向）和混合双模拟（不要求τ动作方向一致）。通过证明混合双模拟满足合关系性质并完全抽象τ动作来验证其合理性。

Result: 展示了不同双模拟概念之间的差异与共同点，提出了两种弱可逆双模拟变体，并证明了混合双模拟是一种合关系，且能够完全抽象掉τ动作的影响。

Conclusion: 混合双模拟是弱可逆情景下最具抽象力的合理等价关系，为可逆进程演算的行为等价理论提供了新的基础。

Abstract: In the context of CCSK, a reversible extension of CCS, we study different notions of bisimilarity (strong/weak, forward-only/reversible) and highlight their differences and commonalities. In particular, for the weak reversible case, not previously studied in the literature, we propose two variants, dubbed directional and mixed bisimilarity, depending on whether $τ$ actions should be in the same direction (forward/backward) as the action being matched or not. We show, in particular, that mixed bisimilarity is a congruence and completely abstracts away from $τ$ actions.

</details>


### [118] [CT-$Δ$Bench: A Benchmark for Longitudinal 3D Medical Imaging Difference Reporting with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11534)
*Kegeng Tang,Jingbo Wang,Shaogang Ren,Zihao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-ΔBench基准和DeltaMed基线模型，用于医学影像中纵向CT比较的差异报告生成任务，并开发了变更感知评估指标和医生验证流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基础模型局限于单次扫描的理解，缺乏对同一患者多次纵向CT扫描之间时间变化的分析能力，而纵向比较在临床决策（如治疗反应评估、复发检测）中至关重要。

Method: 1) 引入CT-ΔBench基准，采用患者级分割防止信息泄漏；2) 开发变更感知指标，超越表面文本相似度以捕捉临床有意义的纵向变化；3) 进行独立医生验证评估合成参考和事件提取管道的可靠性；4) 比较直接配对CT推理与间接两阶段管道（先生成单时间点报告再进行文本差异）；5) 提出DeltaMed基线模型并在基准训练集上训练。

Result: 成功建立了纵向CT差异报告任务的基准与基线方法，验证了变更感知评估指标的可靠性，并对比了直接与间接两种推理路径的性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为时间感知的医学基础模型奠定基础，使其更好地反映真实世界中的纵向临床推理。

Abstract: In medical imaging, the clinical value of Computed Tomography (CT) lies not only in depicting current disease status, but crucially in enabling longitudinal comparison of serial scans to determine disease evolution, a process that underpins response assessment, recurrence detection, and ongoing patient management. Yet, despite this central role of temporal comparison in clinical decision-making, existing medical foundation models remain largely confined to single-study understanding, leaving temporally grounded cross-examination insufficiently addressed. To address this gap, we study longitudinal imaging difference reporting, a task in which a model takes two temporally separated scans from the same patient and generates a clinically meaningful report describing interval changes between them. We introduce CT-$Δ$Bench, a dedicated benchmark for this task with patient-level splitting to prevent information leakage. To better evaluate this task beyond surface-level text similarity, we further develop change-aware metrics specifically designed to capture clinically meaningful longitudinal changes, and conduct an independent physician validation to assess the reliability of the synthesized references and event extraction pipeline. We also compare direct paired-CT reasoning with an indirect two-stage pipeline that first generates single-timepoint reports and then performs textual differencing. Finally, we propose DeltaMed, a baseline model for direct paired-CT difference reporting, and train it on the benchmark training set. Together, these contributions lay the groundwork for temporally aware medical foundation models that better reflect real-world longitudinal clinical reasoning.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Single-Turn Confidence: Trajectory-Adapted Uncertainty Quantification for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11552)
*Dylan Bouchard,Mohit Singh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了单轮不确定性量化（UQ）方法是否适用于LLM智能体的多轮交互轨迹场景。在5个LLM和4个多轮工具使用数据集上评估了三类UQ方法（基于动作标记概率的白盒评分、基于重采样轨迹的黑盒一致性评分、基于自我评估的反身评分），发现迁移效果参差不齐：标记概率对聚合器选择高度敏感，反身评分是多数场景下最强低成本基线，黑盒自洽性通常最强，其中轨迹等价性和动作集一致性表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UQ方法主要在单轮输出上评估，但LLM智能体的观测单元是包含提问、调用工具、更新状态和中间决策的交互轨迹，其中错误会传播到最终结果。需要验证单轮UQ方法是否能在轨迹层面有效迁移。

Method: 在5个LLM和4个多轮工具使用数据集（来自BFCL-v4和τ²-bench）上，系统评估三类UQ方法：基于动作标记概率的白盒评分器、基于重采样轨迹的黑盒一致性评分器、基于模型对轨迹自我评估的反身评分器，并比较不同变体（如不同聚合器和一致性度量方式）。

Result: 三类UQ方法在轨迹层面迁移效果不均：标记概率分数对跨轮聚合器的选择高度敏感；反身分数在多数评估场景下提供最强的低成本基线；黑盒自洽性通常是最强的UQ族，其中轨迹等价性和动作集一致性变体排名最高。

Conclusion: 为单次生成开发的UQ方法应在轨迹层面重新验证，需特别注意一致性度量方式、聚合器选择和计算预算的权衡。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods for language models are typically evaluated on single-turn outputs, where uncertainty is attached to one generated answer. For LLM agents, however, the unit of observation is an interactive trajectory, where the model can ask clarifying questions, call tools, update state, and make intermediate decisions whose errors propagate to the final outcome. We study whether three common families of single-turn UQ methods transfer to this setting. Across five LLMs and four multi-turn tool-use datasets from BFCL-v4 and $τ^2$-bench, we evaluate white-box scorers based on action-token probabilities, black-box consistency scorers based on resampled trajectories, and reflexive scorers based on model self-assessment of the trajectory. We find that transfer is often useful but uneven. Token-probability scores are highly sensitive to the choice of aggregator used across turns, reflexive scores provide the strongest low-cost baseline in most evaluated settings, and black-box self-consistency is often the strongest UQ family, with trajectory-equivalence and action-set consistency typically ranking highest among its variants. These results suggest that UQ methods developed for single generations should be revalidated at the trajectory level, with careful attention to the consistency measurement, aggregator choice, and computational budget.

</details>


### [120] [Reinforcing Step-level Reasoning for Effective Self-Correction in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11573)
*Vu Duc Anh,Nhat M. Hoang,Do Xuan Long,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Ponhvoan Srey,Luu Anh Tuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SFS-DPO是一个基于强化学习的两阶段框架，用于提升大语言模型的步骤级自我验证与自我纠正能力。第一阶段通过步骤级偏好优化强化推理，第二阶段显式训练模型进行自我验证和纠正，并引入教师辅助变体SFS-DPO-R增强纠错信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自我纠正（验证并纠正自身错误）方面仍面临根本性挑战，现有的步骤级训练基线方法效果有限，需要更有效的训练框架来提升模型的自我验证与自我纠正能力。

Method: 提出SFS-DPO两阶段强化学习框架：第一阶段通过步骤级偏好优化（step-level preference optimization）强化推理能力；第二阶段显式训练模型进行自我验证与自我纠正。同时提出教师辅助变体SFS-DPO-R，引入错误验证的解释性理由（explanatory rationales）以提供更强的纠正信号。

Result: 在多个LLM上的领域内和领域外综合评估表明，SFS-DPO和SFS-DPO-R持续优于先前的步骤级训练基线。分析进一步揭示模型在自我纠正频率和有效性方面的提升。

Conclusion: 强化步骤级推理对鲁棒性能至关重要，SFS-DPO系列方法通过分阶段训练有效提升了LLM的自我纠正能力。

Abstract: Achieving effective self-correction, where models verify and correct their own mistakes, remains a fundamental challenge for large language models (LLMs). In this work, we propose Self-Fix Step-DPO (SFS-DPO), a reinforcement learning based, two-stage framework for step-level self-verification and self-correction. The first stage strengthens step-level reasoning via step-level preference optimization, while the second stage explicitly trains models to self-verify and self-correct. We further introduce a teacher-assisted variant, SFS-DPO-R, which incorporates explanatory rationales for error verification to provide stronger corrective signals. Comprehensive in-domain and out-of-domain evaluations across multiple LLMs demonstrate that SFS-DPO and SFS-DPO-R consistently outperform prior step-level training baselines. Our analysis further reveals improvements in self-correction frequency and effectiveness, highlighting the importance of strengthening step-level reasoning for robust performance.

</details>


### [121] [Learning to Persuade Exposes How Easily LLMs Abandon Correct Beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11624)
*Nimet Beyza Bozdag,Emre Can Acikgoz,Gokhan Tur,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型（LLM）在面对单一针对性说服性论证时极易被诱导产生错误结论，即使该论证在事实上是错误的。作者提出对抗性说服（adversarial persuasion）这一威胁形式，并引入对抗性强化学习框架训练说服者（persuader）智能体，使其能在单次交互中改变目标模型的答案。RL训练的说服者将说服成功率从约24%提升至93%以上，且策略可迁移至未见过的模型（Qwen-14B达83%、Llama-3.1-8B达79%、GPT-4o-mini达25%），通过课程学习可进一步提升对GPT-4o-mini的攻击成功率至38%。研究还发现优化后的说服者越来越依赖基于可信度的策略，如捏造引用和虚假权威证据，暴露了当前LLM智能体的关键安全弱点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM越来越多地参与辩论、提供建议以及与人类和其他模型协作决策，抵抗有害说服成为其可靠行为的核心要求。但现有研究表明这一要求远未实现：即使模型初始推理正确，也可能被单一目标性说服论证引导至错误结论。因此，需要正式定义并系统研究这一威胁，以推动说服鲁棒性成为多智能体和人机决策系统必要的安全标准。

Method: 作者形式化定义了"对抗性说服"威胁，并构建了一个对抗性强化学习框架：训练说服者智能体（persuader）通过试错优化说服策略，目标是在单次交互中改变目标模型（persuadee）的答案。框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 通过RL优化说服策略，相比静态提示能暴露更多漏洞；(2) 采用课程学习（curriculum learning），先在更易说服的开源模型上引导（bootstrap），再迁移到更难的闭源模型上进行攻击。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) RL训练的说服者将说服成功率从约24%提升至超过93%（针对训练时的目标模型）；(2) 学习到的策略可迁移至未见模型，在Qwen-14B上达83%、Llama-3.1-8B上达79%、GPT-4o-mini上达25%；(3) 采用课程学习策略后，对GPT-4o-mini的攻击成功率从25%提升至38%；(4) 优化的说服者日益依赖基于可信度的策略，包括捏造引文和虚假权威证据。

Conclusion: 研究揭示当前LLM智能体存在关键安全弱点：即使初始推理正确，也能被优化的自然语言影响力导向错误结论。这意味着说服鲁棒性（persuasion robustness）应被视为多智能体系统和人类-AI协作决策框架的必要安全标准。作者呼吁将对抗说服纳入LLM安全评估与防御研究的核心范畴。

Abstract: Persuasion is a core dynamic of natural language communication, shaping how large language models (LLMs) update beliefs, resolve disagreements, and reach decisions. As LLMs increasingly debate, advise, and think collaboratively with humans and each other, resistance to harmful persuasion becomes a core requirement for reliable behavior. Yet we show that this requirement is far from met: a single targeted persuasive argument is enough to collapse model accuracy to near zero, even when the argument is factually false. We formalize this threat as adversarial persuasion and introduce an adversarial reinforcement learning framework that trains persuader agents to change a target model's answer in a single interaction. First, we show that optimizing persuasion strategies through trial and error exposes vulnerabilities that static prompting misses: RL-trained persuaders raise persuasion success from approximately 24% to over 93% against the training-time persuadee. Second, we find that these learned strategies transfer to unseen models, achieving 83% attack success on Qwen-14B, 79% on Llama-3.1-8B, and 25% on GPT-4o-mini. Third, we demonstrate that a curriculum that bootstraps on more persuadable open-weight models before targeting harder models further increases GPT-4o-mini attack success from 25% to 38%. Moreover, our results reveal that optimized persuaders increasingly rely on credibility-based tactics, including fabricated citations and false authoritative evidence. Together, these findings expose a critical weakness in current LLM agents: even when they initially reason correctly, they can be steered toward false conclusions by optimized natural language influence. This positions persuasion robustness as a necessary safety criterion for multi-agent and human-AI decision-making systems.

</details>


### [122] [Easper: An Accessible ASR Pipeline for Language Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11629)
*Aso Mahmudi,Ting Dang,Ekaterina Vylomova,Nick Thieberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Easper，一个开源无代码工作流，使语言学家能直接从ELAN标注中通过云端资源迭代微调ASR模型。针对冷启动问题（决定先转录哪些录音以引导精确模型），作者在三种瓦努阿图语言上评估了转录优先级策略，发现优先选择词汇丰富的叙事文本并增加声学-语音重复（即使在噪声环境中）能更快提升转录质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频转录是语言文档化的关键瓶颈。虽然Whisper等多语言ASR模型提供了解决方案，但实地语言学家往往缺乏使用这些模型的专业技能，且部署ASR面临冷启动问题，即如何选择初始录音来引导构建准确的模型。

Method: 作者提出Easper，一个开源无代码工作流，允许语言学家通过云端资源直接从ELAN标注迭代微调ASR模型。使用Easper，研究者在三种瓦努阿图语言（比斯拉马语、纳夫桑语、恩古纳语）上按录音会话进行模型微调，并比较优先考虑声学清洁度与优先考虑语言丰富性两种策略下的字符错误率轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，优先选择词汇丰富的叙事材料并增加声学-语音重复（即使在噪声环境下）能带来更快的转录质量提升，优于单纯追求声学清洁度的策略。

Conclusion: Easper为语言学家提供了无需编程即可微调ASR模型的可行途径，同时揭示了在多语言低资源场景下，数据选择策略对ASR冷启动效果有显著影响——语言内容丰富度比声学质量对初始模型提升更为关键。

Abstract: Audio transcription is a critical bottleneck in language documentation. While multilingual Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) models like Whisper offer solutions, field linguists often lack the expertise to utilise them. We present Easper, an open-source, no-code workflow enabling linguists to iteratively fine-tune ASR models via cloud resources directly from ELAN annotations. Deploying ASR also raises a cold start problem: deciding which recordings to transcribe first to bootstrap an accurate model. Using Easper, we evaluate transcription prioritisation strategies on three Vanuatu languages (Bislama, Nafsan, Nguna). We fine-tune models by recording session, comparing Character Error Rate trajectories when prioritising acoustic cleanliness versus linguistic richness. We demonstrate that prioritising lexically rich narratives and increasing acoustic-phonetic repetition, even in noisy environments, leads to faster improvements in transcription quality.

</details>


### [123] [Who Would You Vote For? Auditing Political Alignment in LLMs: An Italian Case-Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11649)
*Simone Mungari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个系统化、可复现的审计框架，用于检测大语言模型（LLM）在政治议题中是否及如何表达对政党和政治领袖的偏好，并以意大利为案例进行了实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着用户在选举期间越来越多地依赖LLM获取政治信息和建议，LLM表达的政治偏好已成为公共关切问题。先前研究表明LLM的交互能影响用户的政治态度和选择，因此有必要研究这些模型自身如何评价政治行为者，但现有研究缺乏系统性、可复现的审计方法。

Method: 提出一个系统化且可复现的审计框架：让多个LLM在九个标准上对政党和政治领袖进行评分。不推断模型的"真实"政治信念，而是聚焦可观察行为，检验跨评测的一致性、模型间差异、拒绝回答率以及对提示措辞的敏感性。进一步研究在被指示采用不同角色（persona）时评测结果如何变化。以意大利为案例研究进行实证演示。

Result: 通过意大利案例研究，提供了对LLM生成的政治评测的系统性分析，涵盖意大利政党和政治领袖，揭示了模型在不同条件下表达政治偏好的行为模式和差异。

Conclusion: 该审计框架提供了一种系统化、可复现的方法来监测和评估LLM在政治语境中的行为，为理解模型如何表达政治偏好以及其对用户政治态度可能产生的影响提供了实证基础。

Abstract: As users increasingly turn to Large Language Models (LLMs) for information and advice on political matters, particularly during election periods, the political preferences expressed by these systems have become a matter of public interest. Prior research has shown that interactions with LLMs can influence users' political attitudes and choices, raising questions about how these models themselves evaluate political actors. In this paper, we investigate whether and how LLMs express preferences toward political parties and political leaders. We introduce a systematic and reproducible auditing framework in which multiple LLMs are prompted to evaluate parties and leaders across nine criteria. Rather than attempting to infer the models' "true" political beliefs, we focus on their observable behavior, examining consistency across evaluations, differences between models, refusal rates, and sensitivity to prompt formulation. We further investigate how these evaluations vary when models are instructed to adopt different personas. We demonstrate the framework through an Italian case study, providing a systematic analysis of LLM-generated political evaluations on italian parties and leaders.

</details>


### [124] [Semantic Lenia: Emergence of Homeostatic Solitons within the Semantic Space of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11657)
*Yoshihiko Kayama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Semantic Lenia的人工生命框架，将LLM推理从静态优化问题转化为宏观logit空间中的连续动力系统，通过非线性稳态反馈平衡语义吸引与语法排斥，实现"自主语义孤子"的涌现，并在混沌边缘维持生成轨迹以触发溯因跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LLM推理被视作静态优化问题，容易陷入重复性"结晶"（即退化输出）。作者希望借鉴人工生命与动力系统视角，让LLM生成过程具备持续演化与创造性突现的能力，而非简单的最小化目标函数。

Method: 构建非线性稳态（homeostatic）反馈回路，在宏观logit空间中动态平衡语义吸引与语法排斥两类力。通过对控制参数的穷举扫描，绘制出"可居住脊"（Habitable Ridge）临界区域，在该区域中施加的引导力与模型固有的语法惯量恰好平衡。采用类似Lenia的人工生命机制，将生成轨迹保持在混沌边缘。

Result: 在参数扫描中发现了临界"可居住脊"，该区域能够维持生成轨迹处于混沌边缘，触发深层的溯因（abductive）跳跃而不发生结构崩塌，并为机器认知建立了物理尺律（scaling law）。涌现出被称为"自主语义孤子"的宏观耗散结构，可避免重复性结晶。

Conclusion: 该工作将人工生命与LLM推理相结合，为机器认知提供了物理学的形式化框架，证明通过动力系统调控可以在LLM中实现自主、创造性且不坍塌的生成行为。

Abstract: We introduce Semantic Lenia, an artificial life framework that transforms Large Language Model (LLM) inference from a static optimization problem into a continuous dynamical system within the macroscopic logit space. By establishing a non-linear homeostatic feedback loop to dynamically balance semantic attraction and syntactic repulsion, we demonstrate the emergence of "Autonomous Semantic Solitons" -- macroscopic dissipative structures that avoid repetitive crystallization. Our exhaustive parameter sweeps map a critical "Habitable Ridge" where applied steering forces perfectly balance the model's intrinsic syntactic inertia. This approach successfully maintains generative trajectories at the edge of chaos, triggering profound abductive leaps without structural collapse and establishing a physical scaling law for machine cognition.

</details>


### [125] [Hybrid-Policy Self-Editing for Composable Unstructured Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11660)
*Tianci Liu,Zihan Dong,Tianchun Li,Yi-Chung Chen,Qiming Cao,Xingchen Wang,Shiyang Wang,Zichen Miao,Linjun Zhang,Haoyu Wang,Jing Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HPSE方法，通过混合rollout的主动自蒸馏机制解决知识编辑中"可组合性"缺失的问题，使编辑后的LLM不仅能回忆注入的段落，还能回答事实级的原子问题并进行多跳推理，在四个LLM骨干和两个知识编辑器中展现出即插即用的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型基于静态语料训练，知识容易过时，因此需要知识编辑（KE）来更新特定知识。现有方法虽然能将自由形式的段落注入模型，但编辑后的模型只能回忆该段落，无法针对其中的原子事实回答问题，也无法将这些事实组合成多跳推理。作者将这一缺失属性命名为"可组合性"（composability），并归因于现有编辑器将固定段落作为唯一学习来源的被动依赖。此外，由于注入知识的新颖性，预编辑模型自身的rollout很少覆盖这些知识，纯on-policy蒸馏效果有限。

Method: HPSE（Hybrid Policy Self-distillation for Editing）方法：(1) 将知识编辑重新定义为从同一模型的特权in-context状态进行的主动自蒸馏，无需外部监督；(2) 提出混合rollout策略——在学生的自身轨迹中，精确地在知识覆盖失败的节点插入缺失的事实，其余位置保持on-policy；从而弥补纯on-policy蒸馏因新知识新颖性导致的覆盖不足问题。

Result: 理论上分析了HPSE相比纯on-policy蒸馏的优势；实验上在四个LLM骨干和两个知识编辑器上，在多种场景下展现出即插即用的性能提升。

Conclusion: HPSE通过主动构建混合rollout，解决了非结构化知识编辑中模型编辑后缺乏可组合性的问题，为UKE提供了一种无需外部监督、即插即用的改进方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance across natural language tasks, yet they are trained on static corpora and their knowledge quickly becomes outdated in a fast-changing world. This motivates knowledge editing (KE), which updates specific knowledge in an LLM without changing unrelated others. Recent works move from structured knowledge triples toward unstructured KE (UKE), where the edit is a free-form passage that may state multiple facts at once. Nonetheless, existing editors inject such a passage yet fail to use it: the edited model can recall the passage, but can neither answer atomic questions about its facts nor compose them into multi-hop reasoning. We attribute this missing property, which we term composability, to editors' passive reliance on the fixed passage as the sole learning source. In response, we cast editing as a proactive self-distillation from a privileged in-context state of the same model, which requires no external supervision. We further reveal that due to the novelty of the injected knowledge, the pre-edited model's own rollouts rarely cover it, which limits the effectiveness of pure on-policy distillation. To close this gap, we propose HPSE, which builds a hybrid rollout that steps in to place missing facts onto the student's own trajectory precisely where its coverage fails, while staying on-policy elsewhere. We theoretically analyze HPSE's advantage over pure on-policy distillation, and empirically establish its plug-and-play improvements across four LLM backbones and two KE editors under various scenarios.

</details>


### [126] [The Wording Effect: Quantifying Two-Way Drift in LLM Benchmark Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11694)
*Shailja Thakur,Sungeun An,Chad DeLuca,Hima Patel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BenchDrift框架，系统性地验证了基准测试中问题措辞的变化（保持语义和答案不变）会导致模型答案发生双向翻转（即漂移）。实验发现，措辞敏感度不随模型能力提升而消失，反而会改变方向：弱模型从重述中获益多于损失，强模型则损失远多于获益，导致最强模型恰恰是最依赖给定措辞的模型。此外，不同模型在哪些重述最易导致错误上高度一致，表明脆弱性源自重述本身而非模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试的每个问题仅使用单一措辞来代表整个问题空间，但这种代表性假设是不合理的。同一问题以不同方式提问可能导致模型答案发生变化，而这一现象未被充分研究。理解措辞敏感性及其与模型能力的关系，对基准测试的可信度和模型的真实评测至关重要。

Method: 提出BenchDrift框架，沿四个维度（语言层面、指代层面、语用层面、结构层面）生成保持问题语义和答案不变的多种变体，然后测量模型正确答案的翻转频率及原因。实验覆盖8个模型和3个基准测试（GSM8K、MMLU、MATH-Hard）。

Result: 漂移在两个方向上都很显著：措辞变化既能使原本失败变为成功，也能使成功变为失败。关键发现有二：(1)措辞敏感度不随模型进步而消失，反而改变符号——弱模型从重述中净获益，强模型净损失，因此最强模型反而对给定措辞的依赖最大；(2)不同模型在哪些重述导致最多正确答案损失上高度一致，表明脆弱性归于重述本身而非具体模型。此外，无论问题是变短还是变长，重述都可能破坏模型原本确信的答案。

Conclusion: 基准测试中问题的单一措辞不足以代表问题的全部形式，模型评分严重受措辞影响。最强的模型反而对基准测试的措辞最为敏感，这提示在评估模型能力时需要更稳健的评测方法，且重述本身的固有脆弱性值得基准测试设计者关注。

Abstract: A benchmark score comes from a single phrasing of each problem. That single phrasing is treated as if it stood for the whole space of ways the same problem could be asked, but it does not. We show that rephrasing a problem while keeping its meaning and answer fixed routinely flips a model's answer in both directions, so some failures become successes and some successes become failures. We call this drift. BenchDrift generates meaning-preserving variations of benchmark problems along four axes, namely linguistic, referential, pragmatic, and structural, and measures how often, and why, correctness flips under each. Across eight models and three benchmarks (GSM8K, MMLU, MATH-Hard), we observe that drift is large in both directions. Two findings stand out. First, phrasing sensitivity does not fade as models get better. Instead, it changes sign. Weak models gain more from rephrasing than they lose, while strong models lose far more than they gain. We find that the best models on a benchmark are therefore the ones whose scores depend most on the wording they happened to be given. Second, the models largely agree on which rephrasings cost the most correct answers even though they differ in how much they drift, so fragility belongs to the rephrasing and not to the model. Furthermore, rephrasing breaks answers a model was confident about, whether the problem is made shorter or longer. Code and Data: https://github.com/IBM/BenchDrift/tree/demo-ui

</details>


### [127] [When the API Speaks the Wrong Language: Revisiting Post-Training for Multilingual Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11715)
*Siddharth Chauhan,Thomas Butler,Abhishek Singhania,Pankaj Porwal,Honey Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言环境下大语言模型（LLM）API调用中的"参数语言不匹配"（ALM）问题，发现监督微调（SFT）作为基线已能显著提升参数语言一致性和函数调用准确率，其性能可与更复杂的强化学习（RL）方法相媲美甚至超越。基于结构化参数感知奖励的RL（如GRPO）虽能改善语言一致性和泛化推理能力，但增益是增量式的，主要在多目标权衡中体现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言场景下LLM的API调用可靠性下降，模型常选中正确工具但生成的语言不一致的参数值（ALM）。这种输出语义正确但操作无效，且现有API调用指标无法捕捉，因此需要系统研究缓解该问题的后训练策略。

Method: 作者构建基准测试，系统性对比了监督微调（SFT）与多种强化学习（RL）方法（如GRPO）在缓解ALM问题上的效果。重点考察了RL中结构化、参数感知奖励机制是否能带来额外收益，并在一致的模型选择条件下进行比较。

Result: SFT作为强基线，显著提升了参数语言一致性和端到端函数调用准确率，在一致的模型选择下性能与RL方法相当甚至更优。GRPO等RL方法能改善语言一致性并更好地保留通用推理能力，但优势有限，主要体现在泛化性和多目标权衡方面。

Conclusion: 多语言API接地的大部分性能可通过精心设计的监督训练实现，RL带来的改进是针对性而非根本性的，其增量价值主要体现在泛化和多目标权衡上。

Abstract: The reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) for API calling degrades in multilingual settings. A common failure occurs when a model selects the correct tool but generates argument values in an inconsistent language, which we term Argument Language Mismatch (ALM). Although semantically correct, such outputs are operationally invalid and not captured by standard API-calling metrics. We revisit post-training strategies for mitigating ALM and find that, in our benchmark, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides a strong baseline, substantially improving argument language consistency and end-to-end function call accuracy. Under consistent model selection, SFT achieves performance comparable to, and sometimes exceeding more complex reinforcement learning (RL) approaches. We further examine whether RL with structured, argument-aware rewards offers additional benefits. While methods such as Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) can improve language consistency and better preserve general reasoning ability, these gains are incremental and most pronounced in generalization and multi-objective trade-offs. Overall, our results suggest that much of the performance in multilingual API grounding can be achieved through careful supervised training, with RL providing targeted rather than fundamental improvements.

</details>


### [128] [Locating and Controlling Implicit Personalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11735)
*Yueru Yan,Siqi Wu,Thai Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在五个LLM上，通过匹配含人口统计线索和中性对话，发现一个局部化的内部激活信号能追踪推荐输出的变化（相关性最高r=0.87）；多个线索同时出现时内部信号近似叠加但输出变化非线性；通过移除单个线索的内部信号可抑制其影响（通常优于提示词方法且保持基准性能），但选择性移除在多属性共存情境下的效果高度依赖模型和属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM常因隐含的人口统计线索而改变输出行为，即使对话中未明确提及用户身份。以往研究仅记录了该现象，但内部激活与行为变化之间的因果关系尚不清楚，缺乏对内部机制的深入理解和因果干预手段。

Method: 使用匹配的含线索对话与中性对话对（matched cued and neutral conversations），在五种LLM上定位与推荐变化高度相关的内部激活信号；通过相关性分析衡量信号与行为变化的关系；通过移除（ablation）特定线索对应的内部信号检验其因果作用，并与提示词干预进行对比。

Result: 存在局部化的内部激活信号跟踪推荐变化（r最高达0.87）；多线索时内部信号近似线性组合但输出变化不简单叠加；移除单一线索的内部信号可有效抑制其影响，且通常优于提示词要求忽略人口统计信息的方法，同时基本保持基准性能；但在多属性共存时选择性移除特定维度影响的能力因模型和属性而异。

Conclusion: 隐含个性化行为与可分析和因果控制的内部信号之间建立了明确联系，为理解和干预LLM中的隐式偏见提供了新的内部机制视角和工具途径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often shift their outputs in response to implicit demographic cues even when users never state a demographic identity. Previous work has documented this behavior, but the connection between these behavioral changes and the model's internal activations remains unclear. Using matched cued and neutral conversations across five LLMs, we establish that a localized internal activation signal tracks changes in recommendations, with correlations up to r=0.87. When multiple cues appear together, their internal signals largely combine, but the changes in output do not simply add up. We further show that removing the internal signal associated with one cue can suppress its influence, often more effectively than asking the model to ignore demographics via prompting, while largely preserving general benchmark performance. However, the ability to selectively remove one dimension's influence while leaving co-present dimensions intact remains highly model- and attribute-specific. These results connect implicit personalization behavior to an internal signal that can be analyzed and causally controlled.

</details>


### [129] [Ripple-Pivot Search: Active Parallel Decoding for Diffusion Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11742)
*Yushi Ye,Xu Chen,Haoyun Jiang,Jinsong Lan,Haihong Tang,Bo Han,Ivor Tsang,Yanfeng Wang,Bo Zheng,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Ripple-Pivot Search (RPS)的训练-free解码方法，通过利用dLLM解码中的"波纹效应"（提前提交中熵枢轴位置可降低剩余掩码位置的不确定性），实现4-10倍的墙钟加速，结合KV缓存时可达18倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的并行解码调度器通常仅在位置满足逐位置标准后才提交，忽略了早期提交可能对后续解码产生的积极影响。作者观察到dLLM解码中存在波纹效应：主动提交中熵枢轴位置可以显著降低剩余掩码位置的不确定性，从而加速整体解码过程。

Method: RPS包含两个核心组件：(1) 寻找中熵位置作为有前景的候选枢轴（即在何处解码）；(2) 通过前瞻评估确定能带来最大下游收益的token分配（即解码什么）。该方法无需训练，直接应用于现有dLLM。

Result: 在3个dLLM和4个推理与代码生成基准上，RPS相比标准解码器实现了4-10倍的墙钟加速，同时保持生成质量；相比此前的前瞻基线，准确率提升最高达5.49%，在大多数设置下吞吐量更高。集成KV缓存后，可达到最高18倍的墙钟加速。

Conclusion: RPS通过利用波纹效应进行主动枢轴选择与前瞻评估，在保持生成质量的同时显著加速dLLM解码，为并行解码调度提供了一种有效的训练-free方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have emerged as a competitive alternative to autoregressive language models, offering the potential for substantially faster inference through parallel decoding. Existing parallel decoding schedulers typically commit positions only after they meet a per-position criterion, overlooking how early commitments may benefit subsequent decoding. We identify a ripple effect in dLLM decoding: proactively committing a mid-entropy pivot position can induce a pronounced reduction in uncertainty across the remaining masked positions. This uncertainty reduction allows subsequent steps to unmask more tokens in parallel, thereby accelerating the overall decoding process. To exploit the ripple effect, we propose Ripple-Pivot Search (RPS), a novel training-free decoding method that seeks mid-entropy positions as promising candidate pivots (where to decode), and determines their token assignment that yields the greatest downstream benefit via lookahead evaluation (what to decode). Across 3 dLLMs and 4 reasoning and code-generation benchmarks, RPS achieves 4-10$\times$ wall-clock speedup over the standard decoder while preserving generation quality, and improves accuracy over the previous lookahead baseline by up to 5.49% while delivering higher throughput in most settings. When integrated with KV caching, RPS further achieves up to 18$\times$ wall-clock speedup over the standard decoder.

</details>


### [130] [LabelFusion-TS: Fusing Large Language Models, Transformer Encoders, and Financial Time Series for Monetary-Policy Stance Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11753)
*Michael Schlee,Fabian Lukassen,Christoph Weisser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个融合金融市场时间序列与文本的分类框架L-FTS，用于对美联储(FOMC)声明中的句子进行鹰派/鸽派/中性分类。该系统通过小型投票网络整合三个独立组件：微调后的RoBERTa编码器、提示式大语言模型(LLM)以及基于时间序列Transformer的融合集成模型，在2015-2022年测试集上达到70.2%的加权F1，显著优于零样本LLM的64.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文本是在市场环境中产生和解读的，但现有金融文本分类器几乎只依赖纯文本输入，忽略了发布前的市场时间序列这一重要模态信息。作者旨在验证金融市场时间序列是否可以作为金融文本分类的额外输入模态，以提升分类性能。

Method: 系统由三个独立训练组件和一个小型投票网络组成：(1) 微调的RoBERTa编码器，先利用LLM自动标注的句子进行预训练，再在约千条人工标注数据上微调；(2) 提示式LLM；(3) 一组时间序列Transformer的融合集成，输入为发布前数月内市场序列。三者通过投票网络结合，在FOMC 2015年前的通信上训练，并在2015-2022年数据上评估。

Result: 在2015-2022年测试期间，融合系统达到70.2%的加权F1分数，相比零样本LLM的64.1%有显著提升。仅需240条人工标注句子即可超越零样本LLM的性能。该结果初步证明了市场时间序列作为金融文本分类输入模态的有效性。

Conclusion: 市场时间序列可以作为一个有价值的信息模态补充到金融文本分类中，融合文本与市场数据的多模态方法优于仅依赖文本的模型。该工作为更广泛的金融文本分类场景引入时序模态提供了初步证据和可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Financial text is produced and interpreted within a market environment, yet financial text classifiers almost always receive text alone. We study whether financial time series are useful as an additional input on the task of classifying sentences from Federal Reserve communication as hawkish, dovish, or neutral. Our system, \lfts{}, extends the \lf{} architecture with this modality: a small voting network combines three independently trained components, a fine-tuned RoBERTa encoder, a prompted large language model (LLM), and a fused ensemble of time-series transformers over the market series of the months preceding publication. Because only about a thousand annotated sentences are available for training, the RoBERTa encoder is first pre-trained on sentences annotated automatically by the LLM and only then fine-tuned on the human labels. Trained on Federal Open Market Committee (FOMC) communication up to 2015 and evaluated on 2015--2022, the fused system achieves 70.2\% weighted F1 -- against 64.1\% for the zero-shot LLM -- and overtakes it with as few as 240 human-labelled sentences. We take this as initial evidence for market time series as an input modality in financial text classification.

</details>


### [131] [AWARe: Mitigating Catastrophic Forgetting via Activation-Weighted Adaptive REtention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11758)
*Juncheng Liao,Jinfan Lv,Guoming Wang,Jupeng Zheng,Ling Xiao,Siliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AWARe（Activation-Weighted Adaptive REtention）微调方法，通过基于激活模式动态控制参数更新来缓解多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在微调下游任务时的灾难性遗忘问题。该方法为参数分配激活重要性分数，选择性冻结关键参数并允许非关键参数适配新任务，无需修改模型架构即可兼容现有推理引擎。实验表明AWARe在保持上游能力的同时获得更优的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLMs在大规模多模态预训练后具备强泛化与推理能力，但在下游任务微调时容易发生灾难性遗忘，即新任务学习导致已有知识退化。原因在于新任务的梯度更新覆盖了先前知识的关键参数，限制了模型的实用部署。

Method: 提出Activation-Weighted Adaptive REtention（AWARe）微调方法。核心思路是：根据激活模式为每个参数分配重要性分数，动态控制参数更新——对保留先前能力至关重要的参数进行选择性冻结，而允许重要性较低的参数灵活适应新任务。该方法无需修改模型架构，与现有推理引擎完全兼容。

Result: 大量实验表明，AWARe在保持上游能力的同时取得了优于现有方法的下游性能，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题。代码已开源。

Conclusion: AWARe通过激活加权自适应保留机制，在不改变模型架构的前提下实现了对MLLMs灾难性遗忘的有效缓解，兼顾了上游知识保留与下游任务适应，为MLLMs的实际部署提供了一种实用且有效的微调方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) exhibit strong generalization and reasoning abilities due to large-scale multimodal pre-training. However, fine-tuning these models on downstream tasks often leads to catastrophic forgetting, where newly learned task-specific knowledge degrades previously acquired capabilities. This issue arises because gradient updates for new tasks overwrite parameters critical to prior knowledge, limiting the practical deployment of MLLMs. To address this challenge, we propose Activation-Weighted Adaptive REtention (AWARe), a fine-tuning method that mitigates catastrophic forgetting by dynamically controlling parameter updates based on activation patterns. AWARe assigns activation-based importance scores to parameters, selectively freezing those essential for preserving prior capabilities while allowing less important parameters to adapt to new tasks. Importantly, AWARe operates without modifying model architectures, ensuring compatibility with existing inference engines. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AWARe effectively preserves upstream capabilities while achieving superior downstream performance compared to existing methods. Code is available at https://github.com/kaln27/AWARe.

</details>


### [132] [Causal Structure is Inducible but Functionally Decoupled: The Routing/Readout Boundary of a Typed Mechanism Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11767)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文报告了Transformer在组织因果知识时的一种解耦现象：证据类型级别的监督诱导出"槽-类型"结构（slot-by-type），该结构组织路由（routing）但与答案读出（readout）功能上解耦。作者在含精确干预真值的因果世界基准上，以冻结协议在两个尺度（22.6M和125M参数）建立了可审计的离散机制库，通过四项预注册发现验证了这一结构属性，并指出该结构是免费的（不影响LM质量）且可编辑状态完全可逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型如何组织因果知识——尤其是模型在回答干预性问题时，其计算组织方式是否依赖于查询所需的证据类型。此前缺乏对Transformer内部因果知识组织结构的精确、可审计的表征。

Method: 构建"类型化机制库"（typed mechanism library）——按证据类型分区的离散机制槽，在状态层面可审计。在具有精确干预真值的因果世界基准上，使用冻结协议，在22.6M和125M两个模型规模下训练并检查结构，配合预注册协议进行受检验证。

Result: 四项预注册发现：(i) 槽-类型结构由类型级监督诱导，无监督对照中不存在，在125M规模下九个检验单元全部通过；(ii) 该结构是带路由/读出边界的类型化路由索引——槽编码支撑路由但不驱动答案读出（|Δŷ|≤3.4×10⁻⁶）；(iii) 结构是"免费的"，LM质量与参数匹配的单体模型相差仅0.0082 nats；(iv) 库状态在编辑下精确局部且可位精确回滚（250次单编辑+1,000次堆叠回滚，零失败）。另发现无监督零点随着规模移动，跨尺度复用零点可能引入混淆。

Conclusion: 证明了Transformer内部的因果知识按证据类型形成可审计的槽-类型组织，该组织负责路由但与读出功能上解耦，因此不能直接声称行为可编辑性；结构对模型质量无成本且状态可逆。论文强调所有主张均绑定预注册且机器可检验的标准，并披露了完整审计轨迹（包括一次失败的标准及协议处理方式）。

Abstract: When a language model answers an interventional question, the computation it must perform depends on the type of evidence the query requires. We report a decoupling in how a transformer organizes causal knowledge: slot-by-type structure induced by type-level supervision organizes routing, yet remains functionally decoupled from answer readout. We establish this with a typed mechanism library -- discrete mechanism slots partitioned by evidence type, auditable at the state level -- on a causal-world benchmark with exact interventional ground truth, under a frozen protocol, at two scales (22.6M and 125M). Four preregistered findings. (i) Origin. Slot-by-type organization is induced by type-level supervision: absent in architecturally identical unsupervised controls, not buyable by content-free gating labels, and statistically attributable to the supervision signal, replicating at 125M under a powered preregistered protocol (all nine cells passed). (ii) Boundary. The induced structure is a typed routing index with a sharp routing/readout boundary: slot codes scaffold routing but do not drive answer readout ($|Δ\hat{y}| \le 3.4\times10^{-6}$, zero collateral, three seeds, stable across a 5.6x scale window) -- we therefore make no behavioral-editability claim. (iii) Cost. The structure is free: LM quality matches a parameter-matched monolith within 0.0082 nats. (iv) Trust. The library state is exactly local under edit and bit-exactly revertible -- 250 single-edit and 1,000 stacked reverts per seed, zero failures. We further find that the unsupervised null itself moves with scale, so comparisons reusing a null calibrated at one scale may be confounded at another. Every claim is tied to a preregistered, machine-checkable criterion archived before the data it governs; the full audit trail, including one criterion we failed and how the frozen protocol handled it, is released as an appendix.

</details>


### [133] [Diagnosis Before Recovery: Turning Agent Failures into Selective Self-Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11772)
*Pan Wang,Yihao Hu,Hang Wang,Zirui Lv,Xin Zhang,Jianshe Li,Jiang-Ming Yang,Wei Wu,Yongqi Tong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DARC是一种基于诊断引导的恢复框架（harness），通过在测试时修正之前分析开发集中的失败模式、从共享恢复库中剪除不匹配的干预措施，并冻结验证器选择的成功-成本策略，将自校正从简单的提示扩展转变为恢复接口设计，在ALFWorld、AppWorld和XBRL Finance三个环境中提升了任务性能并减少了环境步数或检索预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自校正依赖失败信号来指导修复，但编程代理能从编译器、测试和执行轨迹中获得类型化的恢复信号，而广泛的语言代理任务通常只能暴露粗略的任务失败。通用的恢复方案（playbook）在系统需要更窄的修复接口时反而拓宽了代理的上下文，混入了对无效操作、缺失程序和严格格式错误等不相容信号的处理，导致恢复效率低下。

Method: DARC（Diagnosis-guided Recovery harness）包含三个关键步骤：（1）剖析任务家族的失败模式；（2）从共享恢复库中剪除不匹配的干预措施；（3）冻结由验证器选择的成功-成本策略用于部署。该因果顺序使修正具有选择性：先确定哪种失败可被修复，再决定花费多少恢复证据。具体而言，在ALFWorld中表现为动作有效性验证框架，在AppWorld中表现为程序性恢复回退，在XBRL Finance中表现为格式精确检索策略。

Result: 在ALFWorld、AppWorld和XBRL Finance三个环境中，DARC在每种评估设置下都相比基础代理和通用恢复方案提升了平均任务性能，同时减少了环境步数或检索预算。实验表明失败无需统一触发更多上下文——DARC将自校正从提示扩展转变为恢复接口设计。

Conclusion: DARC提供了一条在缺乏类似编译器反馈的领域中实现更可靠代理的实用路径：在扩大上下文之前让失败变得可操作。通过开发集失败来恢复部分缺失的诊断基质，在测试时修正前决定哪些恢复干预是可接受的，从而实现选择性的自校正。

Abstract: Self-correction is particularly useful when a failure constrains the next repair. Coding agents benefit from this property because compilers, tests, and execution traces turn many failures into typed recovery signals, but broad language-agent tasks often expose only a coarse task failure. This creates a tension for generic recovery playbooks: they broaden the agent's context precisely when the system needs a narrower repair interface, mixing incompatible signals for invalid actions, missing procedures, and strict-format errors. Our insight is that development-set failures can recover part of the missing diagnostic substrate by deciding which recovery interventions are admissible before test-time correction. We propose DARC, a diagnosis-guided recovery harness that profiles task-family failure modes, prunes mismatched interventions from a shared recovery library, and freezes a verifier-selected success-cost policy for deployment. This causal order makes correction selective: the harness first determines what kind of failure can be repaired, then decides how much recovery evidence to spend. In ALFWorld, AppWorld, and XBRL Finance, the same protocol yields an action-validity harness, a procedural-recovery fallback, and a format-precision retrieval policy; in each evaluated setting it improves average task performance over base agents and broad playbooks while reducing environment steps or retrieval budget. Our experiments show that failures need not trigger uniformly more context: DARC turns self-correction from prompt expansion into recovery-interface design. DARC provides a practical route toward more reliable agents in domains where compiler-like feedback is absent: making failures actionable before making contexts larger.

</details>


### [134] [Language-Conditional Dequantization: Recovering What Quantization Steals from Non-English Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11786)
*Nirmal Thomas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出语言条件反量化（LCD）方法，通过为已量化模型的语言层附加每语言秩2 LoRA修正，恢复多语言能力，在次4B INT3 GPTQ量化下显著减轻非英语语言性能退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激进量化对多语言能力伤害不成比例：在sub-4B INT3 GPTQ量化下，非英语语言的困惑度退化比英语大2-4倍，亟需高效的恢复方法。

Method: LCD是一种事后方法，为已量化模型的线性层附加每语言秩2 LoRA校正，每语言仅增加0.12%参数，可在单GPU上20分钟内完成训练。还提出层受限变体以验证深度误差传播机制。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.2-3B上，LCD恢复了非拉丁文字语言70-83%的困惑度差距和17-28%的GlobalMMLU准确率差距；比等容量语言无关校正高3-9个百分点；比数据无关低秩基线LQER高一个数量级。

Conclusion: 研究发现量化损伤的深度分布存在困惑度-准确率脱节：Llama的浅层误差向下游传播且难以局部纠正，而Qwen的深层误差则不会。LCD结合层受限变体直接验证了这一机制。

Abstract: Aggressive quantization disproportionately harms multilingual capability: in the sub-4B INT3 GPTQ regime, we measure 2-4x larger perplexity degradation on non-English languages than on English. We propose Language-Conditional Dequantization (LCD), a post-hoc method that attaches per-language rank-2 LoRA corrections to the linear layers of an already-quantized model, adding 0.12% parameters per language and training in under 20 minutes on a single GPU. Across Qwen2.5-3B and Llama-3.2-3B, LCD recovers 70-83% of the perplexity gap for non-Latin script languages and 17-28% of the GlobalMMLU accuracy gap, outperforming a language-agnostic correction of equal capacity by 3-9 points on typologically distant languages and a data-free low-rank baseline (LQER) by an order of magnitude. We further identify a perplexity-accuracy disconnect and trace it to where quantization concentrates damage: early-depth errors (Llama) propagate downstream and resist local correction, while late-depth errors (Qwen) do not. A layer-restricted variant of LCD validates this mechanism directly.

</details>


### [135] [GRPO for Financial Advice Generation: Outperforming Commercial LLMs under CATE Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11787)
*Ofir Ben Shoham,Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Shravan Mohan,Yakov Gazman,Oded Vainas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将财务建议生成建模为强化学习问题，使用GRPO算法微调开源语言模型，并以LLM作为评判者的奖励机制结合安全门控。通过双重稳健的CATE估计器进行独立审计，训练的模型在估计毛利提升上约为最强商业基线的两倍（0.0228 vs 0.0104），并具有最低的下行率和尾部风险。研究表明，基于财务信号的GRPO能产生比商业LLM更有用的商业建议，且独立因果审计是LLM评判的重要补充而非确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从商业记录生成可操作的财务建议需要模型整合数值推理、领域知识和合理判断，同时避免有害建议。直接监督困难重重：历史决策不一定最优，高质量自由文本标签获取昂贵。需要一个不依赖人工标注的学习框架来训练财务建议生成模型。

Method: 将财务建议生成形式化为强化学习问题，微调开源权重语言模型，采用GRPO算法。奖励设计为LLM-as-a-judge评估框架，包含多个二元质量维度评分的准则，并附加安全门用于防止有害建议。此外，使用基于双重稳健条件平均处理效应（CATE）估计量的观察性离策略审计作为独立于评判者的验证方法。

Result: 训练的LLM在估计毛利提升上约为最强商业基线的两倍（0.0228 vs 0.0104），拥有最低的下行率和最少的负尾部风险。值得注意的是，两个评估体系的排名不一致：未训练的基座模型在评判者标准中排最后但在因果审计中排第二，表明审计捕捉到了评判者未发现的信号。

Conclusion: GRPO结合财务驱动的奖励信号能生成比商业LLM更实用的商业建议；判别者独立的因果审计应作为LLM-as-a-judge评估的重要补充而非替代，特别是在金融NLP领域。这一发现对评估方法的信度和效度提出了重要启示。

Abstract: Generating actionable financial advice from business records demands that models integrate numerical reasoning, domain knowledge, and sound judgment, while avoiding recommendations that could harm the business. Direct supervision is difficult: historical decisions are not necessarily optimal, and high-quality free-form labels are expensive to obtain. We formulate financial advice generation as a reinforcement learning problem and fine-tune an open-weight language model using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Our reward is an LLM-as-a-judge rubric that scores each recommendation across multiple binary dimensions of advice quality, augmented with a safety gate for harm prevention.
  Since LLM-based evaluation alone cannot confirm whether improvements reflect genuine business value rather than adaptation to the judge, we complement it with a judge-independent audit based on a standard doubly-robust Conditional Average Treatment Effect (CATE) estimator. Under this observational off-policy audit, our trained LLM achieves approximately twice the estimated gross-profit lift of the strongest evaluated commercial baseline ($0.0228$ vs.\ $0.0104$), together with the lowest downside rate and the least negative tail risk of any policy evaluated. Notably, the two evaluations do not rank the baselines identically: the untrained base model places last on the judge rubric but second on the causal audit, indicating that the audit captures a signal the judge does not. Our results demonstrate that GRPO with a finance-grounded reward signal can produce substantially more useful business recommendations than commercial LLMs, and that a judge-independent causal audit is a valuable complement to, rather than a confirmation of, LLM-as-a-judge assessment in financial NLP.

</details>


### [136] [TELLME: Test-Enhanced Learning for Language Model Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11788)
*Minjun Kim,Inho Won,Hyeonseok Lim,MinKyu Kim,Junghun Yuk,Wooyoung Go,Jongyoul Park,Jungyeul Park,KyungTae Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TELLME是一种融合测试增强学习(TEL)原理的持续预训练方法，通过训练过程中引入测验来提升领域知识获取效率和长期记忆保留，在金融领域相对现有方法最高提升23.6%性能，长期记忆保留提升9.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续预训练(CPT)虽被广泛用于大语言模型的领域适配，但面临两个核心挑战：获取大规模领域特定数据集困难，以及训练计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出TELLME方法，将测试增强学习(TEL)原则与持续预训练(CPT)相结合，通过在训练过程中使用测验(quizzes)来提升模型训练效率，从而促进高效的领域知识获取和长期记忆保持。

Result: 在金融领域，TELLME相较现有方法最高提升23.6%的性能；在长期记忆保留方面实现9.8%的提升。

Conclusion: TELLME通过将测试增强学习与持续预训练相结合，有效缓解了持续预训练中数据获取困难和计算成本高的问题，在提升领域知识获取效率的同时也增强了模型的长期记忆能力。

Abstract: Continual pre-training (CPT) has been widely adopted as a method for domain adaptation in large language models. However, CPT has consistently been accompanied by challenges, such as the difficulty of acquiring large-scale domain-specific datasets and high computational costs. In this study, we propose a novel method called Test-Enhanced Learning for Language Model Enrichment (TELLME) to alleviate these issues. TELLME leverages the TestEnhanced Learning (TEL) principle, whereby the model's training efficiency is improved using quizzes during training. It integrates this principle with CPT, thereby promoting efficient domain-specific knowledge acquisition and long-term memory retention. Experimental results demonstrate that TELLME outperforms existing methods by up to 23.6% in the financial domain and achieves a 9.8% improvement in long-term memory retention.

</details>


### [137] [Hybrid Gated Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11805)
*Zekun Zhou,Ruobing Xie,Lanrui Wang,Weixuan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出混合门控注意力（HyGA）框架，通过三种门控策略从注意力多阶段捕获信息，结合低秩矩阵分解和可学习注意力汇聚点，在效率和效果上超越传统门控注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有门控注意力虽能缓解注意力汇聚点问题并增强表征能力，但在效率-效果帕累托前沿上仍有扩展空间，需要更全面的多源调制信号来控制信息流。

Method: 提出HyGA框架，包含三类门控策略：利用注意力多阶段的多样信息，协同构建元素级/头级门控，从多角度捕获头内与头间信息交互；引入低秩矩阵分解和可学习注意力汇聚点提升训练效率和稳定性。

Result: 在基于不同骨干网络的广泛基准上，HyGA全面改善了训练损失和各项下游任务性能，并在不同计算成本下取得最佳表现，且通过全面的模型分析验证了有效性。

Conclusion: HyGA为注意力机制提供了一种更高效、更稳定、更具表征能力的解决方案，拓展了门控注意力的效率-效果边界。

Abstract: Gated attention is an effective approach to mitigate attention sinks and enhance the representational capacity of attention. To further extend its effectiveness-efficiency Pareto frontier, we propose a Hybrid Gated Attention (HyGA) framework that contains three types of gating strategies. Specifically, these gates leverage diverse information from multiple stages of attention, and collaboratively build element-wise/head-wise gating from multiple perspectives, capturing intra-head and cross-head information interactions. Through our hybrid gating components, HyGA could provide multi-source modulation signals, enabling more comprehensive control over information flow and improving the representational capacity of attention. We also introduce low-rank matrix decomposition and learnable attention sink to further enhance training efficiency and stability. In experiments, we evaluate HyGA on widely-used benchmarks based on different backbones. The experimental results show that our HyGA comprehensively improves both training loss and various downstream performances compared with Gated attention. HyGA has also been verified to achieve the best performance at different computation costs, with comprehensive model analyses for better understanding. The proposed HyGA sheds light on a more effective, efficient, and stable attention mechanism.

</details>


### [138] [Located but Not Releasable: Silent Gate Inversion and Bounded Linear Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11822)
*Xining Xun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本篇论文对"检测-定位-释放"（detect-localize-release）完整管线进行了预注册的端到端压力测试。在25.7M参数的因果证据判别Transformer上，虽然定位（localization）成功——对中层观测证据通道进行干预能恢复目标行为，但门控（gating）在分布外完全失效（检测器对分布外零触发、对分布内6.9-7.3%误触发、对2400条真正需要门控的生成零触发），线性释放（linear release）也受限于平台期（单调剂量-反应曲线远低于预注册阈值），两个失败可解离，且均不推翻定位的成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明语言模型在内部表征了任务相关的潜在结构却未能使用，但"能否将已定位的结构转化为实际行为"这一问题极少被端到端验证。作者旨在对完整的"检测-定位-释放"管线进行严格、可审计的压力测试，检验其是否真正可行。

Method: 作者在25.7M参数的Transformer上进行预注册实验，该模型训练于因果证据判别任务，且已知存在"潜在因果结构存在但行为未使用"的抑制现象。他们将方法分解为检测（detect）、定位（localize）、释放（release）三个环节，并在产生任何数据前哈希存档所有阈值、声明模板和决策树分支。具体包括：对观测证据通道中层进行干预以恢复目标行为（定位）、训练一个门控检测器以判断何时需要干预（检测）、以及无条件进行每实例线性方向注入以测试释放上限（线性释放）。

Result: （i）定位成功：中层观测证据通道的干预在原本受抑制的世界中恢复了目标行为（配对的释放优势为0.563和0.854，97.5%置信区间排除零；最佳位点释放率为0.889）。（ii）门控在分布外失效：检测器校准为零分布外触发，却在6.9-7.3%的分布内生成上触发，而对2400条实际需要门控的held-out生成零触发——完全反转，门控管线静默退化为基座模型。（iii）线性释放受限：无条件注入每实例线性方向产生单调剂量-反应曲线，但平台期远低于预注册释放余量（截距0.382→0.311→0.264 vs. 阈值≤0.08）；每实例自适应性仅增加±0.03以下。两种失效可解离，且均不推翻定位的成立。

Conclusion: 该研究的关键结论是：结构定位的成功并不自动意味着行为释放的成功。"检测"环节的检测器存在分布外逆反失效（OOD-inversion），"释放"环节的线性方向族在该位点和分辨率下整体被限制在充分性阈值以下。两个失效点是可解离的，且定位本身依然稳健，表明研究者需要分别诊断管线各环节的失效，而不能仅凭定位成功就断言整体管线有效。所有数值均可追溯至释放的审计链中的哈希产物，保证了结果的可核查性。

Abstract: A growing body of work reports that language models represent task-relevant latent structure that they fail to use. Whether such structure, once located, can be converted into behavior is a separate question that is rarely tested end to end. We submit the complete pipeline -- detect, localize, and release -- to a fully preregistered stress test on a 25.7M transformer trained on causal-evidence discrimination, where a known suppression phenomenon (latent causal structure present but behaviorally unused) has previously been documented. Every threshold, claim template, and decision-tree branch was hashed and archived before any corresponding data existed. Three findings. (i) Localization succeeds: interventions at observation-evidence channels of mid layers restore target behavior on otherwise-suppressed worlds (paired release advantages $0.563$ and $0.854$, 97.5% CIs excluding zero; best-site release rate $0.889$). (ii) Gating fails out of distribution: a detector calibrated to trigger on zero out-of-distribution calibration worlds triggers on 6.9-7.3% of held-out in-distribution generations and on zero of the 2,400 held-out generations that actually need it -- a complete inversion that silently reduces the gated pipeline to its base model. (iii) Linear release is capped: removing the gate and injecting a per-instance linear direction unconditionally yields a monotone dose-response that plateaus far below the preregistered release margin (intercept $0.382 \to 0.311 \to 0.264$ vs. threshold $\le 0.08$); per-instance adaptivity adds less than $\pm 0.03$. The failure is doubly located: the detector is OOD-inverted, and the entire family of linear release directions at this site and resolution is bounded away from sufficiency. The two failures are dissociable, and neither overturns localization. Every number traces to a hashed artifact in the released audit chain.

</details>


### [139] [When the Knowledge Base Becomes the Gold Standard: Measuring Resource-Shared Evaluation Loops in Entity-Level Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11843)
*Jinhyung Bae,Dain Kil,Seongmin Oh,Seungmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个"评估闭环"问题：在低资源历史领域（如《承政院日记》的韩英翻译）中，由于缺乏专家金标准，研究者用知识库（KB）替代金标准，而该KB恰好也是注入系统的同源资源，导致评分变成自证式度量——衡量的是指令遵从度而非真实翻译质量。通过使用独立于注入管线的专家人名标注（527个标注中有仅31.1%独立于注入管线），研究证实KB注入的增益仅存在于与注入资源重叠的语段中，在独立语段中收益为零或负值，且该度量在同一模型内可复现、在不同模型间可区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 《承政院日记》作为联合国教科文组织世界记忆遗产，仅翻译了37.4%，而自动翻译中最突出的失败模式是人名——误读人名会扭曲历史事实而非仅表面内容。低资源历史领域缺乏专家金标准，实践者用知识库替代金标准，但该知识库恰恰又是注入系统的同一资源，造成评分自指闭环。作者旨在量化这个闭环的大小和性质。

Method: 使用韩国国史编纂委员会（National Institute of Korean History）的专家人名标注作为独立于注入管线的金标准。固定实体集合，仅变化正确读法的来源（注入管线的KB vs 独立专家标注）。对527个专家标注的人名提及进行分类，区分重叠段与独立段。在四个模型上采用双重差分（difference-in-differences）分析，比较KB注入在重叠段与独立段的增益差异；同时构建去除构建过滤器的独立样本，检验度量的复现性与区分度。

Result: 527个专家标注提及中仅31.1%位于注入管线之外（即独立于KB）。在重叠段，注入读法与人译一致率达97.8%，但在独立段仅70.1%，说明"看起来最健康"的段恰是闭环在维持的部分。跨四个模型的双重差分显示，KB注入的增益仅限于与注入资源共享金标准的语段，在独立语段中增益为零或负值。注入后的保持率聚集在0.910-0.996的窄带内，尽管基线能力相差五倍，说明报告的增益是先前表现的补数，较弱模型显得提升更显著。在去除构建过滤器的独立样本上，度量在模型内可复现（区间重叠）、模型间可区分（区间不重叠），反映的是模型属性而非样本属性。

Conclusion: 当评价系统的金标准与注入资源同源时，评估度量的可复现性与区分度并非翻译质量的证明，而是指令遵从度的体现。KB注入的"增益"是闭环维持的表象，实际上掩盖了独立语段中注入无益甚至有害的事实。该研究提示低资源历史领域的评估设计必须打破自指闭环，确保金标准独立于注入管线，否则评测结果将系统性高估注入方法的效果。

Abstract: The Seungjeongwon Ilgi, a UNESCO Memory of the World record, is only 37.4% translated, and the most conspicuous failure mode in automatic translation is the person name -- a misread name corrupts the historical fact rather than merely the surface. Low-resource historical domains have no expert gold standard for entity translation, so practitioners substitute a knowledge base (KB) for the gold. That KB is the same resource injected into the system: scoring becomes self-referential and the metric measures instruction compliance rather than translation quality.
  We measure this loop. Using expert person-name annotations from the National Institute of Korean History as a gold independent of the injection pipeline, we hold the entity set fixed and vary only the provenance of the correct reading. Of 527 expert-annotated mentions, only 31.1% lie outside the injection pipeline, and the residual loop is not uniform -- in the overlapping segment the injected reading agrees with the human translation 97.8% of the time against 70.1% in the independent one, so the segment that looks healthiest is the one the loop is holding up.
  Across four models, a difference-in-differences analysis shows the gain from KB injection is confined to the segment whose gold shares the injected resource; in the independent segment it is at or below zero. Post-injection preservation clusters in a narrow 0.910-0.996 band even though baseline capability differs fivefold, so the reported gain is the complement of prior performance and weaker models appear to improve more dramatically. On an independent sample built by removing the construction filter, the measure replicates within model (overlapping intervals) while discriminating between models (non-overlapping intervals) -- it reflects a property of the model, not of the sample.

</details>


### [140] [Total Recall at What Cost? Benchmarking the Serving Cost of Agentic Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11879)
*Natchanon Pollertlam,Witchayut Kornsuwannawit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对三种记忆系统（Mem0、Hindsight、Mastra Observational Memory）与两种参考策略（固定大小滚动窗口、重发完整对话记录）在长达400轮对话中的服务成本与回答准确率进行了系统性基准测试，发现记忆系统的服务成本无法仅凭对话长度和消息大小预测，盈亏平衡点因系统和骨干网络而异，且没有任何系统能在成本和准确率两方面同时胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的对话代理越来越依赖记忆系统以避免每轮重发整个对话，但记忆系统在服务端带来的成本开销缺乏系统性的基准测试，现有研究中成本与质量往往被分开评估，难以全面指导系统选型。

Method: 在两种骨干网络和最长400轮对话条件下，比较三种记忆系统（Mem0、Hindsight、Mastra Observational Memory）与两种参考策略（固定大小滚动窗口、重发完整记录），将每次成本测量与665个LoCoMo问题上的回答准确率配对分析，并通过回归分析与盈亏平衡分析揭示成本驱动因素和成本优势临界点。

Result: 记忆系统的服务成本不能由对话长度和消息大小单独预测，回归模型对记忆系统的成本估计偏差达18-69%，其成本主要由内部记忆行为驱动；盈亏平衡点高度依赖于具体系统和骨干网络，最便宜的可在前几十轮即低于完整重发成本，最贵的在400轮内都不划算；准确率范围为21-54%，骨干网络选择对成本的影响与记忆系统本身相当，没有任何系统在成本和准确率两个维度上同时占优。

Conclusion: 记忆系统的成本特性复杂且高度依赖内部行为，现有简单模型无法有效预测；系统选型需同时权衡成本与准确率，因为两者不可兼得，且骨干网络的选择对成本影响不容忽视。

Abstract: Long-running conversational agents increasingly rely on a memory system to avoid resending the whole conversation each turn, yet how much that costs to serve has received little systematic benchmarking. We compare three memory systems (Mem0, Hindsight, and Mastra Observational Memory) against two reference strategies -- a fixed-size rolling window and resubmitting the full transcript -- across two backbones and conversations of up to 400 turns, pairing every cost measurement with answer accuracy on 665 LoCoMo questions. First, a memory system's serving cost cannot be predicted from conversation length and message size alone: a regression that tracks the two reference strategies closely misses the memory systems by 18-69%, their cost driven instead by internal memory behavior. Second, a break-even analysis shows that whether -- and when -- a memory system becomes cheaper to serve than the full transcript is highly sensitive to the system and the backbone, from the first tens of turns for the cheapest to never within 400 turns for the most expensive. Third, no system wins on both axes: accuracy spans 21-54%, and the backbone choice drives cost as much as the memory system does.

</details>


### [141] [LazyTrain: Limited-resource Allocation toward Zero-waste Yield Optimization in Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11919)
*Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Honghao Liu,Xueyuan Lin,Xuhui Jiang,Chengjin Xu,Jia Li,Jian Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LazyTrain是一个建立在层流式执行器之上的优化层，通过将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算和CPU-GPU-NVMe通信重叠建模为混合整数调度问题，并配合混合8比特优化器状态与快速梯度裁剪，在有限硬件上显著提升大语言模型训练吞吐，H800上提升约1.24倍TFLOPS，Qwen3.6-27B达到219.95 TFLOPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限硬件（受限GPU算力、主机内存、PCIe传输和存储带宽）上训练大型语言模型本质上是调度问题。现有卸载系统（如MegaTrain）虽采用CPU主控的层流式执行器可在单GPU上训练大模型，但固定的检查点策略和放置启发式方法仍使通信暴露在关键路径上，导致训练效率低下。

Method: 1) 将检查点选择、激活放置、重计算和CPU-GPU-NVMe通信重叠统一建模为混合整数调度问题（MILP），在执行训练时按求解出的策略运行；2) 将8比特优化器状态与快速梯度裁剪耦合为单一混合8比特算子——状态压缩降低优化器显存占用，快速裁剪抵消CPU侧额外的更新开销。

Result: 在H800上从Qwen2.5-3B到Qwen3.6-27B的实验中，LazyTrain相比匹配的基线运行提升约1.24倍持续TFLOPS；RTX 3090实验中每个模型规模最大可行批次大小增加1。主要Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA运行达到219.95 TFLOPS，batch size 72下1361 tokens/s，GPU内存峰值68.84GB，完整评估集上精确匹配准确率95.42%。

Conclusion: LazyTrain通过统一的调度优化和混合8比特算子设计，有效解决了层流式执行场景下通信暴露于关键路径的问题，在有限硬件上实现了显著的大模型训练吞吐提升，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Training large language models on limited hardware is increasingly a scheduling problem across GPU compute, host memory, PCIe transfer, and storage bandwidth. Existing offloading systems reduce GPU residency, and MegaTrain shows that a CPU-master layer-streaming executor can train large models on a single GPU, but fixed checkpointing and placement heuristics still leave communication exposed on the critical path. We propose LazyTrain, an optimization layer over a layer-streaming executor. LazyTrain formulates checkpoint selection, activation placement, recomputation, and CPU-GPU-NVMe communication overlap as a mixed-integer scheduling problem, then executes the solved policy during training. It further couples 8-bit optimizer states with fast gradient clipping as a single Hybrid 8-bit operator: state compression reduces optimizer-state memory, while fast clipping counteracts the additional CPU-side update overhead. Across H800 experiments from Qwen2.5-3B to Qwen3.6-27B, LazyTrain improves sustained TFLOPS over matched baselines runs by approximately 1.24$\times$; RTX 3090 experiments likewise increase the maximum feasible batch size by one at each model scale. In the primary Qwen3.6-27B H800 MetaMathQA run, LazyTrain reaches 219.95 TFLOPS and 1361 tokens/s at batch size 72, peaks at 68.84\,GB of GPU memory, and obtains 95.42\% exact-match accuracy on the full evaluation split. The source code is available at https://github.com/DataArcTech/LazyTrain.

</details>


### [142] [LODESTAR: Trustworthy Entropy Is Navigated, Not Merely Measured -- Reinforced Polarizer Keeps a Frozen LLM from Being Confidently Misled by the Wrong Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11922)
*Po-Jen Ko,Che-Cheng Wu,Hung-Chun Hsu,Li-Yang Chang,Chuan-Ju Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LODESTAR通过强化学习训练一个固定的自然语言"极化器"字符串插入到冻结的respondent LLM的提示中，利用预测分布熵作为选择规则，在检索增强QA中将平均F1从0.5148提升至0.5339（相比重排序器top-ranked passage的0.4769），在5008个问题上超越了14种已发表配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于最低熵的选择规则（检索增强问答中选取answer-token熵最低的候选答案）虽然有效（将F1从0.4769提升至0.5148），但在误导性段落上会失效：误导性内容让respondent模型自信地犯错，熵反而降低，使信号最可信处恰是错误处。作者指出失败根源在于respondent阅读的段落，而该段落被阅读的上下文是可控的输入，由此提出通过干预上下文来改善选择的思路。

Method: LODESTAR是首个通过文本干预引发的不确定性来评分选择的方法。它使用强化学习一次性离线训练一个"极化器"（polarizer）——一个短小的固定自然语言字符串，插入到respondent的prompt中，绝不修改其权重。训练标签离线构建，源自金标准答案和两个LLM裁判；推理时不读取任何金标准。评估时统一在相同的冻结respondent和候选池下比较所有竞争选择器。

Result: LODESTAR在5008个问题上取得最高的推理就绪选择器平均F1（0.5339，对比基线0.5148）、最高精确匹配（0.4136）和最高的GPT-4o裁判分数（0.6435）。三种子平均在70个方法×数据集F1单元中全部胜出14种已发表配置，且所有配对均显著。增益在域内和域外均保持，消融实验显示极化器使respondent读取误导性段落的频率从30.3%降至26.0%。

Conclusion: 预测分布熵是强有效的选择规则，但最低熵规则在误导性段落前会系统性失效；通过LODESTAR引入的文本干预（极化器）可显著减轻这一失败模式，使冻结respondent更少被误导性段落带偏，从而在无需金标准推理的条件下超越所有已发表的推理就绪选择器。该方法首次证明通过第三方冻结respondent的提示文本干预来打分选择的有效性。

Abstract: Predictive-distribution entropy makes a strong selection rule in retrieval-augmented question answering: across five QA benchmarks, keeping the candidate answer that a frozen respondent LLM produces with the lowest answer-token entropy lifts mean answer $F_1$ from 0.4769 to 0.5148 over the retriever's top-ranked passage, with no gold answers. Yet this lowest-entropy rule, which prior entropy-based selectors adopt, fails in a specific and consequential way: a misleading passage makes the respondent confidently wrong, driving its entropy down precisely where the signal looks most trustworthy. We show that the failure comes from the passage the respondent reads -- and the context that passage is read in is an input we can intervene on. We introduce LODESTAR, to our knowledge the first method to score a text intervention by the uncertainty it induces in a third-party frozen respondent, compared across one question's candidates. LODESTAR uses reinforcement learning to train, once and offline, a polarizer -- a short fixed natural-language string inserted into the respondent's prompt and never into its weights; its training labels are built offline from gold answers and two LLM judges, and inference reads neither. Evaluating every competing selector under the same frozen respondent and the same candidate pools on 5,008 questions, LODESTAR attains the highest mean $F_1$ of any inference-ready selector (0.5148 to 0.5339), the highest exact match (0.4136), and the highest GPT-4o judge score of the frozen-respondent configurations judged (0.6435); its three-seed mean wins all 70 method-by-dataset $F_1$ cells against fourteen published configurations while remaining paired-significant against every one. The gain holds both in-domain and out-of-domain, and ablating the polarizer shows it is what makes the respondent read a misleading passage less often (26.0% against 30.3%).

</details>


### [143] [Spark-to-Paper: End-to-End Research Paper Generation as a Composable Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11924)
*Zhuoyang Qian,Biao Wu,Yiran Wang,Chris D Yan,Desan Dai,Liangwei Zheng,Jin Jiang,Junsheng Zhang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Spark-to-Paper，一个在现有编码助手中以十三个可组合技能实现的全流程科研论文生成系统。该系统将模型判断与可执行确定性操作分离，将实验规划与报告分离，并通过确定性完整性检查、自我批评和自反驳循环约束来提升长轨迹可靠性。在八个受控研究主题上实现99.5%的引用有效性、96.4%的图形可编辑性，且抗伪造检测从14%提升至92%，全系统平均消耗1190万token、8.1美元成本和3.2小时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大语言模型论文生成仅关注文本生成，但完整的科研论文生成需要检索文献、设计并执行实验、根据证据修订主张、生成出版级图形并维持长生成过程的一致性。现有方法缺乏端到端的系统化支持。

Method: Spark-to-Paper被实现为现有编码助手内的十三个可组合技能，无需独立的代理平台或编排服务。系统核心设计包括：（1）将模型基于判断的操作与可执行、可检查的确定性操作分离；（2）将实验规划与报告分离，使证据在结果观察之前就被指定，稿件主张根据实测结果修订；（3）结合确定性完整性检查与自我批评提升长轨迹可靠性；（4）通过有界处理"自反驳循环"这一失败模式（反复实验持续拒绝原始研究目标）；（5）通过程序化绘图生成可编辑矢量图形，通过基于代码重构生成方法图。

Result: 在八个受控研究主题上，系统实现99.5%的引用有效性和96.4%的图形可编辑性。受控消融实验将伪造检测率从单次生成的14%提升至完整完整性栈的92%，对抗性审查达到74%的精度。全系统平均消耗1190万token、成本8.1美元、耗时3.2小时。

Conclusion: 该研究证明端到端的科研论文生成可以以轻量、可组合的工作流方式在现有编码助手中实现，同时保持实验证据在主张被接受、修订或放弃过程中处于核心地位。

Abstract: Turning a research idea into a complete paper requires more than text generation: the system must retrieve literature, design and execute experiments, revise claims according to evidence, produce publication-ready figures, and maintain consistency across a long generation process. We present Spark-to-Paper, an end-to-end research paper generation system implemented as thirteen composable skills inside an existing coding assistant, without requiring a separate agent platform or orchestration service. Spark-to-Paper separates model-based judgment from deterministic operations that can be directly executed and checked. It further separates experiment planning from reporting, so that required evidence is specified before results are observed and manuscript claims are revised according to measured outcomes. To improve reliability over long research trajectories, the system combines deterministic integrity checks with self-critique and bounds a failure mode we call the Self-Refutation Loop, in which repeated experiments continue to reject the original research objective. Spark-to-Paper also produces editable vector figures through programmatic plotting for experimental results and code-based reconstruction for generated method diagrams. Across eight controlled research topics, Spark-to-Paper achieves 99.5% citation validity and 96.4% figure editability. A controlled ablation increases fabrication detection from 14% for a single-pass draft to 92% with the full integrity and review stack, while adversarial review achieves 74% precision. The full system uses 11.9M tokens, costs $8.1 per manuscript, and requires 3.2 hours on average. These results show that end-to-end research paper generation can be implemented as a lightweight, composable workflow inside existing coding assistants while keeping experimental evidence central to how claims are accepted, revised, or abandoned.

</details>


### [144] [Accuracy and Order Sensitivity Diverge Under Label-Free Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11947)
*Karl Hanna,Chen Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在多项选择题中因选项顺序产生的位置偏见问题。作者测试了两种消除位置影响的策略：生成后匹配法和选项独立评分法，但两者均未能可靠地提升准确率。完整的分解分析表明瓶颈在于隐藏选项这一操作本身，而非匹配步骤。唯一能与基线持平的是"展示全部选项+LLM匹配器"的配置。消除位置影响本身并不能带来可靠的准确率提升，而循环置换反而常能改善性能。两种用于检测去偏效果的指标（两阶段提示中的召回失衡聚合度量和逐题顺序敏感性度量）均未能可靠地证明去偏效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项选择题基准被广泛用于评估大语言模型，但MCQ分数将模型知识与对选项顺序的敏感度混为一谈，导致分数无法可靠反映模型的真实知识水平。位置偏见（positional bias）会污染评估结果，因此需要探索去除选项顺序影响的方法，以分离知识与位置敏感度，获得更可靠的模型知识评估。

Method: 作者测试了两种消除位置偏见的策略：一是"生成后匹配"（generation-then-matching）方法，即先让模型在看不到选项标签的情况下作答，再将生成结果与选项匹配；二是"选项独立评分"（scoring options in isolation），即逐一单独评分各选项，该方法从构造上就不存在位置偏差。研究者进一步做了完整的分解分析，将隐藏选项与匹配步骤分别拆解验证瓶颈所在，并测试了循环置换和两阶段提示等配置。

Result: 两种去偏策略均未可靠提升准确率。完整分解表明，性能下降的瓶颈在于向模型隐藏选项这一步，而非匹配环节。唯一能稳定达到基线的配置是"展示所有选项+LLM匹配器"的组合，但完全消除位置影响仍然无法可靠带来准确率提升；相反，循环置换（cyclic permutation）常常能改善性能。此外，用于检测去偏效果的两种指标——召回失衡的聚合度量和逐题订单敏感度度量——都未能可靠地证明去偏的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，通过消除选项位置影响来去除位置偏见并不能可靠地提升模型在多项选择题上的表现。位置偏见的去除与准确率提升之间不存在简单的因果关系，且现有去偏策略的有效性难以被现有指标可靠验证。这提示研究者需要重新审视MCQ评估中知识测量的有效性，以及位置偏见对评估结果影响的实际程度。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks are widely used to evaluate large language models, but MCQ scores conflate knowledge with sensitivity to option order, which makes them unreliable measures of model knowledge. In this paper, we test whether preventing a model from seeing option labels while committing to an answer removes positional influence and, in turn, improves performance. We evaluate two different strategies for mitigating bias. The first uses a generation-then-matching approach, and the second scores options in isolation, which is positionally unbiased by construction. Neither reliably improves accuracy. A complete decomposition shows that the bottleneck is withholding options, not the matching step. The only configuration that consistently matches the baseline is the one that shows the model all options paired with an LLM matcher. However, eliminating positional influence entirely still does not reliably yield accuracy gains, while cyclic permutation often improves them. For two-stage prompting, an aggregate measure of recall imbalance and a direct per-question measure of order sensitivity both fail to show reliable debiasing.

</details>


### [145] [Benchmarking Trustworthiness of SLMs: Pre-trained vs. Compressed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11981)
*Haokun Lin,Kaijie Zhu,Haobo Xu,Yichen Wu,Zhichao Lu,Qingfu Zhang,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CL
