TL;DR: 本文系统评估了小型语言模型（SLMs）在公平性、鲁棒性、隐私和伦理等多个维度的可信度。研究发现：量化比剪枝更能保留模型可信度；通过量化压缩可靠的LLM得到的SLM，其可信度和适应性优于从头训练的小模型；从可信教师模型进行知识蒸馏可进一步提升SLM的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLM作为LLM的高效替代方案在资源受限场景中潜力巨大，但现有研究主要关注构建SLM的路径（从头训练或压缩大模型），而如何构建可信赖的SLM这一问题尚未被充分探索。随着语言模型被集成到实际应用中，确保其可信度已成为关键议题。

Method: 作者从多个维度（公平性、鲁棒性、隐私、伦理）对SLM的可信度进行了全面评估。具体地，首先考察剪枝和量化两种压缩方法对可信度的影响；然后对比"通过量化压缩可靠大模型获得的SLM"与"从头训练的小模型"在可信度和适应性上的表现；最后探究从可信教师模型进行知识蒸馏对SLM可靠性的增强效果。

Result: 量化比剪枝在保留模型可信度方面显著更有效；压缩可靠的大模型比从头训练小模型能产生具有更优可信度和适应性的SLM；知识蒸馏可以进一步强化SLM的可靠性。

Conclusion: 研究结果为可信小型语言模型的开发与部署提供了实践指导，并为未来相关工作奠定了基础。建议优先选择量化压缩和知识蒸馏等路径来构建可信的SLM。

Abstract: Small Language Models (SLMs) have emerged as a more efficient alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), offering promising potential in resource-constrained scenarios. Existing approaches to building SLMs typically follow two paths: training compact models from scratch, or compressing larger pre-trained models using methods such as pruning, quantization, or distillation. As language models become increasingly integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their trustworthiness has become a critical concern. However, how to build trustworthy SLMs remains an underexplored question. In this work, we present a comprehensive evaluation of SLM trustworthiness across multiple dimensions, including fairness, robustness, privacy, and ethics. We first examine the effects of pruning and quantization, and find that quantization is significantly more effective in preserving trustworthiness compared to pruning. More importantly, we demonstrate that compressing a reliable large model via quantization can produce SLMs with superior trustworthiness and adaptability compared to using small models trained from scratch. Furthermore, knowledge distillation from trustworthy teacher models can further enhance the reliability of SLMs. We hope our findings provide practical guidance and a foundation for future research into the development and deployment of trustworthy small language models.

</details>


### [146] [Asymptotic Risk Calibration for Selective Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12008)
*Shufan Lin,Sijin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出A-CRC-QA，一种事后校准框架，用于不确定性感知的选择性问答，将选择条件下的误差控制重构为线性期望约束，并采用单调化的经验风险校准程序（受 conformal risk control 启发），实现渐近风险控制而非有限样本控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成流畅但不正确的答案时，传统启发式不确定性分数无法完美区分正确与错误预测，且直接应用固定不确定性阈值无法对已接受答案的错误率提供统计控制。这阻碍了LLM在高可靠性要求场景下的可靠应用。

Method: 1) 将选择条件下的误差控制重新形式化为线性期望约束；2) 应用单调化的经验风险校准程序（基于 conformal risk control）进行校准；3) 由于逐实例损失相对接受阈值通常非单调，框架目标设定为渐近风险控制；4) 模型无关、无需额外训练，可兼容不同不确定性估计器。

Result: 在CoQA和MedMCQA数据集上验证了方法在开放式和封闭式问答场景的有效性，相比未校准基线和基于置信界限的基线，在已接受答案可靠性与答案保留率之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: A-CRC-QA为LLM问答系统提供了一种灵活的、事后可插拔的误差控制方案，在无需修改底层模型的前提下，能够以统计上可控的方式提升选择性问答的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) may generate fluent but incorrect answers, making uncertainty quantification important for reliable question answering. However, heuristic uncertainty scores cannot perfectly distinguish correct predictions from incorrect ones, and directly applying a fixed uncertainty threshold provides no statistical control over the error rate among accepted answers. To address this limitation, we propose A-CRC-QA, a post-hoc calibration framework for uncertainty-aware selective question answering. The proposed method reformulates selection-conditioned error control as a linear expectation constraint and applies a monotonized empirical-risk calibration procedure inspired by conformal risk control. Since the resulting instance-wise loss is generally non-monotone with respect to the acceptance threshold, our framework targets asymptotic rather than finite-sample risk control. A-CRC-QA is model-agnostic, requires no additional training, and can be combined with different uncertainty estimators. Experiments on CoQA and MedMCQA demonstrate its applicability to both open-ended and closed-ended question answering, achieving a favorable trade-off between accepted-answer reliability and answer retention compared with uncalibrated and confidence-bound-based baselines.

</details>


### [147] [Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System for Bangla Regional Dialects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12018)
*Rakib Ullah,Ruhul Islam Rahul,Tanbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的孟加拉语多方言神经机器翻译系统，支持12种孟加拉地方言之间的多向翻译，无需通过标准语作为中转。系统构建了最大的多方言平行语料库（51,531个句子对，含12种方言），采用DoRA微调的BanglaT5模型达到SOTA性能（BLEU 29.26，chrF++ 57.26），并部署为INT8量化模型的开源Web应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语拥有超过2.4亿使用者，存在显著的地域方言变体，在音韵、形态和词汇上均与标准口语孟加拉语（SCB）有较大差异。然而，现有的神经机器翻译（NMT）架构和大语言模型（LLMs）均假设语言分布是均匀的，导致在低资源地域方言翻译上性能严重退化。这促使作者构建一个能够多方向翻译多种方言、无需经过标准语中转的统一系统。

Method: 1）构建孟加拉语迄今最大的多方言平行语料库，包含12种方言的51,531个非空平行句子对，其中2,500对为专家验证的双向平行句对，覆盖5个此前未被研究的方言；2）采用加权分解低秩适配（DoRA）微调序列到序列架构；3)系统性的跨方言迁移分析与数据集规模扩展研究，确立低资源方言适配的经验阈值；4)将模型INT8量化后部署为开源Web应用。

Result: 微调后的BanglaT5模型在翻译性能上达到SOTA（BLEU 29.26，chrF++ 57.26），优于NLLB-200（615M）和mBART-50（611M），同时保持了形态学连贯性。此外，还进行了系统的跨方言迁移分析和数据集缩放研究，并部署了INT8量化的Web应用以促进边缘方言社区的数字包容。

Conclusion: 该工作通过构建大规模多方言平行语料库、引入DoRA微调的BanglaT5模型以及量化的部署方案，有效解决了孟加拉语多方言NMT的性能退化问题，推动了低资源方言的数字包容。数据集已公开发布在Mendeley Data上。

Abstract: Regional dialectal variation poses a fundamental challenge to natural language processing (NLP) in Bangla, where over 240 million speakers communicate across diverse regional variants that diverge significantly from Standard Colloquial Bangla (SCB) in phonology, morphology, and lexicon. Contemporary neural machine trans- lation (NMT) architectures and large language models (LLMs) predominantly as- sume a homogeneous language distribution, resulting in severe performance degra- dation when translating low-resource regional dialects. In this work, we present a unified Poly-Dialectal Neural Machine Translation System capable of multi-directional translation across 12 Bangla regional dialects without routing through an inter- mediary standard pivot. We compile the largest multi-dialect parallel corpus for Bangla to date, comprising 51,531 non-null parallel sentence pairs across 12 di- alects, incorporating 2,500 expert-verified, bidirectional parallel sentence pairs for five previously unaddressed dialects. Evaluating sequence-to-sequence architec- tures under Weight-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation (DoRA), our fine-tuned BanglaT5 model achieves state-of-the-art translation performance (29.26 BLEU, 57.26 chrF++), outperforming NLLB-200 (615M) and mBART-50 (611M) while preserving morphological coherence. Furthermore, we conduct a systematic cross- dialectal transfer analysis and dataset scaling study, establishing empirical thresh- olds for low-resource dialect adaptation. Finally, we deploy the optimized INT8- quantized model as an open-access web application to promote digital inclusion for marginalized dialect communities. The complete dataset is publicly available at Mendeley Data (https://data.mendeley.com/datasets/v9cf66fk2t/2).

</details>


### [148] [Preference Tree Optimization: Enhancing Goal-Oriented Dialogue with Look-Ahead Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12062)
*Lior Baruch,Moshe Butman,Kfir Bar,Doron Friedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为偏好树优化（PTO）的新框架，通过偏好树与前瞻（Look-Ahead）方法生成偏好数据，并结合直接偏好优化（DPO）迭代训练对话代理，以应对目标导向对话系统中数据稀缺的挑战。该框架在动机性访谈（MI）领域通过虚拟患者和评估器模拟对话生成偏好数据，实验表明PTO能显著提升对话代理在关键指标（如会话满意度和工作联盟）上的表现，且更深的前瞻配置带来更稳定和更优的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮目标导向对话系统的开发在专业领域面临重大挑战，尤其是标注数据稀缺的问题。动机性访谈作为一种促进行为改变的咨询技术，是此类目标导向对话的典型应用场景，但缺乏足够的训练数据来开发有效的对话代理。现有方法难以在数据受限条件下生成高质量偏好数据，因此需要一种能通过自我对弈迭代提升代理模型能力的新框架。

Method: 提出偏好树优化（Preference Tree Optimization, PTO）框架，核心包含偏好树与前瞻（Preference Tree with Look-Ahead）偏好数据生成方法。利用虚拟患者与Oracle评估器模拟多轮对话，构建偏好树并引入不同深度的前瞻模拟，生成丰富的偏好数据集；随后将生成数据与直接偏好优化（DPO）相结合，通过迭代训练周期不断提升代理的决策能力。

Result: 在动机性访谈领域的目标导向对话任务中，经PTO框架训练的模型在会话满意度和工作联盟等关键指标上持续优于基线模型。引入前瞻模拟能改善对话代理的长期规划和对话策略有效性，且更深的前瞻配置产生最稳定且高分的实验结果。

Conclusion: PTO框架有效解决了目标导向对话系统中数据稀缺的问题，通过迭代生成偏好数据并优化模型，显著提升了对话代理在动机性访谈等多轮目标导向对话场景中的表现。前瞻深度的增加能进一步强化代理的长期规划能力，为专业领域对话系统的开发提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Developing dialogue systems capable of engaging in multi-turn, goal-oriented conversations remains a significant challenge, especially in specialized domains with limited data. This research proposes a novel framework called Preference Tree Optimization (PTO), designed to iteratively improve agent models in such dialogue systems, by generating preference data using a method called Preference Tree with Look-Ahead. Focusing on Motivational Interviewing (MI) -- a counseling technique aimed at facilitating behavioral change -- we leverage virtual patients and an oracle evaluator to simulate conversations and generate rich preference datasets. By combining this method with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), we aim to enhance the agent's decision-making capabilities over iterative training cycles. The proposed framework addresses data scarcity and advances the development of more nuanced and effective dialogue systems in goal-oriented domains. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that the PTO framework enhances dialogue agents' performance in goal-oriented conversations within the domain of Motivational Interviewing (MI). Models trained with PTO consistently outperformed the baseline in key metrics such as session satisfaction and working alliance. Additionally, incorporating look-ahead simulations led to improved long-term planning and more effective conversational strategies, with deeper look-ahead configurations yielding the most stable and high-scoring results.

</details>


### [149] [Structuring the Space of Perspectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12113)
*Agnese Daffara,Sebastian Padó,Tanise Ceron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了NLP领域中与"视角"（perspective）相关的各类概念（如立场、情感、框架、论点等），定义了区分这些概念的一组属性，并提出了一个沿单一轴线组织的概念层次结构，以帮助研究者根据研究目标选择合适的操作化定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同一事件可以从不同视角被报道，NLP中多个领域都涉及视角研究，但用于捕捉视角的操作性概念（立场、情感、框架、论点等）之间的精确关系尚不明确。缺乏对这些概念的深入理论理解会阻碍视角研究的有效性。

Method: 作者通过系统回顾NLP中视角研究的空间，定义了一组区分视角相关概念的属性，并据此提出了一个将这些概念沿单一轴线线性组织的概念层次结构。

Result: 提出了一个有原则的概念层次结构，该结构可以将NLP中与视角相关的各个概念沿单轴进行线性组织，并能帮助研究者导航该领域，选择与研究目标一致的视角操作化方案。

Conclusion: 这一概念层次结构为NLP中的视角研究提供了理论框架，有助于研究者更清晰地理解不同概念间的关系，并在实证研究中做出更明智的概念选择。

Abstract: The same event can be reported from different perspectives depending on the experiences, background, and beliefs of the writer or speaker. A variety of NLP areas engage with perspectives, spanning from text analysis to algorithm optimization. A wide range of operative concepts (such as stances, sentiment, frames, and arguments) has been used to capture perspectives in texts, however the precise relationships among those concepts remain unclear. Arguably, a deeper theoretical understanding of these concepts would empower more effective research on perspectives. In this paper, we address this gap by reviewing the space of perspectives in NLP and defining a set of properties that help distinguishing perspective-related concepts. Our analysis leads us to posit a hierarchy which organizes these concepts linearly along a single axis. Finally, we show how this principled conceptual hierarchy can help researchers navigate the field and select operationalizations of perspective that align with their specific research objectives.

</details>


### [150] [QV-PIC: Query-Aware Visual Position-Independent Caching for Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12121)
*Yilin Liu,Rui Meng,Wangze Ni,Jianxin Yan,Heng Cao,Libin Zheng,Peng Cheng,Jinfei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出QV-PIC，一种查询感知的双分辨率PIC重用框架，通过模型原生模板离线编译视觉缓存、在线利用累积查询相关分数在高分辨率预算内恢复细粒度文本证据，在六项任务中平均F1提升21.6分，超越优化后的文本PIC并削减83.8%的TTFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在多次查询中重复预填充相同文本块导致冗余计算。虽然位置无关缓存（PIC）可复用预计算的KV对，但文本PIC受大量文本token限制效率；而将文本渲染为图像虽能压缩视觉token，但渲染图像PIC比文本PIC质量退化更严重。现有PIC修复方法主要针对上下文不匹配进行选择性重算，但引入在线计算且无法恢复丢失的文本细节。

Method: QV-PIC框架包含两个关键设计：（1）离线阶段，在模型原生聊天模板前缀下编译视觉缓存，提升PIC质量且无需在线重算；（2）在线阶段，以低分辨率保留全局上下文，同时在给定高清预算内通过累积查询相关分数优先恢复细粒度文本证据，从而在保留视觉压缩效率的同时弥补细节损失。

Result: 在六项任务中，QV-PIC相比原始渲染图像PIC平均F1提升21.6点，几乎弥补了与文本PIC的差距；超越优化后的文本PIC 2.58个F1点，同时TTFT降低17.2%；与完整预填充相比，TTFT削减83.8%。

Conclusion: QV-PIC证明查询感知的双分辨率策略与模型原生模板相结合，可以有效解决渲染图像PIC的质量退化问题，在保持视觉token压缩带来的效率优势的同时，达到甚至超越文本PIC的性能。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) repeatedly prefills identical text chunks across queries, incurring redundant computations. Position-Independent Caching (PIC) mitigates it by reusing precomputed Key-Value (KV) across positions, but its efficiency is constrained by the large volume of text tokens. Rendering text chunks as images can compress the text into fewer visual tokens, but the rendered-image PIC suffers more severe quality degradation than the text PIC. This representation-specific gap primarily arises from contextual mismatches across independently compiled caches and the loss of fine-grained textual evidence during visual compression. Existing PIC repair methods mainly address the former through selective recomputation, but they incur online computation and cannot recover lost textual details. We propose QV-PIC, a query-aware dual-resolution PIC reuse framework guided by model-native templates. Offline, QV-PIC compiles visual caches under the model's native chat-template prefix, improving PIC quality without online recomputation. Online, it preserves global context with low resolution and restores fine-grained textual evidence within a high-resolution budget by cumulative query relevance scores, retaining the efficiency benefit of visual compression. Across six tasks, QV-PIC improves average F1 by 21.6 points over vanilla rendered-image PIC, closes the gap to vanilla text PIC, and surpasses optimized text PIC by 2.58 F1 while reducing TTFT by 17.2\%. Relative to full prefill, it cuts TTFT by 83.8%.

</details>


### [151] [SAG: SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation with Query-Time Dynamic Hyperedges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12129)
*Yuchao Wu,Junqin Li,XingCheng Liang,Yongjie Chen,Yinghao Liang,Linyuan Mo,Guanxian Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAG（SQL检索增强生成）架构，将文档组织为事件-实体索引而非全局知识图谱，以潜在超边保留n元关系，查询时以共享实体为连接键动态构建查询范围内的邻居事件，在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上均取得最佳检索与端到端问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流稠密检索方法在处理结构化约束和多跳推理时存在固有局限；图谱方法虽能解决部分问题，但会碎片化语义、维护成本高且难以增量更新。需要一个既能保持原始文档语义完整性、又能支持多跳结构化检索的架构。

Method: SAG将每个文档块表示为一个语义完整的事件及其关联实体，构成保留n元关系的潜在超边（而非分解为三元组）；查询时利用共享实体作为连接键（join keys）将相关块连接起来，动态生成查询范围内的邻居事件集合，所有证据始终以原始块形式保留。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue三个基准上均取得最佳性能，且收益随着推理链复杂度增加而扩大；在最具挑战性的MuSiQue上Recall@5达到80.36%，超过最强基线11.52个百分点。

Conclusion: SAG为持续增长的组织知识构建了无需全局知识图谱的结构化检索基础设施，为LLM智能体的检索与推理提供了新路径，验证了事件-实体索引结合SQL式连接查询在多跳推理场景中的有效性。

Abstract: While retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has proven effective at giving LLMs access to external knowledge, mainstream dense-retrieval implementations remain inherently limited in handling structured constraints and multi-hop reasoning. Graph-based methods address this by constructing knowledge graphs offline, but they often fragment semantics, incur high maintenance, and complicate incremental updates. We propose SAG (SQL-Retrieval Augmented Generation), a structured retrieval architecture that organizes documents into an event-entity index without building a global knowledge graph. SAG represents each chunk as a semantically complete event paired with its entities, forming a latent hyperedge that preserves n-ary relations without decomposing them into triples. At query time, SAG treats shared entities as join keys to connect related chunks. This dynamically yields a query-scoped neighborhood of events, and yet every piece of evidence remains the original chunk throughout. Experiments on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue show that SAG achieves the best retrieval and end-to-end QA performance on every benchmark, with gains that widen as reasoning-chain complexity increases. On MuSiQue, where multi-hop evidence chaining is most demanding, SAG reaches 80.36% Recall@5, outperforming the strongest baseline by 11.52 points. This work paves the way for knowledge infrastructure that enables LLM agents to retrieve and reason over continually growing organizational knowledge.

</details>


### [152] [A corpus-specific clinical RAG system matches or outperforms newer frontier LLMs on HealthBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12138)
*Praveen Reddy,Charuta Mandke,Suvrankar Datta,Sarah Khan,Siddharth Reddy Anthireddy,Shitij Arora,Vishal Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了VITA——一个专为印度等中低收入国家（LMIC）构建的检索增强生成（RAG）临床系统。该系统在HealthBench基准上以51.9%的分数排名第一，击败GPT-5.4、o4-mini等前沿大语言模型；但在使用更新的模型和中性评判者的500题子集中，VITA与GPT-5.5达到统计无差异的平局，显示其优势在准确性和完整性上持续存在，但沟通分数较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在医学基准上已声称匹配或超越专用临床AI工具，但这些比较基于狭窄的系统集和主要在发达国家开发的标准。作者希望评估在印度等LMIC环境中专门构建的临床RAG系统是否仍具竞争力，以避免前沿模型在真实世界资源受限医疗场景中的过度自信。

Method: VITA是一个为LMIC环境专门构建的RAG系统，从疾病指南、印度抗菌素耐药性数据、国家处方集限制和资源受限护理协议组成的语料库中检索信息。研究在4,023道HealthBench英语题目上进行评估，使用GPT-4.1评判者打分；另取500题子集使用更新的模型和与所有测试系统无关联的开放权重评判者（DeepSeek-V4-Pro）进行稳健性验证。

Result: 在完整测试集上，VITA以51.9%的分数排名第一，超过GPT-5.4（46.1%）、o4-mini（44.3%）、Gemini 3.1 Pro（42.6%）和Claude Sonnet 4.6（37.3%），并在45.4%的题目上得分最高。在500题重测中，VITA与GPT-5.5在平均每题分数上统计无差异，但在加权分数和获胜题数上领先；其准确性优势在评判者更换后仍然存在，但沟通得分较低。

Conclusion: 专门构建的临床RAG系统在开放基准上与前沿通用LLM保持竞争力，语料库特异性作为设计变量可改善基于事实的可靠性（grounding），但以沟通流畅度（communication polish）为代价。这一发现对中低收入国家的临床AI部署具有重要参考意义。

Abstract: General-purpose large language models (LLMs) have recently been reported to match or exceed specialized clinical AI tools on medical benchmarks, but such comparisons draw on a narrow set of systems and on benchmarks developed largely in high-income settings. We evaluate VITA, a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system purpose-built for contextual knowledge retrieval in India and other low- and middle-income (LMIC) settings. VITA retrieves from a curated corpus of disease-specific guidelines, India-specific antimicrobial resistance data, national formulary constraints, and resource-limited care protocols; its architecture and corpus are proprietary, but the benchmark, the physician-written rubrics, and our full response and scoring outputs are public for independent verification. On 4,023 English-language HealthBench questions (80.5% of the benchmark), scored with a GPT-4.1 judge, VITA ranked first with 51.9% of possible rubric points, ahead of GPT-5.4 (46.1%), o4-mini (44.3%), Gemini 3.1 Pro (42.6%), and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (37.3%), and scored highest on 45.4% of questions. To test robustness to newer models and judge lineage, a 500-question subset was re-run against current-generation models (GPT-5.5, Claude Opus 4.8, Gemini 3.5 Pro, Grok 4.3) and graded by a neutral open-weight judge (DeepSeek-V4-Pro) sharing no lineage with any system tested. Here the gap narrowed to parity: VITA and GPT-5.5 were statistically indistinguishable on mean per-question score, while VITA led on points-weighted score and won the most questions. VITA's advantages in accuracy and completeness persisted under the neutral judge; its communication scores were lower. These results indicate that a purpose-built clinical RAG system remains competitive with frontier LLMs on an open benchmark, consistent with corpus specificity as a design variable that improves grounding at some cost to communication polish.

</details>


### [153] [Massive Activations in Hybrid Linear Attention Large Language Models: Pre-Attention Spikes and Inter-Spike Plateaus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12149)
*Zunhai Su,Bohan Sun,Xialie Zhuang,Shuibai Zhang,He Xiao,Jing Xiong,Hengyuan Zhang,Zhongzhu Zhou,Tiantian Zhang,Ngai Wong,Chuan-Wei Kuo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了层交错混合线性注意力（HLA）大语言模型中的大规模激活（MAs），揭示了两种与架构对齐的形态：注意力前尖峰（PAS）和尖峰间平台（ISP），并阐明其形成机制与生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模激活（MAs）在全注意力LLM中已被广泛研究，但在混合线性注意力（HLA）架构中的行为尚不明确。理解MA在HLA中的形态与机制，对于优化混合架构的设计与推理效率至关重要。

Method: 作者系统研究了五种线性注意力架构、六种混合配置、五个数据域以及1.2B至397B参数的多种开源混合模型，并在GDN混合架构上进行最高1.3B规模的控制性预训练实验，结合系统性离群值分析揭示MA的生命周期机制。

Result: 发现两种架构对齐的MA形态：PAS（全注意力层前尖峰）和ISP（通过线性注意力层的尖峰间平台）。全注意力密度增加时，PAS通过ISP连接形成稳定形态。全注意力输出门控强烈衰减MA幅值但不消除层间组织，移除GDN门控则带来相对温和的放大效应。机制上揭示了MA的写入-沉淀-取消生命周期，PAS为局部过程、ISP为延迟取消。

Conclusion: 该研究表明MA在HLA架构中遵循一种由取消时机控制的共享生命周期，为理解混合注意力架构的激活动态提供了统一框架，并开源了代码以供复现。

Abstract: We present the first systematic study of Massive activations (MAs) in layer-interleaved HLA LLMs and uncover two architecture-aligned morphologies: MAs consistently spike immediately before full attention layers, forming pre-attention spikes (PAS), and can persist through intervening linear attention layers, giving rise to inter-spike plateaus (ISP). As full attention becomes denser, successive PAS become increasingly connected through ISP, ultimately recovering the stable MA morphology of full attention LLMs. We establish the recurrence of this organization across five linear attention architectures, six hybridization configurations, five data domains, and representative open-source hybrid models spanning 1.2B to 397B total parameters. Controlled pretraining of GDN-based hybrids at scales up to 1.3B shows that both morphologies emerge early and respond asymmetrically to output gating: full attention output gating strongly attenuates their absolute magnitudes without eliminating their layerwise organization, whereas removing GDN gates yields comparatively modest amplification. Mechanistically, our systematic-outlier analysis supports a shared lifecycle account governed by the timing of MA cancellation. PAS follows a localized write-sink-cancel process, while the extended persistence of ISP is consistent with delayed cancellation. At the full attention limit, this account recovers the stable MA morphology characteristic of full attention LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/StartluxLabs/Massive-Activations-HLA.

</details>


### [154] [Information Abundance Paradox: Long-Context Training Undermines Parametric Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12218)
*Arda Uzunoglu,Benjamin van Durme,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"信息丰裕悖论"：训练上下文中相关信息的增多反而会降低模型将该信息参数化编码的动机，从而增加对上下文的依赖。在预训练中，增大上下文窗口仅在中间最优值之前提升性能，之后持续下降；在监督微调中，训练时丰富的上下文能提升带支持上下文的性能，但降低上下文缺失或误导时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地以长上下文进行训练和部署，隐含假设是更长上下文中的丰富证据只会对模型有帮助。作者质疑这一假设，旨在研究上下文窗口如何塑造模型的学习模式（参数化内化vs上下文化），揭示长上下文训练可能带来的负面影响。

Method: 通过预训练和监督微调实验系统研究上下文窗口大小对模型行为的影响。在预训练阶段使用长文档评估语言建模、自然语言理解、闭卷MCQA性能；在微调阶段考察训练时上下文的丰富度对测试时鲁棒性的影响。机理上，通过分析梯度压力在FFN与注意力模块之间的分配转移，并使用因果干预验证模型对上下文的依赖变化。

Result: 预训练中增大上下文窗口存在中间最优值，超过后性能一致下降；监督微调中更多任务相关的训练上下文改善了带支持上下文的性能，但降低了上下文缺失或误导场景下的鲁棒性。机制上，长上下文训练将梯度压力从FFN（与参数化知识相关）转移到注意力模块，因果干预证实这种转移增加了推理时对上下文的依赖。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持信息丰裕悖论，表明向近无限上下文扩展并不是简单地提供更多数据的问题，即使高质量长上下文数据充足亦然。上下文窗口的选择需要在参数化内化与上下文化之间进行权衡。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly trained and deployed with long contexts that span documents, code repositories, and interaction histories. This scaling reflects the implicit assumption that training on longer contexts will only help the model by exposing it to richer evidence. We challenge this view by studying how the context window shapes a model's mode of learning, shifting it between parametric internalization and contextualization. We propose the Information Abundance Paradox, which hypothesizes that abundant relevant information in the training context can reduce the incentive to encode that information parametrically, thereby increasing reliance on context. In pretraining with long documents, increasing the context window improves language modeling, natural language understanding, and closed-book MCQA only up to an intermediate optimum, after which performance consistently declines. In supervised fine-tuning, more task-relevant train-time context improves performance with supporting context, but reduces robustness when context is absent or misleading at test time. Our analysis suggests that this behavior arises when longer context provides a lower complexity solution. Mechanistically, training with informative context shifts gradient pressure from feed-forward networks, often linked to parametric knowledge, toward attention modules, and causal interventions show that this shift increases reliance on context during inference. Overall, these findings support the Information Abundance Paradox and suggest that scaling toward near-infinite context is not simply a matter of supplying more data, even when high-quality long-context data is abundant.

</details>


### [155] [One Frozen Simulator Is Not Enough: Simulator Collapse in Multi-Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12253)
*Simon Yu,Nicholas Tomlin,Marwa Abdulhai,Ximing Lu,Derek Chong,Abe Hou,Dilara Soylu,Sergey Levine,Christopher D. Manning,Weiyan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多智能体强化学习中用单一LLM模拟用户行为会导致"模拟器坍缩"（simulator collapse），即模拟器因模式坍缩而使策略过拟合到狭窄策略，难以泛化到真实用户。作者提出两种互补方案：推理阶段的Verbalized Sampling和训练阶段的Co-Training，在三个多轮基准上分别带来最高9%和14%的h局外成功率提升，并发布开源框架SCOPE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互的多智能体强化学习通常依赖单一的LLM来模拟用户行为，但这种做法系统性失败于泛化。作者将失败根源追溯至模拟器坍缩——模拟器LLM的模式坍缩导致策略过拟合到模拟器的主导模式，从而使得训练出的策略难以迁移到未见过的模拟器和真实用户。

Method: 作者从理论上形式化描述了模拟器坍缩现象，并提出两种互补方案：(1) 推理阶段方案Verbalized Sampling，通过从语言化的响应分布中采样来拓宽模拟器行为，减少模式坍缩；(2) 训练阶段方案Co-Training，联合优化策略使其面对一群可训练的模拟器，防止对单一模拟器模式的过拟合。

Result: 在三个多轮基准（Persuasion for Good、τ²-bench、CooperBench）上验证：Verbalized Sampling相比单一模拟器RL在held-out成功率上提升最高9%，Co-Training进一步将增益推至14%；人类研究在真实用户上显示相似的增益。两种方案都保留了单一模拟器RL下会坍缩的策略多样性。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，训练环境的多样性（而不仅仅是策略本身）对于多轮RL在真实世界部署中的泛化至关重要。为支持后续研究，作者发布了开源框架SCOPE（Population Co-Training多智能体RL框架）。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning for human-AI interaction typically relies on a single large language model to simulate user behavior. We show that this approach systematically fails to generalize, and trace the failure to simulator collapse: because the simulator LLM is mode-collapsed, an LLM policy trained against it overfits to narrow strategies that exploit the simulator's dominant mode, and such a policy transfers poorly to unseen simulators and real users. We formalize this collapse theoretically and propose two complementary solutions, one at inference time and one at training time. The inference-time solution, Verbalized Sampling, broadens the simulator's behavior by sampling from a verbalized response distribution, reducing mode collapse. The training-time solution, Co-Training, jointly optimizes the policy against a population of trainable simulators, preventing it from overfitting to any single simulator's mode. We validate both solutions on three multi-turn benchmarks: Persuasion for Good, $τ^2$-bench, and CooperBench. Verbalized Sampling improves held-out success by up to 9% over single-simulator RL, and Co-Training pushes gains further to 14%; the human study shows similar gain on real users. Both solutions preserve the policy diversity that collapses under single-simulator RL. To support further work in this direction, we release SCOPE, an open-source framework for Population Co-Training multi-agent RL. More broadly, our results suggest that the diversity of the training environment, not only the policy, is critical to the generalization of multi-turn RL to real-world deployment.

</details>


### [156] [A Cascaded Unsupervised-Supervised NLP Pipeline for Detecting Accusatory Language in Public Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12269)
*Bryan Torres,Daniel Riofrío,José Vega-Sánchez,Nathaly Orozco,Carla Parra,Karen Rosero,Felipe Grijalva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合建模框架，结合无监督聚类与有监督分类的NLP流水线，分析厄瓜多尔公共采购系统(SOCE)中合同前阶段的参与者评论元数据，以检测潜在的采购程序违规行为。实验表明，领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入、GMM聚类与随机森林分类器的组合在严重类别不平衡下仍能达到高精确率和召回率，证明了轻量级领域自适应NLP架构可有效支持公共采购中的风险识别与透明度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共采购涉及大量财政资源分配，亟需持续的审计与监督，但利益相关方评论和公开政府数据往往被忽视，未能充分发挥其在揭示程序违规方面的潜力。

Method: 构建融合无监督聚类与有监督分类的混合NLP框架：使用Word2Vec、LLaMA和RoBERTa生成语义嵌入，采用高斯混合模型(GMM)进行无监督聚类，随后进行有监督分类以识别指控性或举报风格评论。

Result: 领域训练的Word2Vec嵌入与GMM聚类及随机森林分类器组合，在严重类别不平衡条件下仍取得高精确率和召回率。

Conclusion: 轻量级、领域自适应的NLP架构无需大规模计算基础设施，即可有效支持公共采购系统的风险识别与透明度增强。

Abstract: Public procurement involves the allocation of substantial financial resources; therefore, continuous oversight through audits, controls, and monitoring mechanisms is essential. However, stakeholder comments and publicly available government data are often underutilized, despite their potential to reveal procedural irregularities. To address this gap, this paper analyzes metadata from Ecuador's Sistema Oficial de Contratación Pública (SOCE, Official Public Procurement System), with particular emphasis on participant comments generated during the pre-contractual phase. We propose a hybrid modeling framework that integrates unsupervised clustering and supervised classification within a natural language processing (NLP) pipeline to uncover latent patterns and detect potentially irregular procurement processes. Semantic embeddings are generated using Word2Vec, LLaMA, and RoBERTa, followed by Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) for unsupervised clustering. A supervised classification stage is then applied to identify accusatory or whistleblowing-style comments. Experimental results show that the combination of domain-trained Word2Vec embeddings, GMM-based clustering, and a Random Forest classifier achieves high precision and recall, even under severe class imbalance. These findings demonstrate that lightweight, domain-adapted NLP architectures can effectively support risk identification and enhance transparency in public procurement systems without requiring large-scale computational infrastructure.

</details>


### [157] [Structural Silence: When AI Infrastructure Fails Speakers of Underrepresented Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12278)
*Avijit Roy,Proma Roy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过孟加拉语这一案例，系统揭示了AI教育工具在低资源语言群体中的结构性障碍，包括网络内容匮乏、训练Token赤字、分词惩罚和连通性排斥四重相互关联的失败，主张将数据集稀缺视为结构性壁垒，并倡导以离线优先设计作为面向公平的基础设施策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI教育工具被广泛宣传为弥合欠发达地区教育鸿沟的可扩展方案，但底层基础设施（如训练语料库、分词方案、评估基准和部署架构）在模型训练之前就已系统性地排斥少数语言使用者。作者以孟加拉语为案例，旨在揭示这些被忽视的结构性障碍，将数据集稀缺从孤立的技术局限重新框定为结构性问题。

Method: 论文采用案例分析法，识别出四重互锁的结构性失败：(1)网络存在度差距——孟加拉语占全球网络内容不足0.5%，但占全球人口近4%；(2)训练Token赤字——主要多语语料库中英语与孟加拉语的Token比例为67:1；(3)分词惩罚——孟加拉语书写的音节文字（alphasyllabary）导致更高的token生育率，进一步放大数据赤字；(4)连通性排斥——农村地区个人互联网普及率为36.5%，远低于城市地区的71.4%。

Result: 研究证实了孟加拉语在四个维度上同时遭遇系统性排斥，这些失败并非孤立的技术缺陷，而是长期资源分配决策、制度优先级和设计默认值共同作用的结果。离线优先设计被论证为一种面向权益的基础设施策略。

Conclusion: 论文主张应将数据集稀缺理解为结构性壁垒而非技术局限，并呼吁以离线优先设计作为促进教育公平的基础设施策略，同时为语言学和AI研究指出了减少结构性不平等的未来方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence tools for education and language support are increasingly framed as scalable responses to access gaps in under-resourced communities. Yet the infrastructure underlying these tools, including training corpora, tokenization schemes, evaluation benchmarks, and deployment architectures, can systematically disadvantage speakers of underrepresented languages before a model is trained.
  This paper examines these structural barriers through Bengali, one of the world's most widely spoken languages, focusing on AI-assisted education in low-connectivity environments. We identify four interlocking failures: a severe web presence gap, with Bengali accounting for less than 0.5% of global web content despite representing nearly 4% of the global population; a 67:1 training-token deficit between English and Bengali in major multilingual corpora; a tokenization penalty associated with Bengali's alphasyllabary script that compounds the data deficit through higher token fertility; and connectivity exclusion, with individual internet penetration at 36.5% in rural areas compared with 71.4% in urban areas.
  These failures reflect longstanding resource-allocation decisions, institutional priorities, and design defaults that did not center underrepresented languages in mainstream AI development. We argue that dataset scarcity should be understood as a structural barrier rather than an isolated technical limitation, and that offline-first design should be treated as an equity-oriented infrastructure strategy. We conclude with directions for linguistics and AI research aimed at reducing these structural inequalities.

</details>


### [158] [WavePhaseNet: A DFT-Based Method for Constructing Semantic Conceptual Hierarchy Structures (SCHS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14419)
*Kiyotaka Kasubuchi,Kazuo Fukiya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过测度论和频率分析重新审视Transformer/注意力机制，从理论上论证了LLM幻觉是结构性必然，并提出WavePhaseNet方法：利用DFT构建语义概念层次结构，将嵌入空间从24,576维降至约3,000维（基于1/f谱和能量累积分析），再通过Hodge理论的上同调正则化实现语义一致性控制，从而在保持语义完整性的同时抑制幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中幻觉现象普遍存在且难以消除，作者试图从理论层面证明幻觉并非随机错误，而是模型架构的内在结构性局限。通过将嵌入空间建模为σ-代数上的条件期望，揭示其与语义真值集之间缺乏同构性，从而从根本上解释逻辑一致性崩溃的原因，并据此提出可操作的解决方案。

Method: WavePhaseNet方法：(1) 利用DFT沿序列维度将语义信息分解为频率带，低频捕获全局语义/意图，高频表示局部句法/表达，在可对角化空间实现精确语义操控；(2) 基于嵌入空间的1/f谱结构和Zipf定律，通过累积能量分析确定约3,000维为"完整表示"下界，实现从24,576维的系统降维；(3) 在降维空间中通过上同调正则化（重叠局部窗口构造图结构和上链复形），将局部推理不一致量化为上边缘损失，并应用基于Hodge理论的调和投影获取全局最大一致表示。

Result: 理论上证明了幻觉是LLM的必然结构缺陷而非可完全消除的偶然错误；实证上验证了24,576维嵌入存在1/f谱结构，且约3,000维即构成完整语义表示的维度下界；所提出的上同调一致性控制将语义一致性转化为可计算的正则化原则，能够提取全局最大一致的表示。

Conclusion: 幻觉源于嵌入空间在测度论层面无法与语义真值集同构，属于结构性必然；通过频率域分解和维度压缩（24,576→3,000）可以保留语义完整性的同时抑制幻觉；基于Hodge理论的上同调正则化为语义一致性控制提供了坚实的数学框架，代表了一条从理论推导到工程实践的可验证路径。

Abstract: This paper reformulates Transformer/Attention mechanisms in Large Language Models (LLMs) through measure theory and frequency analysis, theoretically demonstrating that hallucination is an inevitable structural limitation. The embedding space functions as a conditional expectation over a σ-algebra, and its failure to be isomorphic to the semantic truth set fundamentally causes logical consistency breakdown. WavePhaseNet Method The authors propose WavePhaseNet, which explicitly constructs a Semantic Conceptual Hierarchy Structure (SCHS) using Discrete Fourier Transform (DFT). By applying DFT along the sequence dimension, semantic information is decomposed into frequency bands: low-frequency components capture global meaning and intent, while high-frequency components represent local syntax and expression. This staged separation enables precise semantic manipulation in diagonalized space. Dimensionality Reduction GPT-4's 24,576-dimensional embedding space exhibits a 1/f spectral structure based on language self-similarity and Zipf's law. Through cumulative energy analysis, the authors derive that approximately 3,000 dimensions constitute the lower bound for "complete representation." This demonstrates that reduction from 24,576 to 3,000 dimensions preserves meaning and intent while enabling rigorous reasoning and suppressing hallucination. Cohomological Consistency Control The reduced embedding space, constructed via cohomological regularization over overlapping local windows, allows defining a graph structure and cochain complex. This quantifies inconsistencies among local inferences as coboundary-based losses. Applying harmonic projection based on Hodge theory positions cohomology as a computable regularization principle for controlling semantic consistency, extracting maximally consistent global representations.

</details>


### [159] [What Iterated Self-Feeding Probes of Language Models Measure, and a test that separates the construction from the model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10986)
*Nicolás Vera Zúñiga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型自我探测方法的度量本质，通过构造"令牌环"（token ring）耦合格劳伯动力学，区分了由构造决定的"运动学"量（如损伤光锥、Lyapunov指数的半径标度，跨19个模型不变）与真正反映模型性质的"动力学"量（如λ_ca的过零点和吸引子占比）。作者指出两类读数看似相似却本质不同，并报告了自己曾将构造性伪相变误认为模型性质的教训，最终给出判别测试与验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一类快速增长的方法通过让语言模型读取自身输出来探测其能力（自一致性、迭代细化、智能体循环）。但这类探测究竟测的是什么并不清楚。作者希望在一个精心构造的"尖锐"设定下回答这个问题，区分哪些测量结果是由探测构造本身决定的（与模型无关），哪些真正反映了语言模型的属性。

Method: 核心方法是令牌环（token ring）构造：在格劳伯动力学下，用模型自身的窗口条件概率p_r(x_i|x_{i±r})对令牌单元进行原位重采样。关键技术改动是耦合方式——采用公共随机数推进两个仅差一个令牌的环，使未损坏副本发散度恰好为零，从而用损伤传播（damage spreading）代替最大耦合下的混合时间。作者提出判别测试：固定构造改变模型，或固定模型改变构造，观察哪些读数移动。验证通过复现Domany-Kinzel损伤场逐位精确匹配独立预测完成。

Result: 两类量被分离：(1) 构造固定的量：损伤光锥是运动学的，token空间Lyapunov指数λ_ca(r)的半径标度在19个模型和跨70x的两个尺度梯子上保持不变；(2) 真正追踪模型的量：λ_ca在训练过程中在可复现的点过零，吸引子占比对格点构建方式稳健地给模型排序。作者报告了自己曾将属于探测构造的伪相变测量到小数点后三位，误认为是模型属性，历时四个月才纠正。判别测试和复现纪律收回了四个错误裁决。

Conclusion: 探测自身输出所测到的东西是两类混合：一类由构造决定（运动学/伪测量），一类真正反映模型（动力学/真实测量）。两者读数相似易混淆。用"固定构造变模型，固定模型变构造"的交叉测试和先复现后测量的纪律，可以将二者分离。方法论已打包发布。

Abstract: A growing class of methods probes a language model by feeding it its own output: self-consistency, iterated refinement, agentic loops. We ask what such a probe measures, in a construction chosen to make the question sharp: a ring of token cells resampled in place by the model's own windowed conditional p_r(x_i | x_{i+-r}). The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences and is not new; what we change is the coupling. Advancing two rings that differ in one token under common random numbers makes undamaged copies diverge by exactly zero, so damage spreading becomes measurable where a maximal coupling gives mixing times instead. The answer is that it measures two different things at once, in readings that look alike. Some quantities are fixed by the construction: the damage light cone is kinematic, and the radius scaling of the token-space Lyapunov exponent lambda_ca(r) is model-invariant across 19 models and two scale ladders spanning 70x. Others genuinely track the model: lambda_ca crosses zero at a reproducible point in training, and the attractor share ranks models consistently however the lattice is built. Left undistinguished, the first kind is readily mistaken for the second -- we did so ourselves for four months, and report a phase transition we measured to three decimal places that belongs to the probe rather than to any language model. We give the test that separates them: hold the construction fixed and vary the model, or hold the model fixed and vary the construction, and see which readings move. We validate the instrument by reproduction first, recovering a Domany-Kinzel damage field bit-exactly against an independent prediction, and we report the estimator failures that this discipline caught -- four retracted verdicts, each on a quantity that looked like a measurement. The methodology ships as a package.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [The Fallacy of Independent Ceilings: Characterizing Coupled Load-Branch Stall Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11380)
*Matthew Constant,Resit Sendag*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究了不规则负载中分支预测错误与缓存缺失之间的交互效应（共生 stall 延迟 SSL），提出 JSS 指标量化联合加速比与独立加速比乘积的偏差，并引入 SSO 轻量级筛选指标及四类软件模式分类，构建了一套测量框架以判断独立上界分析何时失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统性能分析将分支预测错误和数据缓存缺失视为独立瓶颈，用各自完美化(perfect-branch/perfect-cache)的加速比乘积作为联合上界。但在不规则负载中，二者常在同热循环中交互出现：消除一个瓶颈会暴露另一个。这种耦合效应导致独立分析低估联合性能提升空间，需要系统的量化框架来识别这种交互。

Method: 提出三个核心组件：(1) JSS（联合加速比协同度）= 实际联合 perfect-branch/perfect-cache 加速比 ÷ 独立加速比乘积，>1 表示独立上界分析低估；(2) SSO（共生 stall 机会）基于 MPKI 的轻量级筛选指标，用于判断哪些负载值得完整联合仿真；(3) 将高 SSO 负载映射到四类软件模式：邻接访问、哈希查找、链表结构遍历、数据依赖修改，并通过 ROB 占用率、squash rate 和 commit starvation 解释底层机制。

Result: 在 53 个模拟负载中，70% 显示可测量的耦合效应(JSS>1)；40% 超过独立乘积 6% 以上；SSO>20 的内核 JSS 范围为 1.23 到 3.29，即独立分析严重低估可达增益。

Conclusion: 提供了一套实用的测量方法论：用 SSO 筛选、JSS 验证、报告条件性的 branch-after-cache 和 cache-after-branch 增益。该框架明确了独立完美模式分析在何时足够、何时会低估联合性能提升空间，为评估分支预测器、预取器、缓存及分支/内存耦合机制提供了新的评价标准。

Abstract: Branch mispredictions and data-cache misses are usually evaluated as separate bottlenecks: studies report perfect-branch or perfect-cache speedups as isolated upper bounds and often treat their product as the joint ceiling. In irregular workloads, however, hard-to-predict branches and cache-missing loads often occur in the same hot loops. Removing one penalty can expose the other: faster memory reaches mispredicted branches sooner, while better branch prediction leaves more long-latency loads in the out-of-order window. We call this interaction symbiotic stall latency (SSL).
  This paper quantifies when isolated ceilings fail using joint speedup synergy (JSS), the observed joint perfect-branch/perfect-cache speedup divided by the product of the isolated speedups. Values above one mean independent-ceiling analysis understates attainable gain. Across 53 simulated workloads, 70% show measurable coupling (JSS > 1), though many are near unity, especially in lower-pressure cases. With a conservative threshold, 40% exceed the independence product by more than 6%, and kernels with SSO > 20 show JSS from 1.23 to 3.29. We introduce symbiotic stall opportunity (SSO), a lightweight MPKI-based screen for workloads that merit full joint simulation.
  We map high-SSO workloads to four recurring software patterns: neighbor access, hash lookup, linked-structure traversal, and data-dependent modification. We connect SSL to reorder-buffer occupancy, squash rate, and commit starvation under isolated perfect modes. The resulting methodology is simple: use SSO to screen, JSS to validate, and report conditional branch-after-cache and cache-after-branch gains when evaluating branch predictors, prefetchers, caches, or coupled branch/memory mechanisms. Our contribution is a measurement framework showing when isolated perfect modes are adequate and when they understate joint performance headroom.

</details>


### [161] [Uni-SFU: Algorithm-HW Co-Design for Universal SFUs via Mixed-Degree Piecewise Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11577)
*Miao Sun,Yucheng Huang,Mingcong Cao,Jaehyun Park,Partha Pratim Pande,Umit Y. Ogras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Uni-SFU 是一个算法-硬件协同设计框架，通过联合搜索所有目标激活函数、为不均匀分段分配混合次数的多项式，并由RTL级面积成本模型指导，在满足精度约束下找到统一的最优硬件配置，显著降低硅片面积同时保持系统级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性激活函数在深度神经网络中至关重要，但其硬件评估对GPU专用功能单元（SFU）和定制加速器造成很大压力。现有分段多项式逼近技术往往孤立地逐函数近似，采用固定次数多项式和均匀分段，导致硬件冗余和次优精度。因此需要一种联合优化的协同设计方案来同时优化逼近精度与硅片面积。

Method: Uni-SFU 提出一种算法-硬件协同设计框架：1）在多种目标激活函数上进行联合搜索，为不均匀分段分配混合次数多项式；2）利用RTL推导的面积成本模型指导搜索过程；3）在给定精度约束下，识别出实现所有目标激活函数的统一硬件配置。

Result: 在超过700种神经网络变体和3种NLP模型上验证，Uni-SFU 达到优于8.22x10^-8的均方误差（MSE），相对于浮点基线的top-1精度退化限制在1.02%以内。在GF 22nm CMOS工艺下仅占用6800 um2面积，实现了优于SOTA的硅片面积与系统级精度之间的权衡。

Conclusion: Uni-SFU通过联合优化多激活函数的逼近精度与硬件面积，实现了统一硬件配置下多种激活函数的高效实现，在面积效率和系统精度之间取得了优于现有技术的综合表现，证明了算法-硬件协同设计在激活函数硬件加速领域的有效性。

Abstract: Nonlinear activation functions are essential to modern deep neural networks (DNNs), but their hardware evaluation places significant pressure on the special-function units (SFUs) of GPUs and custom accelerators. Therefore, piecewise polynomial approximations are commonly used within allowed error bounds to improve computational efficiency. However, existing techniques often approximate each activation function in isolation using fixed-degree polynomials and uniform segments, leading to hardware redundancy and sub-optimal precision. To address these limitations, we present Uni-SFU, an algorithm-hardware co-design framework that jointly optimizes approximation accuracy and silicon area for a diverse set of activation functions. Uni-SFU leverages a joint search across all target functions to assign mixed-degree polynomials to nonuniform segments, guided by an RTL-derived area cost model. This approach identifies a unified hardware configuration to implement the target activation functions under given accuracy constraints. Validated across over 700 neural network variants and three Natural Language Processing (NLP) models, Uni-SFU achieves a superior Mean Squared Error (MSE) below 8.22x10^-8, limiting top-1 accuracy degradation to within 1.02% compared to floating-point baselines. The proposed design occupies only 6,800 um2 in GF 22nm CMOS technology, achieving a superior trade-off between silicon area and system-level accuracy compared to SOTA counterparts.

</details>


### [162] [A Full-Stack Characterization of High-Bandwidth Flash for KV-Centric LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11668)
*Zhuoran Li,Zhuohang Bian,Xin Huang,Yibo Zhao,Guangyu Sun,Youwei Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文验证了将SSD风格的Mooncake KV-offloading栈后端替换为High-Bandwidth Flash（HBF）的假设，通过扩展TokenSim、四段两小时生产轨迹、五种模型以及H100/B200配置的测试发现，服务性能反而变差（端到端延迟上升2-5.5倍，SLO吞吐率下降1.1-2.7倍），并解释了原因：HBF的收益仅在读I/O为瓶颈、读远多于写且带宽可持续时才成立，而瞬态KV缓存不满足这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HBF在NAND后堆叠宽封装本地接口，相比SSD具有更优的读延迟和带宽，因此作者希望验证"仅将Mooncake KV-offloading的后端替换为HBF"这一看似诱人的假设是否成立。

Method: 使用扩展的TokenSim进行模拟，基于四段真实的Qwen-Bailian两小时生产轨迹，覆盖五种稠密和混合专家模型，并采用H100/B200硬件配置进行端到端性能评估；同时使用3D-ICE热模型分析热限制，并对TLC层寿命与SSD池进行对比。

Result: 相比SSD后端，替换为HBF后平均端到端延迟上升2-5.5倍，最大SLO良好吞吐率下降1.1-2.7倍。服务性能对HBF自身的读写延迟几乎不敏感；基片近存计算也无法提升闪存层在关键路径中的占比。所有轨迹上写请求均多于读请求，HBF面临写密集流并很快触及热极限，且TLC层比同等容量的SSD池更快磨损。

Conclusion: HBF更适合作为服务中的选择性、感知复用、写预算受限且热协调的专用层，而非直接替代SSD的"即插即用"方案。更快的设备反而导致更慢的系统，因为封装层面的代价超过了介质带来的收益。

Abstract: High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) stacks NAND behind a wide, package-local interface, giving flash-scale capacity with far better read latency and bandwidth than an SSD. This makes it tempting to keep an SSD-style Mooncake KV-offloading stack and swap only the backing tier for HBF. We test that substitution with an extended TokenSim, four complete two-hour Qwen-Bailian production traces, five dense and mixture-of-experts models, and H100/B200 profiles. Serving gets worse, not better, and a cost-benefit model explains why. A faster far tier helps only when read I/O is the serving bottleneck, reads outweigh writes, and delivered bandwidth is sustainable. All three must hold together, and transient KV fails every one. The package trade that buys flash costs GPU near-tier capacity and bandwidth, so average end-to-end latency rises 2--5.5x and maximum SLO goodput falls 1.1--2.7x across H100 and B200. Serving is almost insensitive to HBF's own read/write latency, and base-die near-memory compute does not raise the flash tier's share of the critical path. The two-tier hierarchy keeps reuse in the near tier and hands HBF a write-heavy stream, so writes outnumber reads on every trace. A 3D-ICE model shows that stream drives the stack to its thermal limit well below peak bandwidth, and a TLC tier wears out sooner than a capacity-matched SSD pool. The faster device yields a slower system because the package gives up more than the medium returns. HBF is not the problem; using it as a faster SSD for transient KV is. It belongs in serving as a selective, reuse-aware, write-budgeted, and thermally coordinated tier, not as a drop-in SSD replacement.

</details>


### [163] [APEX: Adaptive Expert Prefetching for Memory-Efficient Edge MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11688)
*Alish Kanani,Layan Badawi,Umit Y. Ogras*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: APEX是一个面向边缘部署的MoE（混合专家）模型自适应专家预取框架，通过轻量级预取路由器在注意力块之前预测候选专家并动态预取，实现超过99%的重叠精度，在保证正确性或消除停顿两种模式下分别将每token延迟降低最多26%、能源延迟积（EDP）改善最多41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在边缘部署中虽然计算效率高（每token仅激活少量参数），但受限于内存瓶颈——专家参数体积大且常驻片外内存，导致专家加载成为推理的关键路径瓶颈，严重影响推理延迟。

Method: APEX提出一种预测性资源管理框架，核心包括：(1) 轻量级预取路由器，在注意力块之前预测候选专家；(2) 基于学习的置信度模型动态决定预取哪些额外专家；(3) 两种执行模式——正确性保持模式保证精确路由语义，以及无停顿模式允许使用可用专家消除残余停顿。

Result: APEX在多个MoE模型上实现了超过99%的重叠精度，显著优于固定top-k预取方法。正确性保持模式将每token延迟降低最多26%，EDP改善最多41%；无停顿模式在精度影响可忽略的前提下提供额外的效率增益。

Conclusion: 自适应、置信度驱动的专家预取是边缘系统上高效MoE推理的有效方法，能够在不牺牲准确性的前提下大幅缓解内存带宽瓶颈。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are attractive for edge deployment because they provide high model capacity while activating only a small subset of parameters per token, improving compute efficiency. However, MoE inference at the edge is fundamentally limited by memory. Expert parameters are large and often reside in off-chip memory due to capacity, cost, and power constraints, putting expert loading to the critical path. We present APEX: Adaptive Expert Prefetching, a predictive resource management framework that overlaps expert loading with useful computation. APEX introduces a lightweight prefetch router that predicts candidate experts before the attention block to dynamically fetch additional experts using a learned confidence model. This adaptive strategy achieves over 99% overlap accuracy, significantly outperforming fixed top-k prefetching techniques. APEX supports two execution modes: a correctness-preserving mode that guarantees exact routing semantics, and a stall-free mode that eliminates residual stalls by operating on available experts with negligible impact on application accuracy. Across multiple MoE models, the correctness-preserving mode reduces per-token latency by up to 26% and improves energy-delay product (EDP) by up to 41% over state-of-the-art baselines, while the stall-free mode provides additional efficiency gains with negligible impact on application accuracy. These results establish adaptive, confidence-driven expert prefetching as an effective approach for efficient MoE inference on edge systems.

</details>


### [164] [Spec Sheets Are Not Kernels: An ISA- and Source-Level Audit of INT8 Availability on NVIDIA Blackwell Ultra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11693)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文审计了NVIDIA Blackwell Ultra GPU (B300)上INT8 W8A8量化在实际部署中的可用性，发现虽然规格书声称存在INT8支持，但通过PTX ISA、CUTLASS库、vLLM和SGLang四个堆叠层的追踪，发现INT8支持被系统性撤回：PTX不暴露第五代张量核心整数路径，CUTLASS跳过103a目标的INT8 UMMA生成，vLLM在首次前向传播即崩溃，SGLang的INT8 GEMM仅止于Sm90。仅FP8被完整支持。文章还记录了绕过方法、profiler误判陷阱和故障语义，得出结论：量化格式的可用性是整个技术栈的属性，而非模型或规格书的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA规格书中B300号称FP8与INT8张量核心吞吐比约为30:1（而前代H200和B200均为1:1），这一INT8性能被降级的现象亟需实际验证。作者希望弄清规格书上的这项声明在真实推理栈中意味着什么，即INT8 W8A8支持是否仍然可用，以及不可用时的具体失败模式。

Method: 通过"分层审计"方法追踪INT8 W8A8支持穿透四层技术栈：第一层是发布规格书自身；第二层是PTX ISA（检查sm_103a目标上tcgen05.mma整数路径是否存在）；第三层是NVIDIA的CUTLASS内核库（检查其代码生成器对103a目标的INT8 UMMA支持）；第四层是两个主流开源LLM推理引擎vLLM和SGLang（实测其INT8 GEMM运行行为）。同时记录绕过机制、profiler方法论陷阱及失败语义。

Result: 四个层面的发现：(i) PTX ISA在sm_103a上从不暴露tcgen05.mma的.kind::i8整数路径（尽管同版本PTX为FP4扩展了类型），遗留的warp级IMMA是唯一合法整数张量核心路径；(ii) CUTLASS生成器对103a目标明确跳过INT8 UMMA生成但无条件生成FP8；(iii) vLLM在Blackwell上无INT8 GEMM，模型加载后在第一次前向传播即硬运行时错误；(iv) SGLang的INT8 GEMM止步于Sm90，而其FP8调优配置已覆盖B200。另发现一个逃生通道（通过环境变量将vLLM的INT8路径重路由到JIT编译的Triton后端）、一个sm_103上检测"原生INT8"的profiler方法论假阴性陷阱，以及使朴素测试代价高昂的实际故障语义。

Conclusion: 量化格式的可用性不是模型或规格书的属性，而是整个技术栈的属性。INT8在规格书上名义存在，但默认情况下在此硬件上不可部署。四层中三层由NVIDIA自己控制，却以相互一致的方式撤回INT8支持，而FP8则被完整保留，表明这是有意的架构性降级而非疏忽。

Abstract: NVIDIA's published specifications give the Blackwell Ultra GPU (B300) a dense-compute ratio of roughly 30:1 between FP8 and INT8 tensor-core throughput; its predecessors, H200 and B200, both provide 1:1. We audit what this deprioritization means in practice by tracing INT8 W8A8 support through four layers of the stack: the published specifications, the PTX ISA, NVIDIA's CUTLASS kernel library, and the two major open-source LLM serving engines (vLLM and SGLang). We find a consistent, layered withdrawal: (i) the PTX ISA never exposes the fifth-generation tensor-core integer path (tcgen05.mma with .kind::i8) on sm_103a, even though the same PTX revision extends the FP4 kinds to that target, leaving legacy warp-level IMMA as the only architecturally legal integer tensor-core path on B300; (ii) CUTLASS's kernel generator explicitly skips INT8 UMMA generation for any build targeting 103a, while generating FP8 unconditionally; (iii) vLLM ships no INT8 GEMM for Blackwell and fails with a hard runtime error at the first forward pass, after the model has loaded; and (iv) SGLang's ahead-of-time INT8 GEMM stops at Sm90, while its FP8 tuning configurations already cover B200. We document an escape hatch (rerouting vLLM's INT8 path to a JIT-compiled Triton backend via an environment variable), a false-negative trap in the obvious profiler methodology for detecting "native INT8" on sm_103, and the practical failure semantics that make naive testing expensive. Together, these findings show that a quantization format's availability is a property of the whole stack rather than of the model or the spec sheet. Four distinct layers, three of them NVIDIA's own, withdrew INT8 support in mutually consistent ways, and a format that is nominally present on the datasheet is, by default, undeployable on this hardware.

</details>


### [165] [NITRO: High-Performance 3D NAND Flash-Based In-Storage Computing with Enhanced Activation Dataflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11920)
*Sanghun Shin,Sangyeon Kim,Gisan Ji,Sungju Ryu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于NAND闪存的高性能存储内计算（ISC）架构，通过在快速DRAM子系统中缓冲中间激活值（而非慢速闪存阵列），并引入分布式数据流方案优化NAND-PIM阵列的计算并行性，相比基线可降低高达85%的推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）所需资源近年来急剧增长，而存储密度未能同步扩展。虽然已有研究探索基于NAND闪存的存内处理（NAND-PIM）方案来利用高存储密度，但这些方案未解决中间值的数据流/缓冲问题，通常简单地将中间值存储在极慢的闪存阵列中，导致严重的高延迟开销。

Method: 1) 提出一种具有增强激活缓冲功能的高性能NAND闪存ISC架构，利用快速DRAM子系统缓冲中间激活值，避免将其写入缓慢的TLC NAND闪存。2) 引入面向NAND-PIM阵列的分布式数据流方案，通过高效的面内（intra-plane）数据映射来最大化计算并行性。

Result: 实验结果表明，与基线相比，所提出的架构可将推理延迟降低高达85%，实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过DRAM缓冲中间值和分布式数据流映射，有效克服了NAND-PIM中闪存阵列处理中间值的高延迟限制，为LLM推理提供了高性能的存储内计算解决方案。

Abstract: In-storage computing (ISC) is considered a next-generation memory architecture for its ability to relieve the data bottleneck between the host and the memory. While the required resources of large language models (LLMs) have increased significantly in recent years, the memory density has not scaled accordingly. Recently, several works have studied NAND flash-based processing-in-memory (NAND-PIM) schemes to exploit the high density of the memory. However, they do not address the dataflow/buffer for the intermediate values, so a simple method is to deal with the values in the slow flash memory array. To overcome such a limitation, we propose a high-performance NAND flash-based ISC architecture with enhanced activation buffering. Instead of using the very slow flash memory array for the intermediate values, our architecture buffers the values in a fast DRAM subsystem. This approach effectively handles the high-latency penalties when activations are programmed into slower TLC NAND flash. We also introduce a distributed dataflow approach for the NAND-PIM array. This approach maximizes computational parallelism by employing efficient intra-plane data mapping. The results show that our proposed architecture achieves significant performance improvements, reducing the inference latency by up to 85% compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [166] [FQTree: Fine-grained Quantization and Hardware Generation of Boosted Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12140)
*Zhiqiang Que,Chang Sun,Haiyang Wang,Dinesh Pamunuwa,Roshan Weerasekera,Qijia Tang,Bakhtiar Zadeh,Wayne Luk,Maria Spiropulu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出FQTree算法和QXGB框架，用于对提升决策树（BDT）进行细粒度量化感知训练并自动生成硬件。FQTree采用硬件导向的叶值量化方案（全局量化步长+树级移位），在提升过程中应用量化使后续树适应已量化集成的误差，通过编译器流程将训练模型降至低延迟硬件实现。在JSC、MNIST和NID数据集上，相比最先进的FPGA-BDT设计，LUT使用量减少26-57%，精度持平或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BDT广泛应用于延迟敏感型应用，但现有硬件部署方案通常采用均匀或手动调优的定点格式，导致不必要的硬件成本或精度损失，缺乏自动化的细粒度量化感知训练与硬件生成方法。

Method: FQTree算法提出一种基于全局量化步长和树级移位的叶值量化方案，实现紧凑的非负整数叶表示、可控裁剪/剪枝以及偏置折叠以降低数据通路成本；在提升过程中进行量化，使后续树适应已量化集成的误差；通过基于编译器的QXGB框架将训练模型自动转换为低延迟硬件实现。

Result: 在JSC、MNIST和NID三个数据集上，所提方法相比最先进的基于FPGA的BDT设计将LUT使用量减少26-57%，同时精度与原方案持平或有所提升。

Conclusion: 细粒度量化感知训练与自动硬件生成相结合，可在保持或改善精度的同时显著降低FPGA资源开销，为BDT的硬件高效部署提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Boosted decision trees (BDTs) are widely used in latency-critical applications, but efficient hardware deployment remains challenging. Existing designs often rely on uniform or manually tuned fixed-point formats, which can introduce unnecessary hardware cost or accuracy loss. This work presents the FQTree algorithm{https://github.com/ecs-bristol/FQTree} for fine-grained quantization-aware training of BDTs, together with the QXGB framework for automatic hardware generation. FQTree introduces a hardware-oriented leaf-value quantization scheme that uses a global quantization step together with a tree-wise shift, enabling compact non-negative integer leaf representations, controlled clipping/pruning, and bias folding to reduce datapath cost. This work further applies this quantization during boosting so that later trees adapt to the errors of the already-quantized ensemble, and then lowers the trained model into low-latency hardware implementations through a compiler-based flow. Results on JSC, MNIST, and NID show that our method reduces LUT usage by 26-57\% compared with the state-of-the-art FPGA-based BDT designs while matching or improving accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [Adaptation of Generalist Robot Policies with Minimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11363)
*Shreyas Kowshik,Sreyas Venkataraman,Leo Wang,Niharika Pant,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MiDAS，一种离线到在线的强化学习策略，利用最少一条人类演示（行为克隆锚定）加自主在线交互，将预训练的视觉语言动作（VLA）策略适配到新任务。在LIBERO、RoboCasa及双手机器人YAM平台上验证，MiDAS能从单条演示恢复强任务性能，并在约6小时在线交互中提升鲁棒性，是首个从单条演示实现可靠机器人策略适配的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习的核心目标是从依赖任务特定的人类数据收集转向自主交互学习。但完全自主的强化学习面临稀疏奖励和弱零样本探索的挑战，导致机器人难以从零开始发现成功行为。因此作者研究最小数据适配（minimal-data adaptation）这一机制，作为完全自主改进最接近的可约代理，探索最少的人类指导能否引导自主学习。

Method: MiDAS采用简单的离线到在线强化学习流程：1）首先通过行为克隆（BC）在单条/少量演示上锚定预训练的VLA（视觉语言动作）策略到目标任务；2）然后采用基于残差策略参数化的值函数在线强化学习来进一步提升策略。该流程同时利用预训练知识、单条演示的引导和自主在线交互的持续改进。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboCasa基准上，MiDAS从单条演示即恢复强任务性能，显著优于基线方法，并能泛化到超出演示条件的环境。在双手机器人YAM平台上，从单条演示获得的低成功率脆弱策略出发，MiDAS通过约6小时的在线交互提升了鲁棒性并学习了新的成功行为。

Conclusion: MiDAS证明了最小人类指导（单条演示）足以引导自主强化学习，实现预训练机器人策略对新任务的有效适配。这是首次展示从单条任务演示即可实现可靠机器人策略适配，为迈向全自主机器人学习提供了关键算法基础。

Abstract: A central goal in robot learning is to move beyond task-specific human data collection toward robots that improve through autonomous interaction. Yet fully autonomous learning remains difficult with current policies: sparse rewards and weak zero-shot exploration make it unlikely that a robot will discover successful behavior from scratch. We study minimal-data adaptation, a regime in which a pre-trained robot policy must learn a new task from as little as one demonstration followed by autonomous online interaction. This setting serves as the closest tractable proxy for fully autonomous improvement, allowing us to study whether minimal human guidance can bootstrap autonomous learning and what algorithmic ingredients make it feasible. We build MiDAS, a simple offline-to-online RL recipe that first anchors a pre-trained VLA to the target task with behavior cloning on single/few demonstrations, then improves it through value-based online RL on a residual policy parameterization. Across LIBERO and RoboCasa, MiDAS recovers strong task performance from as little as one demonstration, substantially outperforming baselines and generalizing beyond demonstrated conditions. We further evaluate MiDAS on a bimanual YAM platform. Starting from a fragile low-success policy obtained from a single demonstration, MiDAS improves its robustness and learns new successful behaviors over ~6 hours of online interaction. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of reliable robot policy adaptation from a single task demonstration.

</details>


### [168] [Top-down Traffic Scenario Generation via Joint Initial-Goal Diffusion and Trajectory Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11407)
*Da Saem Lee,Yash Vardhan Pant,Sebastian Fischmeister*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TrafficDiffuser，一个自上而下的交通场景生成框架，通过联合建模初始状态和目标状态对来生成高层交通场景，使初始状态更具可解释性，并将轨迹生成简化为填充问题。在Argoverse 2数据集上的实验表明，该方法在代理初始化和目标状态生成方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交通模拟器需要代理的初始状态来生成轨迹，限制了可扩展性和多样性，且生成的初始状态缺乏可解释性（无法解释为什么代理在特定位置初始化）。同时，轨迹生成也面临目的地可变性和到达时间学习的挑战。

Method: TrafficDiffuser在顶层联合建模"初始状态-目标状态"对来生成高层交通场景，使初始状态更具可解释性，并把轨迹生成降维为简单的填充（infilling）问题。该方法还支持基于不同轨迹模式的约束以及与传统轨迹生成模型的集成。

Result: 在Argoverse 2运动预测数据集上，TrafficDiffuser在生成目标状态的同时，相比次优的代理初始化方法，将速度分布距离降低了55.3%，路外率降低了2.8%。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模初始和目标状态对，TrafficDiffuser为交通场景生成提供了更可解释、更稳健的方案，并展示了其在轨迹生成模型集成方面的灵活性和实用价值。

Abstract: Robust traffic simulators are crucial for developing and testing autonomous vehicles to reduce the costly, labor-intensive real-world data collection process and the need for physical presence on the road. However, existing simulators require agents' initial states to generate trajectories, which limits scalability and diversity due to restrictions on the given initial states. While data-driven agent initialization has been widely studied, the generated initial states are not interpretable in terms of why the agents are initialized at those specific locations. Given known initial states, trajectory generation is also a challenging problem, as the model must learn the variability of the destination and how agents should reach it over time. In this paper, we propose TrafficDiffuser, a top-down traffic scenario generation framework that generates high-level traffic scenarios, defined by initial and goal state pairs, by jointly modeling them. The high-level scenario generation makes initial states better interpretable and reduces trajectory generation into as simple as an infilling problem. We demonstrate how the generated high-level traffic scenarios can be used, including constraining based on different trajectory modes and integrating them with existing trajectory generation models. We conduct extensive experiments on the Argoverse 2 motion prediction dataset to evaluate how well the generated outputs capture real-world distributions. In addition to generating goal states, TrafficDiffuser outperforms the next-best approach for agent initialization, reducing speed distribution distance by 55.3% and the off-road rate by 2.8%.

</details>


### [169] [From Self-Normal-Positioning to Omni-Directional Tracking: Real-Time Surface Modeling Enabled Probe Tilt Control for Robotic Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11409)
*Xihan Ma,Haichong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于机器人超声的万向探头姿控框架，集成RGB-D感知、局部曲面建模和任务空间姿控，使探头能以任意非垂直角度跟踪局部表面，实现诊断性超声视图的获取。实验表明平均角度跟踪误差为1.06±0.66°，可恢复相对法线最大44.39±2.59°的非垂直倾角，并在体模和体内实验中成功获取目标心腔视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人超声系统大多仅支持探头垂直于局部表面的法向定位模式，这在超声心动图等需要非垂直探头角度的检查场景中不适用。临床可用系统需要实时感知局部表面并保持期望的探头朝向。

Method: 提出一个万向探头朝向控制框架，包含三个部分：RGB-D感知融合多视图点云、二次曲面拟合局部表面建模，以及任务空间的探头朝向控制。期望成像方向相对于表面法线进行编码，使探头能够跟踪任意倾斜角度。

Result: 平均角度跟踪误差为1.06±0.66°；系统可恢复相对表面法线高达44.39±2.59°的非垂直倾斜角；在平面跟踪、体模目标角度恢复和体内专家选定视图跟踪实验中均成功获取目标心腔视图。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架证明了机器人超声系统在非垂直探头倾角下仍能保持精确的成像方向跟踪能力，为超声心动图等需要任意探头朝向的临床应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) provides real-time, radiation-free imaging, but the image quality depends strongly on how the probe is oriented against the patient body. Robotic US can reduce operator workload and improve acquisition consistency; however, most existing systems focus on normal positioning, where the probe is maintained perpendicular to the local surface. This constraint is inadequate for examinations like echocardiography, where obtaining a diagnostic view requires a non-normal probe angle. Consequently, a clinically useful robotic system must sense the local surface in real-time and preserve the desired probe orientation. Here, we propose an omni-directional probe-orientation control framework that integrates RGB-D perception, local-surface modeling, and task-space orientation control. The surface model fuses multi-view point clouds and provides a quadratic estimate of the local surface. A desired imaging direction is then encoded relative to the normal, enabling the probe to track arbitrary angles. The framework was evaluated through flat-surface tracking, phantom target-angle recovery, and in-vivo tracking of an expert selected view. Results show that the mean angular tracking error was 1.06 +- 0.66 deg. The system recovered a non-normal tilt angle of up to 44.39 +- 2.59 deg relative to the surface normal, and acquired the desired heart chamber view in the phantom and in-vivo experiments.

</details>


### [170] [Locomotion Variability and User Experience in Smart Wheelchair Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11417)
*Sean Kille,Adina M. Panchea,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了共享控制中不同辅助策略对人类运动变异性、任务性能和主观交互体验的影响，提出了一种保留用户自然运动结构的自主支持型共享控制策略，实验表明该策略在不牺牲任务性能的前提下显著提升了用户感知能动性和感知有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动天然具有变异性，且这种变异性与任务相关性有关（任务关键点更稳定，其他位置更灵活）。然而，HRI中基于模型的辅助策略通常假设人类行为是确定性的并抑制这种变异性，可能导致交互体验受损、降低感知能动性。现有研究虽逐渐认可运动变异性的功能意义，但在辅助交互中主动保留变异性的策略及其对用户体验的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 设计了一种保留用户自然运动结构的自主支持型共享控制策略（variability-preserving assistance），并在用户研究中与无辅助和传统变异性抑制辅助两种条件进行对比。实验场景为参与者推动智能电动轮椅，评估三种条件下的运动变异性、任务性能和主观交互体验（包括感知能动性和感知有用性等指标）。

Result: 在辅助模式下任务相关性能保持可比；与传统的变异性抑制辅助相比，保留自然运动变异性的辅助策略带来了更有利的交互体验——参与者报告了显著更高的感知能动性以及最高的感知有用性。

Conclusion: 变异性感知的辅助策略能够在物理人机协作中同时支持任务性能和用户自主性。更广泛地，研究结果强调辅助机器人系统设计应当尊重人类运动的具体化结构，而非将变异性视为应被忽视或消除的噪声。

Abstract: Human movement is inherently variable, with variability structured according to task relevance: movements are typically more consistent at task-critical points and more flexible elsewhere. In human-robot interaction (HRI), however, model-based assistance strategies commonly assume deterministic human behavior and suppress such variability, potentially altering how interactions are experienced and lowering sense of agency. While movement variability is increasingly recognized as functionally meaningful, its deliberate preservation in assisted interaction, and its consequences for user experience, remain underexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate how different assistance strategies shape human movement variability, task performance, and subjective interaction experience in a shared control setting. We introduce an autonomy-supportive shared control strategy that preserves users' natural movement structure. This approach is evaluated in a user study in which participants push an intelligent powered wheelchair under three conditions: no assistance, conventional variability-reducing assistance, and variability-preserving assistance. While task-relevant performance remained comparable across assisted modes, preserving natural movement variability led to more favorable interaction experiences. In particular, participants reported significantly higher perceived agency compared to conventional assistance and highest perceived usefulness. These findings suggest that variability-aware assistance can support both performance and user autonomy in physical human-robot collaboration. More broadly, the results highlight the importance of designing assistive robotic systems that respect the embodied structure of human movement rather than treating variability as noise to be neglected or eliminated.

</details>


### [171] [Herding End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11451)
*Simón Patiño Idarraga,Erick Silva,Rehana Yasmin,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号安全守卫模块，附着在已训练好的端到端驾驶代理的指令接口上，在执行前根据显式安全规则检查并替换不安全指令，无需重训练。在Fail2Drive和Bench2Drive长尾基准上，使用TransFuser v6进行案例研究，该守卫将成功率提升15%，安全关键碰撞减少53%，同时保持原有驾驶分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代端到端驾驶代理虽然平均性能很高，但仍会违反人类驾驶员绝不会犯的基本交通规则。根本原因在于这些代理学习的是统计模式而非保证安全驾驶的物理条件，导致决策过程不透明且安全约束无法强制执行。

Method: 提出一个轻量级神经符号安全守卫模块，附着在已训练代理的最终命令接口上。在命令到达车辆前，基于显式安全规则对命令进行检查，仅在必要时将其替换为最近的安全替代方案。守卫不包含任何学习组件，无需重训练，每次干预都可直接执行并追溯到触发它的规则。

Result: 在长尾基准Fail2Drive和Bench2Drive上，以SOTA模型TransFuser v6作为案例研究，该守卫将成功率提升15%，安全关键碰撞减少最多53%，同时保持原始驾驶分数不变。

Conclusion: 神经符号安全守卫通过显式规则约束端到端代理的输出，在不牺牲驾驶性能的前提下有效增强安全性和决策可解释性，为自动驾驶安全提供了一种轻量且可追溯的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern end-to-end driving agents can achieve high average performance yet still violate basic traffic rules that a human driver would never miss. The reason is structural: they learn statistical patterns rather than the physical conditions that guarantee safe driving, leaving their decision-making process opaque and safety constraints unenforced. We introduce a neuro-symbolic safety guard, a lightweight module that attaches to the final command interface of an already-trained agent. Immediately before a command reaches the vehicle, it checks the command against explicit safety rules and, only when necessary, replaces it with the nearest safe alternative. Each intervention is directly executable and traceable to the rule that triggered it, while the guard itself requires no retraining and adds no learned component. Evaluated on the long-tail benchmarks Fail2Drive and Bench2Drive using the state-of-the-art TransFuser v6 (TFv6) as a case study, the guard improves Success Rate by 15% and reduces safety-critical collisions by up to 53%, while preserving the original Driving Score.

</details>


### [172] [Koopman Representation of Nonlinear Virtual Environments in Kinesthetic Haptic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11461)
*Yanting Zhou,Jozsef Kövecses,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Koopman算子表示非线性虚拟环境（VE）以实现力觉渲染，并通过仿真的Duffing振荡器VE实验及多用户研究验证了其有效性；同时基于该表示进行闭环稳定性分析，相比传统基于无源性的方法保守性更低，且对建模不确定性更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多需要高精度力反馈的应用中，非线性虚拟环境的力觉渲染至关重要，但传统的表示与稳定性分析方法（如基于无源性的方法）存在保守性过高等局限性。

Method: 利用Koopman算子将非线性虚拟环境的动力学线性化表示为提升状态（lifted states）的线性组合，并将其应用于与力觉系统交互的表示中；基于该Koopman表示对整体力觉系统进行闭环（CL）稳定性分析。

Result: 仿真和实验结果均表明该方法能有效表示Duffing振荡器VE的非线性动力学，且多用户研究进一步证实了该结论；闭环稳定性分析相比传统无源性方法更不保守，且由于采用所有提升状态的线性组合表示非线性，对力觉设备建模不确定性具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Koopman算子为非线性虚拟环境提供了一种新颖的表示方式，既便于闭环稳定性分析，又提升了对建模不确定性的鲁棒性，在力觉渲染领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Rendering haptic feedback with nonlinear virtual environments (VEs) is important in many applications that require highly accurate force feedback. This paper considers the use of the Koopman operator to represent a nonlinear VE interacting with a haptic system. Simulation and experimental results demonstrated that the proposed method provides an effective representation of the nonlinear dynamics of a Duffing-oscillator VE. A multi-user study further confirmed this conclusion. In addition, a closed-loop (CL) stability analysis is performed leveraging the Koopman representation of the nonlinear VE to access stability of the overall haptic system. This alternative way of representing nonlinear VEs enables a convenient CL stability analysis that is less conservative than traditional passivity-based methods. Since a linear combination of all lifted states is used to represent the nonlinearity, such representation is also more robust to uncertainties in the modeling of the haptic device than a traditional nonlinear model.

</details>


### [173] [Keep the Future, Drop the Rollout: RIFT for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11521)
*Chushan Zhang,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RIFT（Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens），通过学习前瞻标记在一次骨干网络前向传播中构建完整的未来K/V缓存，从而在部署时免除迭代视频生成的延迟。在LIBERO和RoboTwin 2.0基准上，RIFT在保持高成功率的同时将动作块延迟降低68.2%-89.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过预测未来状态来调节机器人动作，但部署时迭代式视频回放（iterative video rollout）会引入显著的延迟。作者质疑：动作生成是否真的需要完整的rollout轨迹，还是仅需要其未来表示（K/V缓存）即可？这一问题旨在消除部署时的迭代视频生成开销。

Method: 首先通过在四个WAMs、40个LIBERO任务上进行配对闭环干预实验，证明模型对K/V缓存的数值及其位置赋值敏感。随后发现Joint和Cosmos-2可以用一个固定的最终干净K/V缓存回放几乎无损地维持执行。基于这一分离（缓存消费vs缓存生产），提出RIFT：使用学习到的anticipation tokens在一次backbone前向中构建完整的未来K/V缓存，同时保留原始的未来读取接口。

Result: 在LIBERO上，RIFT达到98.8%成功率，接近基于rollout的Joint、IDM和LingBot-VA（98.4%-98.6%），同时将动作块延迟降低68.2%-89.1%。在RoboTwin 2.0上，RIFT在干净/随机场景分别达到92.9%/92.6%成功率，为所有评估方法中最高。此外，固定K/V缓存回放产生1.7-1.9cm末端执行器平均位移误差和97.9%-98.2%的成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，未来条件化可以在部署时无需迭代视频生成即可实现，通过将rollout过程从部署阶段移除（仅保留在训练/构造阶段），大幅降低延迟而不牺牲成功率，支持免回放的未来条件化范式。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) condition robot actions on predicted futures, but iterative video rollout increases deployment latency. We ask whether action generation requires the evolving rollout trajectory or only its future representation. Across four WAMs on all 40 LIBERO tasks, paired closed-loop interventions show that masking or reassigning future-cache values changes execution and reduces success, indicating sensitivity to future values and their assigned positions. For Joint and Cosmos-2, however, replaying one fixed final-clean key/value (K/V) cache nearly preserves unmodified execution, with $1.7$ to $1.9$~cm end-effector average displacement error and $97.9\%$ to $98.2\%$ success. This separates cache consumption from production: these models can reuse a fixed cache but still require iterative rollout to construct it. We therefore propose RIFT (\emph{Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens}), which uses learned anticipation tokens to construct a complete future K/V cache in one backbone pass while retaining the original future-read interface. On LIBERO, RIFT achieves $98.8\%$ success, close to rollout-based Joint, IDM, and LingBot-VA at $98.4\%$ to $98.6\%$, while reducing action-chunk latency by $68.2\%$ to $89.1\%$. On RoboTwin~2.0, RIFT reaches $92.9/92.6\%$ on clean/randomized scenes, the highest observed among the evaluated methods. These results support rollout-free future conditioning without iterative video generation at deployment.

</details>


### [174] [RoadWeaver: Large-Scale Lane-Level HD Map Generation from Scratch for Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11580)
*Yueyuan Li,Zexi Chen,Weijie Xi,Mingyang Jiang,Songan Zhang,Hanyang Zhuang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoadWeaver是一个从粗到细的框架，用于从零生成多样化、大规模的自动驾驶仿真所需的车道级高精地图（HD maps），在生成速度和端到端对齐精度上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真需要多样化且可扩展的车道级高精地图来支持复杂路网上的长时程评估。现有方法要么依赖手工构建或重建的真实地图（限制可扩展性），要么只能生成局部道路结构而非完整高精地图，无法满足大规模仿真需求。

Method: RoadWeaver采用粗到细（coarse-to-fine）的三阶段框架：首先合成全局道路布局，然后将其扩展为连通的道路网络，最后构建具有拓扑一致车道连通性的车道级几何结构。

Result: RoadWeaver实现了99.8%的可达率、10.7%的死端比例和0.24米的端点点对齐误差。与SOTA生成方法相比，端点点对齐误差降低94.4%，且生成完整高精地图仅需1.39-3.50秒。

Conclusion: 生成的地图可直接部署到驾驶仿真器中，为未来自动驾驶系统的闭环评估提供可扩展的仿真环境。训练代码和开箱即用的实现将在论文被接收后发布。

Abstract: Autonomous driving simulation requires diverse and scalable lane-level HD maps to support long-horizon evaluation across complex road networks. Existing approaches either rely on handcrafted or reconstructed real-world maps, which limits scalability, or generate only local road structures rather than complete HD maps. We present RoadWeaver, a coarse-to-fine framework for from-scratch generation of diverse, large-scale HD maps. RoadWeaver first synthesizes a global road layout, expands it into a connected road network, and then constructs lane-level geometry with topologically consistent lane connectivity. Experimental results show that RoadWeaver achieves a 99.8\% reachability, a 10.7\% dead-end ratio, and an endpoint alignment error of 0.24 m. Compared with SOTA generation methods, it reduces endpoint alignment error by 94.4\% while generating complete HD maps in 1.39--3.50 s. The generated maps can be directly deployed in driving simulators, providing scalable simulation environments for future closed-loop evaluation of autonomous driving systems. The training code and an out-of-the-box implementation of RoadWeaver will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [175] [Video2Track: From Real-World Interaction Videos to Steerable Adversarial Closed-Track Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11592)
*Mengjie Tian,Xinrui Zhang,Tianyu Li,Peizhi Zhang,Guirong Zhou,Haojie Feng,Junpeng Huang,Qixiang Zhang,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Video2Track框架，将真实世界驾驶视频中的交互场景通过视觉语言模型和检索增强生成提取语义并映射到封闭赛道拓扑上，再借助条件扩散模型和Stackelberg博弈生成可操控的对抗性多智能体测试场景，实现真实且可控的自动驾驶系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有封闭赛道测试依赖标准化协议或预定轨迹，导致交互过于脚本化，难以复现公共道路交通的自然复杂性，缺乏可操控且真实的场景再现能力。

Method: 框架由两个紧密耦合的模块组成：1) 场景语义映射模块——利用视觉语言模型从驾驶视频中提取结构化语义，并通过检索增强生成将其落地到封闭赛道拓扑库中，识别兼容的地图片段和交互锚点；2) 动态交互测试模块——基于落地拓扑和锚点，通过条件扩散模型生成多样化的多智能体轨迹，并利用带参数化对抗目标的Stackelberg博弈调节交互强度。

Result: 封闭赛道实验表明，该框架能够忠实复现代表性的真实世界交互场景，并生成具有可控风险等级和交互风格的可执行场景变体。

Conclusion: Video2Track为现实且可操控的ADS验证提供了一种可扩展的方法，弥合了真实道路交互复杂性与封闭赛道可重复测试之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Closed-track testing plays a fundamental role in the verification and validation of automated driving systems (ADS), particularly for safety-critical scenarios, by enabling reproducible evaluation under controlled conditions. However, most existing approaches still rely on standardized protocols or predefined trajectories, leading to overly scripted interactions and limited ability to reproduce the natural complexity of public-road traffic. To address this limitation, we propose Video2Track, a framework that transfers real-world interactive driving scenarios from videos into steerable adversarial closed-track testing. The framework consists of two tightly coupled modules. The first is a scenario semantic mapping module, which extracts structured semantics from driving videos using a vision-language model and grounds them onto a closed-track topology library via retrieval-augmented generation, thereby identifying compatible map segments and interaction anchors. The second is a dynamic interactive testing module, which conditions on the grounded topology and anchors to generate diverse multi-agent trajectories through a conditional diffusion model, while regulating interaction intensity via a Stackelberg game with a parameterized adversarial objective. Closed-track experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can faithfully reproduce representative real-world interaction scenarios and generate executable scenario variants with controllable risk levels and interaction styles, providing a scalable approach for realistic and steerable ADS validation.

</details>


### [176] [IoT-Enabled Autonomous Maritime Navigation in Smart Ports: A Curriculum-Guided Shared Policy Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11597)
*Yuqing Lin,Rangya Zhang,Kum Fai Yuen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对智能港口中物联网自主海事设备在部分可观测和密集交通条件下的船上自主导航问题，提出了一种课程引导的强化学习框架，采用共享循环策略增强时间推理、部署可扩展性和边缘决策鲁棒性。集中训练作为离线设计策略，导航动作完全在船上执行。在多个真实港口环境中的仿真表明，该方法在导航可靠性、碰撞规避和训练稳定性方面优于基线方法，并能泛化到未见的高密度场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能港口基础设施日益依赖由物联网支持的自主海事设备，在拥堵水道中确保可靠的船上导航智能已成为安全、可扩展运行的关键挑战，尤其是在部分可观测性和密集交通条件下。

Method: 提出课程引导的强化学习框架，包含共享循环策略（recurrent policy）以增强时间推理能力；采用集中式训练作为离线设计策略，导航动作完全在船上执行，符合物联网边缘智能范式。

Result: 在多个真实港口环境的广泛仿真中，所提方法在导航可靠性、碰撞规避和训练稳定性方面均优于标准基线方法，且能有效泛化到先前未见的高密度场景。

Conclusion: 课程引导的共享学习为智能港口运营中物联网自主海事设备的可扩展部署提供了实用解决方案，有望支撑安全、可扩展的港口自主导航应用。

Abstract: As smart port infrastructures increasingly rely on autonomous maritime devices enabled by the Internet of Things (IoT), ensuring reliable onboard navigation intelligence has become a critical challenge for safe and scalable operations in congested waterways. This paper investigates onboard autonomous navigation for such IoT devices under partial observability and dense traffic conditions. A curriculum-guided reinforcement learning framework with a shared recurrent policy is developed to enhance temporal reasoning, deployment scalability, and robustness of edge-level decision-making. Centralized training is adopted as an offline design-time strategy, while all navigation actions are executed fully onboard, consistent with IoT edge intelligence paradigms. Extensive simulations in multiple realistic port environments demonstrate that the proposed approach improves navigation reliability, collision avoidance, and training stability compared with standard baseline methods, and generalizes effectively to previously unseen high-density scenarios. The results indicate that curriculum-guided shared learning provides a practical solution for scalable deployment of IoT-enabled autonomous maritime devices in smart port operations.

</details>


### [177] [StellaVLA: In-Context Structured Demonstration for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11671)
*Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Zijian Wang,Dihao Zhu,Chenghao Xia,Chengbin Du,Daochang Liu,Tao Huang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StellaVLA是一个测试时自适应框架，通过利用单个检索到的示例子例，将原始轨迹自动转换为结构化解说（任务规划、子目标描述、3D运动语言化），作为上下文指导帮助策略理解任务而非模仿像素轨迹，从而在不额外收集数据的情况下适应分布外场景，并在VLA-Arena、LIBERO及真实机器人基准上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在分布外场景（视觉、视角或物体变化）下性能显著下降，而传统适应方式需要额外收集数据和微调，成本高昂且效率低下。

Method: (1) 自动化离线流水线将原始轨迹转换为结构化演示（任务计划、子目标描述、语言化3D运动），零人工标注；(2) 将结构化演示作为上下文指导注入策略，使其推理任务意图而非模仿像素轨迹，从而具备跨本体可迁移性；(3) 并行双训练设计，通过联合动作-语言目标在训练中内化推理能力，推理时仅使用动作专家以保证实时高频控制。
