Result: 在VLA-Arena排行榜上以0.63总成绩位居第一（对比π0.5为0.44、LingBot-VLA为0.22）；LIBERO平均成功率98.8%，LIBERO-Plus成功率85.1%；真实机器人基准验证可支持人/机器演示及人-机器人XR演示作为上下文结构化演示。

Conclusion: 通过"传达为什么"而非"模仿做什么"的结构化演示设计，StellaVLA实现了零训练成本的测试时跨本体自适应，显著优于现有VLA模型在分布外场景下的表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can follow instructions and manipulate objects, but their performance often collapses out of distribution (OOD), when the scene, viewpoint, or object differs from training. Adapting to each new situation typically requires collecting more data and fine-tuning. We present StellaVLA, a framework that instead adapts at test time by conditioning on a single retrieved demonstration. The key idea is to move beyond imitating what an expert did and instead convey why: an automated offline pipeline converts each raw trajectory into a structured demonstration, e.g., a task plan, sub-goal descriptions, and verbalized 3D motion, at zero human-annotation cost. Provided as in-context guidance, this structured demonstration lets the policy reason about the task rather than mimic a pixel trajectory, which also makes it transferable across embodiments (real-robot, human-hand, or XR demonstrations). A parallel dual-training design internalizes this reasoning during training through a joint action-and-language objective, while inference uses the action expert alone, preserving real-time, high-frequency control with no added latency. On the VLA-Arena leaderboard(Aug 1, 2026), StellaVLA ranks first with an overall score of 0.63, versus 0.44 and 0.22 for the strong prior models ($π_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA), and it further leads on LIBERO with 98.8% average success rate and LIBERO-Plus with 85.1% success rate. Our real-robot benchmark demonstrates that StellaVLA can use both human/robot demos and human-to-robot (XR) demos as in-context structured demonstration to help VLA model adapt to OOD tasks.

</details>


### [178] [ContactIPM: A Structure-Exploiting Interior-Point Solver for Contact-Implicit Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11731)
*Yucheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactIPM是一种结合了接触隐式轨迹优化与原始-对偶一致性的原对偶方法，通过屏障耦合弹性内点松弛处理互补约束，并利用Riccati递归求解简化的牛顿系统，在多个基准测试中比现有接触专用求解器CRISP和IMPACT更快或更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触隐式轨迹优化可避免预设接触序列，但产生的带互补约束的数学规划（MPCCs）因退化性对传统原始-对偶求解器构成挑战。现有接触专用方法虽改善了对退化性的鲁棒性，但未充分利用阶段最优控制因式分解和原始-对偶一致性；而利用结构的优化控制求解器又不能处理互补约束。因此需要将这两方面能力结合到单一方法中。

Method: ContactIPM方法：（1）识别互补不等式对；（2）通过屏障耦合弹性内点松弛嵌入这些约束；（3）逐步消除松弛变量和对偶变量；（4）使用Riccati递归求解缩减的牛顿系统；（5）采用固定的多阶段MPCC恢复调度，从朴素初始化提供四种延拓和重启尝试；（6）以未松弛的物理互补残差作为终止判据。

Result: 在四个固定的CRISP基准案例上，ContactIPM比CRISP快2.17-8.87倍（20次配对计时重复），并在Push Box和Push-T鲁棒性测试套件上获得更高成功率。对比IMPACT，ContactIPM在Push T上快2.96倍、Cart Transport上快4.91倍，但在Push Box上慢4.46倍。

Conclusion: ContactIPM成功地将阶段最优控制因式分解与原始-对偶一致性融入接触隐式优化，在多数基准上超越现有接触专用求解器。尽管在个别案例（Push Box）上速度不及IMPACT，但整体展示了在MPCC求解中结合结构利用能力与退化鲁棒性的可行性和优势。

Abstract: Contact-implicit trajectory optimization avoids prescribing contact sequences, but yields mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) whose degeneracy challenges conventional primal--dual solvers. Existing contact-specific methods improve robustness to this degeneracy but do not leverage a stagewise optimal-control factorization and primal--dual consistency, while structure-exploiting optimal-control solvers are not designed for complementarity constraints. We show that these capabilities can be combined in a single primal--dual method. ContactIPM identifies complementary inequality pairs, embeds them through a barrier-coupled elastic interior relaxation, eliminates slack and dual variables stagewise, and solves the reduced Newton system using a Riccati recursion. A fixed multi-phase MPCC recovery schedule provides four continuation and restart attempts from naive initializations, while termination is gated by the unrelaxed physical complementarity residual.
  We compare ContactIPM with two contact-specific MPCC solvers, CRISP and IMPACT, using matched benchmark conditions and common post-solve acceptance criteria. On four fixed CRISP benchmark cases, ContactIPM is $2.17$--$8.87\times$ faster over 20 paired timing repetitions per case and achieves higher success on the Push Box and Push-T robustness suites. Against IMPACT, ContactIPM is \(2.96\times\) faster on Push T and \(4.91\times\) faster on Cart Transport, but \(4.46\times\) slower on Push Box. In 50 closed-loop Push Box rollouts spanning model mismatch, measurement noise, initial-pose errors, and state resets,

</details>


### [179] [G0.5: One Autoregressive Stream for Robot Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11739)
*Yicheng Liu,Zibin Dong,Baijun Ye,Tianyuan Yuan,Tao Jiang,Anqi Yang,Shicheng Cao,Haonan Liu,Yue Sun,Zihan Guo,Xiao Liu,Dong Ke,Changxun Pan,Chenru Wu,Tailai Cheng,Xiaoshu Ren,Xinlei Zhang,Jianning Cui,Zijie Zhao,Haoyu Zhang,Kaiming Xu,Haodong Yang,Bowen Zhang,Jiahui Niu,Shaoting Zhu,Shiduo Zhang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出G0.5，一个预训练的自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过单一Transformer解码器在单一目标下同时生成推理token和动作token，克服了传统VLA将VLM仅作为上下文编码器的局限。与将VLM与独立动作专家耦合的常规方案不同，G0.5让模型自身成为决策者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLA模型（如π0.5、GR00T）的通用配方是将预训练VLM与单独训练的flow-matching动作专家耦合，这使得VLM仅充当上下文编码器而非真正的决策者，限制了预训练语言-视觉知识向物理行为的迁移。作者旨在构建一种统一的、自回归的VLA架构，使推理与动作共享同一套权重和优化目标。

Method: G0.5包含三个关键组件：1）可学习的跨具身（cross-embodiment）动作分词器，将异构机器人动作映射为共享词表；2）原生思维链（chain-of-thought）流，将任务分解、目标定位和动作提示与动作token交错生成；3）视觉记忆模块，通过视觉编码器注入多秒级历史信息。模型在大型机器人数据集和VQA样本上联合预训练，使推理与动作在同一目标下联合优化。

Result: G0.5在7个独立基准上均超越SOTA：真实机器人微调（R1lite/R1pro，76.7%对比π0.5的53.3%和GR00T-N1.7的24.4%）；2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge 50个长时程家居移动操作任务（31.4%对比26.3%/26.1%）；DROID后训练后零样本迁移至未见环境（82.5%）；LIBERO语言跟随Pick-and-Place（98.9%）；RoboTwin 2.0（93.3%）；SimplerEnv-Bridge（87.3%）。此外，仅通过prompt即可控制动作粒度、任务时域和分布外场景处理，无需额外训练。

Conclusion: G0.5验证了"推理与动作共享单一权重"的自回归VLA范式在大模型规模下的可行性，展示了VLM预训练能力可完整迁移至物理行为控制，并证明prompt可直接调控行为属性（如动作粒度、任务时域），为该方向提供了新的架构基线和更强的泛化能力。

Abstract: The prevailing recipe for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models couples a pretrained VLM with a separately trained flow-matching action expert. This makes the VLM a context encoder rather than a decision-maker. We introduce G0.5, a pretrained autoregressive VLA in which a single transformer decoder emits reasoning and action tokens under a single objective. Three components make this tractable at foundation-model scale: a learnable cross-embodiment action tokenizer that maps heterogeneous robot actions into a shared vocabulary; a native chain-of-thought stream interleaving task decomposition, object grounding, and action hints with action tokens; and a visual memory module that injects multi-second history through the vision encoder. Because reasoning and action share a single set of weights, the pretrained VLM's capabilities carry over to physical behavior: the model follows instructions closely, and prompts directly steer action granularity, task horizon, and out-of-distribution scene handling without further training. Pretrained on a large collection of robot datasets together with VQA samples, G0.5 surpasses state-of-the-art models across 7 independent regimes: real-world fine-tuning on R1lite and R1pro robots (76.7\% vs.\ 53.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 24.4\% for GR00T-N1.7), the 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge on 50 long-horizon household mobile manipulation tasks using a generalist policy (31.4\% vs.\ 26.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 26.1\% for the challenge winner), DROID post-training followed by zero-shot transfer to an unseen environment and objects (82.5\%), a language-following Pick-and-Place benchmark, LIBERO (98.9\%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.3\%), and SimplerEnv-Bridge (87.3\%).

</details>


### [180] [Policy-Induced Hand Priors in Humanoid Dual-Arm Manipulation: Diagnosing and Mitigating Initial-Pose Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11769)
*Chaeyeon Jung,Juyoun Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于VLA的人形双臂操作任务中初始姿态对策略性能的影响，提出HandPriorScore等指标量化策略固有的手部偏好先验，并在17种初始配置和多种策略下验证了初始姿态与策略之间存在强交互作用，同时发现数据覆盖和训练构成能显著改善初始姿态鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略期望在机器人初始配置变化下鲁棒运行，但聚合任务成功率会掩盖特定姿态下的失败以及手部选择不当的问题。现有评估往往忽略初始姿态对策略手部选择行为的影响，缺乏对策略固有手部先验的系统性量化方法。

Method: 提出三种量化指标：HandPriorScore（策略诱导的早期手部偏好先验评分）、残余手部偏差（residual hand bias）和目标响应度（target responsiveness）。在17种初始配置和多种策略上系统评估初始姿态-策略交互作用，并通过扩展训练数据覆盖、针对低性能配置的定向增强以及调整训练构成（真实数据/辅助数据比例、仿真比例、观测可用性）来研究鲁棒性改善。

Result: 初始姿态与策略间存在强交互：同一姿态在不同策略上的成功率差异显著，单一策略在不同姿态下性能波动大。特定初始臂部配置可抑制或诱导不对称手部偏好，方向与强度因策略而异。腕部相机观测也会影响手部选择与任务性能。扩展初始姿态覆盖显著提升鲁棒性，定向增强低性能配置可提升其成功率。目标仿真任务的充分暴露有益，而真实或辅助数据的效果取决于姿态覆盖、仿真比例和观测可用性。

Conclusion: 研究工作刻画了姿态条件下的手部先验，识别出局部初始臂部配置作为影响手部选择行为的因果手柄，并展示了数据覆盖与训练组成如何影响初始姿态鲁棒性，为VLA策略在双臂操作中的部署提供了可操作的改进方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are expected to operate robustly across variations in the robot's initial configuration, yet aggregate task success can conceal pose-specific failures and inappropriate hand selection. This work investigates initial-pose dependence in VLA-based humanoid dual-arm manipulation. We characterize the initial-condition-dependent early hand preference as a policy-induced hand prior and quantify it using HandPriorScore, residual hand bias, and target responsiveness. Evaluations across multiple policies and 17 initial configurations reveal strong initial-pose--policy interactions: the same pose produces substantially different success rates across policies, while a single policy exhibits large performance variation across poses. Specific initial arm configurations can suppress or induce an asymmetric hand preference, with the resulting effect varying in direction and strength across policies. Wrist-camera observations also influence hand selection and task performance. Expanding initial-pose coverage in the training dataset substantially improves robustness, while targeted augmentation around a low-performing configuration increases its success rate. Comparisons across training configurations show that sufficient exposure to the target simulation task is beneficial, whereas the effect of real or auxiliary data depends on pose coverage, simulation ratio, and observation availability. These findings characterize a pose-conditioned hand prior, identify a localized initial arm configuration as a causal handle on hand-selection behavior, and demonstrate how data coverage and training composition affect initial-pose robustness.

</details>


### [181] [Enhancing Visual Domain Robustness in Behaviour Cloning via Saliency-Guided Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11870)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nils Ingelhag,Ville Kyrki,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboSaGA，一种面向视觉行为克隆的显著性引导叠加增强方法，通过策略驱动的显著性在像素级别动态调整增强强度，在保持任务关键信息的同时对非关键区域进行强增强，显著提升模型对视觉域偏移的鲁棒性，同时不损失域内性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像增强方法（如随机裁剪、颜色抖动）在面对剧烈视觉域偏移（如阴影、干扰物、背景变化）时泛化能力不足；叠加类增强在计算机视觉领域虽有潜力，但直接用于行为克隆会破坏任务关键语义、时空关系和智能体-目标交互。

Method: RoboSaGA属于叠加类增强方法，利用策略驱动的显著性图在像素级别动态调节增强强度：对任务无关区域进行激进增强，对任务关键区域进行保守处理。该方法无需修改网络结构或增加额外学习目标，可无缝集成到现有行为克隆架构中。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，RoboSaGA在保持域内性能的同时，显著提升了对视觉域偏移（包括干扰物、背景变化，以及光照和阴影变化）的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RoboSaGA作为叠加类增强方法，成功将显著性引导引入行为克隆的数据增强中，在域内与域外泛化之间取得了良好平衡，为视觉行为克隆的可泛化训练提供了有效且即插即用的方案。

Abstract: In vision-based behavior cloning (BC), conventional image augmentations such as Random Crop and Color Jitter often fall short under substantial visual domain shifts, including changes in shadows, distractors, and backgrounds. Superimposition-based augmentations, which blend in-domain and out-of-domain images, have shown promise for improving generalization in computer vision, but their suitability for BC remains uncertain because task-critical semantics, spatiotemporal relationships, and agent-target interactions must be preserved. To address this, we introduce RoboSaGA, a Saliency-Guided Augmentation method within the superimposition family tailored for vision-based BC. RoboSaGA dynamically adjusts augmentation intensity at the pixel level using policy-driven saliency, enabling aggressive augmentation in task-irrelevant regions while preserving task-critical information. It integrates seamlessly into existing architectures without requiring structural modifications or additional learning objectives. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings show that RoboSaGA preserves in-domain performance while substantially improving robustness to visual domain shifts, including distractor and background changes, as well as lighting and shadow variations. Code is available at https://github.com/Zheyu-Zhuang/RoboSaGA.

</details>


### [182] [D3D-GEN: Robot-Aware Domain-Grounded Interactive 3D World Generation for Social Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11876)
*Anh Duc Do,Volodymyr Scherbyna,Tai Duc Nguyen,Spaarsh Thakkar,Zhengcheng Shen,Teham Buiyan,Archan Misra,Linh Kästner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D3D-GEN 是一个结合领域代理与检索增强生成（RAG）管道的世界生成系统，能够通过自动化领域知识收集和场景合成，快速生成可完全交互的3D仿真世界，无需依赖固定3D模型数据库，并可直接加载到 Isaac Sim 和 Gazebo 等模拟器中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身AI社会导航的训练与验证高度依赖真实且可模拟的仿真环境，但现有方法难以在真实感与可模拟性之间取得平衡。

Method: 系统包含一个研究代理（research agent），根据领域描述提示自动收集公共领域数据并构建持久化的领域数据库；随后RAG管道通过动态查询用户提供的可扩展语义数据库生成合理的平面图和物体布局，最终输出为Isaac Sim和Gazebo可加载的完全交互式3D世界。

Result: 已为多个常见领域（室内住宅、医院、办公室）构建了数据库，并为每个领域生成数十个不同的合理仿真环境，同时提供了本地Web前端以支持快速交互式世界生成。

Conclusion: D3D-GEN通过领域知识自动收集与RAG驱动的场景合成，实现了领域可扩展、数据库可修改、真实感与可模拟性兼顾的3D世界生成方案，为机器人仿真提供了一条高效且灵活的路径。

Abstract: Training and validation of Embodied AI for social navigation critically depends on realistic simulation environments, yet many current approaches fail to find a balance between realism and simulability. We propose D3D-GEN, a novel world generation system that combines a domain agent with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in that domain. Our system enables users to rapidly generate domain-grounded, fully interactive 3D worlds by automating both the collection of domain knowledge and the synthesis of realistic floorplans and object placements, without dependence on any fixed 3D model database. Given a domain description prompt, the research agent collects publicly accessible domain-specific data and constructs a persistent domain database. Using this database, our RAG pipeline generates plausible floorplans and object placements by dynamically querying a user-provided semantic database, which can be easily extended or modified. The output is a fully interactive 3D world loadable by the popular simulators Isaac Sim and Gazebo. With our approach, we have built databases for several common domains (indoor residential, hospital, office) and generated dozens of distinct, plausible simulation environments for each domain. We present D3D-GEN with a local web frontend that facilitates rapid, interactive world generation for robot simulation.

</details>


### [183] [Scalable Multi-Agent Maze Traversal with Local Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11895)
*Julian Rau,Jahir Argote-Gerald,Grace McFassel,Genki Miyauchi,Paul Trodden,Roderich Groß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式多智能体算法，使多个智能体能够在通信受限的未知迷宫中集体导航并到达未公开的目标节点，通过领导者-跟随者关系与领导者切换实现协作，理论上证明算法完备性、渐近最优性，并通过仿真验证其在平均燃料消耗和性能上优于朴素基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洞穴网络、管道系统等迷宫环境对未知环境下、通信受限的多智能体导航构成重大挑战。现有方法难以同时保证探索的完备性、通信开销可控和协作效率，需要一种能在未知且可能含环的图上让多个智能体协同完成任务的有效算法。

Method: 提出一种分布式算法：智能体在指定起始节点依次进入，通过局部通信建立领导者-跟随者关系并支持领导者切换；任意时刻仅有一个智能体执行探索（运行单智能体迷宫求解器），其余智能体跟随或等待；系统在只有单智能体探索的基础上叠加多智能体协作机制，避免碰撞并共享信息。

Result: 证明算法完备（保证能找到目标），其makespan在智能体数量增长时渐近等价于拥有全局信息的最优策略；推导了时间和空间复杂度。仿真实验（最多625个智能体）表明随智能体数量增加，平均燃料总和（sum-of-fuels）下降，且该方法优于所有智能体独立运行单智能体求解器的朴素基线。

Conclusion: 该分布式算法在未知迷宫环境中实现了多智能体的高效协作导航，兼具完备性、接近全局最优的时间效率和可扩展性，为通信受限环境下的多智能体探索提供了理论基础和有效方案。

Abstract: Cave networks, pipe systems, and similar maze-like environments pose significant challenges for multi-agent navigation in unknown settings with limited communication. We propose a distributed algorithm that enables agents to collectively traverse an unknown, possibly cyclic graph. Agents enter sequentially at a designated start node and are tasked to localize and reach an undisclosed goal while avoiding collisions. They coordinate via local communication using leader-follower relationships and leader switching. At any moment in time, exploration is performed by only one of the agents, which runs a single-agent maze solver. We prove that the algorithm is complete, that its makespan is asymptotically equivalent (in the number of agents) to that of an optimal full-knowledge strategy, and derive its time and space complexity. Simulations with up to $625$ agents show a decreasing average sum-of-fuels as the number of agents increases and demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms a naïve baseline in which all agents independently execute the single-agent solver.

</details>


### [184] [DaViNCi: A Dataset Towards Outdoor Vision-and-Language Navigation with Continuous Actions and Dynamic Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11901)
*Zihao Xie,Pingrui Lai,Yitong Wu,Hua Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了DaViNCi，首个同时包含连续动作和动态因素（连续环境与动态元素）的户外视觉语言导航（VLN）数据集，包含6张地图、6,933条轨迹，实验表明在该数据集上导航成功率显著下降，更贴近真实世界环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有户外VLN数据集基于固定的离散拓扑图构建，无法适应快速变化的真实户外环境，阻碍了VLN智能体从仿真到真实世界的迁移。需要引入同时具备连续运动空间和动态元素的数据集来弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建了DaViNCi数据集：(1) 在户外环境中引入连续动作，使智能体可以自由运动而非受限于离散图节点；(2) 同时引入不可预测的动态元素，要求智能体在连续环境中处理这些动态干扰；(3) 数据集涵盖6个不同地图，共6,933条轨迹。

Result: 综合对比实验显示：在离散环境下，DaViNCi上的成功率相比以往数据集下降了超过10%；在连续设置下性能下降更为显著。实验还分别揭示了动作粒度和动态元素各自对性能的影响。

Conclusion: DaViNCi更具挑战性且更贴近真实户外环境，具有推动户外VLN向真实世界场景发展的实用价值，为后续户外VLN研究提供了更加现实的基准。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has progressively expanded from indoor to outdoor environments. However, existing outdoor VLN datasets still rely on fixed discrete topological graphs for construction. It fails to align with the rapidly changing real-world outdoor environments and impedes the sim-to-real transfer of VLN agents. To address this limitation, we propose DaViNCi (\textbf{D}yn\textbf{a}mic \textbf{Vi}sion-and-Language \textbf{N}avigation in \textbf{C}ont\textbf{i}nuous Environment), the first outdoor VLN dataset that simultaneously introduces both continuous and dynamic factors. The agent not only moves in the outdoor environment using continuous actions but is also required to handle unpredictable dynamic elements. The dataset encompasses six distinct maps with a total of 6,933 trajectories. Through comprehensive comparative experiments, we find that the success rate on DaViNCi decreased by more than 10\% in discrete environments compared to previous datasets. And there is an even greater decline in continuous settings, demonstrating the challenge of DaViNCi. Furthermore, we clarify the impact of action granularity and dynamic elements. These results demonstrate the practical value of DaViNCi in advancing outdoor VLN toward more realistic environments. The website is https://xzh0312.github.io/DaViNCi/.

</details>


### [185] [Learning Loco-Manipulation From SMPC Demonstrations With Sparse Offline-to-Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12063)
*Martin Schuck,Maks Sorokin,Simone Manni,Duy Ta,Angela P. Schoellig,Marco Hutter,Simon Le Cleac'H,Jan Brüdigam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将采样型模型预测控制(SMPC)作为自动专家在仿真中生成大规模离线数据集的方法，使强化学习代理仅使用稀疏任务奖励即可训练，同时结合低层动态稳定性控制器，实现超越原最优控制教师的具身智能行为，并在不同形态机器人（装备机械臂的Spot四足机器人和G1人形机器人）上完成sim-to-real部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的强化学习在复杂任务上的扩展受限于密集奖励塑造这一缓慢且需要人工操作的过程，而该过程严重阻碍了机器人自主性中移动与操作整合能力的发展。作者希望绕过这一瓶颈，消除手动调参需求。

Method: 1) 在仿真环境中使用采样型模型预测控制(SMPC)作为自动且可快速调整的专家，生成大规模离线数据集；2) 利用该数据解决探索问题，使用纯稀疏任务奖励训练离线策略强化学习代理；3) 将高层策略代理与低层动态稳定性控制器集成，形成整体控制框架。

Result: 该框架能够在稀疏奖励条件下高效训练复杂移动操作技能，学习到的策略优于原始的SMPC最优控制教师，并在多种不同形态的机器人（装备机械臂的Spot四足机器人和G1人形机器人）上成功完成sim-to-real部署，验证了框架的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过SMPC专家生成离线数据并结合稀疏奖励的离线RL训练，可以消除密集奖励塑造的人工负担，同时通过高层策略与低层稳定性控制器的整合实现超越教师性能的行为，为复杂移动操作任务的机器人学习提供了一个高效且可扩展的自动化框架。

Abstract: Integrating locomotion and manipulation is essential for robot autonomy, but scaling standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) to complex tasks is severely bottlenecked by the slow, manual process of dense reward shaping. To bypass this limitation, we leverage Sample-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) entirely in simulation as an automated, rapidly tunable expert to generate massive offline datasets. Because this data solves the fundamental exploration problem, we can train an off-policy RL agent using purely sparse task rewards, drastically reducing the time required to learn new skills and eliminating the need for manual tuning. Integrating this high-level agent with a low-level dynamic stability controller yields more optimal behaviors that strictly align with true task objectives, ultimately allowing the learned policies to surpass the original optimal control teacher. We validate the robustness of this sim-to-real framework by successfully deploying complex loco-manipulation skills across different morphologies, including an arm-equipped Spot quadruped and a G1 humanoid.

</details>


### [186] [HandEdit: A Unified Benchmark for Egocentric Human-to-Robot Dexterous Hand Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12122)
*Zhenjie Yang,Xingyu Jiao,Guopeng Zhong,Shuzhe Yang,Shi Che,Chao Wu,Chenyu Jiang,Dongjie Zhang,Yideng Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Muyun Jiang,Haisheng Su,Shuang Jin,Donghang Zhang,Chao Yang,Li Chen,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HandEdit，一个大规模、体感感知的图像编辑数据集与基准，旨在将人形手和手臂的自我中心视频帧转换为多种灵巧机器人形态，包含超过2亿个编辑实例、26种不同的URDF配置，并建立了统一的基准协议以支持URDF条件化评估，突破了人-机器人数据之间的差异瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手操作是具身智能的核心，但高昂的遥操作数据采集成本严重阻碍了其进展。虽然丰富的自我中心人类手部视频提供了可扩展的替代方案，但人类与机器人数据在外观、关节结构和相机视角上的显著差异使得联合训练面临巨大挑战。现有通用图像编辑模型虽能力强，但缺乏体感（embodiment-specific）先验知识来弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: HandEdit包含三方面核心设计：(1) 构建统一的大规模体感感知图像编辑数据集，整合5个不同来源数据集，覆盖26种不同URDF（13种手部only和13种手-臂配置），产生超过2亿个编辑实例；(2) 建立统一基准协议，包含Hand-only和Hand-Arm两个轨道的URDF条件化评估；(3) 采用多维评估体系，包括通用相似度指标、VLM判断和体感感知指标，对11个代表性图像编辑基线进行全面评估。

Result: 对11个代表性图像编辑基线进行了广泛评估，采用多维度的指标套件。HandEdit数据集在规模和多样性上显著超越现有资源，为体感感知图像编辑模型的训练和评估提供了标准化平台，并且验证了其在促进从人类视频数据学习灵巧机器人操作方面的潜力。

Conclusion: HandEdit填补了图像编辑与机器人交叉领域的关键资源空白，推进了体感感知编辑模型的发展，同时为利用丰富的人类视频数据实现可扩展的灵巧机器人大规模学习铺平了道路，有望推动更具泛化能力的具身智能系统的实现。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation with dexterous hands is a cornerstone of Embodied AI, yet its progress is stifled by the high cost of collecting embodiment-aware teleoperation data. While abundant egocentric videos of human hands offer a scalable alternative, the profound discrepancies in appearance, articulation, and camera viewpoints between human and robotic data raise significant challenges for co-training. Though existing general image-editing models demonstrate strong capabilities, they lack necessary embodiment-specific priors to fully bridge this gap. In this work, we present HandEdit, a unified large-scale embodiment-aware image-editing dataset and benchmark specifically designed to transform human hands and arms into various dexterous robotic embodiments within egocentric frames. HandEdit comprises over 200M editing instances derived from five diverse source datasets, covering 26 distinct URDFs, including 13 hand-only and 13 hand-arm configurations. Alongside the dataset, we establish a unified benchmark protocol with two tracks: Hand-only and Hand-Arm, supporting URDF-conditioned evaluation. We conduct extensive evaluations of 11 representative image-editing baselines using a multi-dimensional metric suite, including generic similarity metrics, VLM-based judgment, and embodiment-aware metrics. HandEdit serves as a critical resource at the intersection of image editing and robotics: it advances embodiment-aware editing models while enabling scalable dexterous robotic learning from abundant human video data, paving the way for more generalizable Embodied AI.

</details>


### [187] [Learning-Based Behavior Planning for Automated Driving: Real-World Integration and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12198)
*Jean-Pierre Busch,Guido Linden,Jan Bergmann,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种混合规划架构，将深度学习用于复杂交通场景解析和行为提议，同时用基于优化的监督层验证行为提议并强制执行可驾驶性和安全性约束，兼顾机器学习优势与经典方法的可验证性和确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的运动规划方法在自动驾驶复杂环境中表现出潜力，但其黑盒特性缺乏透明性，导致可解释性、可信度和安全保证困难。

Method: 设计深度神经网络解析复杂交通场景并提出驾驶行为，在此基础上构建基于优化的监督层对提议进行验证，强制执行明确的驾驶性与安全约束，形成混合规划架构。通过开环研究、闭环系统集成分和实车部署进行评估。

Result: 在真实城市数据的开环研究中评估了学习规划器的驾驶行为，讨论了稳定闭环运行的系统集成问题，并报告了在研究车辆karl上的真实部署结果。

Conclusion: 混合架构能够在保留机器学习对复杂场景理解能力的同时，通过优化监督层确保规划行为的可验证、确定性及安全性，从而提升自动驾驶系统的可信度和安全性保证。

Abstract: Recent research in machine and deep learning has shown the potential of learningbased motion planning approaches to improve the driving behavior of automated vehicles, especially in complex environments. However, their complex nature and lack of transparency can hinder explainability and trustworthiness and complicate safety assurance. Motivated by these challenges, we propose a hybrid planning architecture that combines the advantages of machine learning with the verifiability and the determinism of classical approaches. Specifically, we developed a deep neural network to interpret complex traffic scenes and propose driving behavior, while an optimization-based supervision layer validates this proposal and enforces explicit drivability and safety constraints. We evaluate the learned planner's driving behavior in open-loop studies on real-world urban data, discuss system integration aspects for stable closed-loop operation, and report results from real-world deployment on our research vehicle karl..

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [FarSky: Task-Aware Latent-Space Coupling for Generative Intra-Hour Solar Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11254)
*Yann Fabel,Bijan Nouri,Milon Miah,Niklas Blum,Luis F. Zarzalejo,Julia Kowalski,Robert Pitz-Paal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FarSky是一种生成式太阳辐照度预测框架，通过多任务自编码器学习天空图像的共享潜空间表示，并利用潜扩散模型生成未来潜状态，实现概率性辐照预测。在西班牙Almería太阳平台的多年ASI数据集上，FarSky在确定性和概率性预测中均取得最佳性能，预测技能提升最高11个百分点，斜坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的太阳辐照度预测对光伏并网至关重要。虽然全天空成像仪(ASI)结合深度学习方法已显著提升预测精度，但现有方法受限于确定性预测，且难以预测斜坡事件，缺乏概率性预测能力。

Method: FarSky包含两个核心组件：(1) 多任务自编码器，联合学习用于图像重建和辐照估计的共享潜空间表示；(2) 潜扩散模型，基于近期观测生成未来潜状态，从中直接解码辐照预测。通过随机采样天然获得概率性预测。

Result: 在西班牙Almería太阳平台的两个独立测试数据集上，FarSky在确定性和概率性预测方面均优于持久性模型、端到端方法和生成式预测方法，预测技能提升最高11个百分点，斜坡事件检测F1分数超过60%。

Conclusion: 该研究证明将生成模型与任务感知的潜空间耦合相结合，能够有效提升太阳辐射预测的准确性和斜坡事件检测能力，为光伏功率预测提供了可行的新范式。

Abstract: Accurate solar irradiance forecasting is essential for the reliable integration of photovoltaic power into modern electricity grids. All-sky imagers (ASI) provide high-resolution observations of clouds, making them well suited for intra-hour forecasting. Recent deep learning approaches have substantially improved forecast accuracy but are often limited by deterministic predictions and a reduced capability to anticipate ramp events. This work proposes FarSky, a generative forecasting framework that leverages latent-space coupling to learn task-aware representations of sky images. A multi-task autoencoder first learns a shared latent representation for image reconstruction and irradiance estimation. A latent diffusion model then generates future latent states conditioned on recent observations, from which irradiance forecasts are directly decoded. Probabilistic forecasts are inherently obtained through stochastic sampling. The framework is developed using a multi-year ASI dataset acquired at the Plataforma Solar de Almería, Spain, and evaluated on two independent test datasets against persistence, state-of-the-art end-to-end, and generative forecasting approaches. FarSky achieves the best overall deterministic and probabilistic forecasting performance, improving forecast skill by up to 11 percentage points. Furthermore, it substantially improves ramp event detection over existing methods, achieving F1-scores above 60%. These results demonstrate the potential of combining generative models with task-aware latent-space coupling for solar forecasting.

</details>


### [189] [Why AI Detection Fails for Academic Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11256)
*Jonathan A. Karr,Grigorii Khvatskii,Ting Hua,Nitesh V. Chawla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 商业AI检测器无法区分AI辅助编辑与全LLM草稿，导致合规使用AI的被标记为学术不端的风险远高于使用拟人化工具规避检测。研究表明"仅润色摘要"的合规编辑被标记率达64-80%，而经Undetectable AI拟人化后规避率近乎完全（<4%仍被标记）。因此检测分数不应作为独立的不端行为证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机构使用商业AI检测器维护学术诚信，但检测器无法区分AI辅助编辑（合规）与完整LLM草稿（可能不端），可能将两类情况一并视为学术不端。这种政策失败可能导致本应受鼓励的合规AI辅助行为反而面临更高处罚风险，而借助拟人化工具的规避行为却可逃脱检测。

Method: 对已发表英文摘要进行受控研究，覆盖四个领域，对比2013-2015与2023-2025两个时段；在tau=0.50的代理人工/AI标签下，使用Pangram和GPTZero检测器评估了三类样本：仅润色摘要的轻量编辑（代表合规AI辅助）、未修改的2023-2025原始文本、以及经Undetectable AI拟人化处理后的AI改写文本，并分析检测得分与长token及学术词表密度的关联。

Result: 合规的"仅润色摘要"编辑被Pangram/GPTZero标记为AI的概率为64-80%；未修改的2023-2025原文被标记率为9-15%，其中非STEM领域的误报率显著高于STEM（p<0.001）；高分与长token和学术词表密度相关。经Undetectable AI拟人化后，AI标记改写文本的再检出率<4%（漏报率>96%）。

Conclusion: 检测器得分不应作为独立的学术不端证据；当前政策框架下，诚实进行AI辅助编辑的学者面临比借助拟人化工具规避检测者更高的制裁风险，表明现有AI检测在学术诚信治理中造成了严重的不公平与政策失效。

Abstract: Institutions use commercial AI detectors for academic integrity, yet detectors cannot distinguish AI editing from full LLM drafts and may treat both as misconduct. In a controlled study of published English abstracts (four domains; 2013 to 2015 vs. 2023 to 2025), we quantify this policy failure under proxy human/AI labels at tau=0.50. Light "refine abstract only" edits, a proxy for guideline-compliant AI assistance, are flagged at 64 to 80% (Pangram/GPTZero). Unmodified 2023 to 2025 originals are flagged at 9 to 15%, with non-STEM rates far above STEM (p<0.001); elevated scores track long-token and Academic Word List density, not authorship intent alone. After Undetectable AI humanization, evasion is near-total: fewer than 4% of AI-labeled rewrites remain flagged (post-humanization detection rate <4%; FNR >96%). Honest AI-editing results in a higher sanction risk than humanizer-assisted evasion. Therefore, detector scores should not serve as standalone misconduct evidence.

</details>


### [190] [Basin: Efficient and Extensible Numerical Optimization in Rust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11279)
*Johan Larsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Basin是一个Rust编程语言的数值优化库，提供统一的接口来表述和求解最小化函数的问题，包含丰富的求解器目录并支持约束条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值优化在科学和工程领域（如模型拟合、模拟校准、机器学习训练、工程参数选择）中是基础性任务，需要一个统一的、一致的库来支持这类问题的表述与求解。

Method: Basin通过提供单一一致的API来表述优化问题，内置广泛的求解器目录，并将约束条件作为一等公民加以支持。

Result: Basin为Rust社区提供了集求解器多样性与约束支持于一体的数值优化库。

Conclusion: Basin是一个面向Rust生态的数值优化库，强调统一接口、求解器广度和约束支持。

Abstract: Basin is a numerical optimization library for the Rust programming language. Numerical optimization is the task of finding the inputs that minimize a function, and it is a fundamental element across the sciences: fitting a model to data, calibrating a simulation, training a machine learning model, or choosing engineering parameters that minimize cost. Basin gives users a single, consistent way to both state and solve such problems, with a broad catalog of solvers and first-class support for constraints.

</details>


### [191] [Federated Learning for Distributed CNC Tool Wear Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11281)
*Afsana Khan,Morris Stallmann,Marcin Pietrasik,Charis Kouzinopoulos,Anna Wilbik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了联邦学习在CNC刀具磨损预测中的应用，通过将刀具轨迹数据分布到模拟客户端来构建联邦场景，实验表明联邦学习性能接近集中式学习并显著优于本地客户端模型，支持在分布式制造环境中进行协作式刀具磨损预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CNC加工中刀具磨损预测对产品质量和工艺可靠性至关重要。虽然机器学习方法在该任务上展现潜力，但工业环境中加工数据的分布式特性以及跨机器、站点或组织间数据共享的限制，阻碍了集中式机器学习方法的应用。联邦学习为这一场景提供了合适的框架，允许在不传输原始运营数据的情况下进行协作式模型训练。

Method: 将刀具轨迹数据分布到多个模拟客户端以构建联邦学习场景，训练联邦模型并将其与集中式参考模型和本地客户端基线模型进行性能对比。

Result: 联邦学习模型的性能接近集中式学习，同时显著优于各本地客户端单独训练的模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明联邦学习能够支持分布式CNC制造环境中的协作式刀具磨损预测，在保证数据隐私的前提下实现接近集中式的高精度预测性能。

Abstract: Tool wear prediction is an important task in CNC machining, where accurate monitoring of tool condition supports product quality and process reliability. Machine learning methods have shown potential for this task, but their use in industrial environments is limited by the distributed nature of machining data and by restrictions on data sharing between machines, sites, or organizations. Federated learning offers a suitable framework for this setting by enabling collaborative model training without transferring raw operational data. This paper investigates federated learning for CNC tool wear prediction. Tool trajectories are distributed across simulated clients to represent a federated learning scenario. The federated models are compared against centralized references and local client baselines. Results show that federated learning achieves performance close to centralized learning and improves significantly over local client models. These findings indicate that federated learning can support collaborative tool wear prediction in distributed CNC manufacturing environments.

</details>


### [192] [Terminal Symmetry as a Decision Resource: Statewise Refinement for Anytime Verified Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11318)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"终态对称性"作为定向序列构造任务中可复用的决策资源，通过"传输—细化—验证"（transport-refine-certify）框架将完成时的对称性转化为可查询的决策信号，并实例化为\method{}方法，在CAD装配、小程序和精确填充等任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多序列构造任务在完成时呈现精确对称性，但执行过程本身是定向且历史依赖的。现有方法未能利用这种"终态对称"作为结构化的决策资源，导致验证成本高、规划效率低。作者希望建立一种决策-资源视角，将终态对称性转化为可操作、可传输、可验证的决策信号。

Method: \method{}包含四个核心组件：1) 回合固定的传输过程结构（episode-fixed transported process structure）；2) 状态受限的过程秩（state-restricted process rank）；3) 在接受转换后刷新的状态依赖残差秩（state-dependent residual rank）；4) 序数秩交（ordinal rank meet），其top-k集合恰好是两个提议前缀的并集。该设计在前缀覆盖条件下提供完成保证，并在对应前缀信息模型下达到最紧的最坏情况验证器查询下界。

Result: 在CAD装配、Mini-Programs和精确填充任务中，状态级刷新将anytime AUC分别提升最多6.77、21.75和8.68个点。在GRN OOD场景的1,135个目标移除片段中，\method{}在三个尺度上均取得最低的平均封顶验证器成本，优于GRN和CDGS风格规划器。状态级信号还能跨聚合器和调度器组织迁移。

Conclusion: 终态对称性可以被系统性地分解为方向性传输、跨结果传输、状态刷新和固定验证四个环节，成为定向构造任务中可复用的决策资源。\method{}通过秩交机制在保证完成性的同时实现紧的验证查询下界，并在多个实际任务中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Many sequential construction tasks exhibit exact symmetry at completion while their execution remains directed and history-dependent. We develop a decision-resource view of terminal symmetry: process evidence supplies directionality, terminal correspondence transports that structure across equivalent outcomes, realized-state evidence refines its current decision relevance after transitions, and a fixed verifier certifies execution. This decomposition yields transport--refine--certify. \method{} instantiates the principle with an episode-fixed transported process structure, its state-restricted process rank, a state-dependent residual rank refreshed after accepted transitions, and an ordinal rank meet whose top-$k$ set is exactly the union of the two proposal prefixes. The meet provides a completion guarantee under prefix coverage and attains the tight worst-case verifier-query bound under the corresponding prefix information model; a two-state construction predicts a strict post-transition dynamic--static separation. Across CAD assembly, Mini-Programs, and exact-fill packing, statewise refresh improves anytime AUC by up to $6.77$, $21.75$, and $8.68$ points, respectively. On 1,135 target-removal episodes from the official GRN OOD scenes, \method{} attains the lowest mean capped verifier cost at all three scales among the compared GRN and CDGS-style planners. The statewise signal also transfers across aggregation and scheduler organizations. Terminal symmetry thereby becomes a reusable decision resource for directed construction.

</details>


### [193] [Contextual Quality-Diversity Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning for HVAC Control in Tropical Commercial Buildings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11324)
*Tran Le Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上下文质量-多样性进化强化学习控制器（CQD-ERL），用于热带水冷式冷水机组及其风侧的监督控制。该控制器不收敛于单一标量化策略，而是维护一个产品档案，其中索引由数据驱动的运行上下文（每日天气和负荷模式的聚类）与上下文无关的行为描述符共同构成。档案由梯度无关的进化算子和共享回放缓冲区的软演员-评论家策略梯度算子共同填充，每次动作在执行前均通过确定性安全屏蔽进行过滤。控制器在代表新加坡商业建筑潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近度和湿度约束的两层简化环境上训练，并在全年回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习控制器通常收敛到单一标量化策略，难以应对冷水机组系统在不同天气和负荷运行情境下的多模态、多目标控制需求，缺乏灵活性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出CQD-ERL框架，将质量-多样性进化和强化学习结合：其一是产品档案按运行上下文（基于每日天气和负荷聚类）和行为描述符联合索引；其二是结合无梯度的进化算子与软演员-评论家策略梯度算子，两者共享回放缓冲区；其三是每次动作执行前通过确定性安全屏蔽过滤以确保安全性。

Result: 控制器在基于新加坡商业建筑潜在负荷、冷却塔逼近度和湿度约束的两层简化环境中训练，并在全年回测中与ASHRAE Guideline 36基线进行对比评估。

Conclusion: CQD-ERL通过在上下文与行为两个维度上维护多样化策略档案，并结合进化与策略梯度更新的混合优化方式及安全屏蔽机制，为暖通空调系统监督控制提供了一种更具适应性和安全性的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a contextual quality-diversity evolutionary reinforcement-learning controller, CQD-ERL, for the supervisory control of a tropical, water-cooled chiller plant and its associated air side. Rather than converging to a single scalarised policy, the controller maintains a product archive of specialised policies indexed jointly by a data- driven operating context, a cluster of daily weather and load regime, and a context-invariant behaviour descriptor, filled by a gradient-free evolutionary operator and a soft-actor-critic policy-gradient operator that share one replay buffer. Every action is filtered through a deterministic safety shield before execution. The controller is trained on a two-tier reduced-order environment representing the latent load, cooling-tower approach and humidity constraints of a Singapore commercial building, and is evaluated over a full annual backtest against an ASHRAE Guideline 36 baseline.

</details>


### [194] [Long-Horizon Forecasting of Complete Financial Statements with Forma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11327)
*Travis L. Johnson,Jiannan Jiang,Soumyabrata Chaudhuri,Yihao Chen,Lauren Falvey,Donal O'Cofaigh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProForma-20Q基准和Forma模型，用于联合预测长达20个季度的完整财务报表（78个项目），Forma通过将报表建模为（账户、季度、值）元组集合并最大化掩码元组高斯似然，在预测精度上全面超越经典机器学习、梯度提升、时序基础模型和前沿大语言模型，且领先优势随预测周期拉大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作未联合预测超过一年期的完整财务报表，而折现现金流估值中大部分企业价值恰恰位于该窗口之外。缺乏可复现的长期财务报表预测基准，限制了对该核心估值需求的系统研究。

Method: 构建ProForma-20Q基准（78个报表行项目、1-20季度预测、变化空间R²评分）；提出Forma变换器模型，将财务报表建模为（账户、季度、值）元组集合，通过最大化掩码元组高斯似然进行训练与推理。

Result: Forma在所有对比方法中表现最优，包括经典机器学习、链式梯度提升、零样本时序基础模型及前沿大语言模型；领先幅度随预测周期延长而扩大；高斯预测区间从不欠覆盖；预测结果近似满足会计恒等式，且可无损恢复精确一致性；元组接口支持无需重训的情景分析，固定未来收入路径可锐化其余报表项的预测。

Conclusion: 专业训练的专精模型优于通用规模模型，特别在财务预测这类具有结构化约束和长周期估值需求的任务中。Forma的高精度、校准良好的不确定性估计、会计一致性以及情景分析能力，使其成为长期财务报表预测的有力工具，并为后续研究提供了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Specialist training beats generalist scale when forecasting financial statements. To our knowledge, no prior work jointly forecasts complete financial statements beyond one year, yet in a discounted-cash-flow valuation most firm value sits past that window. We release ProForma-20Q, a reproducible benchmark for forecasting 78 statement line items 1-20 quarters ahead, for anonymized firms, from past statements and an industry code, scored by change-space $R^2$. On it, Forma, a transformer that reads statements as sets of (account, quarter, value) tuples and maximizes a masked-tuple Gaussian likelihood, beats every competitor we field: classical machine learning, chained gradient boosting, a zero-shot time-series foundation model, and frontier large language models. Its lead widens with horizon, where valuation needs accuracy most, and its Gaussian predictive intervals never under-cover. Forma's forecasts nearly satisfy accounting identities; exact coherence is recoverable at no statistically significant accuracy cost. Its tuple interface supports scenario analysis without retraining, and we show that pinning future revenue paths sharpens the rest of the statement.

</details>


### [195] [Weightless Fine-Tuning: Personalizing LLMs via Logit-Space Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11342)
*Bohan Zhang,Anqi Ni,Yixin Wang,Paramveer S. Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT)，一种无需权重更新的训练自由解码时方法，通过计算监督残差并利用基于 dropout 诱导交叉协方差估计的跨前缀传输算子，将作者的训练信号传递到当前提示中，以近似 SFT 的分布效果。在 LaMP 个性化基准上，WFT 平均性能最佳，且仅用不到 SFT 7% 的计算量即接近其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT 虽然是将 LLM 适应目标分布的标准方法，但在个性化场景中（如每个作者需要单独的权重访问、优化、存储和重训练），其成本变得过高。需要一种无需权重更新、训练自由的方法来近似 SFT 的效果，以降低个性化适配的计算和存储开销。

Method: WFT 在解码时计算作者的监督残差，并通过一个由 dropout 诱导交叉协方差估计的跨前缀传输算子，将这些残差从作者的训练序列传输到当前提示。该算子建模了一个上下文扰动如何传播到另一上下文的预测，用 logit 空间校正替代基于梯度的参数更新。

Result: 在三个 LaMP 个性化基准上，WFT 取得最佳平均性能，在单个任务上匹配或超过 SFT，并优于其他轻量级基线。在受预算控制的对比中，WFT 以不到 SFT 7% 的有效计算量接近 SFT 性能。logit 级分析显示，WFT 与 SFT 引起的 logit 位移在 95% 的下一个 token 概率质量上余弦相似度达 0.875。

Conclusion: WFT 能够在不对模型权重做任何修改的情况下，近似捕获监督适配的分布效果，为个性化 LLM 适配提供了一种低成本且高效的新路径，其 logit 位移与 SFT 高度相似，确认了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is a standard approach for adapting LLMs to a target distribution, but in settings such as personalization, where each author requires separate weight access, optimization, storage, and retraining, its costs become prohibitive. We propose Weightless Fine-Tuning (WFT), a training-free decoding-time method that approximates the distributional effect of SFT without weight updates. WFT computes supervised residuals on an author's training sequence and transports them to the current prompt through a cross-prefix transport operator estimated from dropout-induced cross-covariance. The operator captures how a perturbation at one context propagates to predictions at another, replacing gradient-based parameter updates with logit-space corrections. On three LaMP personalization benchmarks, WFT achieves the best average performance across datasets, matches or exceeds SFT on individual tasks, and outperforms other lightweight baselines on average. In a budget-controlled comparison, WFT approaches SFT performance using less than 7% of the effective computation. Logit-level analysis shows a cosine similarity of 0.875 between the logit shifts induced by WFT and SFT over 95% of the next-token probability mass, suggesting that WFT captures the distributional effect of supervised adaptation without modifying model weights.

</details>


### [196] [Dynamics Models for Offline Hyperparameter Selection in Real-World RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11349)
*Jordan Coblin,Han Wang,Martha White,Adam White*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将校准模型（calibration models）应用于真实工业场景——市政水处理厂，用于强化学习超参数选择。在非平稳、高维传感器数据上进行nexting预测任务，评估了包括带Laplacian距离度量的k近邻模型在内的多种方法。结果表明这些模型能生成逼真的长视野rollout、恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势，并考察了模型在年尺度数据集上的扩展性、对预训练agent微调学习率选择的支撑能力以及对分布偏移的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在真实系统部署中的关键障碍是超参数选择，特别是在没有模拟器且在线实验成本高昂的情况下。已有研究提出基于离线数据训练的校准模型来近似环境动态以实现离线超参数选择，但此前仅在简单模拟环境中得到评估，缺乏真实工业场景的验证。

Method: 在真实市政水处理厂环境中，评估了多种校准模型方法，包括使用Laplacian距离度量的k近邻模型，应用于高维、非平稳的传感器数据上进行nexting预测任务。研究还考察了模型对年长度数据集的扩展能力、对预训练agent微调学习率选择的辅助能力，以及在分布偏移下的鲁棒性。

Result: 校准模型能够生成逼真的长视野rollout并恢复有意义的超参数敏感性趋势，且在真实工业数据上得到验证。同时揭示了模型在年尺度数据扩展、分布偏移下鲁棒性等方面的实践挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作为使用离线动态模型支持真实环境中RL部署提供了概念验证，证明了校准模型在工业场景中的可行性，同时指出了未来研究需要关注的重要实践问题。

Abstract: A key obstacle to deploying reinforcement learning in real-world systems is hyperparameter selection, particularly when simulators are unavailable and online experimentation is costly. Prior work has proposed calibration models trained on offline data to approximate environment dynamics and enable offline hyperparameter selection, but these methods have so far been evaluated only in simple simulated settings. In this paper, we present the first application of calibration models in a real-world industrial setting: a municipal water treatment plant. We evaluate several calibration model approaches, including a k-nearest neighbors model with a Laplacian distance metric, on high-dimensional, non-stationary sensor data for nexting prediction tasks. Our results show that these models can generate realistic long-horizon rollouts and recover meaningful hyperparameter sensitivity trends. We further examine how calibration models scale to year-long datasets, how they support the selection of fine-tuning learning rates for pre-trained agents, and how robust they are under distribution shift. Overall, our findings provide a proof of concept for using offline dynamics models to support RL deployment in real-world environments, while highlighting important practical challenges for future work.

</details>


### [197] [Market-Information-Aware Gated-LoRA of Foundation Models for Transferable Day-Ahead Electricity Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11359)
*Hang Fan,Wei Wei,Shengwei Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种市场信息感知的适配框架，将Chronos-2时序基础模型迁移到日前电价预测。通过多源市场信息接口对齐7日价格上下文与机组检修、备用、供需等市场变量，并训练源域门控低秩适配器（仅更新约1%参数），在四个中国省级日前现货市场上相较市场感知的零样本Chronos-2平均降低MAE/RMSE 6.24%/7.99%，相较vanilla Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%，为数据稀缺电力市场提供了一条实用的迁移路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电价预测对市场参与者至关重要，但价格波动大、市场特异性强且依赖预期系统条件。现有监督方法高度依赖市场特定的历史数据，在新建立或数据稀缺的市场中受限，亟需一种不依赖目标市场标签的迁移学习方法。

Method: 提出market-information-aware adaptation框架：(1)构建多源市场信息（MSMI）接口，将7日价格上下文与预清算供需、备用、检修、发电容量、联络线等变量对齐；(2)训练源域门控低秩适配器（gated LoRA），仅更新约1%模型参数，无需目标市场标签；(3)门控机制根据备用紧张程度和运行状态信号缩放冻结的源适配器；(4)采用留一市场（leave-one-market-out）协议评估跨市场迁移能力。

Result: 在四个中国省级日前现货市场实验中，该框架相较市场信息感知的零样本Chronos-2将MAE/RMSE平均降低6.24%/7.99%，相较vanilla Source-LoRA降低3.05%/3.52%。消融实验显示，学习全局标量或随机门控初始化无法复现增益，且相对于Source-LoRA的额外提升有限。

Conclusion: 市场结构化输入与状态依赖的门控LoRA可以为数据稀缺的电力市场提供切实可行的迁移路径，但门控带来的额外增益相对有限，说明引入市场结构信息是跨市场迁移的主要贡献来源。

Abstract: Electricity price forecasting is crucial for market participants but remains difficult because prices are volatile, market-specific, and closely tied to anticipated system conditions. Existing supervised methods depend largely on market-specific historical data, limiting their use in newly established or data-scarce markets. This paper proposes a market-information-aware adaptation framework that transfers the Chronos-2 time-series foundation model to day-ahead electricity price forecasting. It first constructs a multi-source market information (MSMI) interface aligning 7-day price context with pre-clearing supply--demand, reserve, maintenance, generator-capacity, and intertie variables, and then trains a source-domain gated low-rank adapter (LoRA), updating about $1\%$ of model parameters without target-market labels. The gate scales the frozen source adapter according to reserve-tightness and operating-state signals. A leave-one-market-out protocol is adopted for evaluating cross-market transferability. Experiments on four Chinese provincial day-ahead spot markets show that the proposed framework reduces the average MAE/RMSE by $6.24\%/7.99\%$ relative to market-information-aware zero-shot Chronos-2 and by $3.05\%/3.52\%$ relative to vanilla Source-LoRA. Experiments show that the gain is not reproduced by a learned global scalar or by random gate initialization, while the additional improvement over Source-LoRA is limited. These results suggest that market-structured inputs and state-dependent gated LoRA can provide a practical transfer path for data-scarce electricity markets.

</details>


### [198] [Lifecycle-Optimal Tokenization: Vocabulary Size as a Deployment-Regime-Dependent Infrastructure Parameter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11361)
*Rima Mittal,Ankit Gubrani,Satyanarayana Kakollu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性实验证明，LLM的词表大小不是一个固定常数，而应根据服务场景（推理批量大小和推理量）进行优化。作者形式化了全生命周期部署成本函数，并在两类GPU上实验发现：推理最优词表随批量大小从32k（B=1）到524k（B≥64）变化16倍；全生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表最大偏差可达16倍，为实际部署提供了具体的容量规划建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词表大小是LLM基础设施的基础设计选择，但通常基于惯例在训练时固定，而非基于部署分析。现有实践忽视了词表大小对推理成本的影响，尤其在记忆受限到计算受限的不同服务场景下，缺乏系统的成本-最优词表分析框架。

Method: 作者将总部署成本形式化为训练成本与推理成本的加权和：C_lifecycle(V) = C_train(V) + λ·C_infer(V,B)，其中λ为推理量，B为批量大小。在A10G（ridge≈117 FLOP/byte）和A100（ridge≈183 FLOP/byte）两类GPU上，跨越记忆受限到计算受限区间进行受控实验，测量词表大小对训练成本、推理成本及质量（BPB）的影响。

Result: (1) 推理最优词表随服务批量从32k（B=1）到524k（B≥64）变化16倍，由V×d unembedding矩阵读取的摊销驱动；(2) 在1.3-2.3B模型规模下，质量最优词表为V=65k，确认词表偏好依赖规模；(3) 全生命周期最优词表与训练最优词表在production场景下偏差可达16倍。质量在最优区间内近似不变（BPB差异<2%）。

Conclusion: 词表大小应被视为纯粹的系统优化问题而非质量优化问题，因为最优范围内质量几乎无损失。实际指南：端侧部署（B=1）应使用V≈32k；数据中心服务（B≥64, λ≥10）应使用V≈131-262k。这为LLM容量规划提供了从惯例驱动转向分析驱动的词表选择方法。

Abstract: Tokenizer vocabulary size is a foundational design choice in large language model (LLM) infrastructure, yet it is typically fixed at training time based on convention rather than deployment analysis. We show that the cost-optimal vocabulary is not a constant but a function of the serving regime. We formalize total deployment cost as $C_{lifecycle}(V) = C_{train}(V) + λ\cdot C_{infer}(V, B)$, where $λ$ is inference volume and $B$ is the serving batch size. Through controlled experiments on two GPU families spanning the memory-bound to compute-bound regimes (A10G, ridge $\approx$ 117 FLOP/byte; A100, ridge $\approx$ 183 FLOP/byte), we demonstrate: (1) the inference-optimal vocabulary shifts 16x with serving batch, from 32k at $B=1$ to 524k at $B=64+$, driven by amortization of the $V \times d$ unembedding matrix read; (2) at 1.3-2.3B model scale, quality (bits per byte, BPB) is optimized at $V=65$k, confirming scale-dependent vocabulary preference; (3) the lifecycle-optimal vocabulary diverges from training-optimal by up to 16x for production deployments. Quality is approximately invariant across the optimal range ($<$2% BPB spread), making vocabulary a pure systems optimization with no quality penalty in the measured range. Our results provide actionable capacity planning guidance: on-device deployments ($B=1$) should use $V \approx 32$k; datacenter serving ($B \geq 64$, $λ\geq 10$) should use $V \approx 131$-262k.

</details>


### [199] [PAIR: Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting for Adaptive Rollout Allocation in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11368)
*Pixel Nomand,Elena Voss,Marcus Hale,Sofia Reyes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR（Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting）方法，将RLVR中组相对评分的梯度重新建模为二阶U统计量（成对rollout对比），通过对比图上的前缀预测与凸优化实现自适应端点选择，并用联合包含概率的逆加权来校正选择偏差，从而在更少的生成token下实现更优的平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR中大部分算力消耗在生成大量长推理轨迹上。现有的分配器根据点态难度或效用为prompt、rollout或token分配预算，但未察觉统计层面的不匹配：未裁剪的留一法组相对评分梯度不是独立点贡献之和，而是关于rollout对的二阶U统计量。完成一个rollout会揭示与其他所有已完成rollout的对比信息，自适应端点选择会改变哪些pair项可被观测，导致现有点态分配器在统计上存在偏差。

Method: 将短rollout前缀视为对比图的顶点、pair梯度项视为边。具体包括：(1) 用前缀预测器估计正确性和剩余token成本；(2) 用凸优化在期望后缀token预算约束下选择正继续概率；(3) 对由已完整顶点诱导出的每条边，按其联合包含概率的对数做逆加权。在条件独立on-policy rollout和未裁剪、未标准化的目标函数下，所得估计器对完整候选pair梯度是设计无偏的。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B上进行的计算匹配RLVR实验中，PAIR相比最强点态分配器将平均准确率提升+1.2和+1.4，同时比全组GRPO少用51%和52%的生成token。冻结种群估计器审计确认未加权的自适应选择存在偏差，而pair包含校正可在匹配的后缀成本下恢复完整pair目标。

Conclusion: PAIR通过显式建模pairwise的梯度结构并校正自适应选择带来的包含偏差，在显著降低计算成本的同时提升了RLVR的优化效果，验证了点态分配器在统计意义上的不足以及pair-wise校正的必要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) spends most of its compute generating groups of long reasoning trajectories. Recent allocators reduce this cost by assigning budgets to prompts, rollouts, or tokens according to a pointwise notion of difficulty or utility. We identify a statistical mismatch: the unclipped leave-one-out group-relative score gradient is not a sum of independent point contributions, but a second-order U-statistic over pairs of rollouts. Completing one rollout therefore reveals contrast with every other completed rollout, and adaptive endpoint selection changes which pair terms are observable. We introduce PAIR (Pairwise-Aware Inclusion Reweighting), which treats short rollout prefixes as vertices and pair-gradient terms as edges of a contrast graph. A prefix-only predictor estimates correctness and remaining token cost; a convex design chooses positive continuation probabilities under an expected suffix-token budget; and each edge induced by completed vertices is inverse-weighted by its logged joint inclusion probability. Under conditionally independent on-policy rollouts and an unclipped, unstandardized objective, the resulting estimator is design-unbiased for the complete candidate-pair gradient. Across compute-matched RLVR runs on Qwen3-1.7B/4B, PAIR improves average accuracy by +1.2 and +1.4 over the strongest pointwise allocator while using 51% and 52% fewer generated tokens than full-group GRPO. A frozen-population estimator audit confirms that unweighted adaptive selection is biased, whereas pair-inclusion correction recovers the complete-pair target at matched suffix cost.

</details>


### [200] [Towards an approach to multivariate outlier detection for District Heating System data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11375)
*Rajko Turudija,Dušan Stojiljković,Milan Zdravković,Marko Ignjatović*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了多种多元离群点检测方法（Z-score、马氏距离、PCA、孤立森林和Hotelling T-squared检验）用于检测区域供热系统变电站传输热能数据中的异常，最终采用三种有效方法（PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling）的集成方法作为最终方案，以发现异常运行并减少天然气消耗和CO2排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的总体目标是揭示区域供热系统中不正常的工厂运行，识别减少中央供暖工厂天然气消耗和CO2排放的机会，同时考虑室外环境温度等特定领域因素。

Method: 针对选定的区域供热系统变电站传输热能数据，结合室外环境温度，测试五种离群点检测方法：Z-score（作为单变量基线）、马氏距离、主成分分析（PCA）、孤立森林（Isolation Forest）和Hotelling T-squared检验。方法考虑特定领域情况，如零传输能量时间点不代表离网运行。最后采用基于三种有效方法（PCA、孤立森林、Hotelling）检测结果一致性的集成方法。

Result: 与领域专家讨论后得出结论：PCA、孤立森林和Hotelling方法的检测结果具有相关性，而其他方法（Z-score和马氏距离）的检测结果不够有效。最终采用三种有效方法在检测出的离群点上达成一致的集成方法作为最终方案。

Conclusion: 集成方法（要求PCA、孤立森林和Hotelling三种方法对离群点检测结果达成一致）被采纳为最终方案，能够有效识别区域供热系统中的异常运行情况。

Abstract: In this paper, we test different methods for multivariate detection of outliers in the data of transmitted heat energy in the selected substation of local District Heating System, by also considering outside ambient temperature, namely Z-score (univariate, as a benchmark), Mahalanobis distances, Principal Component Analysis (PCA), Isolation Forest and Hotelling's T-squared test. The overall research aims at uncovering irregular plant operation, with a wider objective of identifying the opportunities for reducing the consumption of gas in central heating plants as well as the CO2 emission. The proposed approach considers specific domain circumstances, such as irrelevance of zero transmit-ted energy timepoints as indication of off-grid plant. The outcomes of the different methods are discussed with domain experts. It was concluded that PCA, Isolation Forest and Hotelling method provide relevant results. Finally, we adopt the ensemble method (selection based on the agreement of all three methods on the detected outliers) as the final approach.

</details>


### [201] [Reoptimization Algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11383)
*Zhen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题，提出了一种基于上置信界(UCB)算法与再优化技术的简单扩展，实现了$O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$的平均遗憾界，显著优于文献中基于再优化的动态定价问题所达到的$O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题中，决策者需要在有限资源约束下，为每位到达的客户分配产品并同时学习未知的奖励函数。现有文献中基于再优化技术的动态定价算法仅能达到$O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$的遗憾界，本文旨在通过设计更优的算法来显著降低这一遗憾界，提升学习效率。

Method: 本文提出了一种自然且简单的方法：将上置信界(UCB)家族算法自然地扩展到带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题中，并应用再优化(re-optimization)技术。每次决策时，算法在现有对奖励函数置信区间的基础上，通过再优化来做出在线分配决策，从而在探索与利用之间取得平衡。

Result: 通过利用再优化技术的优势，所提出的算法实现了$O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$的平均遗憾界（T为时间跨度）。这一结果显著优于文献中紧密相关的基于再优化的动态定价问题所达到的$O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文证明了将UCB算法与再优化技术相结合，可以在带背包约束的上下文赌博机问题中实现显著更优的遗憾界，为相关在线决策问题的算法设计提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: We study new algorithms for Contextual Bandits with Knapsack. In these problems, there are finitely many types of customers, products, and resources. Each product is made from a fixed combination of resources, and resources have finite capacity. A decision maker must assign each arriving customer one out of a set of multiple possible products. Every assignment of a customer to a product will generate a random reward, which equals an unknown linear function of customer and product features, plus a noise term. The objective is to jointly learn the mean reward function, and to make online assignments to minimize the expected revenue loss relative to an optimal policy that knows the reward function. We propose a natural and simple extension of the Upper-Confidence-Bound (UCB) family of algorithms and apply re-optimization techniques. We show that by taking advantage of re-optimization, our algorithm achieves an average regret of $O(\frac{(\ln T)^3}{T})$ where $T$ is the horizon length. Our bound significantly reduces the $O(\frac{1}{\sqrt{T}})$ bound in the literature for closely related dynamic-pricing problems that are based on re-optimization.

</details>


### [202] [Mechanism Design for Generative Engines: From Exploitation toward Win-Win Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11390)
*Chen Xu,Zitian Guo,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式引擎背景下，内容提供者与平台之间围绕引用的博弈问题，提出基于可验证内容奖励的VCR机制，通过奖励可核查事实性内容的改写，将创作者激励与答案可信度对齐，在三个基准上以平均12.1个百分点的优势超越最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式引擎正在重塑网络生态，使引用成为注意力、归因和下游价值分配的关键机制。这产生策略性张力：内容提供者有动机优化以获得模型引用，而平台需保障答案质量与可信归因。已有研究表明该张力可能升级为"引用战争"，即GEO攻击适应常规防御，产生降低文档质量并引入无依据主张的引用寻求式改写。这一对抗性动态亟需系统性建模与有效防御机制。

Method: 本文将供给者-平台交互建模为具有部分监控的重复Stackelberg博弈；通过局部最优响应分析识别引用竞争何时趋近惰性平稳结果；在此基础上提出VCR（Verifiable-Content Rewards）机制，该机制不仅惩罚可疑改写，还奖励能表面化可核查事实性内容的改写，从而将创作者激励与答案可信度对齐。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，VCR一致地取得最大的净防御效用分数，较最强基线平均提升12.1个百分点，并在经验等价准则下产生双赢结果。

Conclusion: VCR机制通过奖励可验证事实内容而非单纯惩罚可疑行为，能有效化解生成式引擎引用竞争中供给者与平台之间的策略性冲突，同时提升答案可信度并实现平台与创作者的双赢。

Abstract: Generative engines are reshaping the web ecosystem by making citations a key mechanism for allocating attention, attribution, and downstream value. This creates a strategic tension: content providers are incentivized to optimize for model citation, while platforms must preserve answer quality and trustworthy attribution. We show that this tension can escalate into citation wars. In repeated simulations, state-of-the-art generative engine optimization (GEO) attacks adapt to conventional defenses by producing citation-seeking rewrites that degrade document quality and introduce unsupported claims. To study this problem, we formulate the supplier--platform interaction as a repeated Stackelberg game with partial monitoring. A local best-response analysis identifies when citation competition approaches an inert stationary outcome. Motivated by this finding, we propose a platform--creator mechanism called VCR based on verifiable-content rewards. Rather than only penalizing suspicious rewrites, the platform also credits rewrites that surface checkable factual substance, aligning creator incentives with answer trustworthiness. Experiments on three benchmarks show that VCR consistently achieves the largest Net defense-utility score, outperforming the strongest baseline by an average of 12.1 percentage points, and produces a win--win outcome under our empirical equivalence criterion.

</details>


### [203] [Unmasking Toxic Mimicry in Medical Offline Reinforcement Learning for ICU Sepsis Management via Counterfactual Clinical Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11410)
*Hangqi Ren,Junyi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实临床审计（CCA）框架，用于检测离线强化学习智能体在ICU治疗决策中的"毒性模仿"失败模式，并通过MIMIC-III数据库验证了该方法能揭露MedDT模型在乳酸升高时错误降低血管升压药剂量、违背复苏指南的问题，而HCT-RL模型则表现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线RL评估指标（MSE和FQE）仅衡量行为模仿程度，无法检测"毒性模仿"——即智能体复制有害治疗模式（如在舒适护理过渡期随意撤药）的失败模式。统计拟合度与临床安全性之间存在系统性错位，亟需新的评估标准。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA)框架，基于脓毒症生存运动（SSC）指南锚定生理扰动，对RL智能体进行压力测试。对两个模型进行审计：Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT)和采用Causal Action Shielding、倾向性重要性加权及Conservative Q-Learning的Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL)。

Result: CCA揭示MedDT在乳酸水平升高时反常地降低血管升压药剂量，与复苏指南相矛盾；而HCT-RL在生理扰动下保持一致的响应。这暴露了统计拟合与临床安全之间的系统性错位。

Conclusion: 反事实审计应作为医疗RL的必要评估标准，仅依赖MSE和FQE等传统指标不足以确保临床安全性。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (RL) offers considerable promise for optimizing ICU treatment decisions, yet standard evaluation metrics Mean Squared Error (MSE) and Fitted Q-Evaluation (FQE) assess only behavioral imitation and cannot detect Toxic Mimicry, a failure mode in which agents replicate harmful patterns such as treatment withdrawal during comfort-care transitions. Using the MIMIC-III database, we propose the Counterfactual Clinical Audit (CCA) framework, which stress-tests RL agents through physiological perturbations anchored in Surviving Sepsis Campaign (SSC) guidelines. We audit a Medical Decision Transformer (MedDT) and a Historical Causal Transformer (HCT-RL), the latter employing Causal Action Shielding, propensity-based importance weighting, and Conservative Q-Learning. CCA reveals that MedDT paradoxically reduces vasopressor dosage as lactate escalates, contradicting resuscitation guidelines, while HCT-RL maintains physiologically consistent responses. These findings expose a systemic misalignment between statistical fit and clinical safety, supporting counterfactual audits as a necessary evaluation standard for medical RL.

</details>


### [204] [Diffusion-Based Data-Driven Assortment Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11419)
*Junyi Liao,Xiaohui Jiang,Zhengwei Tong,Ethan X. Fang,Vahid Tarokh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于引导式离散扩散的模型无关（model-agnostic）集合优化框架，用于收益管理中的集合（assortment）优化问题。该方法将集合表示为二进制向量，通过学习的逆向扩散过程进行随机搜索，并引入奖励引导机制利用期望收益估计来偏置局部转移，有效平衡探索与利用。实验表明该方法在高维设定下能一致地识别高质量集合，对模型误设定具有鲁棒性，并能生成多样化的高性能集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集合优化通常依赖参数化选择模型（如MNL及其变体），虽然计算上可处理，但性能对模型误设定敏感，且难以捕捉复杂的顾客行为。为此，需要一种不依赖于特定参数模型的、更具鲁棒性和表达能力的方法。

Method: 提出模型无关的框架，将集合编码为二进制向量，利用学习到的离散扩散逆向过程进行随机搜索（避免显式组合枚举）。引入奖励引导机制，通过期望收益估计偏置局部转移，从而实现探索与利用的平衡。

Result: 实验显示所提方法能够一致地识别高质量集合，在模型误设定下保持鲁棒性，在高维场景中常能恢复近最优解；同时生成式扩散可产出多样化的高性能集合，超越单一确定性解。

Conclusion: 生成式建模为数据驱动的组合优化提供了一种可扩展且鲁棒的新范式，展示了其在集合优化乃至更广泛的组合优化问题中的潜力。

Abstract: Assortment optimization is a fundamental problem in revenue management, typically addressed using parametric choice models such as the multinomial logit (MNL) and its variants. While these models enable tractable formulations, their performance is sensitive to model misspecification and often struggles to capture complex customer behavior. In this paper, we propose a model-agnostic framework for assortment optimization based on guided discrete diffusion. We represent assortments as binary vectors and perform stochastic search via a learned reverse diffusion process, avoiding explicit combinatorial enumeration. To incorporate decision objectives, we introduce a reward-guided mechanism that biases local transitions using estimates of expected revenue. This allows the method to effectively balance exploration and exploitation during generation. Empirically, we show that the proposed approach consistently identifies high-quality assortments and remains robust under model misspecification, often recovering near-optimal solutions in high-dimensional settings. Moreover, the generative nature of diffusion enables the production of diverse high-performing assortments, offering flexibility beyond a single deterministic solution. These results highlight the potential of generative modeling as a scalable and robust paradigm for combinatorial optimization in data-driven decision-making.

</details>


### [205] [Analysis of Federated Aggregation under Model Poisoning and Backdoor Attacks: A Reconstructed Cross-Dataset and Cross-Architecture Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11423)
*Soumya Mazumdar,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对联邦聚合方法进行了系统性的鲁棒性比较研究，重建了一个500单元的评估矩阵，覆盖五种聚合方法、五个数据集和五种架构。研究确认了Trimmed Mean在干净数据下表现最佳（76.02%准确率），Krum在攻击场景下表现最佳，并通过对BadNets指标和FedPARETO框架的审计揭示了潜在的方法论缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦聚合方法（如FedAvg、Trimmed Mean、Krum等）的鲁棒性比较需要同时考虑预测性能、威胁定义、指标语义和执行来源，但此前缺乏对这些因素进行联合考量的系统评估。作者旨在通过重建大规模评估矩阵并提供审计级别的透明度，确保比较的可靠性和可解释性。

Method: 研究重建了一个500单元（种子1）的评估矩阵，涵盖五种聚合方法、五个数据集、五种架构和四种条件（干净、符号翻转、高斯和BadNets）。对454个原始运行和36个修复/重运行的执行日志进行验证，并对BadNets指标实现和FedPARETO框架进行了独立审计。通过宏平均准确率、组内排名等指标进行相对比较，同时用21个具备完整日志的任务对进行敏感性分析。

Result: Trimmed Mean在干净条件下取得最高宏平均准确率（76.02%）和最低平均组内排名（1.70）；Krum在符号翻转和高斯攻击下取得最高准确率。在限制为21个有完整日志的任务对时，排名保持不变。审计发现BadNets指标实现在目标标签计数前已触发所有测试输入，实际测量的是TTLR而非传统的排除目标的攻击成功率；FedPARETO框架存在预测摘要与聚合更新之间不对应的潜在缺陷。

Conclusion: 由于矩阵中每个单元仅有一个种子，且攻击与配置的完整溯源不完整，本研究的发现应被解释为在记录配置内的描述性比较，而非统计估计或关于鲁棒性的普遍性结论。审计结果同时强调了联邦学习评估中方法实现细节和指标语义验证的重要性。

Abstract: Robust comparisons of federated aggregation methods require joint consideration of predictive performance, threat definitions, metric semantics, and execution provenance. A 500-cell seed-1 evaluation matrix was reconstructed across five aggregation methods, five datasets, five architectures, and four recorded conditions: clean, sign-flipping, Gaussian, and BadNets. Successful execution logs were identified for 454 original runs and 36 repaired or rerun executions, whereas 10 clean SVHN cells were supported by summary-only provenance. Trimmed Mean achieved the highest clean macro-mean accuracy (76.02%) and the lowest mean within-task rank (1.70). Krum attained the highest recorded accuracy under both sign-flipping and Gaussian configurations. These relative rankings remained unchanged when analysis was restricted to 21 task pairs for which original successful logs were available for every method-condition combination. Audit of the supplied BadNets metric implementation established that every test input is triggered prior to target-label counting; consequently, the retained metric represents Triggered Target-Label Rate (TTLR) rather than a conventional target-excluding attack success rate. An audit of the supplied FedPARETO scaffold further identified a pathway in which predictive summaries may characterize an uncorrupted local model while the aggregation weight is applied to a separately corrupted update, introducing a potential discrepancy between reported predictive outcomes and the updates used for aggregation. The canonical matrix contains a single identified seed for each cell, and exact attack and configuration lineage is incomplete. Accordingly, the findings should be interpreted as descriptive comparisons within the recorded configurations and not as statistical estimates or universal claims regarding robustness.

</details>


### [206] [Click2Poly: A VLM for vector mapping buildings and walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11424)
*Nicolas Girard,Jawher Ben Abdallah,Arno Gobbin,Liuyun Duan,Sacha Lepretre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Click2Poly是一个基于人类反馈的AI辅助工具，通过扩展Florence-2视觉语言模型，让用户通过点击直接编辑建筑物和墙壁矢量图层，以QGIS插件形式实现，在真实生产环境中加速人工质检和修正流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑物和墙壁的精确矢量制图对地理空间应用至关重要，但现有深度学习自动提取方法仍无法满足制图标准，需要人工进行质量控制和复杂案例修正，而这一人工步骤劳动密集且效率低下。

Method: 扩展Florence-2视觉语言模型（VLM），使其通过响应点击操作直接编辑建筑或墙壁矢量层，并将系统实现为QGIS插件以集成到真实生产工作流中。

Result: Click2Poly以QGIS插件形式实现，在真实生产环境中成功加速了建筑和墙壁矢量图层的人工编辑流程。

Conclusion: 通过人类在环（human-in-the-loop）的AI辅助方式，Click2Poly有效缩短了深度学习自动提取结果达到制图标准所需的人工修正时间，提升了地理空间矢量制图的整体效率。

Abstract: Accurate vector mapping of buildings and walls is critical for geospatial applications but remains a labor-intensive process. While recent deep learning methods have improved automatic extraction, in order to meet cartographic standards they always require a human to perform quality control and fix complex cases in the extraction. We present Click2Poly, a human-in-the-loop AI assistant designed to speed up this manual step. Extending the Florence-2 Vision Language Model (VLM), Click2Poly responds to user clicks by editing the building or wall vector layer directly. Implemented as a QGIS plugin, Click2Poly speeds up the manual editing of building and wall vector layers in a real-world production environment.

</details>


### [207] [Three Tokens Force Exponential Feature Rank in Nonnegative Kernel Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11427)
*Vicente Opazo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在包含两个竞争候选的首个上下文长度上，全注意力（暴露每个词元对）与核注意力（压缩为固定维度草图）之间存在指数级差距。对于布尔输入上的Min-IP任务，rank-one归一化核注意力能精确解决长度至多为2的序列，但任何能对所有三词元序列实现误差低于1/2的归一化非负核注意力头都需要2^Ω(m)个特征，而稠密softmax用m维分数和常数温度即可解决同一任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解全注意力和核注意力之间的表达能力差距，特别是它们在不同上下文长度下的根本性差异，为注意力机制的近似方法提供理论边界。

Method: 通过构造Min-IP（最小内积）布尔任务，分析rank-one归一化核注意力在短序列上的精确性，并利用信息论和组合论证推导非负核注意力在较长序列（三词元）上的特征维度下界，同时延伸至位置相关词元映射、因果最终查询和确定性多头多层草图模型的传输下界。

Result: 在长度≤2时核注意力可以精确求解，但在长度=3时任何归一化非负核注意力头若想误差<1/2至少需要2^Ω(m)个特征，且该下界随上下文长度增长趋近精确的2^m特征实现；稠密softmax仅需m维和常数温度。此外，有限字母表草图的确定性多头多层模型具有线性于独立答案数、对数于字母表大小的传输下界。

Conclusion: 上下文长度从2增长到3时，核注意力与全注意力之间出现指数级的表达能力鸿沟，这表明固定维度的核压缩机制在应对包含多个竞争候选的更长序列时存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Full attention exposes every token pair, whereas kernel attention compresses a sequence into a fixed-dimensional sketch. We show that this distinction becomes exponential at the first context length containing two competing candidates. On Min-IP over Boolean inputs, rank-one normalized kernel attention solves every sequence of length at most two exactly. In contrast, any single normalized nonnegative kernel-attention head that succeeds on all three-token sequences with error strictly below $1/2$ requires $2^{Ω(m)}$ features, even with arbitrary finite-dimensional tokenwise values and an arbitrary query-dependent affine readout. Dense softmax solves the same task with $m$-dimensional scores and constant temperature. The conclusion survives position-dependent token maps and a causal final query. As context length grows, the lower bound approaches the exact $2^m$-feature realization. Separately, for deterministic multihead, multilayer sketch models whose cross-token channels have finite alphabets, we prove a transcript lower bound linear in the number of independent answers and logarithmic in their alphabet size.

</details>


### [208] [AutoGrable: What Is a Good Graph for a Table?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11431)
*Tamara Cucumides,Floris Geerts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoGrable提出了一种无需训练图模型的准则来自动为表格和关系数据库构建图结构，通过标签对齐风险来评估候选列子集，能够廉价地搜索并选择最优图构建方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格和关系数据库本身不包含图结构，如何为GNN选择合适的图构建方式（哪些实体作为节点、如何连接）是困难的。现有方法依赖人工设计、模式启发式或对每个候选图训练模型，代价高昂且效率低下。

Method: 论文首先建立最小表到图抽象：每行作为节点，消息传递GNN受1-WL约束，图构建的效果取决于对行的颜色细化划分。好的构建应分离不同标签的行且不分裂同标签的行。AutoGrable将构建问题归结为选择列子集（对于关联构建，划分由选择的列固定），并通过标签对齐风险来打分：即在其块上保持恒定的最佳预测器的留出风险，加上衡量块填充稀疏度的占用项惩罚。该分数无需实例化图或训练GNN，因此可以贪心且廉价地搜索子集空间。

Result: 实验表明：AutoGrable在候选图空间中能丢弃大部分候选而保留最佳者；在受控任务中能恢复生成标签的列；在真实任务中在固定预测器下优于固定的、随机的和任务感知的构建方法；并且是唯一能够在不适合建图时主动放弃构建图的方法。

Conclusion: AutoGrable提供了一种高效且无需训练模型的方法来自动为表和图数据库构建图结构，既能找到最优图，又能在图无帮助时拒绝构建，具有显著的实用价值。

Abstract: Graph learning presupposes a graph, and tables and relational databases do not come with one. Applying a GNN to them requires deciding which entities become nodes, which of them to connect, and through which relations---a decision made by hand, by schema heuristics, or by training a model on every candidate graph and keeping the best. We give a criterion that requires no trained graph model. In the minimal table-to-graph abstraction each row is a node, so a message-passing GNN, bounded by 1-WL, sees a construction only as a partition of the rows into colour-refinement classes: a construction is good for a task when that partition separates rows with different labels and does not split rows that share one. AutoGrable turns this criterion into a construction procedure. For incidence constructions the partition is fixed by the selected columns, so building a graph reduces to choosing them, and we score a candidate subset by a label-alignment risk: the held-out risk of the best predictor constant on its blocks, penalised by an occupancy term measuring how thinly the blocks are populated. The score materialises no graph and trains no GNN, so AutoGrable can search the space of subsets greedily and cheaply, and returns the resulting grable for single tables and for foreign-key schemas alike. Our experiments show that over a space of candidate graphs the score discards a large fraction while retaining the best; that AutoGrable recovers the columns that generate the label on controlled tasks and outperforms fixed, random, and task-aware constructors on real tasks under a fixed predictor; and that it is the only method compared that can decline to build a graph when none helps.

</details>


### [209] [Variational Parameter Calibration with Physics-Aware Latent-Space Surrogates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11435)
*Qiyao Zhou,Xujia Zhu,Pierre Joli,Yu Cong,Sibo Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理感知神经网络的潜空间框架，将降阶前向建模与变分参数估计端到端耦合，在CFD基准上验证了其相比标准代理模型在参数校准精度和鲁棒性上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数动力系统的前向与逆向建模需要代理模型既能准确预测状态，又能为参数校准提供信息。然而，目前缺乏将基于深度学习的降阶代理与变分参数估计进行系统性端到端可微耦合的方法。重构精度高并不足以保证逆向建模有效，因此需要一种物理感知且端到端可微的框架来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 提出一个基于自编码器的潜空间框架：(1) 前向路径通过潜表示建立从物理参数到预测流场的可微代理映射；(2) 离线训练阶段施加可观测监督（observable supervision），促使潜变量保留与系统参数相关的信息；(3) 在线逆向问题在参数空间中通过代理诱导的观测算子进行变分参数估计，实现端到端可微的校准过程。

Result: 在两个CFD基准上：(1) 重构精度单独不足以支撑逆向建模，因缺少端到端可微性或物理感知；(2) 可观测监督提升了潜表示的案例间可分性与时间组织性；(3) 在噪声、低分辨率、随机掩蔽、块状部分观测等现实测量条件下，该框架较标准代理模型普遍降低了校准误差与变异性。

Conclusion: 所提出的物理感知潜空间框架有效实现了降阶前向建模与变分参数估计的一体化，在复杂测量条件下具有更强的鲁棒性和更优的参数校准性能，为参数动力系统的逆向建模提供了可微、物理感知的可靠方案。

Abstract: Forward and inverse modeling of parametric dynamical systems requires surrogate models that are not only accurate for state prediction, but also informative for parameter calibration. However, a systematic end-to-end differentiable formulation for coupling deep-learning-based reduced-order surrogates with variational parameter estimation remains underdeveloped. In this work, we introduce a physics-aware neural-network-based latent-space framework for reduced-order forward modeling and variational parameter estimation. The proposed autoencoder-based approach yields a differentiable surrogate that maps physical parameters to predicted flow fields through a latent representation. The observable supervision is used during offline training to encourage the latent variables to retain information correlated with system parameters, while the online inverse problem is solved in the parameter space through the surrogate-induced observation operator. The method is evaluated on two computational-fluid-dynamics benchmarks. The results show that reconstruction accuracy alone is insufficient for inverse modeling, owing to the lack of end-to-end differentiability or physics awareness for variational parameter calibration. Quantitative latent-space analysis further shows that observable supervision improves case-level separability and temporal organization of latent representations. Experiments with realistic measurement settings, including noisy, low-resolution, randomly masked, and block-wise partial observations, demonstrate the robustness of the proposed framework and show that it generally reduces calibration error and variability compared with the standard surrogate models.

</details>


### [210] [XGBoost "is all you need": the case of forecasting transmitted heat energy in District Heating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11446)
*Milan Zdravković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了XGBoost和LSTM两种方法在区域供热系统（DHS）传递热能预测任务中的表现，结果表明XGBoost在该特定时序预测任务中持续优于LSTM，同时具有更低的计算成本和更小的环境足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在时间序列预测中传统机器学习算法相对于深度学习网络的优势场景，并评估其在计算成本和环境影响方面的附带收益，为能源系统数据预测任务的模型选择提供依据。

Method: 在真实世界区域供热系统数据集上，对XGBoost（传统机器学习方法）和LSTM（深度学习网络）两种方法进行对比实验，并通过误差分布分析解释两者性能差异的潜在原因。

Result: XGBoost在DHS传递热能预测任务中始终优于LSTM；误差分布显示LSTM在数据可用性较低的区间会产生更显著的预测误差；传统ML方法的低计算需求带来了成本节省并减少了碳排放。

Conclusion: 在特定时间序列预测场景下，传统机器学习方法可以超越深度学习模型，且在计算效率和环境保护方面具有显著优势，值得在能源系统预测任务中优先考虑。

Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of two distinct approaches, XGBoost and Long-Short Term Memory (LSTM), for forecasting transmitted heat energy in District Heating Systems (DHS). The objective is to explore scenarios in which conventional ML algorithms demonstrate better performance over deep learning networks in time series forecasting and the associated benefits in terms of computational cost and environmental impact. The study focuses on a real-world DHS dataset. Through experimentation and analysis, it is demonstrated that XGBoost consistently outperforms LSTM in this specific forecasting task. The difference is explained by the error distribution illustrating that LSTM makes more significant errors in the intervals of less data availability. The reduced computational demands of conventional ML approaches not only result in cost savings but also minimize the carbon footprint associated with data analysis tasks in energy systems.

</details>


### [211] [PAC-Bayes Beyond Parameter Space: Behavioral Equivalence, Z-Information, and Exact Complexity Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11465)
*Vasant G. Honavar,Satish Kumar Keshri,Neil Ashtekar,Zehao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从行为等价性出发，通过测度分解将经典PAC-Bayes KL散度精确分解为行为选择项和实现级项，定义Z-信息量化两者差距，并证明行为选择项具有变分刻画和规范化代表元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典PAC-Bayes理论用假设表示空间上的KL散度控制泛化误差，但预测风险只取决于假设的预测行为，而非其内部实现。在过参数化系统中，大量不同配置产生相同预测行为，经典KL散度无法区分行为层面的不确定性与行为等价实现之间的变化，因此需要重新审视PAC-Bayes复杂度的结构。

Method: 通过可测行为映射形式化行为等价性，利用测度分解（measure disintegration）将配置空间上的概率测度分解为预测行为上的分布和行为纤维上的条件分布。由此将经典PAC-Bayes KL散度精确分解为行为选择项与实现级项（纤维内期望条件KL）。定义Z-信息为实现级贡献的负值，并证明行为选择项可通过纤维对称化的规范代表元达到最小KL的变分刻画。

Result: 得到了经典PAC-Bayes KL散度的精确结构分解；定义了Z-信息量化行为复杂度与总KL散度之间的精确间隙；证明了行为选择项的最小KL变分刻画由规范纤维对称化代表元实现；展示了对称性、行为保持方向、纤维几何等结构均自然来源于行为映射结构。

Conclusion: 预测行为应被视为PAC-Bayes复杂度的自然对象。行为等价性带来的KL散度冗余可以通过Z-信息量化并剥离，从而实现更精确的泛化界和更清晰的复杂度解释。

Abstract: PAC-Bayes theory provides generalization guarantees by controlling the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between posterior and prior distributions over a chosen hypothesis representation. However, predictive risk depends only on the predictive behavior induced by a hypothesis, not on the particular internal realization that implements that behavior. In over-parameterized systems, many distinct configurations induce identical predictive behavior, yet the classical PAC-Bayes KL divergence does not distinguish uncertainty over predictive behavior from variation among behaviorally equivalent realizations.
  We show that this distinction induces an exact structural decomposition of classical PAC-Bayes complexity. We formalize behavioral equivalence through a measurable behavior map and use measure disintegration to decompose probability measures on the configuration space into a distribution over predictive behaviors and conditional distributions over behavioral fibers. This yields an exact decomposition of the classical PAC-Bayes KL divergence into a behavior-selection term and a realization-level term given by an expected conditional KL within fibers.
  We define Z-information as the negative of this realization-level contribution: the exact gap between the KL divergence and the complexity of uncertainty over predictive behavior alone. We further show that the behavior-selection term admits an exact variational characterization: it is the minimum KL divergence among all posteriors inducing the same distribution over predictive behaviors, attained by a canonical fiber-symmetrized representative.
  Finally, we show that symmetry, behavior-preserving directions, fiber geometry, and invariance under fiber-preserving perturbations arise naturally from the same behavior-map structure. Together, these results identify predictive behavior as the natural object of PAC-Bayes complexity.

</details>


### [212] [Dual-Primal Graph VAEs for Noisy Label Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11473)
*Patrick Stinson,Nikolaus Kriegeskorte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图变分自编码器（Graph VAE）的众包标签聚合模型，利用GAT消息传递机制在众包数据及其对偶图的邻接结构上进行编码和解码，将真实标签视为潜在变量进行无监督表示学习，在众包基准上达到SOTA性能，并能通过注入外部信息进一步提升分类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从含噪众包标签中推断真实标签是重要问题。虽然神经网络方法避免了传统贝叶斯模型需要指定生成模型族的限制，但现有方法要么仍依赖较简单的生成模型进行推断，要么需要伪标签或合成数据训练聚合分类器。因此需要一种无需这些限制的端到端学习方法。

Method: 提出图VAE架构：解码器使用GAT消息传递在众包数据集的邻接图上工作，编码器在其对偶图上工作。将真实标签建模为潜在变量，实现无需单独分类器的无监督表示学习。原始众包图可通过加入侧信息（如基于噪声标签训练的分类器表征）进行增强。

Result: 在众包标准基准上达到state-of-the-art性能；通过将神经网络分类器的表示作为侧信息注入图结构后，能显著提升分类准确率。

Conclusion: 基于图VAE的众包标签聚合方法兼具理论优雅性和实用通用性，能有效处理真实标签推断问题，且易于扩展整合外部信息以进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Inferring the ground-truth from noisy crowdsourced labels is an important theoretical and practical problem. Neural network-based methods offer an alternative to classical Bayesian models which require specifying a family of generative models used for inference. However, current models either still rely on fairly simple generative models for inference or require pseudo-labels or synthetic data to train the aggregate classifier.
