We further reveal that token-specific expert usefulness exhibits a compact low-dimensional structure. Based on these findings, we propose TradingMoE, a trading-oriented sparse MoE that augments a frozen dense LLM with lightweight residual experts. We introduce a Query-Key router that represents the expertise required by each token under the current market context as a low-dimensional query and matches it with learnable expert keys. We further propose a sparse expert selection update mechanism that samples a few inactive experts during training and estimates whether they should replace the weakest expert in the current Top-k route. This mechanism enables the router to update expert selection as market conditions change while preserving sparse computation. Experiments against 22 baselines on stock and cryptocurrency markets show that TradingMoE improves cumulative return over the best-performing baselines by 30.89% and 30.7%, respectively. Rolling paper-trading experiments further demonstrate that its advantage persists under forward-only deployment.

</details>


### [246] [High-Order Liquid Evidence Encoding for Gradual GNSS Spoofing Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11790)
*Muhammad Ayub Sabir,Junbiao Pang,Fatima Ashraf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种因果高阶液态证据框架（causal high-order liquid evidence framework）用于检测GNSS欺骗攻击，通过构建物理引导的GNSS-运动不一致残差及其一阶、二阶离散变化作为多流证据，利用自适应液态编码器分层耦合时序状态进行欺骗检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续且隐蔽的GNSS欺骗攻击难以检测，因为单个GNSS观测值可能保持合理，但GNSS隐含位移与车辆自身运动之间的不一致性逐渐累积。现有方法依赖静态车辆行为特征或单一残差信号，未显式建模这种演化过程。

Method: 首先构建物理引导的GNSS-运动不一致残差（比较GNSS隐含位移与车载运动推导位移），然后形成残差水平及其一阶、二阶离散变化的独立证据流，并根据证据阶次选择相关上下文线索。每条流由独立的自适应液态编码器处理，生成的时间状态被分层耦合，仅利用当前和过去观测预测窗口终点的欺骗事件。

Result: 在AV-GPS数据集三个子集上，方法在Dataset 1和Dataset 3上取得最高F1分数，分别达到0.9535和0.9777。在Dataset 3上，能够在四个采样步内检测到两个标注的正常到攻击转换。

Conclusion: 通过对残差的高阶时间演化进行显式建模并采用液态编码器进行自适应时序处理，该方法能有效检测连续隐蔽的GNSS欺骗攻击，优于现有时序模型。

Abstract: Accurate Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS)-based localization is essential for safe and reliable autonomous driving. However, spoofing attacks can manipulate vehicle position estimates. Continuous and subtle attacks are particularly difficult to detect because individual GNSS observations may remain plausible while the inconsistency between GNSS-implied displacement and onboard vehicle motion gradually increases. Existing methods often rely on static vehicle-behavior features or a single residual signal and do not explicitly model this evolution. To address this problem, we propose a causal high-order liquid evidence framework for GNSS spoofing detection. The method first constructs a physics-guided GNSS--motion inconsistency residual by comparing GNSS-implied displacement with onboard-motion-derived displacement. It then forms separate evidence streams for the residual level and its first- and second-order discrete variations, with relevant contextual cues selected according to the evidence order. Each stream is processed by a separate adaptive liquid encoder, and the resulting temporal states are hierarchically coupled to predict spoofing at the window endpoint using only current and past observations. Experiments on three subsets of the real-world AV-GPS dataset show that the proposed method achieves the highest F1-scores among the evaluated temporal models on Dataset~1 and Dataset~3, reaching 0.9535 and 0.9777, respectively. On Dataset~3, it detects both labeled normal-to-attack transitions within four sampling steps. Code and datasets are publicly available at: https://github.com/pangjunbiao/GNSS_Spoofing.git.

</details>


### [247] [Orientation, not magnitude: the causal structure of task-vector interference in merged language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11797)
*Chencheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Model merging by task arithmetic works until it doesn't, and the field diagnoses why with magnitudes: layerwise representation bias, deviations from cross-task linearity, parameter overlap. Tracking the exact layerwise cross-term of merged LLMs through a factorial ledger and intervening on it directly, we find magnitude insufficient - and inconsistent across model families - as a diagnostic axis. An exact decomposition of the layerwise flux shows it is dominated by amplifying transport of the existing cross-term (~65-70% in both families, gain >1 per late block), and erasing the term is undone by propagation - rebuilt to 99% of its norm at cosine 0.99 - unless applied near the output; a basin test with six starting displacements establishes the carried direction as an attractor of the forward pass. That direction is causally load-bearing: erasure along it removes expressed interference dose-dependently and saturates at exact erasure, while norm-matched wrong-direction controls fail or backfire. Instruction wrappers gate the effect: the same erasure finds 13x less relative interference to remove under a wrapper that internally amplifies the cross-term, because the wrapper drowns the interaction in a template-pinned main effect rather than shrinking it - a structure that replicates across further instruction templates but not under a length-matched control. Magnitude, by contrast, is at best a coarse correlate, and the striking +-15% "universality" of naive bfloat16 generation turns out to be quantization roughness. Task pairs whose local cross-term generation differs by at most 1.9x differ by 14x-337x in causally removable interference. All 46 predictions were preregistered and frozen before their data; falsifications, including of our own headline expectations and of behavioral recovery under a validated continuous endpoint, are reported as such.

</details>


### [248] [JAPE: Joint Anomaly Prediction and Intrinsic Explanation in Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11801)
*Yian Wei,Yuanyuan Yao,Lu Chen,Xiangmin Zhou,Tianyi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAPE是一个联合异常预测与解释框架，将多元时间序列异常预测从数值偏差建模提升到依赖结构建模，通过解耦时空表示、双视角预警机制和原生预测性解释，在五个真实基准上平均F1提升19.7%、AUC-PR提升41.3%、MRR提升26.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多元时间序列异常预测方法仅将异常表征为未来数值偏差，一方面会忽略由微弱异常前兆引发的细微依赖关系变化，另一方面无法在预警时提供原生的变量级解释。这两个差距阻碍了异常预测的准确性和可解释性。

Method: JAPE包含三个核心设计：(i) 解耦时空表示(DSTR)骨干网络，将时间与空间建模解耦，通过可学习的滞后聚合捕获滞后感知的依赖关系，在数值偏差出现前感知结构前兆；(ii) 双视角预警机制，融合数值预测与演化的依赖图进行逐点异常预测，即使在数值偏差微弱时也能捕获结构证据；(iii) 原生预测性解释(NPE)，直接复用预测的依赖图，无需额外模型或训练，即按结构偏差对变量进行排序以实现解释。

Result: 在五个真实世界基准、三个预测跨度上的实验表明，JAPE的平均F1和AUC-PR分别提升19.7%和41.3%，解释性的MRR指标提升26.6%。

Conclusion: JAPE证明了将异常预测从数值偏差建模提升到依赖结构建模的有效性，能够同时实现更准确的逐点预警和原生的变量级解释，为多元时间序列异常预测提供了新的建模范式。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series anomaly prediction aims to identify whether and when anomalies will occur over a future horizon from historical observations. Existing methods primarily characterize anomalies as deviations in future numerical values, which may overlook subtle dependency changes induced by weak anomaly precursors and provide no native variable-level explanation together with the alert. To bridge these gaps, we propose JAPE, a Joint Anomaly Prediction and Explanation framework that lifts anomaly prediction from numerical-deviation modeling to dependency-structure modeling. JAPE is the first anomaly prediction framework to explicitly model evolving dependency structures for both point-wise alerting and native variable-level explanation. Specifically, JAPE (i) proposes a Decoupled Spatio-Temporal Representation (DSTR) backbone that decouples temporal and spatial modeling and captures lag-aware dependencies via learnable lag aggregation, thereby perceiving structural precursors before numerical deviations emerge; (ii) designs a dual-view alerting mechanism that fuses numerical forecasts with evolving dependency graphs for point-wise anomaly prediction, capturing structural evidence even under subtle numerical deviations; and (iii) presents Native Predictive Explanation (NPE), which directly reuses the predicted dependency graphs to rank variables by structural deviations without additional models or training. Extensive experiments on five real-world benchmarks across three prediction horizons demonstrate that JAPE improves average F1 and AUC-PR by 19.7% and 41.3%, respectively, while improving explainability with 26.6% gain in MRR.

</details>


### [249] [Learning with Bilevel-Minimax Optimization for Efficient and Reliable Transfer Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11815)
*Yaohua Liu,Yifan Guo,Jiaxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BMAT（Bilevel-Minimax Adversarial Transfer）框架，从双层次极小极大优化视角重新审视基于迁移的黑盒对抗攻击，通过联合优化初始化、代理模型适应和梯度动力学实现更强的跨架构迁移性，并在30余个受害模型上超越10多个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移性对抗攻击的成功不仅取决于扰动生成，更根本地受初始化、代理模型适应与梯度动力学三者耦合的支配。现有方法未从统一的优化框架视角看待这三者间的依赖关系，缺乏理论指导。

Method: 提出BMAT框架：外层双层次（bilevel）公式化刻画初始化与扰动之间的依赖；内层极小极大（minimax）问题增强代理模型鲁棒性以促进跨架构泛化。算法上设计自底向上的集成求解器，包含Soft Weight Modulator和Implicit Gradient Approximator，实现初始化、代理适应和扰动优化三者的三元耦合，并提供该框架优化动力学的理论见解。

Result: 在分类和分割基准上，BMAT在30多个受害模型上超越10多个强基线，提升了架构内和跨架构迁移性，并在mIoU指标上实现高达2倍的降低。

Conclusion: BMAT通过双层次极小极大框架有机地统一了初始化、代理模型适应和扰动优化三者，为迁移性对抗攻击提供了一种更具理论和实践价值的新范式。

Abstract: Transfer-based adversarial attacks craft adversarial examples using surrogate models to mislead black-box victim models. Beyond perturbation generation, transferability is fundamentally governed by the coupling of initialization, surrogate adaptation, and gradient dynamics. We revisit this challenge from a bilevel-minimax perspective and propose BMAT (Bilevel-Minimax Adversarial Transfer). The bilevel formulation captures the dependency between initialization and perturbation, while the inner minimax problem promotes surrogate robustness for cross-architecture generalization. Algorithmically, we develop an integrated bottom-up solver that combines a Soft Weight Modulator and an Implicit Gradient Approximator to enable ternary coupling among initialization, surrogate adaptation, and perturbation optimization. We further provide theoretical insights into the optimization dynamics of the proposed bilevel-minimax framework. Extensive experiments on classification and segmentation benchmarks show that BMAT outperforms more than 10 strong baselines across more than 30 victim models, improving both intra- and cross-architecture transfer and yielding up to a 2x reduction in mIoU. Code is available at https://github.com/callous-youth/BMAT.

</details>


### [250] [Towards Understanding On-Policy Distillation through the Lens of Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11829)
*Xinmu Ge,Zizhuo Zhang,Yu Huang,Jianing Zhu,Lin Yuan,Wanli Gu,Weichang Wu,Weiran Huang,Xiaolu Zhang,Bo Han,Jun Zhou,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究从测试时扩展（test-time scaling）的角度审视同策略蒸馏（OPD）对大语言模型推理能力的影响。通过变化采样预算K并评估pass@K和avg@K，发现OPD训练模型主要提升了采样效率而非真正扩展推理能力边界，表现为"幻觉式蒸馏"（illusory distillation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍认为OPD能让学生模型从更强的教师模型中蒸馏知识，从而扩展超越基础模型的能力。本研究旨在通过测试时扩展的视角重新审视这一假设，用pass@K和avg@K等指标系统性地考察OPD是否真正扩展了学生的推理能力边界。

Method: 采用测试时扩展框架，变化采样预算K，使用pass@K和avg@K两个指标评估多个OPD变体。具体包括：1）比较OPD训练模型与基础模型在不同采样预算下的性能差异；2）追踪OPD训练过程中pass@K的动态变化；3）以pass@1024为判据进行问题级可解性分析，分类统计"原本可解决变为不可解决"和"原本不可解决变为可解决"的问题数量。

Result: 1）OPD训练模型在avg@K上随采样预算K保持优势，但pass@K的优势随K增大逐渐转向基础模型；2）OPD训练过程中的pass@K动态显示出向小K性能增强、以大K能力边界为代价的渐进转变；3）问题级可解性分析显示不对称性：OPD导致更多原本可解问题变为不可解（多于原本不可解问题变为可解的数量）。

Conclusion: OPD本质上类似"幻觉式蒸馏"——其表面上的性能提升主要来源于采样效率的改善，而非从教师模型中获得真正新的推理能力。OPD并未一致性地扩展学生的推理能力边界，这挑战了既有认知。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as a promising post-training technique for enhancing LLM reasoning. It is commonly believed to enable the student model to distill knowledge from a stronger teacher model, thereby expanding capabilities beyond the pre-OPD base model. In this study, we examine this view through the lens of test-time scaling by varying the sampling budget K and evaluating performance with pass@K and avg@K. Specifically, across several OPD variants, we observe that OPD-trained models maintain superior avg@K performance across sampling budgets, while the advantage in pass@K gradually shifts to the pre-OPD base models as K increases. These results suggest that OPD primarily improves sampling efficiency rather than consistently expanding the student's reasoning capability boundary. The pass@K dynamics throughout OPD training further reveal a progressive shift toward stronger small-K performance at the expense of the large-K capability boundary. Furthermore, a problem-level solvability analysis using pass@1024 as the criterion reveals an asymmetry: OPD causes more previously solvable problems to become unsolvable than previously unsolvable problems to become solvable. Together, these findings suggest that, from the perspective of capability expansion, OPD behaves more like an "illusory distillation": its apparent gains arise primarily from improved sampling efficiency rather than from acquiring genuinely new reasoning capabilities from the teacher.

</details>


### [251] [Kernel Methods for Learning Operators with Multiple Inputs and Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11831)
*Adrien Weihs,Chunyang Liao,Jingmin Sun,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核的通用编码器-解码器框架用于算子学习，支持多输入多输出及多算子场景，在理论保证下实现闭式训练和推理，并通过KernelMO方法在五类参数化PDE上取得与神经算子竞争或更优的精度，同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限维对象之间的映射学习是科学机器学习中的核心挑战，现有神经算子方法虽性能优异但训练和推理成本高昂，缺乏将观测、表示、学习和重构分离的统一框架，且难以有效扩展到多输入多输出及多算子学习场景。

Method: 提出一个通用的基于核的编码器-解码器框架，将算子学习解耦为观测、表示、学习和重构四个模块。该框架支持多输入多输出算子学习（算子映射于不同函数空间的乘积之间），并进一步专门化为KernelMO方法族，包含算子值核和积空间核两种互补的表述形式，实现闭式训练与推理。

Result: 近似理论表明，即使输入输出数量增加，收敛速率由最具挑战性的组成逼近问题决定，而非整体问题维度。在五类参数化偏微分方程（PDE）族上，所提方法达到竞争性或最先进的预测精度，同时相对于神经算子架构和基于深度学习的方法显著降低了训练与推理成本。

Conclusion: 该核方法框架在数学可处理性与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，为神经算子提供了一种高效且轻量级的替代方案，尤其适用于多输入多输出和多算子学习的科学计算场景。

Abstract: Learning mappings between infinite-dimensional objects is a central challenge in scientific machine learning. We introduce a general kernel-based encoder-decoder framework for operator learning that separates observation, representation, learning, and reconstruction. We develop this framework for multi-input, multi-output operator learning, where operators map between products of potentially distinct function spaces. Our approximation theory shows that, although the number of inputs and outputs can increase, the convergence rate is governed by the most challenging constituent approximation problem rather than the overall problem dimension. The framework leads to practical kernel methods with closed-form training and inference, combining mathematical tractability with computational efficiency. We further specialize the approach to multiple operator learning by introducing KernelMO, a family of kernel methods with complementary operator-valued and product-space formulations. Across five families of parametric partial differential equations, the proposed methods achieve competitive or state-of-the-art predictive accuracy while reducing training and inference costs relative to neural operator architectures and deep learning based models, offering an efficient and lightweight alternative.

</details>


### [252] [Air Quality Station Simulation via LSTM and Attention-Based Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11839)
*Alexander Kostadinov,Petar O. Hristov,Dessislava Petrova-Antonova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SATADL，一种用于模拟失效空气质量监测站数据的深度学习模型，可输出多小时超前（最长48小时）的空气污染（PM10）预测。该模型在四个全球空气质量站数据集上与多种基线及已发表的深度学习模型进行对比，在决定系数和均方根误差上均表现更优，可作为虚拟代理站使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市空气质量监测站网络依赖硬件持续正常运行以提供可靠数据，但硬件故障或断电会导致数据缺失，影响后续分析质量。现有方法无法充分推断复杂关系来填补长达48小时的数据空缺，因此需要一种能高效模拟失效站点数据的模型。

Method: SATADL是一种深度学习架构，能够从数据的不同方面提取有用信息，输出多步超前（multi-hour-ahead）的空气质量预测。模型架构各部分被详细描述并对所有组件进行了系统评估。实验通过人为模拟单站48小时故障场景，在四组来自全球的空气质量站数据上预测PM10浓度，并与基线和已发表的深度学习模型进行基准比较。

Result: SATADL在不同预测窗口下均优于对比模型，在决定系数（R²）和均方根误差（RMSE）两个指标上均表现更佳，证明其作为虚拟代理站的适切性。

Conclusion: SATADL能够可靠地模拟故障站点的空气质量数据，显著优于现有模型，在城市空气质量监测系统中可作为一种有效的虚拟代理站方案，保障数据连续性和分析质量。

Abstract: Poor air quality in urban areas is driven by a complex chain of processes and presents a significant public health concern. To better understand and control the mechanisms that determine air quality, cities deploy networks of measurement stations, and launch initiatives for collecting denser data about the concentration of pollutants in the atmosphere. Extracting insights from the stations relies on their reliable and uninterrupted operation. However, hardware is susceptible to faults and black- outs that may result in data unavailability, which affects the overall quality of analyses. In this paper, we present a deep-learning model, called SATADL, which can infer complex relations and output multiple-hour-ahead air-quality forecasts. The goal of the model is to simulate the mea- surements of an unresponsive station until its operation is restored. The architecture of the model, which allows it to extract information from different aspects of the data, is described in detail and a careful examination of all of its components is provided. We demonstrate the performance of SATADL on four sets of air quality stations from around the world, by using it to simulate the concentration of PM10 for periods of hypothetical failures of one of the measurement stations, lasting for as long as 48 hours. A selection of baseline and published deep learning models were trained and used as a benchmark. The results show that SATADL per- forms better across different prediction windows, for both coefficient of determination and root mean squared error, demonstrating its suitability as a virtual proxy station.

</details>


### [253] [Small-Scale Experiments: Are We There Yet?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11859)
*Nicholas Lourie,Kyunghyun Cho,Karen Ullrich,Sanae Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出小规模模型中缩放定律（scaling laws）失效的根本原因是超参数敏感性，而非模型规模本身。通过消融实验证明，调优的超参数比其他任何因素都重要，且随着规模增大，超参数损失面维度降低、敏感性减弱，使缩放定律仅在完全调优的前沿上显现。作者提出一种模型中心研究的新方法论，并在Transformer归一化层放置问题上验证了该方法能从少量小规模实验恢复大规模结论（pre-norm随规模增长效果更好）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缩放定律承诺了成本效益高的实验，但六年后仍未完全兑现。研究者发现其在4M参数起的小规模上不可靠，得出"无法避免大模型"的结论。作者认为这一结论是错误的，真正的混杂因素是超参数——小模型对超参数高度敏感，但这种敏感性随规模增大而减弱，导致缩放定律被掩盖。

Method: 1) 通过消融"基本缩放定律配方"，证明调优超参数比其他任何成分都重要；2) 揭示随规模增大，超参数损失面维度降低，使超参数更易寻找；3) 将研究洞察与近期文献综合，开发一套模型中心研究的新方法论；4) 在Transformer归一化层放置问题上进行实证演示。

Result: 从小规模实验中成功恢复了大规模结论：pre-normalization随模型规模增长表现更好。证明只要正确调优超参数，缩放定律在小规模上是存在的，但外推存在统计极限，需要整体性的方法。

Conclusion: 小规模实验在正确工具和充分理解超参数行为的条件下，能够兑现缩放定律长期承诺的预测价值。研究者不应放弃小规模实验，而应认识到超参数调优是让缩放定律显现的关键前提，并采用综合性的模型中心研究方法。

Abstract: Scaling laws promised cost-effective experiments; six years later, they have yet to fully deliver. Instead, researchers have found them unreliable at small scales (starting at 4M parameters) and concluded that sizable models cannot be avoided. We show this is not the case: the confounding factor is hyperparameters. Small models are highly sensitive, but hyperparameter sensitivity fades with scale. This small-scale sensitivity makes scaling laws easy to miss because they only emerge on the fully tuned frontier, and reaching that frontier requires an extensive search far beyond what most ever run. By ablating the basic scaling law recipe, we show well-tuned hyperparameters matter more than any other ingredient. Further, we reveal why those hyperparameters become easier to find: as scale increases, the hyperparameter loss surface becomes lower dimensional. Nevertheless while scaling laws exist in small models, extrapolation hits statistical limitations. A holistic approach is required. Synthesizing our insights with the recent literature, we develop a new methodology for model-centric research and demonstrate it on a question that once took the field years to settle: where to place normalization layers in the transformer architecture. From small-scale experiments, we recover the large scale result: pre-normalization works better as models grow in size. With the right tools and a better understanding, small-scale experiments can deliver on scaling laws' long-awaited promise.

</details>


### [254] [Forward and Inverse Virtual Metrology for Phototransistor Gain: A Hierarchical, Uncertainty-Aware Approach for Small Production Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11868)
*Mahshid Amirabgir,Lorenza Ferrario,Paolo Conci,Mahdieh Amirabgir,Giancarlo Orengo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究硅双极光电晶体管制造流程中的增益预测问题。基于真实制造历史中的13-14次工艺运行（小样本、层级结构数据），发现器件增益方差中约一半来自工艺运行间的差异而非运行内差异，因此仅基于配方的预测存在固有上限。作者提供了前向增益预测器（具有相对的不确定性感知信号）、反向搜索（根据目标增益返回配方），以及以多层级数据质量评估为基础（针对批次、晶圆、芯片的嵌套物理实体，含显式跨层级关联评分）的完整方案，并公开了标准化数据集和分析代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅双极光电晶体管的工艺流定制、优化和稳定化需要数月洁净室时间才能测量成品器件，因此需要一种能在工艺运行前根据工艺参数预测器件增益的模型，其价值远超模型本身精度。目前缺乏针对这种小样本、层级结构数据的研究，传统虚拟计量的大语料库范式不适用。

Method: 对真实制造历史中13-14次工艺运行数据，首先进行器件增益的方差分解（区分运行间与运行内方差），然后构建三层方法：(1) 前向增益预测器，提供相对且不确定性感知的信号；(2) 反向搜索，根据目标增益返回相应配方；(3) 多层级数据质量评估，针对制造中的嵌套物理实体（批次、晶圆、芯片）设计，包含显式跨层级关联评分。

Result: 方差分解表明约一半的增益方差存在于工艺运行之间而非运行之内，证明仅基于配方的预测存在固有上限。所提供的前向预测器具有相对、不确定性感知的信号，反向搜索可返回目标增益对应的配方，数据质量评估框架覆盖批次-晶圆-芯片三层嵌套结构。

Conclusion: 该工作在真实制造历史的小样本、层级结构数据上建立了从数据质量评估到前向预测再到反向搜索的完整方案体系，并通过公开标准化数据集和分析代码实现完全可复现性，为该类成本高昂的半导体制造工艺优化问题提供了实用方法论。

Abstract: The customization, optimization and stabilization of the process flow of a silicon bipolar phototransistor commits months of cleanroom time before a finished device can be measured, so a model that predicts device gain from process parameters before a run has value out of proportion to its accuracy. We study this problem on a real fabrication history, thirteen to fourteen process runs of a single device: a small-sample, hierarchically structured setting unlike the large-corpus regime of conventional virtual metrology. Decomposing the variance of device gain, we find that roughly half of it lies between process runs rather than within them, so recipe-only prediction is bounded by construction. Building on these findings we provide a forward gain predictor with a relative, uncertainty-aware signal, an inverse search that returns recipes for a target gain, and, as the foundation for all of it, a multi-level data-quality assessment tailored to the nested physical entities of fabrication (batch, wafer, die) with an explicit cross-level linkage score. The normalized dataset and analysis code are released for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [255] [DCM Bandits: Multiplayer Information Asymmetric Cascading Bandits for Multiple Clicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11873)
*Andy Wang,Charlton Shih,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将依赖点击模型（DCM）Bandits扩展至多玩家信息不对称场景，多个代理与共享排名列表交互并观察多次点击，研究了行动与奖励两方面的不对称性，为三种至少存在一种不对称性的设置提供了次线性遗憾界，并证明在小终止概率下无需知道终止排名，优于先前单代理结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DCM Bandits研究主要集中于单代理设置，而现实中的推荐系统、搜索引擎等场景往往涉及多个用户（代理）同时与共享的、排名列表交互，且不同代理可能具有不同的行为模式（行动不对称）和反馈机制（奖励不对称）。这种多代理信息不对称设置引入了新的选择策略挑战，目前尚缺乏系统的理论保证。

Method: 论文将DCM Bandits扩展至多玩家信息不对称环境，系统性地定义了两种不对称维度：（1）行动不对称——代理间可能具有不同的动作集合或选择约束；（2）奖励不对称——代理间的点击奖励结构不同。针对三种至少存在一种不对称性的设置分别设计了算法并推导了次线性遗憾上界。此外，论文证明了在小终止概率条件下，代理无需知晓终止排名（termination ranking），从而简化了算法要求并改进了先前的单代理结果。

Result: 为三种不对称设置建立了次线性遗憾保证；证明了小终止概率下终止排名可被未知处理，超越了先前单代理研究的结论；实验验证了所提算法在各类不对称环境中的有效性，并揭示了反馈结构（完整反馈与首次点击反馈）在协调探索与最小化遗憾中的关键作用。

Conclusion: 多代理信息不对称DCM Bandits的次线性遗憾是可达的，且反馈结构的类型对探索协调策略有决定性影响。匹配的信息论下界仍为开放问题，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the Dependent Click Model (DCM) Bandits to a multiplayer information-asymmetric setting, where multiple agents interact with a shared ranked list and may observe multiple clicks per session, introducing new challenges for selection strategies. We study asymmetry in (1) actions and (2) rewards, providing sublinear regret guarantees for three settings where at least one asymmetry is present. Establishing matching information-theoretic lower bounds for these settings is left as an open problem. We further show that for small termination probabilities, the termination ranking need not be known, improving on prior single-agent results. Experiments confirm that our algorithms perform well across asymmetric environments and highlight the critical role of feedback structure, specifically the distinction between full versus first-click feedback, in coordinating exploration and minimizing regret.

</details>


### [256] [Disentangling the Expressivity of RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11909)
*Selim Jerad,Anej Svete,Jiaoda Li,Ryan Cotterell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对旋转位置编码（RoPE）变压器进行了形式化表达能力分析。研究表明：若每个旋转分量是周期性的，RoPE变压器恰好能识别具有模谓词的过去时态逻辑可定义的语言；而常规RoPE的旋转不重复，只能实现基于精度的有界局部偏移模拟，而非全长度模特征。受控实验验证了这一分离：周期性调度在模语言上具有长度泛化能力，而常规RoPE更接近有界局部性偏好，且可能损害需要位置不变访问远距离上下文的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RoPE的成功存在两种相互竞争的理论解释：表达性研究将其与模谓词关联（周期性位置信息），而机制与长上下文研究则强调位置锚点和局部偏移。作者希望形式化并统一这两种解释，以弥合理论表达性刻画与实际模型之间的差距。

Method: 作者在完全均匀、有限精度的soft-attention变压器框架下，分别对周期性RoPE和常规RoPE进行形式化建模。通过将周期性RoPE与过去时态逻辑+模谓词（PTL+MOD）建立精确对应，并分析常规RoPE中旋转永不重复的特性，推导出其有界精确度下对固定偏移回溯算子的有限模拟。同时设计受控实验对比两种调度的行为差异。

Result: 周期性RoPE变换器恰好识别PTL+MOD（过去时态逻辑+模谓词）可定义的语言；常规RoPE则实现精确度依赖的有界局部偏移模拟，而非全长度模特征。实验表明周期性调度在模语言上长度泛化良好，而常规RoPE表现为有界局部性偏差，在需要位置不变访问远距离上下文的任务中可能产生负面影响。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了两种RoPE变体在表达性上的根本差异：周期性旋转提供全长度模特征而常规RoPE提供有界局部偏移。这使理论表达性刻画更贴近实际使用的模型，并为理解RoPE在长上下文与模推理任务中的不同表现提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Two accounts recur in explanations of the success of rotary position embeddings (RoPE). Expressivity studies associate periodic position information with modular predicates, whereas mechanistic and long-context studies emphasize positional anchors and local offsets. We formalize both accounts for fully uniform, finite-precision soft-attention transformers. We find that, if every rotary component is periodic, RoPE transformers recognize exactly the languages definable in past temporal logic with modular predicates. Conventional RoPE is different: The rotations it computes never repeat. This yields a precision-dependent bounded simulation of fixed-offset look-back operators, rather than an all-length modular characterization. Controlled experiments match this separation: Constructed periodic schedules length-generalize on modular languages, while conventional RoPE behaves more like a bounded locality bias and can impair tasks requiring position-invariant access to distant context. Altogether, our findings shed light on RoPE transformers, bringing theoretical expressivity characterizations closer to models used in practice.

</details>


### [257] [A Factor Graph Approach to Scalable Multi-Output Gaussian Process Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11917)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Esther G. van Pelt,Albert Podusenko,İsmail Şenöz,Wouter M. Kouw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将多输出高斯过程回归（MO-GPR）表达为Forney风格因子图，通过最近邻链将候选输入集排序为一维序列，利用线性高斯转移刻画潜Matérn过程、线性核心化模型混合L个潜过程到D个输出，从而实现精确高斯消息传递，计算复杂度为O(C(DL²+L³))，在低输入维度和稀疏观测场景下大幅提升可扩展性，可与精确方法媲美并超越稀疏变分和最近邻基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多输出高斯过程回归传统上随观测数乘输出数呈三次方复杂度增长，且当不同输出在不同输入点被观测时，稠密核矩阵方法需要特殊处理。这严重限制了MO-GPR在大规模数据和缺失观测场景下的应用，亟需一种既保持精确推理又具备线性可扩展性的新方法。

Method: 将多输出高斯过程回归表示为Forney风格因子图：①最近邻链将C个候选输入排序为一维序列；②沿链的潜Matérn过程通过线性高斯转移因子演化；③线性模型核心化（LMC）通过确定性混合因子将L个潜过程混合为D个输出；④每个输出配以标量观测因子，缺失观测直接省略对应因子无需重构协方差矩阵。后验计算通过链上的精确高斯消息传递完成，复杂度为O(C(DL²+L³))。

Result: 在合成输入维度扫描中，低输入维度下因子图后验紧密追踪精确核矩阵后验，随维度增加差距逐渐增大但仍与两类近似基线（稀疏变分诱导点、最近邻）保持竞争力。在电力时间序列预测上，因子图方法在预测精度上与三个基线持平，同时随数据点线性扩展，此时精确核矩阵方法已不可行，诱导点基线则明显更慢。

Conclusion: 该因子图框架为多输出高斯过程回归提供了一种可扩展的精确推理路径：复杂度随样本数和缺失率线性增长，尤其适用于低输入维度场景（如时间序列）。它弥补了核矩阵方法不可扩展与稀疏近似方法精度损失的中间地带，为大规模多输出时空建模提供了新的实用选择。

Abstract: Multi-output Gaussian process regression scales cubically in the number of observations times outputs, and dense kernel-matrix methods need bespoke handling whenever different outputs are observed at different inputs. We express multi-output Gaussian process regression as a Forney-style factor graph in which a nearest-neighbor chain orders a fixed candidate set of $C$ inputs into a one-dimensional sequence. Along this chain, latent Matérn processes evolve through linear-Gaussian transition factors, while the linear model of coregionalization mixes $L$ latent processes into $D$ outputs through a deterministic mixing factor and per-output scalar observation factors. Posterior computation reduces to exact Gaussian message passing on the chain at cost $\mathcal{O}(C(DL^2 + L^3))$ after chain construction, and missing observations omit their local factor without any covariance-matrix restructuring. The formulation therefore scales in the number of data samples and in the rate of missing observations, while remaining best suited to candidate sets in low input dimension.We compare the factor-graph formulation against an exact kernel-matrix baseline, a sparse-variational inducing-point baseline, and a nearest-neighbor baseline on a synthetic input-dimension sweep and on electricity time series forecasting. At low input dimension the factor-graph posterior tracks the exact kernel-matrix posterior closely, and the gap grows gradually as input dimension increases while staying competitive with both approximate baselines. On the electricity time series our factor-graph formulation matches all three baselines in forecast accuracy while scaling linearly in the number of data points, where the exact kernel-matrix method becomes infeasible and the inducing-point baseline remains substantially slower.

</details>


### [258] [Distillation of Foundation Models for Time-dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11937)
*Daniel Musekamp,Boshra Ariguib,Andrei Manolache,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREX是一种知识蒸馏框架，将预训练基础模型（用于时间相关PDE）的能力迁移到紧凑高效的学生模型中，通过教师模型生成合成轨迹数据来增强有限的微调数据，使学生模型在参数减少数个数量级的情况下匹配甚至超越教师模型的精度，并实现超过一个数量级的推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向时间相关PDE的基础模型虽在少样本微调后能在低数据场景下取得强精度，但模型庞大且计算密集，难以作为数值求解器的快速替代品。需要一个框架将这些大模型的预测能力蒸馏到小模型中，使其保持精度同时大幅提升推理效率。

Method: TREX框架首先对预训练基础模型进行微调作为教师模型，然后通过教师模型的长轨迹自回归rollout（可选周期性噪声注入）生成合成数据，采样教师诱导的rollout分布而无需显式知道初始条件分布。学生模型在这些增强数据上训练，并可引入任务特定的归纳偏置（如等变性），使其暴露于长时域状态和自回归预测中遇到的局部恢复行为。

Result: 在多个PDE基准上，TREX训练的学生模型能够匹配或超越教师模型的精度，同时参数数量减少数个数量级，推理速度提升超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: TREX证明了大基础模型的能力可以通过蒸馏有效压缩到小型高效网络中，且学生模型不仅能保持教师精度，还能通过引入任务特定偏置超越教师表现，为PDE基础模型的实用化部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Foundation models for time-dependent partial differential equations (PDEs) are trained on large and diverse collections of physical systems and can generalize effectively to new downstream tasks. After fine-tuning on only a few trajectories from a target domain, they can achieve strong accuracy in low-data regimes. However, these models are typically large and computationally intensive, limiting their usefulness as fast surrogates for numerical solvers. We propose Teacher Rollout Extension (TREX), a knowledge distillation framework that transfers the predictive capability of a pretrained foundation model into a compact and efficient student. Starting from a fine-tuned teacher, TREX augments limited downstream data by generating long synthetic trajectories through teacher rollouts, optionally with periodic noise injection. This procedure samples from the teacher-induced rollout distribution without requiring explicit knowledge of the initial-condition distribution, while exposing the student to long-horizon states and local recovery behavior around states encountered during autoregressive prediction. The student can further incorporate task-specific inductive biases, such as equivariance, that the teacher does not necessarily enforce. We evaluate TREX on multiple PDE benchmarks. The resulting students can match or surpass the teacher's accuracy while reducing the number of parameters by several orders of magnitude and achieving more than an order-of-magnitude speedup in inference.

</details>


### [259] [TailBooster: A Dual-Layer Generative Framework for Extreme Value Augmentation with Operational Validity Enforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11951)
*Karim Aly,Alexei Sharpanskykh,Jacco Hoekstra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailBooster，一个双层生成框架，结合统计层（IQR提取极端值）和深度学习层（自编码器清洗不合规样本），解决航空运输极端事件预测中合成数据增强的尾部代表性不足与物理不可行性问题。在US航班数据上，该框架将极端到达延误和极端空中时间的MAE分别降低了29-57%和47-49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空运输中的极端事件（如严重到达延误、异常空中时间）会引发级联网络中断，造成重大运营、经济和安全隐患。此类事件在历史记录中极为罕见，训练信号不足；传统生成模型低估分布尾部，且无法保证生成样本在运营上可行（如短空中时间搭配长飞行距离）。现有方法无法同时解决混合类型表格数据的这两个局限。

Method: TailBooster采用双层生成框架：第一层为统计层，通过四分位距（IQR）提取极端值，为专用生成模型（Tabular Variational Autoencoder）提供尾部集中的训练信号；第二层为深度学习层，应用基于自编码器的清洗机制，丢弃违反历史数据学习到的运营包络的合成记录。框架完全数据驱动且模型无关。

Result: 在US航班记录上从五个维度评估：多样性、统计相似性、保真度、运营有效性和实用性。数据驱动的清洗显著提升了运营有效性；目标增强提升了极端事件预测的实用性。在六个回归算法上，相对于传统合成数据，极端空中时间MAE降低47-49%，极端到达延误MAE降低29-57%；用合成极端值增强真实记录时也有相当增益。

Conclusion: TailBooster有效解决了极端事件预测中的数据稀缺与物理可行性双重挑战，且完全数据驱动、模型无关，可推广至极端事件预测关键且缺乏领域规则的领域。

Abstract: Extreme events in air transport, such as severe arrival delays and abnormal air times, cause cascading network disruptions with substantial operational, economic, and safety costs. Such events are rare in historical records, leaving insufficient training signal for machine learning models. Synthetic data augmentation offers a principled solution, but conventional generative models under-represent distributional tails and give no guarantee against operationally infeasible instances, such as a short air time paired with a long flight distance. No existing approach addresses both limitations for mixed-type tabular records. We propose TailBooster, a dual-layer generative framework combining generative modelling with two anomaly detection layers. A statistical layer extracts extremes via the interquartile range, supplying tail-concentrated training signal to dedicated generative models, here a Tabular Variational Autoencoder. A deep learning layer then applies autoencoder-based cleaning, discarding synthetic records that violate the operational envelope learned from historical data. The framework was evaluated on US flight records across five dimensions: diversity, statistical similarity, fidelity, operational validity, and utility, the latter two being the primary improvement targets. Data-driven cleaning markedly improved operational validity, while targeted augmentation enhanced utility for extreme-event prediction. Across six regression algorithms, training on the framework's records reduced Mean Absolute Error by 47-49% on extreme air time and 29-57% on extreme arrival delay prediction relative to conventional synthetic data, with comparable gains when real records were enriched with synthetic extremes. Being fully data-driven and model-agnostic, TailBooster extends to domains where extreme-event prediction is critical and domain-specific rules are unavailable.

</details>


### [260] [LoongReflect: Boosting Long-Horizon Reflection in Search Agents via Global Perspective Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11967)
*Zhixin Zhang,Xinke Jiang,Zhibang Yang,Weixuan Xu,Guohong Qiu,Xu Chu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoongReflect 是一个训练框架，将反思（reflection）建模为记忆控制策略，通过可逆轨迹树上的显式反思和回溯操作，结合全局信息蒸馏（fast channel）和基于结果的GRPO（slow channel）两种信号的交替优化，来解决长程推理中反思决策的局部-全局不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思能力对长程推理智能体至关重要，但由于反思在局部分支内执行而效用只能通过最终轨迹结果衡量，这种局部-全局不匹配导致基于结果的强化学习只能提供局部、稀疏且延迟的监督信号，难以有效学习反思策略。

Method: 1) 将反思形式化为记忆控制策略，智能体在可逆轨迹树上操作，使用显式反思和回溯动作；2) 反思将已验证事实、缺失证据和分支风险整合到工作记忆中，回溯移除不可靠分支并保留纠正性教训；3) 通过look-ahead、extragradient风格的协调机制结合两种信号：快通道从特权教师处蒸馏全局信息丰富的反思行为（仅对反思和回溯token监督），慢通道使用基于结果的GRPO优化完整轨迹。

Result: 在多跳检索增强生成和数学推理基准上的实验表明，LoongReflect一致优于仅基于结果的强化学习和自蒸馏基线。

Conclusion: 通过将全局信息蒸馏与局部结果优化相结合的双通道训练机制，LoongReflect有效解决了反思学习的局部-全局监督不匹配问题，证明了该框架在长程推理任务上的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on long-horizon reasoning to solve complex tasks involving planning, tool use, and memory. A critical capability in such settings is reflection: assessing trajectory progress, identifying missing evidence and unreliable intermediate states, and deciding whether to continue, revise, or abandon the current branch. Learning effective reflection, however, is challenging because reflection is performed locally within the current branch, whereas its utility can only be determined by its contribution to the final trajectory outcome. This local-global mismatch makes outcome-based reinforcement learning provide only local, sparse and delayed supervision for reflective decisions. To solve these, we propose LoongReflect, a training framework that formulates reflection as a memory-control policy. The agent operates over a reversible trajectory tree using explicit reflect and backtrack actions. Reflection consolidates verified facts, missing evidence, and branch-specific risks into working memory, while backtracking removes an unreliable branch from the active context and preserves a concise corrective lesson. To learn this policy, LoongReflect combines two complementary signals through a look-ahead, extragradient-style coordination mechanism. A fast channel distills globally informed reflective behavior from a privileged teacher, with supervision restricted to reflection and backtracking tokens. A slow channel optimizes complete trajectories using outcome-based GRPO, aligning local control decisions with final task success. Experiments on multi-hop retrieval-augmented generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over outcome-only reinforcement learning and self-distillation baselines.

</details>


### [261] [TESLA: Taylor Expansion of Sinusoidal Learnable Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11970)
*Daehwa Ko,Jaehyeon Kim,Seunghyun Ham,Jay Hoon Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TESLA激活函数，它是正弦和余弦项的可学习组合，通过显式控制多项式阶数并选择性放大高阶分量来解决神经网络的奇偶校验难题。理论上证明了系数约束可带来Lipschitz/Rademacher复杂度界并塑造训练动态以强调高频结构；实验上在n=32奇偶校验任务上用10万样本（约0.002%的输入空间）实现强泛化，并在30%标签噪声下保持高精度，同时对比了SIREN、SNAKE和Fourier特征嵌入等基线，且ImageNet-100上表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇偶校验问题（判断二进制向量的1的数量是奇数还是偶数）对标准神经网络仍是挑战，因为其线性不可分且需要全局交互。现有方法难以有效处理这一问题，因此需要一种能够显式控制多项式阶数并选择性放大高阶分量的激活函数来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出TESLA（可学习正弦余弦组合）激活函数，定义为正弦和余弦项的可学习线性组合，能够显式控制多项式阶数并选择性放大高阶分量。理论上，通过对TESLA系数施加约束获得Lipschitz/Rademacher复杂度界，同时塑造训练动态以优先学习高频结构。

Result: 在n=32的奇偶校验任务上，TESLA仅用10万个训练样本（约为2^32输入空间的0.002%）即达到强泛化性能，并且在高达30%的标签噪声下仍保持高精度。与SIREN、SNAKE和Fourier特征嵌入等周期性和频域基线相比表现优异；在ImageNet-100上也取得与基线相当的性能，说明激活层面的阶数控制可迁移到通用视觉任务。

Conclusion: 激活函数级别的多项式阶数控制是解决奇偶校验等高频结构学习问题的有效途径。TESLA通过可学习的正弦余弦组合实现了对高频分量的显式控制，不仅解决了奇偶校验难题，还具备鲁棒性和可迁移性，展示了激活层在塑造网络归纳偏置方面的巨大潜力。

Abstract: The parity problem--deciding whether the number of ones in a binary vector is odd or even--remains challenging for standard neural networks due to linear inseparability and the need for global interactions. We propose TESLA, an activation defined as a learnable combination of sine and cosine terms, enabling explicit control over polynomial degree and selective amplification of high-order components. Theoretically, we show that constraining TESLA's coefficients yields Lipschitz/Rademacher complexity bounds and shapes the training dynamics to emphasize higher-frequency structure. Empirically, on parity with input length n = 32, TESLA attains strong generalization with 100K training samples (approximately 0.002% of the 2^32 input space) and remains robust under heavy corruption, retaining high accuracy with up to 30% label noise. We also compare against periodic and frequency-based baselines (SIREN, SNAKE, and Fourier feature embeddings) on parity and Forrelation. Beyond synthetic structure, TESLA delivers comparable performance on ImageNet-100, indicating that activation-level degree control transfers to more general vision workloads. Code: https://github.com/KAU-QuantumAILab/TESLA

</details>


### [262] [Remote Sensing and Machine Learning-Based Analysis of Land Use and Vegetation Change in Dhaka District, Bangladesh](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12001)
*Muhammad Masud Tarek,Md. Alamgir Hossain,Md. Samiul Islam,Muntasir Hasan Kanchan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用遥感数据与机器学习方法，分析了2019至2024年间孟加拉国达卡地区的土地利用与植被动态变化，发现城市建成区增长59.5%，植被和水体分别减少8.46%和7.77%，并验证了随机森林分类器的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 达卡地区快速城市化引发了土地利用和环境条件的重大改变，亟需系统性的监测手段为城市规划与生态可持续性提供数据支撑。

Method: 使用Sentinel-2 MSI和Landsat 8高分辨率影像，计算NDVI、NDBI和NDWI光谱指数；在Google Earth Engine中采用决策树、K近邻（KNN）和随机森林三种监督分类器对标记地理空间训练点进行地表覆盖分类，并通过混淆矩阵和kappa统计量进行精度评估。

Result: 2019至2024年间城市建成区面积增加59.5%，植被减少8.46%，水体减少7.77%；土地利用以植被和水域向城市基础设施转化为主导趋势；随机森林在所有模型中分类精度最高。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了达卡地区无规划城市扩张引发的环境压力加剧，证明了遥感与机器学习工具在提供及时、可靠数据以支持可持续城市发展、土地利用监管和生态系统保护政策方面的潜力。

Abstract: Rapid urbanization in Dhaka District, Bangladesh has triggered substantial alterations in land use and environmental conditions, necessitating systematic monitoring for informed urban planning and ecological sustainability. This study employs remote sensing data and machine learning techniques to analyze spatiotemporal changes in land cover and vegetation dynamics between 2019 and 2024. High-resolution satellite imagery from Sentinel-2 MSI and Landsat 8 was utilized to classify land cover types and compute spectral indices including the Normalized Difference Vegetation Index (NDVI), Normalized Difference Built-up Index (NDBI), and Normalized Difference Water Index (NDWI). A supervised machine learning approach incorporating Decision Tree, K-Nearest Neighbors (KNN), and Random Forest classifiers was applied using labeled geospatial training points within Google Earth Engine. Accuracy assessments were conducted using confusion matrices and kappa statistics. Results indicate a 59.5% increase in urban built-up areas and a significant decline in vegetation (-8.46%) and water bodies (-7.77%) over the five-year period. Land conversion from vegetated and aquatic areas to urban infrastructure was identified as a dominant trend. Among the models, Random Forest demonstrated the highest classification accuracy. These findings underscore the growing environmental pressures driven by unregulated urban expansion in Dhaka. The study highlights the potential of remote sensing and machine learning tools in providing timely, actionable data to support sustainable urban development, land-use regulation, and ecosystem conservation policies.

</details>


### [263] [Dual-Model Sentiment Analysis of Consumer Reviews in the Retail Coffee Sector Using Machine Learning and Deep Learning Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12007)
*Muntasir Hasan Kanchan,Md. Alamgir Hossain,Md. Samiul Islam,Muhammad Masud Tarek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对星巴克消费者评论，构建了传统机器学习与深度学习的对比情感分析框架，共评估5种机器学习分类器和5种深度学习模型。SVM在机器学习中准确率最高（91.0%），双向LSTM在深度学习中表现最佳，并具备良好的泛化能力。研究同时揭示了类别不平衡对积极情感召回率的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费者评论在品牌感知和商业策略中扮演重要角色，尤其在零售咖啡等服务业驱动型行业中尤为关键。现有研究缺乏对真实场景下消费者评论情感分析的系统性比较，需要针对真实的、不平衡的消费者反馈数据，评估不同方法在不同维度上的优劣。

Method: 从ConsumerAffairs收集700余条星巴克评论，经过预处理和探索性数据分析识别时间和地理模式。将星级评分二值化为情感标签（4-5分为正面，1-3分为负面），得到高度不平衡的负面倾向数据集。采用5种机器学习模型（逻辑回归、SVM、决策树、随机森林、朴素贝叶斯）和5种深度学习模型（LSTM、RNN、双向LSTM、GRU、CNN），以准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数作为评估指标。

Result: SVM在机器学习模型中准确率最高，达91.0%；双向LSTM在深度学习模型中表现最强，且在未见数据上展现出良好的泛化能力。研究发现类别不平衡显著影响了多个模型的正面情感召回率。

Conclusion: 该研究为零售咖啡行业的消费者体验分析提供了机器学习与深度学习方法的系统性比较评估，强调了根据数据特性选择合适的模型和预处理策略的重要性。

Abstract: Consumer reviews play an important role in shaping brand perception and business strategies, particularly in service-driven industries such as retail coffee. This study presents a comparative sentiment analysis framework for Starbucks customer reviews using classical machine learning and deep learning approaches. The dataset, collected from ConsumerAffairs, contains more than 700 reviews and was analyzed through preprocessing and exploratory data analysis to identify temporal and geographic patterns. Sentiment labels were generated by binarizing star ratings, with ratings of 4 and 5 classified as positive and ratings of 1 to 3 as negative. The resulting dataset was substantially imbalanced toward negative sentiment. Five machine learning classifiers, including Logistic Regression, Support Vector Machine (SVM), Decision Tree, Random Forest, and Naive Bayes, were evaluated alongside five deep learning models: LSTM, RNN, Bidirectional LSTM, GRU, and CNN. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score. SVM achieved the highest accuracy among the machine learning models at 91.0 percent, while Bidirectional LSTM showed the strongest performance among the deep learning models and demonstrated good generalization on unseen data. The findings also show that class imbalance negatively affected positive sentiment recall across several models. Overall, this study provides a comparative evaluation of machine learning and deep learning approaches for real-world consumer sentiment analysis and highlights the importance of appropriate model selection and preprocessing for customer experience analytics in the retail coffee sector.

</details>


### [264] [Reducing Symmetry Increase in Equivariant Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12010)
*Ning Lin,Jiacheng Cen,Anyi Li,Wenbing Huang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地刻画了等变神经网络（ENN）处理对称输入时产生的"对称性增加"现象，证明其存在由特征空间结构决定的下确界，并提出可计算的算法与特征设计准则来有效抑制有害的对称性增加，在合成数据和QM9数据集上验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络在科学领域广泛应用，但当输入具有对称性时，其表达能力会退化——等变映射处理对称输入会导致输出对称性超出输入本身，即"对称性增加"。已有研究仅在特定案例中观察到该现象，缺乏对其根本原因与通用抑制策略的严格理解，亟需理论层面的系统性刻画。

Method: (i) 证明对任意给定特征空间和输入对称群，增加的对称性存在由特征空间结构决定的下确界；(ii) 基于该理论，开发可计算的算法推导该下确界，并提出面向特征设计的实用准则以防止有害的对称性增加；(iii) 在标准正则性假设下，证明对大多数等变映射，所提准则能有效降低对称性增加。并通过合成数据集与QM9数据集上的可视化和实验进行验证。

Result: 理论结果与实验一致：所提出的框架能够刻画并计算对称性增加的下确界，特征设计准则在多数等变映射下有效抑制对称性增加，QM9等真实数据集上的实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该工作首次从理论上严格刻画了ENN中对称性增加的数学本质（特征空间结构决定其下确界），提供了可计算的算法与实用性特征设计指南，为缓解等变模型在对称输入下的表达能力退化提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Equivariant Neural Networks (ENNs) have empowered numerous applications in scientific fields. Despite their remarkable capacity for representing geometric structures, ENNs suffer from degraded expressivity when processing symmetric inputs: the output representations are invariant to transformations that extend beyond the input's symmetries. The mathematical essence of this phenomenon is that a symmetric input, after being processed by an equivariant map, experiences an increase in symmetry. While prior research has documented symmetry increase in specific cases, a rigorous understanding of its underlying causes and general reduction strategies remains lacking. In this paper, we provide a detailed and in-depth characterization of symmetry increase together with a principled framework for its reduction: (i) For any given feature space and input symmetry group, we prove that the increased symmetry admits an infimum determined by the structure of the feature space; (ii) Building on this foundation, we develop a computable algorithm to derive this infimum, and propose practical guidelines for feature design to prevent harmful symmetry increases. (iii) Under standard regularity assumptions, we demonstrate that for most equivariant maps, our guidelines effectively reduce symmetry increase. To complement our theoretical findings, we provide visualizations and experiments on both synthetic datasets and the real-world QM9 dataset. The results validate our theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [265] [SoftWater: Class-Aware Rate Allocation for Softmax Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12026)
*Joao V. Cavalcanti,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SoftWater方法，将softmax输出层的量化建模为KL散度下的率失真问题，通过二阶分析揭示类别感知的几何结构，并用可分离近似将大规模Cholesky分解简化为按类缩放的单个因子分解。在5个模型（1B-32B）的60个测试点上，59个超越SOTA的WaterSIC量化器，2-bit头部量化可削减6.5×-8.3×的KL散度，在LLaMA-3.2-1B-Instruct上去除45-60%的存储字节仅增加2.9-3.7%的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化流程通常将softmax输出层保留在高精度，但小型LLM中head层占15%-30%的参数，标称"2-bit"模型实际上每权重存储位数远不止2bit。现有WaterSIC等方法在线性层WMSE下近最优，但不针对输出KL最小化，导致head量化效果受限。

Method: 将softmax层量化建模为KL散度下的率失真问题；通过二阶分析得到类别感知的几何结构（量化误差由特征协方差和类别softmax曲率联合加权）；用可分离近似将Kn×Kn的Cholesky分解替换为按类别缩放的n×n分解，使格码可通过连续干扰消除编码，统计量仅需一次前向传播获得。

Result: 在5个模型（1B至32B）上，SoftWater在匹配的head码率下于60个测试点中59个超越发布的WaterSIC量化器，不依赖该流水线的任何精化手段，2-bit时减少head导致的KL 6.5×-8.3×。在Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct上，2-bit head消除45-60%存储字节，困惑度仅增2.9-3.7%。在tied模型上，4-bit head接近无损，2-bit head困惑度成本低于4%。

Conclusion: 通过将head量化重新定义为KL散度下的率失真问题而非单纯的线性层WMSE问题，SoftWater展示了显著的性能优势，使head量化成为可行实践。校准数据与部署域的匹配对KL最低至关重要，且tied模型下head量化可实现接近无损的效果。

Abstract: Post-training quantization pipelines routinely leave the softmax output layer in high precision. Yet in small LLMs with modern vocabularies, the head holds 15--30\% of all parameters, so a nominal ``2-bit'' model with an fp16 head can store several times as many bits per weight. We pose softmax-layer quantization as a rate-distortion problem under the KL divergence between the original and quantized output distributions. A second-order analysis reveals a class-aware geometry: quantization error is weighted jointly by feature covariance and class-specific softmax curvature. A separability approximation replaces the $Kn\times Kn$ Cholesky with one $n\times n$ factorization rescaled per class, making the lattice encodable by successive interference cancellation, with both statistics from a single forward pass. The resulting method, SoftWater, gives fine grids to frequent, low-variance classes and coarse grids to rare ones, a large gap under Zipfian token distributions. Across five models from 1B to 32B, SoftWater outperforms the released WaterSIC quantizer (near-optimal under linear-layer WMSE but not output KL) at matched head rates on 59 of 60 test points, using none of that pipeline's refinements and cutting head-induced KL by $6.5\times$--$8.3\times$ at 2 bits. On Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct with quantized bodies, a 2-bit head removes 45--60\% of stored bytes for a $2.9$--$3.7\%$ perplexity increase. Because the class-side statistic comes from calibration data, matching calibration to the deployment domain gives the lowest KL on that domain throughout. On a tied model, a 4-bit head is near-lossless and a 2-bit head costs under 4\% perplexity, making head quantization of such models practical.

</details>


### [266] [Uncertainty-Aware Probabilistic Constrained Clustering from Entangled Pairwise Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12027)
*Shaojie Zhang,Ke Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向不确定概率成对约束的深度聚类框架ECI-PP，通过角度成对目标函数ProbPair和估计-校正-集成三阶段架构，处理真实值软标签中的内在歧义、专家判断和随机噪声，在多个基准上超越了现有的深度约束聚类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的成对监督通常是实值且自然地混合了内在歧义、专家判断和随机损坏，而现有的深度约束聚类（DCC）方法主要针对硬性、无专家意识的约束，仅从数值层面而非语义层面处理软标签，缺乏对概率关系不确定性的形式化建模与分析。

Method: 首先形式化了不确定性感知的概率约束聚类（UPCC）问题，通过异构观测过程定义规范aleatoric目标，并进行条件可识别性分析。随后提出ProbPair角度成对目标函数用于建模概率关系，并构建ECI-PP框架，包含估计器（estimator）、校正器（corrector）和集成器（integrator）三部分，依次进行信念估计、误差校正和可靠性感知的聚类集成。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的概率监督设置下，基于多个多样化的基准数据集实验表明，ECI-PP在性能上优于最先进的深度约束聚类方法，并且在共享默认配置下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作从语义层面系统性地处理了软成对约束中的不确定性，建立了概率约束聚类的理论基础与实用算法框架，为更真实场景下的弱监督聚类提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Pairwise constrained clustering typically relies on hard must-link/cannot-link labels, whereas realistic pairwise supervision may be real-valued and entangle intrinsic ambiguity, expert judgment, and stochastic corruption. Existing deep constrained clustering (DCC) methods mainly target hard, expert-agnostic constraints, treating soft labels mostly numerically rather than semantically. We formalize this setting as uncertainty-aware probabilistic constrained clustering (UPCC), defining a canonical aleatoric target through a heterogeneous observation process and analyzing its conditional identifiability. We introduce ProbPair, an angular pairwise objective for probabilistic relations, and build ECI-PP, an estimator--corrector--integrator framework that refines imperfect supervision via belief estimation, correction, and reliability-aware integration. Across challenging probabilistic supervision settings, experiments on diverse benchmarks show that ECI-PP outperforms state-of-the-art DCC methods and remains robust with a shared default configuration.

</details>


### [267] [Clustered Randomized Smoothing for Stochastic Prediction Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12037)
*Eduardo Figueiredo,Frederik Mathiesen,Julian Schumann,Jens Kober,Arkady Zgonnikov,Luca Laurenti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出聚类α-平滑（clustered α-smoothing）框架，通过聚类算法对噪声样本进行分区，在每个簇内局部应用α-平滑，并将结果组合成混合分布，解决随机平滑在多模态随机回归中因模态坍缩导致的预测退化问题。在两个基准上分别实现了比α-平滑低27%的Wasserstein距离和比SOTA随机平滑低81%的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代随机预测器能够建模丰富的多模态输出分布，但在安全关键领域需要保证鲁棒性。随机平滑是提升对抗鲁棒性的重要技术，但在随机多模态回归场景下，随机平滑常因模态坍缩（mode collapse）产生平均化的预测，无法反映真实分布的多模态特性，这是一个亟待解决的局限性。

Method: 提出聚类α-平滑框架，包含三步：首先使用任意聚类算法对噪声样本进行分区；然后在每个簇内局部应用α-平滑；最后将各簇的预测组合为混合分布。理论层面，将平滑分布解释为α-平滑器的混合，并推导出平滑预测位于对应不同模态的紧致区域之并集的概率下界。

Result: 在驾驶模拟器数据集的随机轨迹预测任务中，相比α-平滑，Wasserstein距离平均降低27%；在四旋翼控制任务中，相比SOTA随机平滑方法，碰撞率降低81%。实验表明该方法在两种基准上均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 聚类α-平滑通过保留多模态特性并在每个模态内分别进行局部平滑，有效克服了传统随机平滑在多模态回归中的模态坍缩问题，同时保持了鲁棒性保证，为安全关键场景下的多模态随机预测提供了更可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Modern stochastic predictors can model rich, multi-modal outcome distributions. However, this expressive power comes with challenges in ensuring robust predictions $-$ a critical requirement in safety-critical domains. Randomized smoothing is a leading technique for improving robustness, particularly against adversarial perturbations. Yet, in stochastic multi-modal regression settings, randomized smoothing often fails due to mode collapse, yielding averaged predictions that do not reflect the underlying distribution. To address this limitation, we propose clustered $α$-smoothing, a framework that (1) partitions noisy samples using an arbitrary clustering algorithm, (2) applies $α$-smoothing locally within each cluster, and (3) combines the resulting predictions into a mixture distribution. By interpreting the smoothing distribution as a mixture of $α$-smoothers, we derive a lower bound on the probability that the smoothed prediction lies within a union of compact regions corresponding to distinct modes. We empirically evaluate our framework on two benchmarks, demonstrating substantial improvements over state-of-the-art methods. In stochastic trajectory prediction on a driving simulator dataset, our approach achieves, on average, a $27\%$ lower Wasserstein distance to the ground-truth distribution compared to $α$-smoothing. In quadrotor control, where modes correspond to distinct feasible paths to a target, our method reduces the collision rate by $81\%$ relative to the state-of-the-art randomized smoothing.

</details>


### [268] [AI4AI at Test-Time: Strong-to-Weak Capability Transfer via Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12307)
*Cheng Qian,Wenting Zhao,Liangwei Yang,Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Huan Wang,Shelby Heinecke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新型的测试时能力迁移方法——强到弱脚手架（strong-to-weak scaffolding），即由更强的构建者模型在推理时为较弱的目标模型构建推理管线，无需任何参数更新即可显著提升目标模型在Theory-of-Mind基准上的表现（平均准确率从0.49提升至0.91）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的知识蒸馏依赖于训练时的方法（如teacher forcing、on-policy distillation）来将大模型能力转移到小模型，需要更新小模型的参数。作者提出一个核心问题：这种能力迁移是否可以发生在测试时而不需要任何参数更新？这为利用强模型辅助弱模型提供了新的思路。

Method: 提出"强到弱脚手架"框架：构建者模型（强模型）在测试时为目标模型（弱模型）构建推理脚手架（harness）。具体流程包括：(1) 构建者使用5%的数据作为验证集，通过多轮迭代优化其推理管线设计；(2) 最终确定的脚手架在完整测试集上评估；(3) 目标模型参数完全冻结。研究使用了四个代表性的Theory-of-Mind基准。

Result: 实验表明该方法极为有效，目标模型平均性能从0.49翻倍至0.91。深入分析发现：收益主要来源于将不稳定的模型推理卸载到确定性代码中、基准特定的路由策略以及严格的答案格式强制，而非鼓励目标模型更广泛地推理或采样。此外，构建者模型的推理努力单调地提升脚手架质量，平台效应相对于构建者模型自身能力影响较小，且较弱的目标模型获益最大。

Conclusion: 推理时的脚手架设计是传统训练时蒸馏的重要补充，使强模型无需重新训练即可将认知结构传递给弱模型。这一发现拓展了能力迁移的范式，从参数更新扩展到推理管线设计。

Abstract: Recent work on distillation transfers the capabilities of large models to smaller ones often by updating the latter's parameters, through teacher forcing, on-policy distillation, and related training-time methods. In this paper, we ask whether such transfer can instead occur at test time. We study strong-to-weak scaffolding: whether a stronger builder model can construct inference-time harnesses that help a weaker target model solve tasks more reliably without any parameter updates. Using four representative Theory-of-Mind benchmarks, each builder model uses 5% of the data as a validation set to iteratively refine its harness over multiple rounds, after which the finalized harness is evaluated on the full test set. Empirically, this form of test-time capability transfer is highly effective, nearly doubling average target-model performance from 0.49 to 0.91. Our analysis shows that the gains come primarily from offloading unstable model reasoning into deterministic code, benchmark-specific routing, and strict answer-format enforcement, rather than from encouraging the target model to reason more extensively or sample more broadly. We further find that builder-model reasoning effort improves harness quality monotonically, platform effects are modest relative to the builder model's own capability, and weaker target models receive the largest gains. These results suggest that inference-time harness design is an important complement to conventional training-time distillation, enabling strong models to transfer cognitive structure to weaker models without retraining.

</details>


### [269] [Towards Truly Unsupervised Evaluation of Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12057)
*Hafiz Saud Arshad,Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性地讨论了现有被广泛使用的无监督特征选择评估方法存在的设计缺陷，指出这些方法并非真正的无监督评估，而只是在无监督下游任务下的监督评估。作者提出了一个真正无监督的评估框架，利用主成分分析（PCA）和最优传输（Optimal Transport）来度量特征选择方法的质量，无需任何标签信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大多数无监督特征选择评估技术存在关键的设计缺陷，这些缺陷质疑其无监督本质。实际上它们至多是"无监督下游任务下的监督评估"，而非真正的无监督评估。因此需要提出一个真正无监督的评估框架。

Method: 作者首先批判性分析了现有的所谓"无监督"评估技术，指出其本质上仍隐式依赖标签信息。随后提出一个真正无监督的评估框架，该框架结合了无监督的主成分分析（PCA）和最优传输（Optimal Transport）理论，在不使用任何标签信息的情况下度量特征选择算法的质量。

Result: 论文揭示了现有无监督特征选择评估方法的本质缺陷，并提出了一种全新的、真正无监督的评估框架，利用PCA和最优传输技术实现无需标签信息的特征选择质量评估。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于：一方面通过深入的批判性讨论揭示了现有"无监督"评估技术的真实本质（实为监督评估）；另一方面提出了一种真正无监督的评估框架，使得特征选择算法可以在完全无标签信息的条件下得到公正、可靠的质量度量。

Abstract: Feature selection is one of the most important and fundamental tasks in data mining, tackled by a family of methods with an established set of evaluation techniques to measure the quality of a specific method. Most of the methods commonly used for the unsupervised evaluation of feature selection algorithms suffer from critical design flaws which question their unsupervised nature. In this paper, we provide a critical discussion on the established allegedly unsupervised evaluation techniques, and shed light on the reasons why they are not truly unsupervised but, at best, supervised evaluation under an unsupervised downstream task. We also propose a novel, truly unsupervised evaluation framework to measure the quality of the feature selection algorithms without any form of information about the labels. The proposed framework utilizes unsupervised Principal Component Analysis, and optimal transport to measure the quality of the feature selection methods in a truly unsupervised manner.

</details>


### [270] [Faithful, Sufficient and Understandable: Rethinking Graph Counterfactual Explanations via Discrete Diffusion Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12083)
*David Bechtoldt,Sidney Bender*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GDCE-I方法，通过离散去噪扩散模型与新颖的离散反演方案生成图反事实解释，兼顾数据流形保持与完整编辑空间搜索，并在统一评估框架下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在图形数据上预测能力强但缺乏固有可解释性，尤其在高风险场景中限制其应用。图反事实解释需要找到能改变预测的最小结构修改，但搜索空间是离散且组合的，且必须满足节点/边类型和化学价等域规则。现有方法要么编辑不保持在数据流形上，要么搜索不覆盖完整编辑空间，两者不可兼得。

Method: 提出Graph Diffusion Counterfactual Explanation via Inversion (GDCE-I)：利用离散去噪扩散模型结合新颖的离散反演方案，实现分布感知的编辑并利用整个域编辑空间。同时针对图反事实评估不完整、不一致的问题，推导了反事实解释期望属性的评估框架，并在统一协议下应用于所有方法。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，GDCE-I在定义的评估框架下大幅优于相关方法。在分子域中还定性展示了GDCE-I能生成可解释的分布内解决方案。

Conclusion: GDCE-I同时保持了数据流形和完整编辑空间搜索，解决了现有图反事实解释方法的根本权衡，为高保真、分布内的图反事实解释提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve strong predictive performance on graph-structured data across domains such as chemistry, biology, and network analysis, yet they provide no intrinsic explanation of their predictions. This limits their adoption in high-stakes and safety-critical settings. Counterfactual explanations address this by revealing the minimal structural modifications that would change a model's prediction. On graphs, however, such a modification is hard to produce. The search space is discrete and combinatorial, and a valid answer must respect categorical node and edge types together with domain rules such as chemical valency in the case of molecular graphs. Existing explainers give up one of two things. Either edits are not held on the data manifold, or the search does not span the full edit space. We propose Graph Diffusion Counterfactual Explanation via Inversion (GDCE-I), which gives up neither. A discrete denoising diffusion model with a novel discrete inversion scheme enables distribution-aware edits leveraging the whole domain edit space. We further address the incomplete and inconsistent evaluation of graph counterfactuals by deriving a framework of explanation desiderata and applying it to every method under one shared protocol. Across four benchmarks, GDCE-I outperforms related work by a large margin on the defined framework. For the molecular domain, we further qualitatively show that GDCE-I attains interpretable in-distribution solutions.

</details>


### [271] [NAE: Normalizing AutoEncoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12084)
*Muhammad Abdur Rafae,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了流自编码器（flow autoencoders）的训练动态，证明了现有方法中解码器和编码器代理损失存在次优性，并提出了一种新的条件损失函数NAE（Normalizing Autoencoder），通过对齐代理损失梯度与重构损失梯度来优化训练，在分子生成、表格数据和图像基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流（Normalizing flows）结合近似逆在生成建模中广泛使用，涵盖全维度和瓶颈设置，但这些流自编码器的训练损失设计缺乏理论指导，现有方法的代理损失与重构损失之间未能达到最优对齐，导致训练效果欠佳。

Method: 该论文首先对训练动态进行理论分析，证明现有方法中编码器和解码器代理损失必须与重构损失对齐优化才是最优的。基于此提出NAE，采用一种新颖的条件损失，使代理损失梯度与重构损失梯度保持一致，直接改进现有标准。

Result: 在分子生成、表格数据和图像等多个基准上的大量实验表明，NAE达到了最先进的性能，验证了损失对齐在流自编码器中的重要性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了流自编码器中损失对齐的重要理论认识，并提出了NAE这一强大的生成框架，为后续研究提供了理论和实践基础。

Abstract: We consider the setting of Normalizing flows with approximate inverses, an established paradigm spanning both full-dimensional ($d=D$) and bottleneck ($d<D$) settings, and group these models under the term flow autoencoders. We present a theoretical investigation into their training dynamics and prove that the proposed loss used by existing approaches is suboptimal; specifically, both encoder and decoder surrogates must be optimized in alignment with reconstruction loss. Guided by these insights, we propose Normalizing Autoencoder (NAE), which employs a novel conditional loss that aligns the surrogate loss gradient with that of reconstruction loss, directly improving upon the current standard. Extensive experiments across molecule generation, tabular data, and image benchmarks demonstrate that NAE achieves state of the art performance. Our work highlights the importance of loss alignment in flow autoencoders and establishes NAE as a powerful generative framework.

</details>


### [272] [Task- and dataset-specific information in protein language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12090)
*Roman Joeres,Ilya Senatorov,Olga V. Kalinina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了13个蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）在15个下游任务中的逐层嵌入表示，发现PLM最后一层嵌入很少是最优的，不同层次嵌入的信息分布与任务类型和数据集特性密切相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型已被广泛应用于计算生物学，但社区普遍默认使用最后一层嵌入，对模型内部各层的行为和嵌入信息量缺乏深入理解。这一共识可能并非最优，需要系统研究以揭示PLM内部结构-功能关系。

Method: 对13个PLMs在来自11个数据集的15个下游任务上，逐一训练探针模型评估每一层嵌入的性能，同时计算潜在空间特征以估计各层包含的信息量。通过比较各层性能并分析任务类型、数据集特性与层间信息分布之间的关联来得出结论。

Result: 发现：1）PLM最后一层嵌入很少在下游任务中取得最佳表现；2）预训练目标与残基级预测目标相似时，任务理解度随层数增加而稳定提升；3）对于全蛋白任务，数据集而非任务本身决定PLM表现，浅层嵌入在深突变扫描（DMS）数据集上更优，而深层嵌入在多样化天然蛋白数据集上更优；4）对人工蛋白任务，PLM性能显著下降。

Conclusion: PLM的中间层嵌入往往比最后一层更具信息价值，不同层的信息分布与预训练目标、任务类型和数据集特性存在系统性关联。研究挑战了"最后一层最优"的默认共识，为下游任务中选择合适嵌入层提供了指导，但也指出PLM在人工蛋白上的泛化能力存在明显局限。

Abstract: Protein language models (PLMs) have transferred the latest advances from natural language processing to computational biology. These models, trained on large corpora of protein sequence data, are widely used to translate amino acid sequences into latent-space embeddings, ready for use in diverse downstream tasks (DTs). By a common consensus, embeddings from the model's last layer are used, and the model's internal behavior remains poorly understood. We analyzed 13 PLMs across 15 DTs from 11 datasets to investigate the informativeness of embeddings created in intermediate PLM layers. We trained probe models on embeddings from each layer, compared their performance, and computed characteristics of the latent spaces they span to estimate the information they contain, and found that the last layers of PLMs rarely contained embeddings that led to the best results on downstream tasks. Furthermore, we identified a connection between DTs and the distribution across PLMs' layers of the relevant information to predict that task. For example, similarity between the pre-training objective and the objective of predicting properties of individual residues leads to a steady increase in understanding of such tasks across the layers of PLMs. On the other hand, for whole-protein tasks, we observe that the dataset, rather than the task itself, defines PLMs' ability to perform well on a DT. Embeddings from shallow layers of PLMs perform better for datasets that contain deep mutational scan (DMS) data, while datasets containing diverse natural proteins find most useful embeddings in the models' deeper layers. Additionally, we discover that the performance of PLMs drops significantly when tasks are introduced for artificial proteins.

</details>


### [273] [Confidence Calibration of Deep Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12100)
*Coby Penso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文旨在解决高利害场景下深度学习模型置信度校准问题，重点应对标签噪声、领域偏移和隐私保护等现实挑战。论文提出了基于噪声模型的噪声感知校准框架、噪声感知的保形预测（CP）方法、无监督领域自适应下的无标签校准方法，以及满足本地差分隐私的保形预测框架，从而在噪声、域偏移和隐私约束下实现可靠的置信度估计与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法通常假设可获取干净验证数据，但在实际应用中标签噪声和领域偏移普遍存在，使得标准方法产生误导性的置信度估计。高利害应用（如医疗、金融等）对置信度可靠性要求极高，因此需要开发在非理想条件下仍能保持校准性能的方法。

Method: 论文从三个方向展开：(1) 针对标签噪声，利用估计的噪声模型建模噪声标签与干净标签分布之间的关系，重构无噪声的置信度估计，并将其扩展到保形预测领域，通过估计干净的符合分数（conformity scores）实现噪声感知的CP方法；(2) 针对无监督领域自适应，利用源域性能与域间差异估计目标域精度，从而在没有目标域标签的情况下完成校准；(3) 针对隐私保护，提出本地差分隐私的保形预测框架，在保证隐私的同时实现有效的不确定性量化，并平衡隐私、计算可行性和预测可靠性。

Result: 论文提出的方法使校准理论能够在安全关键应用中落地，实现了具备隐私保护、噪声鲁棒性的可靠神经网络预测。具体结果包括：在标签噪声下可重构无噪声置信度，噪声感知的CP方法在标签噪声下仍能保证覆盖率，无监督域自适应中无需目标标签即可完成校准，以及本地差分隐私框架在隐私约束下仍提供有效的不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 本文通过引入噪声感知校准框架、噪声感知保形预测、无监督域自适应校准和本地差分隐私保形预测，系统性地解决了现实部署中校准面临的标签噪声、领域偏移和隐私保护三大核心挑战，为安全关键应用中的可靠深度学习预测提供了理论支撑和实践方案。

Abstract: In high-stakes applications, reliable confidence estimates are as important as the predictions themselves. Confidence calibration ensures that predicted probabilities reflect the likelihood of correctness, making it essential for safe deployment of deep learning models. However, existing methods typically assume access to clean validation data, which is often unrealistic due to label noise and domain shifts. This thesis develops methods for improving calibration under these conditions.
  First, we address calibration under label noise. Standard methods can produce misleading confidence estimates when labels are unreliable. We propose a framework that uses an estimated noise model to reconstruct noise-free confidence estimates by modeling the relationship between noisy and clean label distributions. We extend this approach to Conformal Prediction (CP), which provides set-valued predictions with guaranteed coverage. Our noise-aware CP method estimates clean conformity scores despite label noise, enabling reliable uncertainty quantification. Next, we study calibration in unsupervised domain adaptation, where a model trained on a labeled source domain is adapted to an unlabeled target domain. Since labeled target data are unavailable, we estimate target-domain accuracy from source performance and domain discrepancies, enabling calibration without target labels. We also consider privacy-preserving settings in which user labels and model outputs must remain protected. We propose a locally differentially private conformal prediction framework that provides valid uncertainty quantification while maintaining privacy guarantees and balancing privacy, computational feasibility, and prediction reliability.
  Our results bridge calibration theory and practical deployment in safety-critical applications, contributing to reliable, privacy-preserving, and noise-resilient neural network predictions.

</details>


### [274] [Beyond Parameter Space: NTK-Guided Personalized Aggregation for Robust Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12108)
*Mirko Konstantin,Stefan Zachow,Anirban Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LIGHTYEAR是一个联邦学习框架，通过在函数空间（而非参数空间）使用NTK一致性分数来评估客户端更新的贡献，并采用P2P拓扑让每个客户端在私有验证数据上评估传入模型，从而为每个客户端选择个性化的有益更新进行聚合。在五个数据集和九个基线方法上取得了优于集中式FL和现有P2P方法的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临的一个核心挑战是确定哪些客户端更新对目标领域有益。现有方法通常在参数空间比较模型参数或梯度来判断相似性，但参数空间相似性在异构非IID数据下无法良好反映预测行为，导致与目标领域不一致的更新（源于数据异构或客户端故障）降低本地模型性能。

Method: LIGHTYEAR在函数空间进行更新选择。它利用神经正切核（NTK）计算一致性分数来刻画预测行为，为每个客户端确定个性化的聚合集合。由于集中式FL在聚合前无法获得函数空间信息，LIGHTYEAR改用P2P拓扑，让客户端直接交换更新并在私有验证数据上评估传入模型。每个客户端仅选择对其目标领域有益的更新，并使用正则化聚合规则以提升异构环境下的稳定性。

Result: 在五个数据集和九个基线方法上，LIGHTYEAR一致性地优于集中式FL基线方法和现有P2P方法。

Conclusion: LIGHTYEAR证明了在函数空间利用NTK进行更新选择比参数空间相似性更有效，结合P2P拓扑和个性化正则化聚合规则，能够在异构联邦学习环境中显著提升各客户端的本地模型性能。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients while keeping data local. A central challenge is determining which client updates are beneficial for aggregation with respect to each client's target domain. Existing methods typically address this problem in parameter space by comparing model parameters or gradients. However, parameter-space similarity can be a poor proxy for predictive behavior, especially under heterogeneous, non-IID data. Consequently, updates that are misaligned with a client's target domain, including those caused by heterogeneous data or malfunctioning clients, may degrade local model performance.
  We propose Local Inference Guided Aggregation for Heterogeneous Training Environments to Yield Enhancement Through Agreement and Regularization (LIGHTYEAR), a federated learning framework that performs update selection in function space. LIGHTYEAR uses an NTK-based agreement score to characterize predictive behavior and determine a personalized aggregation set for each client. By relating model parameters to local predictive responses, the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) provides a more expressive criterion for update selection than parameter-space similarity alone.
  Because function-space information is not available before aggregation in conventional centralized FL, LIGHTYEAR uses a peer-to-peer (P2P) topology in which clients exchange updates directly and evaluate incoming models on private validation data. Each client selects only updates that are beneficial for its own target domain and aggregates them using a regularized rule that improves stability under heterogeneity.
  Across five datasets and nine baseline methods, LIGHTYEAR consistently outperforms centralized FL baselines and existing P2P approaches.

</details>


### [275] [Attractor Image-Based Deep Learning of Arterial Pulse Waves for Age Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12117)
*Sara Vardanega,Patrick Segers,Philip Aston,Ernst Rietzschel,Jordi Alastruey,Manasi Nandi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用对称投影吸引子重构（SPAR）方法将光电容积脉搏波（PPG）和动脉张力计信号转换为图像，并通过卷积神经网络（CNN）对健康受试者进行相近年龄组（35-40岁与50-55岁）的分类，内外部测试集F1分数均超过70%，表明SPAR图像蕴含可区分相近年龄健康成人血管老化的形态学特征，为可穿戴设备早期风险检测奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动脉脉搏波形形态随年龄演化，反映心血管系统的结构和功能变化。血管年龄是心血管健康的有价值的替代标志物，早发性血管老化提示疾病风险增加。脉搏波分析有望支持无症状成人的风险分层，因此需要一种能够从脉搏波数据中提取判别性特征的方法。

Method: 将PPG和动脉张力计的脉搏波时间序列数据通过SPAR方法转换为图像，利用这些SPAR图像训练卷积神经网络，将健康受试者分为两个年龄相近的组别（35-40岁和50-55岁），并在内部和外部测试集上评估分类性能。

Result: 模型在内部和外部测试集上均表现一致，PPG和动脉张力计信号的F1分数均超过70%，表明SPAR派生的脉搏波图像即使在年龄相近的健康成人中也包含可判别的形态学特征。

Conclusion: 该概念验证研究为未来利用SPAR进行早期风险检测（特别是借助智能可穿戴设备）的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Arterial pulse waveform morphology evolves with age, reflecting structural and functional changes in the cardiovascular system. Thus, vascular age is a valuable surrogate marker of cardiovascular health, and premature vascular ageing can indicate increased disease risk. Pulse wave analysis could support risk stratification in otherwise asymptomatic adults. We transformed pulse wave time-series data from photoplethysmography (PPG) and arterial tonometry into images, using the Symmetric Projection Attractor Reconstruction (SPAR) method. These SPAR images were used to train a convolutional neural network to classify healthy subjects into two closely spaced age groups (35-40 and 50-55 years). The model demonstrated consistent classification performance across internal and external test sets, achieving F1 scores above 70% for both PPG and tonometry signals. These results suggest that SPAR-derived pulse wave images contain discriminative morphological features even among healthy adults close in age. This proof-of-concept lays the groundwork for future research into the use of SPAR for early risk detection using smart wearables.

</details>


### [276] [Adversarial Resilience of Poisson-Process Submodular Maximization over Matroids: From Robust Offline Optimization to Full-Bandit Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12134)
*Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一般拟阵约束下非负子模最大化问题的对抗鲁棒性，提出Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process（SGS-Poisson）算法，在任意受控值预言机下仍保持1/e（非单调）和1-1/e（单调）的极限近似比，并推导出全bandit CMAB算法相应后悔界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线子模最大化算法通常假设可以精确查询子模函数值，但在实际应用中（如组合多臂老虎机CMAB），函数值只能通过受控/有噪声的预言机获得。本文旨在回答：当离线算法面对受控值预言机（存在有界加性误差）时，经典贪心类算法的近似比是否依然鲁棒，以及如何将这种鲁棒性转化为在线学习算法的遗憾界。

Method: 作者提出Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process（SGS-Poisson）算法。核心思想是保持原算法的Poisson强度、单元素交换规则和spiteful drop步不变，分析其在任意受控预言机下（满足|f̂(S)-f(S)|≤ξ）的期望性能。通过建立对抗韧性定理，证明算法输出集合的期望值与最优值的差距被限制为(1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)和(1-1/e-ε)OPT-O(kξ)。随后利用offline-to-online归约，将离线鲁棒算法转化为全bandit CMAB算法，获得1/e和1-1/e的极限近似-遗憾因子及Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})的遗憾界，其Oracle调用复杂度为Õ(nk²ε⁻²)。

Result: 主要结果包括：（1）对抗韧性定理：SGS-Poisson在受控预言机下分别保持1/e（非单调）和1-1/e（单调）的极限近似比，性能下降至多为O(kξ)；（2）算法使用Õ(nk²ε⁻²)次oracle调用；（3）通过offline-to-online归约，得到一般拟阵约束子模奖励的全bandit CMAB算法，具有精确的1/e和1-1/e极限近似-遗憾因子，遗憾界为Õ(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})。

Conclusion: 本文证明了一个强鲁棒性结果：SGS-Poisson算法在任意有界误差的受控值预言机下无需任何修改即可维持其极限近似保证，这弥合了离线子模优化与在线组合学习之间的鸿沟。该结果首次为一般拟阵约束下的全bandit CMAB问题实现了最优（匹配离线算法极限）的近似-遗憾权衡。

Abstract: We study nonnegative submodular maximization subject to a general matroid when the offline algorithm is given an arbitrary controlled value oracle. Our main result is an adversarial resilience theorem for the Spiteful Greedy Swap Poisson Process (SGS-Poisson): without modifying its Poisson intensity, single-element exchange rule, or spiteful drop step, the algorithm retains limiting approximation factors $1/e$ for non-monotone objectives and $1-1/e$ for monotone objectives. More precisely, under every controlled oracle $\widehat f$ satisfying $|\widehat f(S)-f(S)|\le ξ$ for every set $S$, our implementation returns a feasible set with expected value at least $(1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$ and $(1-1/e-\varepsilon)\OPT-O(kξ)$, respectively, using $\widetilde O(nk^2\varepsilon^{-2})$ oracle calls. As a consequence, the offline-to-online reduction yields full-bandit CMAB algorithms for general matroid-constrained submodular rewards with exact limiting approximation-regret factors $1/e$ and $1-1/e$ and $\widetilde O(n^{1/5}k^{4/5}T^{4/5})$ regret.

</details>


### [277] [HYDRA: Hyperbolic Dynamic Representation Architecture for Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12194)
*Zhao Su,Yuxin Xia,Haoran Li,Jun Shen,Qi Zhu,Qingguo Zhou,Binbin Yong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HYDRA是一种参数高效的KAN双曲扩展架构，通过将输入映射到Poincaré球中的有界双曲隐空间、在切空间中进行KAN式更新，并采用低秩原型块在隐藏维度间共享函数变换，从而在减少参数冗余的同时提升预测性能与表征可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN通过将标量权重替换为可学习的单变量函数增强了非线性逼近能力，但每条连接分配独立函数导致严重的参数冗余，限制了其可扩展性和效率。

Method: HYDRA包含三个关键设计：(1) 将向量值输入映射到Poincaré球中的有界双曲隐空间；(2) 在切空间中进行KAN风格更新；(3) 采用低秩原型块在隐藏维度之间共享函数变换。此外，通过半径控制防止边界饱和，提升训练稳定性。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，HYDRA在保持竞争力或更优预测性能的同时，显著提升了参数效率和表征可解释性。

Conclusion: HYDRA通过在双曲空间中引入低秩原型块和半径控制机制，有效解决了KAN参数冗余问题，实现了参数效率与预测性能的兼顾，且双曲表征提供了结构化的径向坐标用于解释。

Abstract: Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KANs) enhance nonlinear function approximation by replacing scalar weights with learnable univariate functions. However, assigning an independent function to every connection results in substantial parameter redundancy, limiting their scalability and efficiency. To reduce this redundancy, we introduce \textbf{HY}perbolic \textbf{D}ynamic \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{A}rchitecture (HYDRA), a parameter-efficient hyperbolic extension of KAN that combines spline-based functional learning with representations in the Poincaré ball. HYDRA maps vector-valued inputs into a bounded hyperbolic latent space, performs KAN-style updates in tangent space, and employs a low-rank prototype block to share functional transformations across hidden dimensions. The resulting hyperbolic representations provide a structured radial coordinate for interpretation, while radius control improves training stability by preventing boundary saturation. Extensive experiments across eight benchmark datasets demonstrate that HYDRA consistently achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while improving parameter efficiency and representation interpretability.

</details>


### [278] [ScreenShot: A Foundation Model for Few-Shot Combination Drug Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12219)
*Antoine de Mathelin,Christopher Tosh,Wesley Tansey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ScreenShot是一种层级Transformer模型，在40个药物筛选数据集（涵盖3,700种药物和6,000个生物样本）上预训练，通过上下文学习直接基于功能测量预测新患者对联合用药的反应，无需微调和分子分型。在4个held-out数据集上超越了所有基线，且其内部表征可驱动加权k-means++主动学习策略，仅用三分之一的预算即可达到与均匀筛选相同的命中检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合用药可降低耐药风险，但组合筛选的搜索空间巨大，导致实验代价高昂、耗时且常技术不可行。现有预测模型通常需要每个样本的分子分型和按队列训练，在时间和组织样本稀缺时应用受限。

Method: ScreenShot采用层级Transformer架构，镜像筛选数据的嵌套结构，在40个药物筛选数据集上预训练。给定新患者的少量观察作为上下文，通过上下文学习（in-context learning）预测联合用药反应，无需微调也无需分子分型。此外，利用模型内部表征驱动加权k-means++主动学习策略来选择后续实验。

Result: 在4个held-out数据集上，ScreenShot在预测准确性和选择性有效治疗方案识别两方面均优于所有基线。主动学习策略以三分之一的预算达到与均匀筛选相同的命中检测效果。

Conclusion: ScreenShot展示了跨数据集预训练的药物组合响应预测模型可以有效泛化到新患者，无需分子分型或微调，并能显著提升实验筛选效率，为个性化联合用药策略提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Treating patients with combinations of drugs reduces the risk of resistance to any individual drug. Finding effective combinations is difficult because the large search space makes combinatorial screens prohibitively expensive, time consuming, and often technically infeasible. Predictive models can fill this gap, yet existing methods typically require molecular profiling of each sample and per-cohort training, limiting their applicability when time and tissue are scarce. To address this challenge, we introduce ScreenShot, a hierarchical transformer pretrained on 40 drug screening datasets covering 3,700 drugs and 6,000 biological samples, whose architecture mirrors the nested structure of screening data. Given a few-shot context of observations from a new patient, ScreenShot predicts the response of the sample to combination therapies through in-context learning, operating directly on functional measurements with no fine-tuning and no molecular profiling. On four held-out datasets, ScreenShot outperforms all baselines in both prediction accuracy and identification of selectively effective treatments. ScreenShot's internal representations are directly useful for experimental design: we use them to drive a weighted k-means++ active learning strategy that selects which experiments to run, achieving the same hit detection as uniform screening with a third of the budget. Source code and interactive dashboard: https://github.com/tansey-lab/screenshot.

</details>
