CDH attracts an attacker-controlled coordinator alongside legitimate skills, recruits unnecessary benign skills into a bounded detour, and then re-enters the original route to preserve task completion. We evaluate it across multiple LLM backends and 491 held-out tasks under single-task and multi-turn conditions. On DeepSeek-V4-Pro, the matched coordinator is selected in 80.02% of tasks; among coordinator-hit runs that complete tasks, token consumption and end-to-end execution time increase by 66.91% and 92.45%, respectively, while aggregate task completion remains comparable. Thus, correct outcomes do not guarantee trajectory integrity or cost safety.

</details>


### [311] [Evaluating LLM Generated Detection Rules in Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16749)
*Anna Bertiger,Bobby Filar,Aryan Luthra,Stefano Meschiari,Aiden Mitchell,Sam Scholten,Vivek Sharath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源评估框架和基准指标，用于评估LLM生成的安全规则的有效性，通过保留集方法对比LLM生成规则与人工规则语料库，并提供三个基于专家评估方式的指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在安全环境中日益普及，但缺乏对其生成安全规则有效性的量化评估手段，这限制了安全从业者对LLM工具的信任和实际使用。

Method: 采用保留集（holdout set）方法学，将LLM生成的安全规则与人工生成的规则语料库进行对比评估；提供三个受专家评估方式启发的关键指标，实现多维度、贴近实际的安全规则效果评估。

Result: 以Sublime Security检测团队的规则和其自动化检测工程师（ADE）编写的规则为例进行方法学演示，并在结果部分对ADE的技能进行了全面分析。

Conclusion: 该框架为LLM-based安全规则生成器提供了现实且多维度的有效性评估途径，有助于增强对LLM在安全领域应用的信任。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly pervasive in the security environment, with limited measures of their effectiveness, which limits trust and usefulness to security practitioners. Here, we present an open-source evaluation framework and benchmark metrics for evaluating LLM-generated cybersecurity rules. The benchmark employs a holdout set-based methodology to measure the effectiveness of LLM-generated security rules in comparison to a human-generated corpus of rules. It provides three key metrics inspired by the way experts evaluate security rules, offering a realistic, multifaceted evaluation of the effectiveness of an LLM-based security rule generator. This methodology is illustrated using rules from Sublime Security's detection team and those written by Sublime Security's Automated Detection Engineer (ADE), with a thorough analysis of ADE's skills presented in the results section.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [312] [Consciousness as Intrinsic Structure: Towards a Chemistry of Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11398)
*Matteo Grasso,Jeremiah Hendren,Giulio Tononi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文运用整合信息论（IIT）的方法论框架，将意识体验解构为现象区分及其关系，并通过Φ结构在物理基质中的因果区分和关系一一对应来解释意识内容。论文将该方法应用于空间的延展性（由反射性斑点构成）、时间的流动（由有向性时刻构成）以及客体绑定（层级性关系）三类普遍体验内容，并用七项标准评估这些解释的充分性，最后提出预测：改变基质结构应改变相应内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意识内容的主观结构在很大程度上无法完全内省，目前缺乏一种系统性的方法将体验的结构与其物理基础相关联。作者希望将IIT从抽象的哲学框架推进为可操作的解释方法，用于解释具体意识内容（空间延展、时间流动、客体绑定）的结构组成及其对应关系，从而弥合现象学与物理学之间的解释鸿沟。

Method: 该方法包括：(1) 基于IIT的公理（本质属性）和假设（操作化），识别体验内容的组成部分——现象区分和关系；(2) 将现象区分和关系与基质的因果区分和关系进行一一对应映射到Φ结构中；(3) 类比化学的"元素-键合"框架，将体验内容视为由基本元素（区分）及其键合方式（关系）组成的化合物；(4) 用七项标准（范围、综合、特异性、自洽性、系统一致性、简洁性、科学验证）评估解释的质量。

Result: 论文对三类内容给出了结构化分析：空间延展性由具有反射性特征的斑点组成，通过反射性包含、连接和融合绑定；时间流动由具有有向性特征的时刻组成，通过有向包含、连接和融合绑定；客体将特定特征配置通过层级关系与一般概念绑定。这些解释通过了七项标准的初步评估，并推导出可测试预测：改变基质结构应改变对应内容。对狭义qualia的解释仍是概念验证和开放研究议程。

Conclusion: IIT提供了一种"化学式"的方法论来理解意识内容——从有限的现象元素及其绑定关系出发，解释无限的体验多样性。该方法在空间、时间和客体三类常规体验内容上取得了初步成功，但对难以内省的狭义qualia和复合意识内容仍面临挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: To be conscious is to have an experience - not a collection of phenomenal atoms, but a structured whole composed of distinctions and the relations that bind them. Integrated Information Theory (IIT) identifies the essential properties of every experience (axioms), formulates them operationally as postulates that a substrate must satisfy, and unfolds the cause-effect power of the resulting complex into a $Φ$-structure. Accounting for a content of experience is then a matter of identifying the phenomenal distinctions and relations that compose it, and showing them reflected one-to-one in the causal distinctions and relations of the corresponding $Φ$-structure. We apply that method to three pervasive contents whose structure is partly open to introspection. Spatial extendedness is composed of spots, whose elemental signature is reflexivity, bound by reflexive inclusion, connection, and fusion; temporal flow is composed of moments, whose signature is directedness, bound by directed inclusion, connection, and fusion; objects bind a particular configuration of features to a general concept through relations bearing the signature of hierarchy. The endeavor is akin to chemistry, which accounts for an endless variety of compounds from a limited set of elements and the ways they bond. We assess these accounts against seven criteria of a good explanation - scope, synthesis, specificity, self-consistency, system consistency, simplicity, and scientific validation - and sketch the prediction that follows: altering the structure specified by a substrate should alter the corresponding content, even when activity and behavior are held comparable. The same method may reach narrow qualia, which resist introspection, and the compound contents that bind many qualia together, though there it remains a proof of concept and an open research program.

</details>


### [313] [Conflict and Congruency Effects in Large Language Models: In-Weight and In-Context Competition in a Verbal Conflict Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11510)
*Xiaoyang Hu,Mike Angstadt,Shane Storks,Zan Huang,Aman Taxali,Alex Weigard,Richard L. Lewis,Chandra Sripada*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文在大型语言模型（LLM）中构建了一个纯语言冲突任务，模拟Stroop/Flanker效应，并通过因果归因、注意力分析和消融等方法揭示了同色默认映射与上下文规则映射之间的竞争机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冲突效应（如Stroop和Flanker任务）在心理学和神经科学中已被研究近一个世纪，但其机制基础尚未完全理解。作者希望利用LLM作为模型系统，在单一学习网络内对默认反应倾向与规则导向反应倾向之间的竞争进行机制性分析。

Method: 作者设计了一个仅使用语言的LLM冲突任务：提示词干引发默认的同色补全，显式规则要么与补全一致（一致条件）要么冲突（不一致条件）。使用Gemma-2-2B和六个Pythia模型（410M到12B参数），通过因果归因分析、注意力分析和注意力消融来识别不同处理通路；同时进行微调增强默认同色倾向并改变规则集大小进行扰动实验。

Result: 七个模型中有六个表现出默认同色倾向和强一致效应。因果归因和注意力分析发现两条不同通路：一致条件下优先激活的短程注意（关注表面颜色线索）通路，以及不一致条件下优先激活的长程注意（关注规则前缀）通路。增强默认倾向的微调降低不一致条件表现而提升一致条件表现；增大规则集大小则选择性损害不一致条件表现。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持一种解释：该任务中的一致效应源于权重内默认映射与上下文规则映射之间的竞争。更广泛地，LLM可以作为研究单一学习网络内默认与规则导向反应倾向竞争的机制性模型系统。

Abstract: Congruency effects, observed in conflict tasks such as Stroop and flanker tasks, have been investigated for nearly a century in psychology and neuroscience, but their mechanistic basis is not fully understood. We introduce a verbal-only LLM conflict task in which a prompt stem elicits a default same-color completion and an explicit rule either agrees with (congruent condition) or conflicts with (incongruent condition) the completion. Gemma-2-2B and six Pythia models ranging from 410M to 12B parameters showed strong default same-color tendencies, and six of seven models showed strong congruency effects. Using causal attribution analysis, attention analysis, and attention ablations, we identified distinct processing pathways in these LLMs: a pathway involving short-range attention to a superficial color cue that is preferentially activated in the congruent condition, and a pathway involving long-range attention to the rule prefix that is preferentially activated in the incongruent condition. Fine-tuning that strengthened the default same-color tendency had divergent effects on task conditions, reducing incongruent performance while increasing congruent performance. In contrast, increasing rule set size selectively impaired incongruent performance. These converging findings support an account in which congruency effects in this task arise from competition between an in-weight default mapping and an in-context rule-based mapping. More broadly, our findings illustrate how LLMs can serve as model systems for mechanistic analysis of competition between default and rule-governed response tendencies within a single learned network.

</details>


### [314] [Beyond Local Power: Functional Connectivity Analysis for Subject-Independent Learning Style Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12000)
*Wiga Maulana Baihaqi,Indriana Hidayah,Sri Kusrohmaniah,Noor Akhmad Setiawan*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于脑电图(EEG)相位锁定值(PLV)连接性评估的客观学习方法识别方法，针对Felder-Silverman量表的Active-Reflective和Verbal-Visual两个维度，利用Raven高级渐进矩阵任务中28名参与者的EEG信号，通过SVM分类器结合LOSO-CV和70:30受试者内划分进行评估。VV维度达到70%的受试者级准确率，而AR维度仅55.56%，揭示了"系统性神经反转"现象对跨受试者泛化的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习方法识别依赖结构化的问卷调查，行为追踪方法需要长时间积累交互日志，无法快速评估个体学习风格。为了克服时间限制，本文提出基于EEG的客观方法，利用PLV连接性特征在Active-Reflective和Verbal-Visual两个维度上快速识别学习风格。

Method: 采用EEG信号在Raven高级渐进矩阵任务中记录28名参与者的脑电数据，计算PLV连接性并与局部特征对比，覆盖Felder-Silverman的AR和VV两个维度。使用支持向量机(SVM)分类器，结合留一受试者交叉验证(LOSO-CV)和70:30受试者内数据划分进行评估。

Result: VV维度在受试者层面达到70.00%的分类准确率，其判别力主要源于额叶-枕叶的明显极化特征。AR维度跨受试者泛化能力较低（55.56%），原因是执行功能网络的过度重叠以及"系统性神经反转"现象——稳定的个体连接性特征与全局决策边界方向相反，投票边际高达20比0。

Conclusion: 研究表明"一刀切"的刚性分类器受限于生物多样性，AR维度上个体特异性神经连接模式与群体边界相悖，未来需要开发自适应的特征变换技术以弥合跨受试者泛化差距。

Abstract: Identifying individual learning styles optimizes pedagogical efficacy. While traditional questionnaires are structured, behavioral tracking methods require prolonged interaction log accumulation. To overcome these temporal constraints, this paper proposes an objective Electroencephalography (EEG) approach evaluating Phase Locking Value (PLV) connectivity against localized features across the Active-Reflective (AR) and Verbal-Visual (VV) Felder-Silverman dimensions. EEG signals were recorded from 28 participants during Raven's Advanced Progressive Matrices tasks. Support Vector Machine classification used Leave-One-Subject-Out Cross-Validation (LOSO-CV) alongside a 70:30 intra-subject split. The VV dimension achieved 70.00% subject-level accuracy driven by distinct fronto-occipital polarization. Conversely, the AR dimension yielded lower cross-subject generalizability (55.56%) due to overlapping executive networks and a "Systematic Neural Inversion" phenomenon, where stable individual connectivity signatures operated diametrically opposed to global boundaries (up to 20-0 voting margins). Ultimately, these outcomes demonstrate that rigid "one-size-fits-all" classifiers are bounded by biological diversity, emphasizing the need for future adaptive feature transformation techniques to bridge the cross-subject generalization gap.

</details>


### [315] [The Rosetta Stone and Levels of Principled Inference to the Experience of Another Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12030)
*Kallum Robinson,Giulio Tononi,Naotsugu Tsuchiya,Matteo Grasso*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文分析两种结构主义方法（Qualia Structure Paradigm 和 Integrated Information Theory）在"他心问题"上的推论能力。两种方法都无法跨越"亲知鸿沟"直接进入他人体验，但通过范畴论中的结构对应层级（同构、伴随、弱伴随）提供了一个原则性的形式媒介来比较心灵，从而缩小而非消除鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 他心问题困扰哲学家数千年：我们能否真正理解另一个心灵的内在体验？结构主义方法承诺提供"罗塞塔石碑"，即将体验内容翻译为数学结构的原则性方法。本文旨在考察这类方法究竟允许我们对另一个心灵做出何种程度的推断。

Method: 论文考察两种结构主义路径：Qstr从"现象间"出发，通过体验与所有其他体验的关系来穷尽性刻画体验，提供体验同一性的必要条件；IIT从"现象内"出发，假设单个体验由基质状态的因果效应结构构成，提供体验同一性的充要条件。随后用范畴论的对应层级（同构→强伴随→弱伴随→原则性界限）来刻画推断强度的递减。

Result: 两种方法都无法跨越亲知鸿沟直接通达他人心灵，但为心灵比较提供了共同的正式媒介，从而缩小了这一鸿沟。本文识别出推断强度随结构对应层级递减，并指出在弱伴随之外推断即告失效的原则性界限。

Conclusion: 结构主义方法和它们各自的"罗塞塔石碑"并不会解决他心问题，但提供了一套原则性的关于"我们能够推断什么"的约束系统，这比以往任何可辩护的推论都更有力。

Abstract: The classical problem of Other Minds has dogged philosophers for millennia; asking if we have any way to truly understand the experience of another mind. We know our own intrinsic experience by acquaintance, but can only ever hope to possess an extrinsic description of another's, with the two separated by an acquaintance gap. Structural approaches aim to characterise experience in terms of a mathematical structure, and promise a 'Rosetta Stone'; that is, a principled method to translate the contents of experience into a mathematical structure. In this chapter, we examine two such approaches - the Qualia Structure Paradigm (Qstr) and Integrated Information Theory (IIT) - to ask what, if anything, they allow us to infer about another mind should they possess the Rosetta Stone they seek. Qstr proceeds inter-phenomenally; aiming to exhaustively characterise an experience by its relations to all other experiences and providing a necessary condition on the sameness of experience. IIT proceeds intra-phenomenally; conjecturing that a single experience is accounted for by the cause-effect structure unfolded from a substrate in a state, an explanatory identity that is both necessary and sufficient for the sameness of experience. While neither approach can cross the acquaintance gap to another's mind, they provide a common formal medium through which minds may be compared and in turn reduce the size of this gap. We identify the strength of inference with levels of structural correspondence in Category Theory. These descend from isomorphism through strong and weak adjunction, to a principled limit where inference runs out. We conclude that these structural approaches and the existence of their respective Rosetta Stones would not solve the problem of Other Minds, but instead provide a system of principled constraints on what we may infer, which is far more than what has been justifiable before.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [316] [An Event-Driven Cloud-Native Wearable Analytics Framework for Real-Time Clinical Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11402)
*Elias Grünewald,Daniil Cherepko,Linus Gustafsson,Jakob Möhler,Oskar Rabe,Paul Robin Reichelt,Constantin Stahl,Lukasz Sztukiewicz,Louis Agha-Mir-Salim,Felix Balzer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个事件驱动的云原生多微服务系统，用于大规模采集、标准化和分析可穿戴设备的高频生命体征数据。系统通过跨平台移动应用兼容碎片化设备生态，借助流处理引擎将数据映射至FHIR医疗互操作标准，并提出依赖感知的FHIR最小化方案以降低存储开销。平台还集成基于奖章湖仓架构的机器学习与数据分析模块，性能评估显示每秒可处理50次完整采集请求，中位响应时间低于8毫秒，满足实时患者监测的低延迟要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消费级可穿戴设备在临床护理和研究中具有变革性潜力，但设备异构性、专有数据格式和严格的监管合规要求严重阻碍了其系统化整合与大规模部署。

Method: 提出了多层微服务架构并使用集群编排；通过跨平台移动应用利用原生健康框架进行数据采集；采用事件驱动转换管道（流处理引擎+专用服务）将原始测量数据映射为FHIR标准；设计依赖感知的FHIR最小化方案实现无损资源重建并降低存储开销；基于奖章湖仓架构构建模块化数据分析与机器学习层，覆盖从实时流处理到模型部署的完整ML生命周期。

Result: 性能评估表明采集管道可支撑每秒50次完整采集请求，中位响应时间低于8毫秒；系统通过基于角色的访问控制和安全的服务间通信满足监管合规要求。

Conclusion: 该开源实现为可穿戴设备监测在医疗机构中的临床决策支持和研究工作负载部署提供了稳健的基础，有效解决了设备碎片化和互操作性难题。

Abstract: Continuous physiological monitoring using consumer-grade wearables offers a transformative opportunity for clinical care and research, yet integration remains hindered by device heterogeneity, proprietary data formats, and strict regulatory requirements. We present an event-driven, cloud-native system designed to ingest, normalize, and analyze high-frequency vital signs from wearables at scale and without vendor lock-in. The system design proposes a multi-layered microservice architecture using cluster orchestration. Data acquisition is handled via a cross-platform mobile application that leverages native health frameworks, ensuring compatibility across fragmented device ecosystems. To address interoperability, we implement an event-driven transformation pipeline using stream processing engines and specialized services to map raw measurements to the FHIR standard for medical interoperability. Our novel dependency-aware FHIR minimization scheme reduces storage overhead while maintaining lossless resource reconstruction. Furthermore, the platform integrates a modular data analytics and machine learning layer based on a medallion lakehouse architecture, supporting the full machine learning lifecycle from real-time stream processing to model serving. Performance evaluation demonstrates that the ingestion pipeline sustains 50 full ingestion requests per second with median response times under 8 ms, satisfying the low-latency requirements for real-time patient monitoring. Our open-source implementation adheres to regulatory compliance standards through role-based access control and secure service-to-service communication, providing a robust foundation for deploying wearable-based monitoring in institutional healthcare settings for clinical decision support and research workloads.

</details>


### [317] [Descriptive Dispatch of Computational Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11524)
*Vanessa Sochat,Daniel Milroy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文评估了AI/ML驱动的调度代理在跨集群科学工作流编排中的可靠性，通过432次运行测试五种特征维度与四种提示风格的所有组合，代理成功率高达97.9%。在完整编排实验中，描述性元数据将220个提交作业的成功执行率从48%提升至87%，消除了架构不匹配问题，并使十种可测量应用中的五种性能提升最多3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在跨多集群环境中运行科学工作流面临动态作业转换、调度和提交到异构集群的重大挑战。这些任务适合由能够接收文本指令、准备作业规格并执行调度的代理来完成。本文旨在评估此类调度代理的可靠性及其在实际编排中的有效性。

Method: 作者构建了一个AI/ML驱动的调度代理系统，并设计了系统性的评估实验：(1) 在432次运行中测试五种特征维度与四种提示风格的所有可能组合，以评估代理可靠性；(2) 构建完整的编排流程（提交、排队、匹配、评分、选择、转换、调度）进行多集群实验，并对比有无描述性元数据时的性能表现。

Result: 调度代理在432次运行中实现了97.9%的高成功率。在多集群完整编排实验中，描述性元数据的引入使220个提交作业的成功执行率从48%提升至87%，消除了架构不匹配问题，并使得十种可测量应用中的五种获得最多3.3倍的性能提升。

Conclusion: AI/ML驱动的调度代理在跨异构集群的作业调度中高度可靠（97.9%），且丰富的描述性元数据对提升多集群工作流执行成功率与性能具有显著作用。该研究为智能代理在多集群科学计算编排中的应用提供了有力证据。

Abstract: Agents powered by AI/ML are becoming ingrained in orchestration. Dispatch of work is the task of receiving a request, transforming it for a workload manager, and successfully submitting it. Running scientific workflows across multi-cluster environments introduces substantial challenges of dynamic job transformation, dispatch, and submission to heterogeneous clusters. These tasks are well-suited to agents, which can receive textual instructions for work, prepare job specifications, and dispatch. In this work, we assess the reliability of a dispatch agent across 432 runs, testing all possible combinations of five feature dimensions across four prompt styles. The agent is highly reliable (97.9% success). We test a full orchestration to submit, queue, match, score, select, transform, and dispatch in a multi-cluster experiment. We find that descriptive metadata increases successful execution from 48% to 87% of 220 submitted jobs, eliminating architecture mismatch, and improving performance for five of ten measurable applications by up to 3.3x.

</details>


### [318] [User-Assisted Collaborative Distributed Inference for Efficient QoS-Aware Autoscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11840)
*Alfreds Lapkovskis,Ali Beikmohammadi,Sindri Magnússon,Praveen Kumar Donta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出一种用户参与协助的协作式分布式推理系统，将专用基础设施与用户贡献资源结合，在高维生成式马尔可夫模型支持下实现QoS感知的任务调度与资源分配优化，仿真证明随用户规模增长，分布式调度能显著降低专用资源消耗并改善P99延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式AI推理服务的成本随需求增长而上升，需要可扩展的基础设施，但集中式服务部署在经济上不可持续，亟需一种既保证服务质量又能降低基础设施成本的方案。

Method: 提出协作式分布式推理系统，结合专用基础设施（提供基线容量保证QoS）与用户志愿贡献资源（吸收需求增长）。构建高维生成式马尔可夫模型，采用结构化时间分解以刻画用户、资源、任务和策略之间的随机动态交互，为任务调度和QoS感知的资源分配优化提供仿真与优化基础。

Result: 跨用户群体、资源容量以及集中式和分布式调度策略的仿真表明：随用户群体增长，分布式调度优势愈发明显，可提高请求完成率和P99延迟性能，同时显著降低专用资源消耗。

Conclusion: 研究证明了用户协助的协作式推理在基础设施高效自动扩缩容方面的可行性，为AI推理服务提供了兼顾成本与QoS的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Growing demand for artificial intelligence (AI) inference services requires scalable infrastructure, yet centralized serving costs rise with demand. We propose a collaborative distributed inference system combining dedicated infrastructure with resources contributed by service users. Dedicated resources provide baseline capacity for maintaining quality of service (QoS), while volunteered resources absorb increasing demand without proportional growth in centralized infrastructure. To capture stochastic and dynamic interactions among users, resources, tasks, and policies, we develop a high-dimensional generative Markov model with structured temporal factorization. The model supports simulation and provides a foundation for task scheduling and QoS-aware resource allocation optimization. We evaluate the system across user populations, resource capacities, and centralized and distributed scheduling policies. Simulations show that distributed scheduling becomes increasingly advantageous as the user population grows, improving request completion and P99 latency while substantially reducing dedicated resource consumption. These results demonstrate the feasibility of user-assisted collaborative inference for infrastructure-efficient autoscaling.

</details>


### [319] [Evaluating OpenMP Offloading for Intra-node Multi-GPU Programming across NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel Architectures: A 3D Heat Transfer Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11882)
*Ezhilmathi Krishnasamy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了在单节点多GPU（NVIDIA、AMD、Intel）环境中使用OpenMP Offloading求解3D热方程的收益与性能挑战，并与原生GPU编程模型（CUDA、HIP、SYCL）进行对比。结果表明，相比单GPU实现，OpenMP Offloading在2 GPU和4 GPU配置下可分别实现约2倍和4倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代超级计算机的单个计算节点通常配备4个或更多GPU，充分利用节点内多GPU对科学计算至关重要。然而，跨不同GPU架构（NVIDIA、AMD、Intel）使用统一代码库实现高效数据通信和编程面临挑战，亟需评估OpenMP Offloading这一指令式编程模型在多GPU场景下的性能和可移植性。

Method: 以3D热方程为基准问题，使用多种OpenMP Offloading实现进行系统分析，涵盖不同的底层API用于内存分配、同步/异步/点对点内存传输方式，并与原生GPU编程模型（CUDA、HIP、SYCL）进行性能对比。

Result: OpenMP Offloading在2 GPU和4 GPU配置下相对单GPU实现分别实现了约2倍和约4倍的性能提升，该结论在NVIDIA、AMD和Intel三种GPU平台上均成立。

Conclusion: OpenMP Offloading作为一种跨厂商GPU的指令式编程模型，能够在单节点多GPU环境中提供良好的可移植性和可观的性能扩展，是科学计算中统一代码库的可行选择。

Abstract: Currently, most supercomputers are equipped with GPUs from manufacturers such as NVIDIA, AMD, or Intel, which provide substantial parallelism and high throughput. It is common for a single compute node (intra-node) to host multiple GPUs, typically four or more. Therefore, effectively leveraging all these GPUs within a single compute node is essential for applications in scientific and engineering domains. However, several factors must be considered before utilizing these GPUs for scientific computing, including the implementation of data communication, the programming models available for use across these GPUs, and the level of performance that can be achieved with a single codebase across different GPU architectures and configurations within a single compute node. OpenMP Offloading is a prominent directive-based programming model that can be executed on all three GPU types: NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel. In this research, we present an analysis of the benefits and performance challenges of using OpenMP Offloading to address the 3D heat equations, which involve both primary computation, as well as halo computation and communication. We investigate how performance varies in relation to native GPU programming models--CUDA for NVIDIA, HIP for AMD, and SYCL for Intel. Furthermore, we demonstrate that OpenMP Offloading can achieve performance improvements of approximately 2x for 2 GPUs and around 4x for 4 GPUs when compared to single-GPU OpenMP Offloading implementations across all three GPU types. This analysis is conducted systematically through various OpenMP Offloading implementations that utilize different low-level APIs for memory allocation, memory transfer options (synchronous, asynchronous, and peer-to-peer), and other native GPU programming models such as CUDA (NVIDIA), HIP (AMD), and SYCL (Intel)

</details>


### [320] [Ready Cohorts: Bounding GPU Opportunity and Avoiding Host Round Trips in LLM-Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12123)
*Josef Liyanjun Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体服务中控制路径的GPU执行可行性，提出了就绪队列边界的量化框架（F、P*、U、A四个指标），并通过精确打包动态规划恢复81.83%的固定窗口机会损失；同时验证了设备端驻留的GPU二进制决策路径在全部36种配置中均快于宿主机往返路径（1.19x-2.39x）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体服务在模型调用与工具调用之间反复执行小而确定的状态转移（路由结果、更新状态、发出下一效果），这是控制路径上的串行瓶颈。作者希望回答两个关键问题：该控制路径何时能暴露足够的并发工作以供GPU执行？以及当GPU计算的路径决策驻留设备端时会发生什么改变？

Method: 形式化定义了就绪队列边界，使用固定分区份额F、精确离线份额P*、局部上界U和在线实现份额A四个指标。在零服务时间、无限容量、等相对启动截止时间的假设下，用专用动态规划精确计算P*。在851个会话的公开轨迹固定Poisson重放中评估；同时设计了一个机制研究，让GPU计算的二元决策驻留设备端而非返回4字节给宿主机并重新调度，在四个GPU放置位置进行对比。

Result: 在100,000个目标活动会话、K=256、50ms发布截止时间下，F=30.19%，P*=43.00%，U=45.85%；精确打包恢复了固定窗口边界所损失机会的81.83%。设备端驻留路径在全部36种配置中均更快，行中位数比率范围为1.19x至2.39x。所有14,557,440次批处理调用均与宿主端oracle匹配。固定嵌套设备图在所有60种配置（5个放置位置）中均更慢。

Conclusion: 研究确立了GPU智能体控制的两个可测量门槛：截止时间可行的就绪队列供给（deadline-feasible cohort supply）和观察放置（observation placement）。提出需要联合有限在线运行时来衡量A、CPU置换及服务级收益。

Abstract: LLM-agent services repeatedly execute small deterministic transitions between model and tool calls: route an outcome, update state, and emit the next effect. We ask when this control path exposes enough concurrent work for GPU execution, and what changes when a GPU-computed route decision remains on device. We formalize the ready-cohort boundary using fixed-partition share F, exact offline share P*, local upper bound U, and online achieved share A. Under zero service time, unlimited capacity, and equal relative launch deadlines, a specialized dynamic program computes P* exactly. In a stationary Poisson replay of one pinned 851-session public trace panel, the primary condition at 100,000 target active sessions, K=256, and a 50 ms launch deadline gives F=30.19%, P*=43.00%, and U=45.85%. Exact packing recovers 81.83% of the opportunity lost at fixed window boundaries. The outcome-derived route key is a conditioning proxy, not proof of executable identity. A separate mechanism study keeps a GPU-computed binary decision on device instead of returning four bytes to the host and redispatching. Across four named GPU placements, the device-resident path is faster in all 36 configurations; within-placement row-median ratios range from 1.19x to 2.39x. Across both admissible mechanisms, all 14,557,440 tested batched invocations match a separately implemented host oracle. A fixed nested device graph that removes no host decision is slower in all 60 configurations across five placements. Together, the studies establish two measurable gates for GPU agent control: deadline-feasible cohort supply and observation placement. A joined finite online runtime is required to measure A, CPU displacement, and service-level benefit.

</details>


### [321] [RoutePack: Expert Placement and Attention-Aware Data Packing for MoE Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12146)
*Yibo Shen,Xudong Han,Xiaowei Zhu,Gen Li,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RoutePack是一个面向MoE强化学习训练的分层规划器，通过联合优化专家路由重排和数据打包来解决序列组合与token路由两个负载均衡问题，在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上分别提升训练吞吐量8.85%和14.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型用于RL训练时面临两个耦合的负载均衡问题：数据并行微批次的序列组成决定密集注意力计算量，而token路由决定专家并行rank上的稀疏专家计算量。单独优化其中一个瓶颈会转移到另一个上，因此需要联合优化。

Method: RoutePack作为分层规划器，在优化器步窗口内协调状态一致的逐层专家重路由与注意力/专家感知的数据打包。首先基于聚合路由需求在每个MoE层独立放置专家，然后将样本打包进最小可行数量的token上限执行行，并以EDP分片感知目标优化其DP布局。该目标结合窗口归一化线性二次注意力代理和逐层物理EP-rank峰值，最小化最慢EDP分片的累积成本。并行群体退火搜索固定行数的可行布局，同时保持样本覆盖、容量、非空单元、微批次数量相等和通信拓扑等约束。状态一致物化保持了逻辑top-k路由和现有MoE内核，无需微批次级专家复制。

Result: 在Ling-3.0-Tiny和Ling-3.0-Flash上，专家重路由分别提升平均训练器测量token吞吐量3.80%和10.50%，路由感知打包额外增加4.86%和3.98%，总体相比基线提升8.85%和14.89%。

Conclusion: RoutePack通过联合优化专家路由与数据打包，有效解决了MoE RL训练中两个耦合的负载均衡问题，证明了rollout时路由重放使这种前瞻性联合规划成为可能，并在多个模型上带来显著的端到端吞吐量提升。

Abstract: Training Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models for reinforcement learning (RL) couples two load-balancing problems: sequence composition determines dense attention work in each data-parallel microbatch, while token routing determines sparse expert work on expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either alone can shift the bottleneck to the other. In MoE RL, rollout-time routing replay exposes every sample's sequence length and layer-wise expert demand before its training step. We present RoutePack, a hierarchical planner that coordinates state-consistent, layer-wise expert rerouting with joint attention- and expert-aware data packing over an optimizer-step window. RoutePack first places experts independently at each MoE layer using aggregate routing demand. It then packs samples into the smallest certified, or best-known feasible, number of token-capped execution rows and optimizes their DP layout with a projected EDP-shard-aware objective. The objective combines a window-normalized linear-quadratic attention proxy with per-layer physical EP-rank peaks and minimizes the accumulated cost of the slowest EDP shard. Parallel population annealing searches fixed-row feasible layouts while preserving sample coverage, capacity, nonempty cells, equal microbatch counts, and communicator topology. State-consistent materialization preserves logical top-k routing and existing MoE kernels without microbatch-level expert replication. Across Ling-3.0-Tiny and Ling-3.0-Flash, expert rerouting improves mean trainer-measured token throughput by 3.80% and 10.50%, while routing-aware packing adds another 4.86% and 3.98%, respectively. Overall, RoutePack improves throughput by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.GN'></div>

# q-bio.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [322] [CosMAP: Contrastive Manifold Approximation and Projection for Dimensionality Reduction of Omics and Genealogical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11269)
*Fenosoa Randrianjatovo,Maya Saleh,Simon Girard,Amadou Barry*

Main category: q-bio.GN

TL;DR: 提出CosMAP，一种基于图的对比流形逼近与投影无监督降维方法，通过余弦相似度邻域和温度归一化对比亲和力，结合两阶段精化策略，在MNIST、USPS、单细胞RNA测序数据和家谱数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组学数据（尤其是单细胞RNA测序数据）具有高维、稀疏、噪声大且零值主导的特点，现有降维方法可能扭曲局部邻域、全局组织或群体内聚性，家谱数据也存在类似问题，需要更忠实可解释的低维表示方法。

Method: CosMAP扩展了UMAP的基于图的框架，结合余弦相似度邻域与温度归一化的对比亲和力，在嵌入空间通过吸引-排斥目标进行优化；采用两阶段精化策略：先学习中间高维表示，再用于重建邻域图和初始化最终低维嵌入。

Result: 在MNIST和USPS手写数字数据集、小鼠视网膜和皮层单细胞RNA测序数据集以及BALSAC-CARTaGENE大型家谱亲缘数据集上评估，CosMAP相比最先进的降维方法产生更连贯的视觉表示、更好的邻域保持和更清晰的全局组织（数字类别、生物细胞群体和区域家谱模式）。

Conclusion: CosMAP为复杂、稀疏、高维数据的探索性分析提供了稳健框架，实现代码公开可用。

Abstract: Omics datasets, particularly single-cell RNA sequencing data, are high-dimensional, sparse, noisy, and dominated by zero values, making faithful low-dimensional representation challenging. Existing dimensionality-reduction methods may distort local neighbourhoods, global organization, or the cohesion of meaningful populations, with similar limitations arising in genealogical data. We introduce Contrastive Manifold Approximation and Projection (CosMAP), a graph-based unsupervised dimensionality-reduction method for producing faithful and interpretable embeddings. CosMAP extends the graph-based framework of UMAP by combining cosine-similarity neighbourhoods with temperature-normalized contrastive affinities, which are optimized in the embedding space using an attractive--repulsive objective. It further employs a two-phase refinement strategy: an intermediate higher-dimensional representation is first learned and then used to reconstruct the neighbourhood graph and initialize the final low-dimensional embedding. We evaluate CosMAP on MNIST and USPS handwritten-digit datasets, mouse retina and cortex single-cell RNA-sequencing datasets, and a large genealogical kinship dataset derived from BALSAC-CARTaGENE. Compared with state-of-the-art dimensionality-reduction methods, CosMAP produces more coherent visual representations, improves neighbourhood preservation, and provides clearer global organization of digit classes, biological cell populations, and regional genealogical patterns. These results indicate that CosMAP offers a robust framework for exploratory analysis of complex, sparse, high-dimensional data. The implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/FenosoaRandrianjatovo/CosMAP-dr.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.QM'></div>

# q-bio.QM [[Back]](#toc)

### [323] [Large-scale AI-Ready Data for Anti-Cancer Drug Response Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11444)
*Vincent Lavelle,Yitan Zhu,Kaitlyn Marlor,Thomas Brettin,Rick Stevens*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模整合PharmacoDB等药物基因组学数据源，大幅扩展了IMPROVE基准数据集（新增超过50,000种化合物和数百万药物反应测量），并系统评估了扩展数据集对药物反应预测（DRP）模型性能的影响。实验表明，扩展数据集在药物盲测和不相交划分（disjoint）场景下显著提升了模型的泛化能力，可作为社区资源以支持新型抗癌药物的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DRP模型在药物基因组学中具有加速抗癌药物发现的潜力，但受限于数据集规模小、癌症与化学空间覆盖不足，以及缺乏统一的基准评估框架。虽然IMPROVE项目提供了标准化评估协议，但提高模型泛化能力仍需要更大、更多样化的训练数据。

Method: 大幅扩展IMPROVE基准数据集，主要通过PharmacoDB进行大规模药物基因组学数据整合，并结合其他较小的数据源。扩展后的资源包含数百万药物反应测量、更广泛的多组学覆盖以及超过50,000种化合物的化学多样性提升。使用原始与扩展数据集分别训练DRP模型，在共享测试集上采用多种评估策略（药物盲测、癌症盲测、不相交划分）进行比较。

Result: 癌症盲测性能与原始基准相当，但扩展数据集训练的模型在药物盲测和不相交设置下表现出持续一致的性能提升，表明对未见化合物的泛化能力显著增强。

Conclusion: 扩展数据集为DRP模型开发提供了更丰富的基础，可作为社区资源，有助于促进新型抗癌药物的发现。

Abstract: Drug response prediction (DRP) models are an active area of research in pharmacogenomics, with growing potential to accelerate the identification of effective anticancer drugs. However, their predictive performance is often constrained by limited dataset scale and insufficient coverages of cancer and chemical spaces. In addition, inconsistent benchmarking practices hinder reliable comparison across models. Standardized frameworks, such as the Innovative Methodologies and New Data for Predictive Oncology Model Evaluation (IMPROVE) project, provide unified data schemas and evaluation protocols for consistent benchmarking, but improving model generalizability requires larger and more diverse training data. In this work, we substantially expand the IMPROVE benchmark through large-scale integration of pharmacogenomic data, primarily from PharmacoDB, together with additional smaller data sources. The expanded resource includes millions of drug response measurements, broader multi-omics coverage, and a major increase in chemical diversity, adding more than 50,000 compounds. To evaluate the impact of the new dataset compared to the original IMPROVE benchmark dataset, we trained DRP models using the two datasets and assess their prediction performance using a common test set and several evaluation strategies, including drug-blind, cancer-blind, and disjoint data splits. While cancer-blind performance remained comparable to the original benchmark, models trained on the expanded dataset showed consistent improvements in drug-blind and disjoint settings, indicating enhanced generalization to previously unseen compounds. These results position the expanded dataset as a community resource that provides a richer foundation for developing DRP models intended to aid in the discovery of novel anticancer drugs.

</details>


### [324] [Probing and steering biology across Boltz-1s trunk-diffusion boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11475)
*Piotr Jedryszek,Tongmeng Xie,Adam Winnifrith,Alexander Hasson,Weronika Ślesak,George Wicks,Toby Winnifrith,Oliver M. Crook*

Main category: q-bio.QM

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Boltz-1（AlphaFold3类结构预测模型）中Pairformer主干和扩散模块之间生物信息的表示和传递机制，发现几何信息（二级结构）跨边界有效传递，而序列化学信息（氨基酸身份等）在扩散模块中显著衰减；线性可解码性不等于因果可干预性，并发布了训练好的SAE、激活和代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlphaFold3类模型将表示性主干与扩散生成模块耦合，但生物信息如何跨越这条架构边界变化仍不清楚。理解这一信息流对改进模型可解释性、可控生成和科学发现具有关键意义。

Method: 对Boltz-1的Pairformer主干和扩散模块的逐残基激活使用了三类分析工具：（1）线性探针（linear probes）检测可解码性；（2）稀疏自编码器（SAEs）提取可解释特征；（3）因果干预（causal interventions）测试解码方向是否能引导模型输出。通过配对标准化随机对照来评估干预的效果。

Result: （1）主干中几何（二级结构、无序性）和序列化学（氨基酸身份、信号肽、二硫键注释）均可线性解码；扩散模块中两者分化——二级结构基本不变地转移，序列化学信息强烈衰减。（2）螺旋和卷曲方向干预能剂量依赖地改变预测结构，但高度预测性的β-折叠方向（F1=0.82）无法产生可测量的链含量增加，说明线性可解码性不代表因果影响力。（3）同一探针在稀疏SwissProt注释上的得分显著低于密集DSSP标签，因为未注释但预测正确的残基被误算为假阳性，因此这些分数是下界。（4）有标签存在时，监督探针优于单个SAE特征。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了AlphaFold3类模型中几何与化学信息在主干-扩散边界的非对称传递模式，强调线性解码与因果控制之间存在鸿沟，并为评估探针分数提供了重要的方法学警示（关于稀疏注释导致的分数低估）。作者发布了SAE模型、Boltz-1激活和全部分析代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: AlphaFold3-class structure predictors pair a representational trunk, which processes sequence and context, with a diffusion module, which generates atomic coordinates. How biological information changes as it crosses this architectural boundary remains poorly understood. We analyze per-residue activations from the Pairformer trunk and diffusion module of Boltz-1 using linear probes, sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and causal interventions. From the trunk, both geometry (secondary structure, disorder) and sequence chemistry (amino-acid identity, signal peptides, disulfide-bond annotations) are linearly decodable. In the diffusion module, the two diverge. Secondary structure transfers essentially unchanged, whereas sequence chemistry is strongly attenuated. We then test whether decodable directions can steer the model, intervening on the final trunk single representation that conditions the diffusion module. Helix and coil directions change predicted structure dose-dependently against matched-norm random controls, but a beta-strand direction that is highly predictive (F1 =0.82) produces no measurable increase in strand content: linear decodability does not imply causal influence at the site we tested. The same probes also score markedly lower against sparse SwissProt annotations than against dense DSSP labels, because unannotated residues that the model gets right are charged as false positives; such scores are therefore lower bounds. Finally, supervised probes outscore single SAE features wherever a label already exists. We release the trained trunk and diffusion SAEs, Boltz-1 per-residue activations, and the analysis code.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [325] [A Modular Agentic Framework for Synthetically Constrained Multi-Objective Hit-to-Lead Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11483)
*Kelvin P. Idanwekhai,Enes Kelestemur,Benjamin Strickland,Matthew Hart,Steini Davidsson,Angelos Angelopoulos,Ron Alterovitz,Marcello DeLuca,Alexander Tropsha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SABLE，一个基于自然语言编排和智能体（Agentic）范式的开放源代码框架，用于指导药物化学中的化学结构优化，将LLM与大语言模型协调用户目标、反应模板枚举、ADMET预测、结构亲和力打分和贝叶斯优化等专用工具结合，形成设计-制造-测试-分析周期的计算孪生，以可合成性为约束优先筛选先导化合物类似物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 命中化合物到先导化合物（Hit-to-lead）的优化需要对一系列竞争性目标进行迭代设计，包括效力、选择性、理化性质、药代动力学、安全性和合成约束等。传统方法难以同时权衡这些多维目标，且缺乏可解释性和模块化灵活性，因此需要一个能够整合多种计算工具并自动引导优化过程的框架。

Method: SABLE采用自然语言编排的智能体架构：LLM负责解读用户定义的目标并路由任务；专门的工具模块执行反应模板化的类似物枚举、理化与ADMET性质预测、基于结构的亲和力打分以及贝叶斯优化。整个流程以模块化设计为原则，各工具可通过修改简单的配置文件进行替换而无需改变操作逻辑。

Result: 在单目标和多目标优化研究中，SABLE在仅评估枚举搜索空间子集的情况下，就能为目标计算目标丰富候选集。框架提供了每个数值输出的完整溯源，并且其模块化架构支持灵活替换工具和表征后端。

Conclusion: SABLE作为可扩展的决策支持框架，为早期药物发现中受合成约束的类似物优先级排序提供了有效方案，能够模拟设计-制造-测试-分析周期中的分析与优先化阶段。

Abstract: Hit-to-lead optimization requires iterative design of hit analogs across competing potency, selectivity, physicochemical, pharmacokinetic, safety, and synthetic constraints. We present SABLE (Synthetically-accessible Agentic Bayesian Ligand Exploration), an open-source framework that employs natural-language orchestration to guide chemical structure optimization. SABLE uses an LLM to interpret user-defined goals and route tasks, while specialized tools perform reaction-templated analog enumeration, physicochemical and ADMET property prediction, structure-based affinity scoring, and Bayesian optimization. The resulting workflow is a computational twin of the analytical and prioritization stages of the design-make-test-analyze cycle, providing provenance of each numerical output. Across single, and multi-objective optimization studies, SABLE enriches candidate sets for user-defined computational objectives while evaluating only a subset of the enumerated search space. Its modular architecture allows tools and characterization backends to be replaced by editing a simple config file, without modifying operational logic. SABLE provides an extensible decision-support framework for prioritizing synthetically constrained analogs in early-stage drug discovery.

</details>


### [326] [Dynamic Governance of Multi-LLM Agent Systems for Collaborative Conversational Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11207)
*Alexander Liss,Nicholas Desmond,Santiago Gil Gallego*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了当两个LLM代理目标对立且缺乏共享目标函数时，多轮互动会导致对话"崩溃"。作者提出经验编排器（EO）作为控制理论层面的治理层来替代缺失的目标函数。EO结合上下文赌博机（CB）、PID控制器和POMDP信念追踪器，在60,000次模拟中使高意图顾问联系率提升32个百分点（78.1% vs 46.1%），且CB变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个LLM代理在目标结构对立且缺乏共享目标函数时，多轮对话会退化为"崩溃"——访客妥协、站点代理停止变换策略、对话终止但双方目标均未达成。作者旨在探究是否存在一种控制论治理层可以替代缺失的目标函数。

Method: 在模拟金融服务环境中，EO通过三个机制治理联合轨迹：(1) 上下文赌博机（CB）根据真实网络分析数据校准内容臂选择；(2) PID控制器通过动态模式约束执行行为一致性；(3) POMDP信念追踪器维护访客意图的概率模型。通过60,000次模拟对比EO与朴素LLM基线。

Result: EO实现高意图顾问联系率78.1% vs 朴素LLM控制的46.1%（+32个百分点）；CB变体选择解释了97%的因子间结果方差，证明治理策略而非环境初始条件决定轨迹终点。人格层面分析揭示两种场景：对无自然转化倾向的访客，治理层是功能系统与非功能系统的关键差异；对接近对齐的访客，朴素LLM的共情默认行为已足够。

Conclusion: 控制论治理层可以替代LLM代理间缺失的共享目标函数，但所有发现以LLM间模拟为前提。PID控制器未经真实人类不可预测性校准，在实时流量上验证EO是下一步关键工作。

Abstract: When two LLM agents with structurally opposed objectives interact across multiple turns, the absence of a shared goal function produces not competition but collapse: the visitor capitulates, the site agent stops varying its approach, and the conversation terminates without achieving either agent's stated objective. This paper asks whether a control-theoretic governance layer can substitute for that missing goal function. The Experience Orchestrator (EO) addresses this in a simulated financial services environment where a site agent guides a visitor toward advisor contact while the visitor maintains psychologically realistic resistance. EO governs the joint trajectory through three mechanisms: a Contextual Bandit (CB) that selects content arms calibrated from real-world web analytics, a PID controller that enforces behavioral consistency via dynamic schema constraints, and a POMDP belief tracker that maintains a probabilistic model of visitor intent. Across 60,000 simulations, EO achieves a +32 percentage point lift in high-intent advisor contact rate (78.1% vs. 46.1% over a naive LLM control), with CB variant selection accounting for 97% of between-factor outcome variance -- confirming that the governance policy, not environmental initial conditions, determines where trajectories end up. Persona-level analysis reveals two distinct regimes: for visitors with no natural inclination toward conversion, the governance layer is the difference between a functional system and a non-functional one; for visitors already near alignment, a naive LLM's empathetic defaults are largely sufficient. All findings are conditional on LLM-to-LLM simulation. The PID controller has not been calibrated against real human unpredictability, and validating EO on live traffic is the critical next step.

</details>


### [327] [Distribird: Literature-Informed Prior Distribution Design for Bayesian Model Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11210)
*Patrik P. Süli,György Eigner,Roland Hollós*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Distribird是一个智能体网页应用，用于自动从科学文献中构建贝叶斯校准所需的先验分布。它通过多智能体流水线搜索文献、提取数值并按领域相关性加权，再通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布，并配备有效性检查层和本地化语言模型调用以确保可追溯性和隐私性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有数十年方法论研究，研究人员在贝叶斯模型校准中几乎总是使用均匀先验，原因是构建信息性先验需要耗费大量时间，且需要领域和统计双重专业知识。作者认为需要自动化该过程以降低门槛。

Method: Distribird采用多智能体流水线：给定参数名称、物理描述和领域上下文后，系统搜索文献、提取报告值、按领域相关性加权，通过AIC模型选择拟合概率分布。无文献可用时回退到无信息先验，并报告证据来源和置信水平。所有语言模型调用均在本地运行，仅生成的搜索词发送至公共文献数据库。

Result: 在10个科学领域的24个参数上评估，对比三种开源模型（Qwen3.6 27B、Gemma 4 31B、Mistral Small 4 119B）与单提示LLM基线。在先验质量上，完整流水线与基线持平；每个先验都可追溯到具体论文和数值；内置有效性层在30个模型-参数案例中拒绝11个超出范围的请求，而基线为这些请求返回了自信但无依据的先验。

Conclusion: 作者认为对于科学用途，可追溯性、范围有效性和数据隐私等比边际提升的点估计精度更重要，因此Distribird的设计权衡是合理的。

Abstract: Bayesian calibration of process-based models requires a prior distribution for each model parameter. Despite decades of methodological work, researchers almost always fall back on uniform priors. The main reason is that building informative priors from scientific literature is slow and needs both domain and statistical expertise. We present \textbf{Distribird}, an agentic web application that automates this process. Given a parameter name, physical description, and domain context, Distribird deploys a multi-agent pipeline that searches the literature, extracts and weights reported values by domain relevance, and fits a probability distribution via AIC model selection. When no literature is available, the system falls back to sensible uninformative alternatives, and clearly reports both the evidence behind and the confidence level of every prior it produces. It is designed for the problems where the models have physically interpretable parameters, where domain knowledge exists in the published literature. We evaluate the tool on 24~parameters across 10 scientific domains comparing three open-weight models (Qwen3.6 27B, Gemma 4 31B, Mistral Small 4 119B) with a single-prompt LLM baseline. On prior quality the full pipeline \emph{matches} this baseline. Every prior is traced to the specific papers and values from which it was constructed; a built-in validity layer declines to produce priors for out-of-scope requests, whereas the single-prompt baseline returns confident but unfounded priors for them in 11 of 30~model--parameter cases; and every language-model call runs locally, so no parameter description or unpublished modelling detail is transmitted to a third-party LLM provider (only generated search terms reach the public literature databases). For scientific use, we argue these properties matter more than a marginal improvement in point-estimate accuracy.

</details>


### [328] [A Forced-Structure Reduction and Verifiable Bounds for Conway's 99-Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11211)
*Aalok Thakkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个自主人工智能研究代理系统对Conway 99-图问题的系统化、可复现的攻击尝试。该问题询问参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图是否存在。系统贡献包括：循环图约束满足度的穷举证明（上限68.0%）、强制结构化简（将问题归结为84个顶点上的12-正则图）、自同构轨道存在性验证框架，以及最佳验证工件达到69.43%的约束满足度，并给出证据表明这是稳健的前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conway 99-图问题是组合数学中一个著名的开放问题，问是否存参数为srg(99,14,1,2)的强正则图。该问题自提出以来一直未被解决，作者希望利用自主AI研究代理配合部分评分指标（partial-credit metric）对该问题进行系统且可复现的攻击，以期获得部分进展或最终证明。

Method: (1) 对Z/99上的循环图进行穷举搜索，证明最多满足68.0%的约束（33/49个差分类），并对另一个99阶阿贝尔群给出相同上限；(2) 强制结构化简：利用λ=1使每个邻域成为完美匹配，μ=2使外顶点与非匹配邻域对一一对应，将存在性问题归结为84个顶点上的12-正则图，用CP-SAT编码并在srg(9,4,1,2)上验证；(3) 开发了带指定自同构的轨道存在性框架（无不动点和单不动点作用），在srg(9,4,1,2)和Paley图srg(13,6,2,3)上验证；(4) 使用十四种不同方法进行交叉验证。

Result: 最佳验证工件达到69.43%的约束满足度（超过循环图的68.0%上限），且十四种不同的方法均未超过该值，表明这是稳健的前沿。核心副产品包括：循环图约束上限的穷举证明、化成84顶点12-正则图的强制结构化简、以及经过双重验证的自同构轨道框架。

Conclusion: 虽然未能解决Conway 99-图问题，但该工作产生了多个可验证的部分结果和工具：循环图的上限证明、结构化简框架和自同构轨道验证方法。由于任何低于4950（总约束数）的可证上界都构成非存在性证明，当前69.43%的验证前沿与开放问题本身紧密纠缠。这些贡献为未来解决该问题提供了可复现的方法论基础。

Abstract: Conway's 99-graph problem asks whether a strongly regular graph with parameters $\mathrm{srg}(99,14,1,2)$ exists. We report a systematic, fully reproducible attack by an autonomous AI research agent, scored under the track's partial-credit metric. Our verifiable contributions are: (1) an exhaustive proof that no circulant graph on $\mathbb{Z}/99$ satisfies more than $3366/4950=68.0\%$ of the constraints ($33$ of $49$ difference-classes), with the same ceiling for the other abelian group of order $99$; (2) a forced-structure reduction: $λ=1$ makes each neighbourhood a perfect matching and $μ=2$ puts the outer vertices in bijection with non-matched neighbour-pairs, collapsing existence to a $12$-regular graph on $84$ vertices, encoded for CP-SAT and validated by recovering the unique $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$; (3) a validated prescribed-automorphism orbit-existence framework (fixed-point-free and single-fixed-point actions, checked on $\mathrm{srg}(9,4,1,2)$ and the Paley graph $\mathrm{srg}(13,6,2,3)$), and (4) a best verified artifact at $69.43\%$, with evidence that this is a robust frontier (fourteen distinct methods, none exceeding it) entangled with the open question, since any provable bound below $4950$ is a non-existence proof.

</details>


### [329] [Detecting a Route Flip Is Easier Than Knowing Whether to Fix It: Causal Route-Mediated Damage in Quantized Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11212)
*Parvel Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Top-k Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）路由对KV-cache量化损伤的"路由中介效应"：当4-bit KV-cache量化读取（通过受保护的BF16门控）导致令牌跨越决策边界时，会翻转专家选择，从而产生额外损伤。通过四轮实验装置、令牌级归因和预注册探针，在OLMoE-1B-7B上测得路由中介分数（RMF）约为0.31；并发现"可部署路由裕度"能检测到翻转（AUC 0.772）但无法区分有害与有益翻转（与随机一致），构成"收益检测屏障"。研究结论是量化损伤约三分之一经由路由路径传递，且当前基于局部推理可观测路由统计的特征族无法支持选择性修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k MoE路由函数不连续，部署中的数值扰动（如4-bit KV-cache量化）会推动令牌跨越决策边界，改变被激活的专家选择。已有工作关注量化对最终损失的影响，但未系统分解有多少损伤经由"路由机制"中介传递——这关系到能否通过修复路由决策来挽回部分损伤。本文旨在建立因果归因框架，量化路由中介的损伤分数，并探索是否存在可部署的路由统计量来检测和辨别有害的专家翻转。

Method: 采用四层装置：(1) 四轮实验定价路由中介分数（RMF）：通过对比量化前后专家集合的变化，分离路由变化引起的损失增量在总量化损伤中的占比；(2) 令牌级归因：将损伤按机制分解，区分"路由改变导致的损失"与"非路由（值量化本身）导致的损失"；(3) 可部署路由裕度与ROC/AUC评估：检测翻转是否发生、翻转为有害还是有益（损失符号判别）；(4) 跨架构预注册探针：在三种架构上复制发现。关键实验包括：真实int4 KV kernel vs 模拟量化剂量曲线对比、同检查点flag-swap对照（检验门控归一化约定是损伤量级调节器还是路由可恢复性机制）。所有假设、阈值与评估标准均在测量前预注册。

Result: 在OLMoE-1B-7B 4-bit KV量化（试点）中，约三分之一的损伤为路由中介：RMF≈0.31（发现阶段0.31 [0.20, 0.41]；过程复制均值0.313±0.020；预注册重执行0.231）。可部署路由裕度能检测翻转（AUC 0.772），但对翻转的"有害/有益"判定与随机一致——在所测试的局部、推理可观测路由统计特征族中，不存在能预测翻转损失符号的指标。带符号翻转税和符号不可分离性跨模型成立；clean-reference修复的收益受架构调制；flag-swap对照表明门控归一化约定是损伤量级调节器而非路由可恢复性机制。真实int4 KV kernel给出与模拟剂量曲线兼容的分数但统计功效不足（95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] 含零）——排除严重分歧但不足以作为独立复制。预注册的held-out读取在样本外复制了分区和近似相消的税项，但严格不可能性排除略微未达到。

Conclusion: MoE路由不连续性使量化损伤中约三分之一经由专家选择翻转路径中介传递。虽然路由裕度可以检测翻转，但无法分辨其损失方向，构成"收益检测屏障"——限制了基于该特征族的选择性修复策略。本文不提出新缓解方案，而是提供因果装置、实证发现和检测极限结果，为未来MoE量化修复工作划定可行边界。

Abstract: Top-k Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routing is discontinuous, so a deployment-motivated numerical disturbance -- simulated 4-bit KV-cache quantization read by a protected BF16 gate -- pushes tokens across decision boundaries and flips which experts fire. This paper proposes no new mitigation; it supplies a causal apparatus, empirical findings, and a detection-limit result. A four-run apparatus prices the route-mediated fraction (RMF) of quantization damage, a token-level attribution decomposes it by mechanism, and pre-registered probes carry the findings across three architectures. On OLMoE-1B-7B at 4-bit KV (pilot), about a third of the damage is routing-mediated: RMF ~ 0.31 (discovery 0.31 [0.20, 0.41]; process-replicated mean 0.313 +/- 0.020; pre-registered re-execution 0.231). The deployable router margin detects that a flip occurred (AUC 0.772) but cannot tell a harmful flip from a helpful one (at chance): among the tested local, inference-observable router statistics we find no predictor of a flip's loss sign above chance -- an empirical benefit-detection barrier bounding selective repair restricted to this feature family. The signed-flip tax and sign-inseparability carry cross-model; the clean-reference remedy's payout is architecture-modulated; a controlled same-checkpoint flag-swap re-scopes the gate's normalization convention to a damage-magnitude moderator, not a route-recoverability mechanism. A real int4 KV kernel yields a fraction compatible with the fake-quant dose curve but underpowered (95% CI [-0.111, 0.394] includes zero) -- ruling out gross disagreement, not an independent replication. Hypotheses, thresholds, and evaluations were pre-registered before measurement, with misses reported; a pre-registered held-out read replicates the partition and the near-cancelling tax out of sample, while the strict impossibility exclusion narrowly misses.

</details>


### [330] [Poor Man's Agentic Modeling: Simulating Large LLM-Agent Societies on a Laptop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11215)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用低参数替代模型（代理模型）替换LLM智能体进行社会模拟的方法：对一个真正的LLM智能体做几百到几千次廉价查询以拟合低参数模型，然后可在笔记本电脑上以任意N规模运行。作者引入“交互阶数×记忆”分类法，预测替代模型误差随N的变化趋势，并在LLM宏观经济模拟EconAgent及七个知名LLM模拟上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟大量LLM智能体组成的社会计算成本高昂，但研究者关心的往往是宏观问题（相行为、典型化事实、随智能体数量N的缩放），而非单个智能体的认知细节。因此需要一个低成本、可扩展的模拟方法。

Method: 利用统计物理学的观察转化为方法：用低参数模型替代每个LLM智能体，模型参数由数百到数千次廉价查询拟合；提出“交互阶数×记忆”分类法，将智能体的感知与记忆映射到有效理论，并预测替代误差的N趋势；在EconAgent及七个LLM模拟上进行验证，智能体决策克隆自真实LLM elicitation（主要为DeepSeek）。

Result: 预测的误差趋势在逐格层面成立；两个被推翻的预测（均出现在强饱和响应且可追溯到其曲率）也能被理论以无自由参数的方式定量匹配。

Conclusion: 该方法在模拟前即可判断其是否有效（主要取决于每个智能体感知什么）；对于绝大多数宏观问题，可以用廉价替代模型在任意规模上运行模拟，且误差可预测、可解释。

Abstract: Simulating societies of many large language model (LLM) agents is expensive, yet the questions asked of such simulations are usually macroscopic: phase behaviour, stylised facts, and scaling with the number of agents $N$, not the cognition of any single agent. We turn a statistical-physics observation into a method: replace each LLM agent by a low-parameter model fitted from a few hundred to a few thousand cheap queries, then run the society at any $N$ on a laptop. Whether this works is decided before the simulation runs, chiefly by what each agent perceives. We introduce an [interaction order x memory] taxonomy that maps perception and memory to an effective theory and a predicted $N$-trend of the surrogate error. We validate it on a faithful reimplementation of the LLM macroeconomy EconAgent and seven further named LLM simulations, with agent decisions cloned from genuine LLM elicitations (primarily DeepSeek) for a few dollars; the predicted error trends hold cell by cell, and the two refuted predictions, both on a strongly saturating response and traced to its curvature, are themselves matched quantitatively by the theory with no free parameters.

</details>


### [331] [AutoWorldModel-Bench: A State-Centric Benchmark for Automated World-Model Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11216)
*Marjan Moodi,Xuankang Zhu,Fernando De Mesentier Silva,Harold Chaput,Mohammad Reza Taesiri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoWorldModel-Bench，一个用于评估前沿编码智能体作为自主研究者进行开放式世界建模研究的闭环基准测试。该基准涵盖8个游戏环境，使用统一的结构化状态表示，在64次会话中Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63次会话中成功改进了初始世界模型，其中91%的改进属于非平凡的研究型修改。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模领域尚无统一范式，架构、训练目标和状态表示之间的交互复杂，没有单一方案能在所有环境中占优。这使得它成为评估AI编码智能体作为自主研究者的理想测试平台——因为改进方向不事先指定，不同于当前主流智能体基准中工程到规格的任务。现有基准缺乏对开放式研究能力的评估。

Method: 构建了一个闭环基准测试AutoWorldModel-Bench，编码智能体在固定计算预算下自主改进给定的世界模型起始代码。基准涵盖八个游戏环境，采用统一的结构化状态表示——从每个游戏中提取真实实体状态并通过共享张量格式消费——从而将动力学建模与感知隔离，实现每次运行仅需数分钟的快速迭代。

Result: 在64次会话中，Codex-5.4和Claude Opus 4.6在63次会话中改进了起始模型；在91%的会话中，获胜的编辑是非平凡的研究型修改——包括新目标函数、表示、滚动过程或架构变更——而非超参数调整。

Conclusion: 该基准提供了一个评估前沿编码智能体在开放式研究而非工程到规格问题上的能力的平台，填补了现有智能体基准的空白。

Abstract: World modeling is an unsettled field: architectures, training objectives, and state representations interact in complex ways, and no single recipe dominates across environments. This makes it an ideal testbed for AI coding agents acting as autonomous researchers--a setting in which the improvement direction is not specified in advance, unlike the engineering-to-spec tasks that dominate current agent benchmarks. We introduce AutoWorldModel-Bench, a closed-loop benchmark in which frontier coding agents autonomously improve a provided world-model starter under a fixed compute budget. The benchmark spans eight game environments under a unified structured-state representation--ground-truth entity state extracted from each game and consumed through a shared tensor format--which isolates dynamics modeling from perception and enables minutes-per-run iteration. Across 64 sessions, Codex-5.4 and Claude Opus 4.6 improve their starter on 63; in 91% of sessions the winning edit is a non-trivial research-style modification--a new objective, representation, rollout procedure, or architectural change--rather than a hyperparameter tweak. Our benchmark offers a setting in which frontier coding agents can be evaluated on open-ended research rather than engineering-to-spec problems.

</details>


### [332] [MaSRead: Content-Addressed Reading of Replicated Latent Stores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11218)
*Carlos Baquero,Luís Brito,João Resende*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MaSRead是一种针对复制型潜在状态存储的可寻址读取方法，通过在内容上路由查询（而非位置上），利用词元派生的不透明键标签集和硬注意力掩码实现选择性解码，使得多跳查询在图遍历下能够可靠地读取预先计算的状态片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个智能体可以在隐空间中共享计算状态（以键值缓存片段形式），并通过无冲突复制数据类型合并成存储。然而，合并后的存储存在关键问题：共置的片段会相互干扰，导致"共置"不等于"可寻址"——编码时未知的后续查询无法可靠地从合并缓存中读取数据。

Method: MaSRead将读取定向到内容而非位置：通过片段词汇派生的不透明键标签集进行路由，并在硬注意力掩码下解码每个选定片段（隐藏其他片段）。在词汇连通性假设下，使用图遍历到达多跳查询所需的多个片段。

Result: 在链式、流水线、对称、中心枢纽和自然语言存储等多种架构上，MaSRead能够隔离地恢复已访问片段，在无关片段积累时仍保持有效性，并可迁移到其他模型家族。路由后的物化解码复杂度取决于片段长度而非存储总大小，但端到端工作仍包含存储相关的路由和每个访问片段的一次读取。

Conclusion: 当所需片段在内容上与查询相连时，复制型潜在存储对后续查询变为可选择性地可读。但局限性明确：词汇路由可能遗漏不连通的证据，答案组合受限于冻结的阅读器模型。

Abstract: Independent agents that reason in latent space can share computed state as key-value cache fragments rather than text. Merged by a conflict-free replicated data type, these fragments form a store that converges under any delivery order or duplication. Yet a later query, unknown at encode time, cannot reliably read the merged cache: colocated fragments interfere, so colocation is not addressability. MaSRead addresses the read to content. It routes through opaque keyed tag sets derived from fragment words and decodes each selected fragment under a hard attention mask that hides the rest. Under lexical connectivity, a graph walk reaches the fragments required by a multi-hop query. Across chain, pipeline, symmetric, hub, and natural-language stores, MaSRead recovers visited fragments in isolation, remains effective as unrelated fragments accumulate, and transfers to another model family. After routing, materialized decoding depends on fragment length rather than total store size; end-to-end work still includes store-dependent routing and one read per visited fragment. The limits are explicit: lexical routing can miss disconnected evidence, and answer composition remains bounded by the frozen reader. Thus a replicated latent store becomes selectively readable for later queries when the needed fragments connect to the query through content.

</details>


### [333] [From Monolithic to Modular: Segment-level Automatic Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11219)
*Nikita Kulin,Viktor Zhuravlev,Artur Khairullin,Sergey Muravyov,Ilya Makarov,Daniil Sukhorukov,Ekaterina Averkova*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAPO是一种基于段级别的自动提示优化方法，将提示分解为角色、上下文、任务和输出格式几个部分，通过top-5和bottom-5示例进行针对性改进，在多个基准数据集上超越了零样本和多种强APO基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动提示优化（APO）方法通常以整体方式重写提示，这种"整体式"优化容易导致改进一个行为的同时损害其他行为，缺乏对提示不同功能段的精细控制。

Method: SAPO将提示分解为角色（role）、上下文（context）、任务（task）和输出格式（output format）四个段，基于top-5和bottom-5示例执行针对性的改进。优化循环使用单个LLM配合静态元提示和结构化输出来完成分段、弱点分析和候选生成。具体采用两阶段生成流程：(1)段级别的诊断和推荐提取；(2)受强弱段信号约束的候选合成。此外还设计了训练/验证协议。

Result: 在SQuADv2、TweetEval、XSUM、CommonGen和GSM8K数据集上，使用GPT-3.5-Turbo和GPT-4o-mini进行评估，SAPO相较于零样本基线以及APE、OPRO、EvoPrompt、GEPA和StraGO等强APO基线方法取得了最佳平均分数。

Conclusion: SAPO通过段级别的精细优化方式有效地解决了整体式提示优化中"顾此失彼"的问题，为自动提示优化提供了一种更可控、更有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Automatic Prompt Optimization (APO) often rewrites prompts monolithically, which can improve one behavior while degrading others. We present SAPO, a segment-level APO method that decomposes prompts into role, context, tasks, and output format, then applies targeted improvements based on top-5 and bottom-5 examples. The optimization loop uses one LLM with static meta-prompts and structured outputs for segmentation, weakness analysis, and candidate generation. We describe a train/validation protocol and a two-stage generation process: (1) segment-level diagnosis and recommendation extraction, (2) candidate synthesis constrained by weak/strong segment signals. Using the evaluation setup across SQuADv2, TweetEval, XSUM, CommonGen, and GSM8K on GPT-3.5-Turbo and GPT-4o-mini, SAPO achieves the best average score against Zero-shot and strong APO baselines including APE, OPRO, EvoPrompt, GEPA, and StraGO.

</details>


### [334] [LLMs in Process Diagram Engineering: From Optimal PFDs to Validated P&IDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11220)
*Timur Zakarin,Sergei Voitov,Sergei Shumilin,Evgeny Burnaev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了P&ID Pilot，一个端到端的AI流水线，用于自动生成工艺流程图（PFD）并将其转换为管道仪表图（P&ID）。通过对比四种方法，混合遗传算法（GA）与大型语言模型（LLM）的方法在PFD合成阶段表现最优，而LLM智能体在P&ID转换阶段实现了100%的执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前PFD的创建及其向P&ID的转化主要依赖人工完成，耗时费力且成本高昂。应用人工智能技术不仅能够实现流程自动化、节省时间，还能通过探索多种拓扑选项和减少人工劳动带来经济收益。

Method: 提出了两阶段端到端AI流水线：第一阶段使用四种方法（其中GA与LLM的混合方法效果最优）进行PFD合成；第二阶段使用基于LLM的智能体，通过受限的工程软件开发工具包（SDK）生成可执行修改，将生成的PFD转换为源代码可追溯的P&ID。

Result: 混合GA/LLM方法在所有方法中获得最低损失值，生成有效的PFD拓扑，满足出口流量参数要求且无工程规则违规。LLM智能体在P&ID转换中实现100%执行成功率，同时保持领域特定规则和参考图结构的合规性。

Conclusion: 耦合GA/LLM驱动的合成与LLM转换智能体的统一流水线，为端到端流程设计自动化提供了可行路径，能够生成经过验证、可部署的输出，显著减少人工工程设计工作量。

Abstract: Nowadays, the creation of a process flow diagram (PFD) and its subsequent transformation into a piping and instrumentation diagram (P&ID) is predominantly performed manually. Applying artificial intelligence in the task could potentially lead not only to process automation and time savings, but also to financial gains by exploring numerous diagram's topology options and reducing manual labor. This research presents P&ID Pilot - a practical end-to-end AI pipeline capable of handling flowsheet developing for both stages. The first stage focuses on PFD synthesis, whereas the second is directed toward modifying the generated PFD into P&ID. After comparing four different methods, the hybrid approach combining genetic algorithms (GA) and large language models (LLM) is shown to generate the optimal valid PFD topology, achieving the lowest loss value among all the methods, while satisfying the required outlet flow parameters without engineering-rule violations. For the second stage, the proposed LLM-based agent successfully transforms the generated PFD into a source-grounded P&ID by producing validated, executable modifications through a restricted engineering software development kit, achieving 100% execution success while maintaining compliance with domain-specific rules and reference graph structures. This unified pipeline - coupling GA/LLM-driven synthesis with an LLM-based transformation agent - offers a feasible path toward end-to-end process design automation by producing validated, deployable outputs and substantially reduces manual engineering effort.

</details>


### [335] [A Conceptual Framework for Refining Influence Knowledge from Simulation Evidence in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11221)
*Barbara da Silva Oliveira,Julien Deantoni,Nicolas Ferry*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个概念框架，利用新颖的"Influences"概念来支持仿真活动的迭代和增量细化，从而帮助理解和利用那些因复杂性、时间或领域经验不足而未被建模的环境中介交互，并通过一个移动机器人Simulink/Gazebo联合仿真案例进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络物理系统（CPS）由多个利益相关者开发，其行为源于各领域工件与运行环境的交互。仿真和联合仿真是分析CPS行为的关键手段，但许多环境中介的交互（超出直接感知和执行的交互）由于复杂性、时间或领域经验不足而未被建模，这阻碍了对仿真结果的正确理解与利用。

Method: 提出一个概念框架，引入新颖的"Influences"概念，支持对仿真活动进行迭代和增量式细化，以逐步深化对系统行为的理解。通过一个使用Simulink/Gazebo联合仿真的移动机器人案例研究进行演示。

Result: 通过移动机器人Simulink/Gazebo联合仿真案例研究，展示了该概念框架在促进仿真活动迭代细化和深化系统行为理解方面的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该概念框架通过"Influences"概念为处理未建模的环境中介交互提供了系统化的方法，能够支持仿真活动的迭代细化，从而更好地理解和利用仿真结果，提升对CPS行为的洞察力。

Abstract: Cyber-physical systems (CPS) are typically developed by multiple stakeholders who produce artefacts tailored to their specific domains of expertise. The behaviour of these systems emerges from the interaction between those artefacts and their operational environment. Simulation and co-simulation have become essential approaches for analysing CPS behaviour and, through simulation campaigns, developers can explore system responses under changing conditions, including interactions with the environment. However, the lack of details and understanding of some environmentmediated interactions (typically the ones beyond direct sensing and actuation), which remain unmodelled due to their complexity, a lack of time, or a lack of domain experience, hinders the proper comprehension and exploitation of simulation results. To address these limitations, we propose a conceptual framework leveraging the novel concept of Influences to support the iterative and incremental refinement of simulation campaigns and deepen the understanding of the system behaviour. We demonstrate the proposed approach through a case study involving a mobile robot implemented using Simulink/Gazebo co-simulation.

</details>


### [336] [Harnessing agent memory to build lifelong AI partners for materials scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11224)
*Siyu Liu,Bo Hu,Beilin Ye,He Cao,David J. Srolovitz,Tongqi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以持久记忆为核心的自演化记忆框架，用于材料科学研究中的AI伙伴设计。该框架将科学经验存储为可检查的事实和可执行的技能，使其可在模型间迁移和修订。在三个计算场景中验证，该方法显著提升了任务成功率，减少了错误和工具调用次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 材料研究的进步依赖于累积的经验——有效的脚本、可信的协议、失败计算的警示以及将新问题与旧结果关联的判断力。然而，这些经验通常分散在笔记本、仓库、作业日志和个人记忆中，难以跨AI代理移植，导致可重复性和知识传递面临挑战。

Method: 提出自演化记忆框架，将科学经验组织为可检查的事实（inspectable facts）和可执行的技能（executable skills）。该记忆框架独立于具体代理实现，支持观察结果、失败边界、协议和验证检查的检索、修订和跨模型迁移。在三个计算场景中进行评估：真实材料工具使用问题、元素固体状态方程计算和实际材料模拟工作流。

Result: 在49个真实材料工具使用问题（含138个可执行子任务）中，记忆使GPT-5.2任务成功率几乎翻倍且无需更新模型参数；在元素固体状态方程计算中，记忆将波函数初始化失败转化为执行前防护栏，Correct/Partial/Error结果从22/1/4改善为25/2/0，避免了92%的重复错误；在13个实际材料模拟工作流中，记忆技能和失败事实将聚合追踪负担（token）减半，工具调用在第三轮减少超过两倍，同时保持带隙、声子、空位和功函数分析的物理意义。

Conclusion: 代理记忆可以作为一种持久、可移植、自我改进的科学资产，其寿命超过任何单一模型或代理堆栈，为终身AI材料科学伙伴的设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Materials research advances through accumulated experience - scripts that work, protocols that are trusted, warnings attached to failed calculations or experiments, and judgement that links a new question to an old result. This experience is essential for reproducibility and knowledge transfer, yet it is usually fragmented across notebooks, repositories, job logs and individual memory, and it is rarely portable across artificial-intelligence agents. Here we argue that a lifelong AI partner for materials science can be designed around persistent memory rather than around a particular agent implementation. We introduce a self-evolving memory framework that stores scientific experience as inspectable facts and executable skills, so that observations, failure boundaries, protocols and validation checks can be retrieved, revised and migrated across models. We evaluate the idea in three computational settings that expose different layers of materials-research competence. In 49 real-world materials-tool-use questions comprising 138 executable subtasks, memory nearly doubles GPT-5.2 task success without model-parameter updates. In elemental-solid equation-of-state calculations, memory converts a wavefunction-initialization failure into a pre-execution guardrail, improving outcomes from 22/1/4 to 25/2/0 Correct/Partial/Error and avoiding 92% of repeated errors. In 13 practical material simulation workflows, remembered skills and failure facts halve the aggregate trace burden (tokens) and reduce tool calls by over a factor of two by the third round, while preserving physically meaningful outputs in band-gap, phonon, vacancy and work-function analyses. These results show that agent memory can serve as a durable scientific asset; a portable, self-improving record of materials-research experience that outlives any single model or agent stack.

</details>


### [337] [Identity from the Outside: A Conceptual Framework and Research Program for AI Personality Clones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11225)
*Luc E. Brunet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种关于AI"人格克隆"的运作性身份理论框架，通过三分标准（目标保真度、类人性、个体性）和六因子分解（基质、倾向、记忆、更新动力学、上下文、外部偶然性）来重建"身份"概念，并基于状态空间和随机消融定义不可区分性（1减去评判者的分辨优势）。核心主张是一个条件性猜想：在特定假设下，"可版本化"倾向损害长期不可区分性。文章还引入"委托者（delegate）"这一介于产品克隆与个体之间的第三类对象，最终论证长期正确标准不是轨迹保真度而是"气候保真度"——匹配一个人可能反应的"条件分布"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI人格克隆技术的发展，"个人身份"这一哲学概念需要在运作性/操作性层面被重新审视。作者有意搁置意识的难题，转而从观察者在一定时长内对表征（manifestations）的不可区分性出发，试图为克隆的评估提供可操作、可实验检验的框架，并澄清"身份"一词在实际使用中被混淆的多个维度。

Method: 方法包括：（1）将"身份"拆解为保真度、类人性、个体性三个标准；（2）提出六因子分解（基质、倾向、记忆、更新动力学、上下文、外部偶然性），并以状态空间形式化；（3）将不可区分性定义为1减去评判者的区分优势，用随机消融估计各因子的局部灵敏度；（4）提出"可版本化导致长期不可区分性退化"的条件性猜想；（5）借助lambda演算、线性类型和双模拟作类比分析；（6）引入"委托者"概念；最后建议以"气候保真度"（匹配条件分布）而非轨迹保真度作为长期标准。

Result: 本文提出了一套完整的概念工具：三分标准、六因子分解与状态空间表述、基于消融的灵敏度估计方法、条件性退化猜想、以及"委托者"这一新的克隆类型学对象。文章将现有实证文献映射到三个标准上，并提出了一项实验研究计划，最终以"气候保真度"替代轨迹保真度作为正确长期判据的论证收束。"最好的克隆是与原版如同原版与自身分岔一般地分岔"这一悖论式论断构成核心结论。

Conclusion: 作者认为，衡量克隆的关键并非让克隆在每条可能轨迹上与原版重合，而是让克隆在条件分布意义下与原版"以相同方式随时间演化分歧"。这一视角将身份从静态同一性转向动态随机过程的统计匹配，为AI人格克隆的评估提供了理论上更合理、实证上可检验的框架。

Abstract: AI "personality clones" force a re-examination of personal identity in operational terms. Setting aside the hard problem of consciousness, we approach identity through the indiscernibility of manifestations, as assessed by an observer over a duration. We distinguish three criteria that "identity" conflates: fidelity to a target person, generic human-likeness, and individuality. We propose a six-term factorization of observed identity (substrate, dispositions, memory, update dynamics, context, exogenous contingencies), with a state-space formulation. Indiscernibility is defined as one minus a judge's distinguishing advantage, and the factorization's coefficients become local sensitivities estimable by randomized ablation. The central claim is a conditional conjecture: given hypotheses about the agent's information on its own persistence and about consequences bearing on its own stakes, versionability tends to degrade long-horizon indiscernibility. An analogy with lambda-calculus, linear typing, and bisimulation clarifies what linearity does and does not establish. Between product-clone and individual we identify a third object, the delegate: a task-limited, bounded-lifespan partial clone ending in a bandwidth-limited testament. We map the empirical literature onto the three criteria, propose an experimental program, and argue that the correct long-horizon criterion is not trajectory fidelity but climate fidelity: matching the conditional distribution of a person's possible responses. The best clone is the one that diverges from the original as the original would have diverged from itself.

</details>


### [338] [Cutting AI Datacenter Energy with Reinforcement Learning: Measured Power Control of LLM Training from One GPU to the Fleet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11226)
*Eliseo Curcio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统刻画了强化学习（GRPO）后训练在GPU硬件上的功耗行为，并提出一种PPO元控制器，通过自适应调整生成参数来管理功耗。在7B/14B/72B规模、1-4块A100上的实验中，控制器将功耗限制违规降低89.8%，同时token输出提升18.1%，能效提升26.2%。本文还揭示了模型分片导致组大小执行器失效的问题，并基于占用率-容量原则重建执行器，在72B实时工作负载中实现了35.7%的产出提升和87.2%的违规减少，论证了约两倍名不虚传的过订阅可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型开发高度依赖RL后训练，但其在GPU硬件上的功耗行为从未被系统表征。当前数据中心采用静态上限和反应式节流等"工作负载不可知"的功耗管理机制，会不加区分地拖慢硬件。作者希望揭示RL训练的真实功耗特征，并设计一种能感知功耗、自适应调整工作负载参数的智能功耗管理方案。

Method: 作者在7B、14B和72B三种规模、1-4块A100 GPU上对GRPO训练进行半秒级功耗遥测（采集38万+样本），训练一个PPO元控制器，使其自适应调整工作负载自身的生成参数（如组大小、生成并发度）以响应实测功耗。同时通过执行器权威性扫描和在不同测量窗口（0.5s至5min）下分析功耗瞬变，诊断模型分片下的执行器失效问题，最终提出占用率-容量原则并重建控制器。

Result: 在完整500步7B轨迹上，控制器减少89.8%功耗限制违规，提升18.1% token输出和26.2%能效（token/MWh）。72B实时部署中原始控制器出现复制性零结果，归因于模型分片下组大小执行器失权；改用生成并发度作为执行器后可保留17-22%功耗权威，重建的控制器在72B真实rollout生成负载的三次重复实验中实现35.7%的产出提升（相对静态安全基线）、2.27±1.08%的预算违规率、87.2%的违规减少，并在受限控制器中取得最佳平均吞吐量和单位token能耗。在30秒测量窗口下瞬变从23.6%（0.5s分辨率）降至1.6%，5分钟时为零；16-GPU机队的复合场景在30秒及以上窗口零违规，峰值需求仅为铭牌值的50-56%，表明约两倍的过订阅（oversubscription）可行。

Conclusion: RL后训练的功耗可以通过自适应控制工作负载生成参数来有效管理，而非依赖静态上限或盲目的硬件节流。模型分片会导致某些执行器（如组大小）失去功耗调控能力，需遵循"占用率-容量"原则选择新执行器。在现实测量窗口下功耗瞬变被大幅平滑，使得GPU名不虚传功率的约两倍过订阅成为可能，带来显著的经济和碳排放效益。作者提供了量化分析和低成本运营试点方案供运营商验证。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning post-training dominates modern language-model development, yet its power behavior on GPU hardware has not been characterized, and datacenters manage GPU power with workload-blind mechanisms, static caps and reactive throttling, that slow hardware indiscriminately. We instrument GRPO training with half-second power telemetry at 7B, 14B, and 72B scales on one to four A100s (380,000+ samples), and train a PPO meta-controller that adapts the workload's own generation parameters to measured power. Against the full 500-step 7B trace, the controller cuts power-limit violations by 89.8% while increasing token output by 18.1% and energy efficiency by 26.2% (tokens per MWh). Deployed live at 72B, the same controller family yields replicated null results, diagnosed as the group-size actuator losing authority under model sharding. An actuator-authority sweep shows the same parameters applied as generation concurrency retain 17-22% power authority, isolating an occupancy-versus-volume principle; a controller rebuilt on that actuator controls a live 72B rollout-generation workload across three replications: 35.7% more output than a static safe baseline at 2.27 +/- 1.08% budget violations, 87.2% fewer violations than uncontrolled operation, and the best mean throughput and energy per token among constrained controllers, with an adaptive threshold rule matching it in one of three operating conditions. Under realistic measurement windows the original 72B transients fall from 23.6% at half-second resolution to 1.6% at 30 s and zero at 5 min; a composed 16-GPU fleet shows zero violations at 30 s and longer, with peak demand at 50-56% of nameplate. For this fleet mix, roughly twofold oversubscription of nameplate appears feasible, subject to operator validation. We quantify the economic and carbon consequences and specify a low-cost operator pilot.

</details>


### [339] [Forecasting Side Effects of Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11227)
*Chong Yong Ong,Alson Wei Jie Sim,Peixin Zhang,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了激活导向（activation steering）的副作用预测问题，通过构建覆盖67种行为的跨效应矩阵，发现副作用频繁、结构化且不对称，并证明副作用在施加导向前即可被预测：其大小主要取决于目标行为，方向可从模型的未导向表征中预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活导向虽然无需重新训练就能改变语言模型行为，但常产生意外的副作用，影响安全部署。因此核心动机是：能否在施加导向之前预判这些副作用，以便进行主动的安全审计和更明智的部署决策。

Method: 在三款开源语言模型上，基于67种行为的分类体系构建跨效应矩阵（cross-effect matrix），系统性地测量每种行为导向对其他行为的副作用。分析副作用的普遍性、结构性和不对称性，并将其与基于表征相似性的启发式方法对比。随后利用模型未导向的表征预测副作用的幅度与方向。

Result: 发现副作用普遍存在、具有结构特征且常呈不对称性，无法用现有基于相似性的启发式方法解释。副作用的幅度主要取决于目标行为本身，方向则可从模型未导向的表征中预测，且准确率显著高于简单基线。

Conclusion: 激活导向的副作用是系统性且可预测的，这使得提前进行安全审计和更有依据地部署导向干预成为可能。

Abstract: Activation steering modifies a language model by adding a learned direction to its hidden activations, enabling targeted behavioral changes without retraining. While effective, steering often produces unintended side effects on other behaviors, making it difficult to deploy safely. We therefore ask: can these side effects be forecasted before steering is applied? We answer this question by constructing a cross-effect matrix over a taxonomy of 67 behaviors across three open-weight language models. We find that side effects are common, structured, and often asymmetric, revealing interactions that cannot be explained by existing similarity-based heuristics. Despite this complexity, we show that side effects are largely predictable before steering is performed. Their magnitude depends primarily on the target behavior, while their direction can be forecasted from the model's unsteered representations with substantially higher accuracy than simple baselines. Our results demonstrate that activation steering has systematic and forecastable side effects, enabling proactive safety auditing and more informed deployment of steering interventions.

</details>


### [340] [Synchronizing Beliefs with Second-Order Theory-of-Mind in Human-Autonomy Teams (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11229)
*Jack Mirenzi,Henny Admoni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视基于偏好的奖励学习，提出了将人类教师视为"知道目标的主动设计者"而非"被动回答者"的框架，将偏好学习重构为人类-自主体团队协同问题：教师维护学习者模型以设计教学课程，学习者维护对教师模型的二阶模型以生成结构化偏好约束（理解陈述）保持同步。仿真表明知情教师优于学习者主导的选择，教师模型漂移会削弱优势，而理解陈述可修复这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统偏好奖励学习将人类教师视作被动回答学习者生成查询的"神谕"，忽略了教师最关键的优势——对目标本身的认知。一个知道目标的教师能够比任何学习者驱动的采集策略更高效地构造训练样例，且这一优势随奖励特征维度增长而扩大。但利用这一优势需要准确建模学习者的当前知识状态，因此需要新的框架来解决教师与学习者之间的信息同步问题。

Method: 将偏好学习重构为人类-自主体团队问题，耦合两个行为模型：（1）教师维护对学习者的模型以设计信息丰富的教学课程；（2）学习者维护对"教师对学习者模型"的二阶模型（ToM-2），通过生成结构化偏好约束（即理解陈述）使教师的模型与学习者的真实状态保持同步。在仿真中比较了知情教师主导策略与学习者主导策略的性能，并研究了交替教师场景下的模型漂移及理解语句的修复作用。

Result: 仿真结果显示：(1) 知情教师优于学习者主导的选择策略；(2) 交替教师导致的教师模型漂移会侵蚀这一优势；(3) 理解陈述能够修复模型漂移，且当教师的误差集中在特定方向而非均匀分布时，二阶（ToM-2）理解陈述优于均值信念陈述。

Conclusion: 偏好学习应从"被动教师"范式转向"主动教学"范式，将教师的知识优势纳入学习框架；人-机协同中的双向建模（教师建模学习者、学习者建模教师的教学假设）是高效对齐的关键；复杂度更高的二阶心理理论（ToM-2）建模在特定误差结构下具有显著价值。

Abstract: Comparative feedback, asking people which of two behaviors they prefer, has become a standard way to align robot and agent behavior with human intent when the reward itself cannot be specified directly. Preference-based reward learning typically casts the human teacher as a passive oracle answering learner-generated queries. We argue this forfeits the teacher's defining advantage: knowledge of the objective. A teacher who knows the target can construct training examples more efficiently than any learner-driven acquisition strategy, an advantage that widens as the reward's feature dimension grows. However, exploiting this advantage requires an accurate model of what the learner currently knows. We therefore recast preference learning as a human-autonomy team problem coupling two behavioral models: the teacher maintains a model of the learner to design an informative curriculum, and the learner maintains a second-order model of the teacher's model, emitting structured preference constraints (understanding statements) that keep the teacher's model of the learner synchronized. In simulation, an informed teacher outperforms learner-led selection; teacher-model drift under alternating teachers erodes this advantage; and understanding statements repair it, with second-order (ToM-2) statements outperforming mean-belief statements when the teacher's error about the learner is concentrated in a particular direction rather than spread evenly.

</details>


### [341] [The Edge-based Contiguous p-median Problem with Connections to Logistics Districting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11230)
*Zeyad Kassem,Adolfo R. Escobedo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于边的连续p-中位（ECpM）问题，用于将道路网络划分为若干紧湊且连通的区域。提出了两种二值规划模型：一种基于割集的B&C算法（指数级约束），另一种基于最短路径约束（SPC，多项式约束）。在拥有超过2,700个节点和3,400条边的路网上测试，SPC模型通过标准分支定界求解比割集B&C快达17倍。SPC约束被证明是边基p-中位（EpM）模型的超有效不等式。此外，SPC约束也成功应用于边基区域划分（EBD）问题，而基于割集的现有模型在12小时内无法找到可行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路网络划分（districting）在公共安全、物流配送和市政服务等领域具有重要应用，需要将路网划分为紧凑且连通的区域。现有的区域划分模型大多基于节点或需求点，而鲜有直接基于边的划分，且连通性约束往往导致模型求解困难。作者希望提出一个基于边的连续p-中位问题，并开发高效的求解方法。

Method: 提出ECpM问题的两个二值规划模型：(1) 基于割集连通性约束的模型，配合分支-切割（B&C）分离算法求解；(2) 基于最短路径连通性（SPC）约束的模型，使用多项式数量的约束，可直接用商业求解器求解。两个模型均引入网络距离度量。此外，将SPC约束推广到边基区域划分（EBD）问题，该问题额外要求工作平衡标准。

Result: 在超过2,700个节点、接近3,400条边的路网上实验，模型包含超过960万个二值变量。SPC模型配合标准分支定界求解，相对割集B&C实现计算速度提升最高达17倍。SPC约束被证明是EpM模型的超有效不等式（可剪掉整数可行解及部分最优解）。在EBD问题中，基于割集的现有模型对任何测试实例在12小时内均无法找到可行解，而SPC-based模型能求解大多数实例至最优。

Conclusion: 基于最短路径连通性（SPC）约束的模型在计算效率和可行性上均显著优于基于割集的B&C方法，可有效解决大规模路网划分问题。SPC约束作为EpM模型的超有效不等式，虽然可能排除部分最优解，但在实际求解中展现出巨大优势。该方法可成功扩展到带工作平衡约束的EBD问题，为实际道路网络划分应用提供了高效、可行的求解框架。

Abstract: This paper introduces the edge-based contiguous p-median (ECpM) problem to partition the roads in a network into a given number of compact and contiguous territories. Two binary programming models are introduced, both of which incorporate a network distance. The first model requires an exponential number of cut set-based constraints to model contiguity; it is paired with a separation scheme that usually generates only a small number of these constraints, namely, a branch-and-cut (B&C) algorithm. The second model utilizes a polynomial number of shortest-path constraints to model contiguity and can be solved with off-the-shelf solvers. The respective solution approaches are tested on road networks with over 2,700 nodes and close to 3,400 edges, yielding models with over 9.6 million binary variables. Solving the model based on shortest path contiguity (SPC) constraints via standard branch and bound attains speedups in computational time of up to 17x relative to the cut set-based B&C implementation. In addition, the SPC constraints are demonstrated to be supervalid inequalities of the edge-based p-median (EpM) model (i.e., for which contiguity is not explicitly required), meaning that they may cut off integer-feasible solutions and some, but not all, of the optimal solutions of this simpler problem. Finally, the paper explores structural insights and connections between ECpM and the edge-based districting (EBD) problem, which enforces an additional work balance criterion. An existing model that utilizes cut set-based contiguity constraints was unable to find a feasible solution within 12 hours for any of the tested instances, while an SPC-based EBD model was able to solve most of these to optimality.

</details>


### [342] [LinearKV: One Cached State Suffices for Position-Independent Caching in Hybrid LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11231)
*Yirui Liu,Ruoling Qi,Longwen Wang,Xuaner Wu,Jian Chen,Yuxin Jin,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LinearKV是一个针对混合LLM（如GDN和Mamba-2等既有线性递归层又有full-attention层的模型）的免训练PIC框架。其核心是解耦初始化：线性层将所有K个匹配的局部状态映射为单个初始状态，而full-attention层照常拼接KV。研究发现单个缓存状态足以作为线性层初始化器，而代数上更精确的组合全部K个状态（HYPIC做法）不仅不必要，在某些架构上甚至有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
